--// Core Initialization & Services
local Players, UserInputService, TweenService, RunService, CoreGui, Lighting =
    game:GetService("Players"), game:GetService("UserInputService"), game:GetService("TweenService"), game:GetService("RunService"),
    game:GetService("CoreGui"), game:GetService("Lighting")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer or Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
if not Player then
    Player = Players.LocalPlayer
end
repeat task.wait() until Player and Player.Parent
-- some executors inject before PlayerGui exists
pcall(function()
    Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
end)

local UI = {}
local Utils = {}

Utils.MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local DAHOOD_PLACE_ID, HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID = 2788229376, 9825515356
-- set by game-select screen (not only PlaceId)
local selectedGameMode, isDaHood, isHoodCustoms = nil, false, false


-- Snapshot + restore game lighting (kills custom pink fog etc.)
Utils._lightingSnapshot = Utils._lightingSnapshot or nil
Utils.captureGameLighting = function()
    pcall(function()
        Utils._lightingSnapshot = {
            FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
            FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
            FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
            Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
            OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
            Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
            ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
            GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
            ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
            ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
        }
        originalFogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
        originalFogStart = Lighting.FogStart
        originalFogColor = Lighting.FogColor
        originalAmbient = Lighting.Ambient
        originalBrightness = Lighting.Brightness
        originalClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then
            originalFov = workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView
        end
    end)
end

Utils.restoreGameLighting = function()
    pcall(function()
        -- kill script-owned effects
        for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            local n = e.Name or ""
            if n:find("Rays") or n:find("ForceHit") or n:find("Zenith") then
                pcall(function() e:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        local s = Utils._lightingSnapshot
        if s then
            Lighting.FogEnd = s.FogEnd
            Lighting.FogStart = s.FogStart
            Lighting.FogColor = s.FogColor
            Lighting.Ambient = s.Ambient
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = s.OutdoorAmbient
            Lighting.Brightness = s.Brightness
            Lighting.ClockTime = s.ClockTime
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = s.GlobalShadows
            pcall(function() Lighting.ColorShift_Top = s.ColorShift_Top end)
            pcall(function() Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = s.ColorShift_Bottom end)
        else
            if originalFogEnd ~= nil then Lighting.FogEnd = originalFogEnd end
            if originalFogStart ~= nil then Lighting.FogStart = originalFogStart end
            if originalFogColor ~= nil then Lighting.FogColor = originalFogColor end
            if originalAmbient ~= nil then Lighting.Ambient = originalAmbient end
            if originalBrightness ~= nil then Lighting.Brightness = originalBrightness end
            if originalClockTime ~= nil then Lighting.ClockTime = originalClockTime end
        end
        -- clear ColorCorrection left behind
        for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and (e.Name:find("Rays") or e.Name:find("Tint")) then
                pcall(function() e.Enabled = false; e:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

Utils.resetSessionDefaults = function()
    pcall(function()
        if silentAim then
            silentAim.enabled = false
            silentAim.fovCircle = false
            silentAim.tracer = false
            silentAim.visualizer = false
        end
        if camlock then
            camlock.enabled = false
            camlock.fovCircle = false
            if camlock.zoomAim ~= nil then camlock.zoomAim = false end
        end
        if Utils.forceHit then
            Utils.forceHit.enabled = false
            Utils.forceHit.current = nil
        end
        if Utils.flameLock then Utils.flameLock.enabled = false end
        if Utils.trigger then Utils.trigger.enabled = false end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        espActive = false
        espShowNames = false
        espShowDistance = false
        espShowBox = false
        espShowHealth = false
        hitboxActive = false
        if clearAllESP then clearAllESP() end
    end)
    -- ALWAYS force fog / lighting toggles off and restore game defaults
    pcall(function()
        fogActive = false
        fullbrightActive = false
        forceTimeActive = false
        brightnessActive = false
        ambienceActive = false
        fovActive = false
        if Utils.pl then
            Utils.pl.bloomOn = false
            Utils.pl.sunraysOn = false
            Utils.pl.dofOn = false
        end
        if Utils.env then
            Utils.env.tintOn = false
            Utils.env.outdoorOn = false
        end
        Utils.restoreGameLighting()
        if originalFov and workspace.CurrentCamera then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = originalFov
        end
        if Utils.applyFullbright then pcall(Utils.applyFullbright, false) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        speedActive = false
        spinbotActive = false
        if Utils.mov then
            Utils.mov.freecam = false
            Utils.mov.fly = false
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj then Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj.Visible = false end
        if silentVisObj then silentVisObj.Visible = false end
        if silentTracerObj then silentTracerObj.Visible = false end
        if silentFovCircleObj then silentFovCircleObj.Visible = false end
        if camlockFovCircleObj then camlockFovCircleObj.Visible = false end
    end)
end

local function applyGameMode(mode)
    selectedGameMode = mode
    pcall(function()
        fogActive = false
        fullbrightActive = false
        forceTimeActive = false
        if Utils and Utils.resetSessionDefaults then Utils.resetSessionDefaults() end
        if Utils and Utils.restoreGameLighting then Utils.restoreGameLighting() end
    end)
    if mode == "Da Hood" then
        isDaHood = true
        isHoodCustoms = false
    elseif mode == "Hood Customs" then
        isDaHood = false
        isHoodCustoms = true
        pcall(function()
            if Utils and Utils.skinSetEnabled then Utils.skinSetEnabled(false) end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.15)
            if selectedGameMode ~= "Hood Customs" then return end
            pcall(function()
                if Utils.hcSetEnabled then Utils.hcSetEnabled(true) end
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)
            if selectedGameMode ~= "Hood Customs" then return end
            pcall(function()
                if Utils.hcSetEnabled then Utils.hcSetEnabled(false) end
                if Utils.hcRestoreGunVisuals then Utils.hcRestoreGunVisuals() end
            end)
        end)
    else
        -- Universal / MM2: no extra game modules yet
        isDaHood = false
        isHoodCustoms = false
    end
end

function Utils.themeHasBgImage(name)
    name = name or currentThemeName
    return name == "Midnight" or name == "Crimson" or name == "Cotton Candy"
        or name == "Bubblegum" or name == "Ash" or name == "Galaxy"
end

--// Connection tracking (for clean eject)
local trackedConns = {}
function Utils.track(conn)
    if conn then table.insert(trackedConns, conn) end
    return conn
end

    Hide = "rbxassetid://137957059235157",
    Unhide = "rbxassetid://115916891254154",
    Startup = "rbxassetid://131390520971848",
    Eject = "rbxassetid://1524543584"
}

function Utils.playSound(id, volume)
    local ok, snd = pcall(function()
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.SoundId = id
        s.Volume = volume or 1.2
        s.Parent = Utils.SoundService
        s:Play()
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s, 3)
        return s
    end)
    if not ok then end
end

function Utils.playClick() Utils.playSound(SoundIds.Click, 2) end

-- Keybind helpers (keyboard + mouse buttons)
local MOUSE_BIND_TYPES = {
    [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1] = true,
    [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2] = true,
    [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3] = true,
}

local function bindDisplayName(bind)
    if not bind then return "None" end
    local ok, name = pcall(function() return bind.Name end)
    if not (ok and name) then return "None" end
    if name == "MouseButton1" then return "M1" end
    if name == "MouseButton2" then return "M2" end
    if name == "MouseButton3" then return "M3" end
    return name
end

local function captureBindFromInput(input)
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
        return nil, true
    end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
        if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
            return input.KeyCode, false
        end
    elseif MOUSE_BIND_TYPES[input.UserInputType] then
        return input.UserInputType, false
    end
    return nil, false
end

local function inputMatchesBind(input, bind)
    if not bind then return false end
    local ok, enumType = pcall(function() return bind.EnumType end)
    if not ok or not enumType then return false end
    if enumType == Enum.KeyCode then
        return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == bind
    end
    if enumType == Enum.UserInputType then
        return input.UserInputType == bind
    end
    return false
end

local THEME_NAMES = {"Crimson", "Bubblegum", "Ash", "Cotton Candy", "Midnight", "Galaxy", "Ocean", "Lavender", "Hacker"}
local currentThemeName, panelBgVisible, Theme = "Midnight", false, {}
for k, v in pairs(ThemePresets["Midnight"]) do
    Theme[k] = v
end
-- optional override; when set, keeps Accent even after applyTheme
Utils.themeCustom = Utils.themeCustom or { accent = nil, text = nil, outline = nil, bg = nil, outlineOn = true }

--// Registries
local accentRegistry, fontSizeRegistry, knobRegistry, trackRegistry = {}, {}, {}, {}

local function dimAccentTrack()
    local a = Theme.Accent or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    -- dark track so only the fill shows accent
    return Color3.new(a.R * 0.22 + 0.08, a.G * 0.22 + 0.08, a.B * 0.22 + 0.08)
end

function UI.regTrack(inst)
    if inst then table.insert(trackRegistry, inst) end
    return inst
end

function Utils.applyAccentEverywhere()
    local a = Theme.Accent
    if Utils.themeCustom.accent then
        Theme.Accent = Utils.themeCustom.accent
        a = Utils.themeCustom.accent
    end
    for _, e in ipairs(accentRegistry) do
        if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then
            pcall(function() e.inst[e.prop] = a end)
        end
    end
    local trackCol = dimAccentTrack()
    for _, inst in ipairs(trackRegistry) do
        if inst and inst.Parent then
            pcall(function() inst.BackgroundColor3 = trackCol end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if FpsLabel then FpsLabel.TextColor3 = a end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if MainStroke then
            if Utils.themeCustom.outline then
                MainStroke.Color = Utils.themeCustom.outline
            elseif currentThemeName == "Midnight" then
                MainStroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            else
                MainStroke.Color = Theme.Border or a
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if AP and AP.Stroke and Utils.themeCustom.accent then
            AP.Stroke.Color = a
        end
    end)
    -- refresh active toggle switches (on = accent)
    pcall(function()
        for _, d in ipairs(MainFrame:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("TextButton") and d.Size.X.Offset == 36 and d.Size.Y.Offset == 18 then
                -- on-state roughly: higher R/G/B or matches old accent - re-apply by indicator position
                local ind = d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
                if ind and ind.Position.X.Offset >= 18 then
                    d.BackgroundColor3 = a
                    d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(Utils.applyThemeOverrides)
end

function Utils.applyThemeOverrides()
    local U = Utils.ui or {}
    local preset = ThemePresets[currentThemeName] or ThemePresets.Midnight
    if Utils.themeCustom.text then
        Theme.Text = Utils.themeCustom.text
        Theme.TextDark = Color3.new(
            Utils.themeCustom.text.R * 0.7 + 0.08,
            Utils.themeCustom.text.G * 0.7 + 0.08,
            Utils.themeCustom.text.B * 0.7 + 0.08
        )
    else
        Theme.Text = preset.Text
        Theme.TextDark = preset.TextDark
    end
    local title = U.TitleLabel
    if title then title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end
    if U.CloseButton then U.CloseButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end
    if U.MinimizeButton then U.MinimizeButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end
    if Utils.sideSearch then
        Utils.sideSearch.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        Utils.sideSearch.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    end
    local gui = U.ScreenGui
    if gui then
        for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
            if d.Name == "RaysTitle" or d.Name == "RaysTitle" then
                d.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            end
            if d.Name == "EaseSeg" then
                d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
            elseif d.Name == "RaysPartPick" or d:GetAttribute("RaysPartPick") then
                if d:GetAttribute("PartOn") then
                    d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if Utils.themeCustom.outline then
        local oc = Utils.themeCustom.outline
        Theme.Border = oc
        if U.MainStroke then U.MainStroke.Color = oc end
        if AP and AP.Stroke then AP.Stroke.Color = oc end
        if gui then
            for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    d.Color = oc
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if Utils.themeCustom.bg then
        local raw = Utils.themeCustom.bg
        local minC = 18 / 255
        local bg = Color3.new(math.max(raw.R, minC), math.max(raw.G, minC), math.max(raw.B, minC))
        Utils.themeCustom.bg = bg
        Theme.Primary = bg
        Theme.Secondary = Color3.new(math.clamp(bg.R * 0.72, 0, 1), math.clamp(bg.G * 0.72, 0, 1), math.clamp(bg.B * 0.72, 0, 1))
        Theme.GradTop = Color3.new(math.clamp(bg.R * 0.55, 0, 1), math.clamp(bg.G * 0.55, 0, 1), math.clamp(bg.B * 0.55, 0, 1))
        Theme.GradMid = bg
        Theme.GradBottom = Color3.new(math.clamp(bg.R * 0.4, 0, 1), math.clamp(bg.G * 0.4, 0, 1), math.clamp(bg.B * 0.4, 0, 1))
        Theme.CardColor = Theme.Secondary
        if U.MainFrame then
            U.MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = bg
            U.MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        end
        if U.MainGradient then
            U.MainGradient.Enabled = true
            U.MainGradient.Rotation = 45
            U.MainGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.GradTop),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.GradMid),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.GradBottom),
            })
        end
        if U.Sidebar then
            U.Sidebar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.CardColor or Theme.Secondary
            U.Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
        end
        if U.PageScroll then
            U.PageScroll.BackgroundColor3 = bg
        end
        if AP and AP.Frame then
            AP.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = bg
            if AP.Grad then
                AP.Grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.GradTop),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.GradMid),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.GradBottom),
                })
            end
        end
        if U.TopBar then
            U.TopBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end
    end
    if U.Sidebar then
        U.Sidebar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.CardColor or Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
        U.Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    end
    if U.MainStroke then
        U.MainStroke.Enabled = Utils.themeCustom.outlineOn ~= false
        if U.OutlineGrad then
            local oc = U.MainStroke.Color
            U.OutlineGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, oc),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.new(math.clamp(oc.R * 1.25, 0, 1), math.clamp(oc.G * 1.25, 0, 1), math.clamp(oc.B * 1.25, 0, 1))),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, oc),
            })
        end
    end
end


function UI.regAccent(inst, prop)
    table.insert(accentRegistry, {inst = inst, prop = prop})
    return inst
end

function UI.regKnob(inst)
    table.insert(knobRegistry, inst)
    return inst
end

function UI.applyPanelFont(inst)
    if not inst then return inst end
    pcall(function()
        inst.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    end)
    return inst
end

function UI.regFont(inst, base)
    table.insert(fontSizeRegistry, {inst = inst, base = base})
    UI.applyPanelFont(inst)
    return inst
end

function UI.addCorner(parent, radius)
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
    corner.Parent = parent
end

function UI.addGlassStroke(inst, thick)
    if not inst then return nil end
    local st = inst:FindFirstChild("GlassStroke")
    if not st then
        st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Name = "GlassStroke"
        st.Parent = inst
    end
    st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    st.Transparency = 0.76
    st.Thickness = thick or 1
    st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    st.Enabled = glassUIActive and true or false
    return st
end

ScreenGui.Name = "RaysPanelCollapsible"
ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = false

local successGui = pcall(function()
    ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui
end)
if not successGui then
    ScreenGui.Parent = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

--// Main Frame (Matcha-like wide layout)
local PANEL_W, PANEL_H = 780, 800
local MainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
MainFrame.Name = "RaysMain"
MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, PANEL_H)
MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -math.floor(PANEL_W / 2), 0.5, -math.floor(PANEL_H / 2))
MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
MainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
MainFrame.Active = true
MainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
MainFrame.Parent = ScreenGui
UI.addCorner(MainFrame, UDim.new(0, 10))

function UI.cardColor()
    if Theme.CardColor then
        return Theme.CardColor
    end
    if Theme.CardT and Theme.CardT > 0 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 214)
    end
    return Theme.Secondary
end

function UI.cardTransparency()
    return Theme.CardT or 0
end

function UI.styleCard(inst)
    if not inst then return inst end
    if inst.Name == "RaysMain" or inst.Name == "TopBar" or inst.Name == "TopBarCover" or inst.Name == "PanelBgImage" or inst.Name == "RaysToggle" then
        return inst
    end
    inst.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    inst.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    local st = inst:FindFirstChild("FeatureStroke")
    if not st then
        st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Name = "FeatureStroke"
        st.Parent = inst
    end
    st.Enabled = false
    st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    return inst
end

function Utils.unifyFeatureSurfaces(root)
    if not root then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
            local skip = d.BackgroundTransparency >= 0.98
            if d:IsA("Frame") and d.Parent and d.Parent:IsA("TextButton") and d.Size.Y.Scale == 1 and d.Size.X.Scale > 0 then
                skip = true
            end
            if d.Size.X.Offset > 0 and d.Size.X.Offset <= 22 and d.Size.Y.Offset <= 22 then
                skip = true
            end
            if (d.Size.X.Offset == 36 and d.Size.Y.Offset == 18) or (d.Size.X.Offset == 14 and d.Size.Y.Offset == 14) then
                skip = true
            end
            if d.Name == "ColorPreview" or d.Name == "SVMap" or d.Name == "HueBar" or d.Name == "RaysToggle" or d.Name == "RaysPartPick" or d:GetAttribute("RaysPartPick") or d.Name == "EaseGridH" or d.Name == "EaseGridV" or d.Name == "EasePlot" then
                skip = true
            end
            if d:IsA("Frame") and d.BackgroundColor3 == Theme.Accent then
                skip = true
            end
            if not skip then
                UI.styleCard(d)
            end
        end
    end
end

local MainStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

    local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    grad.Name = "OutlineGrad"
    grad.Rotation = 45
    grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, MainStroke.Color),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.new(
            math.clamp(MainStroke.Color.R * 1.25, 0, 1),
            math.clamp(MainStroke.Color.G * 1.25, 0, 1),
            math.clamp(MainStroke.Color.B * 1.25, 0, 1)
        )),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, MainStroke.Color),
    })
    grad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.35),
    })
    grad.Parent = MainStroke
    Utils.ui = Utils.ui or {}
    Utils.ui.OutlineGrad = grad
end
Utils.ui = Utils.ui or {}

function Utils.updatePanelBgImage()
    if not PanelBgImage then return end
    local id, t = nil, 0.97
    if currentThemeName == "Midnight" then
        id = "rbxassetid://17793479623"
        t = 0.88
    elseif currentThemeName == "Crimson" then
        id = "rbxassetid://79903315865408"
        t = 0.94
    elseif currentThemeName == "Cotton Candy" then
        id = "rbxassetid://14424561053"
        t = 0.78
    elseif currentThemeName == "Bubblegum" then
        id = "rbxassetid://97023542098667"
        t = 0.82
    elseif currentThemeName == "Ash" then
        id = "rbxassetid://138505460833612"
        t = 0.9
    elseif currentThemeName == "Galaxy" then
        id = "rbxassetid://1025773173"
        t = 0.9
    end
    local show = panelBgVisible and id ~= nil
    PanelBgImage.Visible = show
    if id then
        PanelBgImage.Image = id
    end
    PanelBgImage.ImageTransparency = t
    if AP and AP.Bg then
        AP.Bg.Visible = show
        if id then AP.Bg.Image = id end
        AP.Bg.ImageTransparency = t
    end
end

-- Glass / blur UI (Matcha-style). Toggle in Settings.
local glassUIActive, glassRegistry, worldBlur, cardRegistry = false, {}, nil, {}
function UI.regCard(inst)
    if inst then table.insert(cardRegistry, inst) end
    return inst
end

function UI.regGlass(inst, solidT, glassT)
    if not inst then return inst end
    table.insert(glassRegistry, {
        inst = inst,
        solid = solidT or 0,
        glass = glassT or 0.42,
    })
    UI.regCard(inst)
    return inst
end

function Utils.applyGlassUI(on)
    glassUIActive = on and true or false
    MainStroke.Enabled = true
    MainStroke.Thickness = 2
    MainStroke.Transparency = glassUIActive and 0.2 or 0.05
    MainStroke.Color = Theme.Border or Color3.fromRGB(99, 18, 22)
    TopBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    if TopBarCover then
        TopBarCover.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    end
    if glassUIActive then
        MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.42
        if not worldBlur or not worldBlur.Parent then
            worldBlur = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysGlassBlur")
            if not worldBlur then
                worldBlur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
                worldBlur.Name = "RaysGlassBlur"
                worldBlur.Parent = Lighting
            end
        end
        worldBlur.Size = 18
        worldBlur.Enabled = true
    else
        MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        if worldBlur then
            worldBlur.Enabled = false
        end
    end
    for _, e in ipairs(glassRegistry) do
        if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                UI.styleCard(e.inst)
                if glassUIActive then
                    e.inst.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp((e.glass or 0.55), 0.35, 0.8)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    for _, inst in ipairs(cardRegistry) do
        if inst and inst.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                UI.styleCard(inst)
                if glassUIActive then
                    inst.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp((Theme.CardT or 0.5) + 0.18, 0.4, 0.82)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if Utils.updateSidebarSelection then
            Utils.updateSidebarSelection()
        end
    end)
end

-- Draggable Window
local draggingMain, dragInputMain, dragStartMain, startPosMain
TopBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingMain = true
        dragStartMain = input.Position
        startPosMain = MainFrame.Position
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                draggingMain = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)

TopBar.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragInputMain = input
    end
end)

Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input == dragInputMain and draggingMain then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStartMain
        local targetPos = UDim2.new(
            startPosMain.X.Scale,
            startPosMain.X.Offset + delta.X,
            startPosMain.Y.Scale,
            startPosMain.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )
        TweenService:Create(MainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.06, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = targetPos}):Play()
    end
end))

Utils.forceHitValid = function(plr)
    if not plr or plr == Player or not plr.Character then return false end
    local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    -- Health check / floor KO (always when silentAim.healthCheck or forceHit.knockCheck)
    if (silentAim and silentAim.healthCheck) or Utils.forceHit.knockCheck then
        if Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character) then return false end
        local knocked = false
        pcall(function()
            local be = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
            if be then
                for _, n in ipairs({"K.O", "KO", "Knocked", "Downed"}) do
                    local v = be:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if v and ((typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value) or v == true) then
                        knocked = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if knocked then return false end
    end
    if false and Utils.forceHit.knockCheck then
        local knocked = false
        pcall(function()
            local be = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
            if be and be:FindFirstChild("K.O") then
                knocked = be["K.O"].Value == true
            end
        end)
        if knocked or Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character) then return false end
    end
    do
        local ok, wl = pcall(function() return Utils.skipProtected(plr) end)
        if ok and wl then return false end
    end
    return true
end


-- early stub; replaced later with full implementation if needed
Utils.silentPartValid = Utils.silentPartValid or function(part)
    if not part or not part.Parent then return false end
    local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not model then return false end
    local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    if silentAim and silentAim.healthCheck and Utils.isCharacterDowned then
        local ok, down = pcall(Utils.isCharacterDowned, model)
        if ok and down then return false end
    end
    local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
    if not plr or plr.Character ~= model then return false end
    return true
end

Utils.forceHitClosestPart = function(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    -- Da Hood Force Hit: torso only
    if selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" then
        return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    -- Hood Customs: closest body part to mouse within FOV logic (all parts)
    local mouseLoc = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then
        return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
    for _, name in ipairs(Utils.forceHit.parts or {}) do
        local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if part then
            local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if on then
                local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mouseLoc).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then
                    bestD = d
                    best = part
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

Utils.forceHitTarget = function()
    local mouseLoc = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil end
    local best, bestD = nil, tonumber(Utils.forceHit.fov) or 100
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if Utils.forceHitValid(plr) then
            local part = Utils.forceHitClosestPart(plr)
            if part then
                local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                if on then
                    local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mouseLoc).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD then
                        bestD = d
                        best = part
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

Utils.forceHitBarrel = function()
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if tool then
        local h = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or tool:FindFirstChild("Barrel") or tool:FindFirstChild("Muzzle")
        if h and h:IsA("BasePart") then return h.Position end
    end
    local arm = char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
    if arm and arm:IsA("BasePart") then return arm.Position end
    return char:GetPivot().Position
end

Utils.forceHitTracer = function(a, b)
    if not a or not b or (b - a).Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
    pcall(function()
        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        beam.Segments = 1
        beam.Width0 = 0.1
        beam.Width1 = 0.1
        beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0))
        beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.4)
        beam.FaceCamera = true
        a0.WorldPosition = a
        a1.WorldPosition = b
        a0.Parent = workspace.Terrain
        a1.Parent = workspace.Terrain
        beam.Attachment0 = a0
        beam.Attachment1 = a1
        beam.Parent = workspace.Terrain
        task.delay(0.08, function()
            pcall(function() beam:Destroy() end)
            pcall(function() a0:Destroy() end)
            pcall(function() a1:Destroy() end)
        end)
    end)
end

-- Local bullet VFX matching normal HC multi-pellet beams + muzzle flash.
-- Force-hit never runs the gun fire path, so we spawn these client-side only.
Utils.forceHitShowBullet = function(a, b)
    if not a or not b then return end
    local delta = b - a
    if delta.Magnitude < 0.05 then return end

    pcall(function()
        local rng = Random.new()
        local dir = delta.Unit
        local dist = delta.Magnitude
        local vizType = tostring(Utils.forceHit.bulletVizType or "Bullets")
        local yellow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 228, 55)
        local red = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)

        local function makeBeam(fromPos, toPos, color, width0, width1, lifetime, segments)
            local p0 = Instance.new("Part")
            p0.Anchored = true
            p0.CanCollide = false
            p0.CanQuery = false
            p0.CanTouch = false
            p0.Transparency = 1
            p0.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)
            p0.CFrame = CFrame.new(fromPos)
            p0.Parent = workspace.Terrain
            local p1 = Instance.new("Part")
            p1.Anchored = true
            p1.CanCollide = false
            p1.CanQuery = false
            p1.CanTouch = false
            p1.Transparency = 1
            p1.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)
            p1.CFrame = CFrame.new(toPos)
            p1.Parent = workspace.Terrain
            local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
            att0.Parent = p0
            local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
            att1.Parent = p1
            local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
            beam.Attachment0 = att0
            beam.Attachment1 = att1
            beam.FaceCamera = true
            beam.LightEmission = 1
            beam.LightInfluence = 0
            beam.Segments = segments or 4
            beam.Texture = ""
            beam.Width0 = width0 or 0.08
            beam.Width1 = width1 or 0.06
            beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
            beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.15)
            beam.Parent = p0
            task.delay(lifetime or 0.28, function()
                pcall(function() p0:Destroy() end)
                pcall(function() p1:Destroy() end)
            end)
            return beam, p0
        end

        -- Muzzle flash (Bullets / Lightning only)
        if vizType ~= "Laser" then
            pcall(function()
                local char = Player.Character
                local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                local handle = tool and (tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") or tool:FindFirstChild("Gun") or tool.PrimaryPart)
                local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
                light.Brightness = 2.0
                light.Range = 11
                light.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                if handle then
                    light.Parent = handle
                else
                    local host = Instance.new("Part")
                    host.Anchored = true
                    host.CanCollide = false
                    host.Transparency = 1
                    host.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
                    host.CFrame = CFrame.new(a)
                    host.Parent = workspace.Terrain
                    light.Parent = host
                    task.delay(0.35, function() pcall(function() host:Destroy() end) end)
                end
                task.delay(0.2, function() pcall(function() light:Destroy() end) end)
            end)
        end

        if vizType == "Laser" then
            -- Neon red solid line, fades after 0.5s
            local p0 = Instance.new("Part")
            p0.Anchored = true
            p0.CanCollide = false
            p0.CanQuery = false
            p0.Transparency = 1
            p0.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)
            p0.CFrame = CFrame.new(a)
            p0.Parent = workspace.Terrain
            local p1 = Instance.new("Part")
            p1.Anchored = true
            p1.CanCollide = false
            p1.CanQuery = false
            p1.Transparency = 1
            p1.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05)
            p1.CFrame = CFrame.new(b)
            p1.Parent = workspace.Terrain
            local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
            att0.Parent = p0
            local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
            att1.Parent = p1
            -- Core beam
            local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
            beam.Attachment0 = att0
            beam.Attachment1 = att1
            beam.FaceCamera = true
            beam.LightEmission = 1
            beam.LightInfluence = 0
            beam.Segments = 2
            beam.Width0 = 0.2
            beam.Width1 = 0.16
            beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30))
            beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.05)
            beam.Parent = p0
            -- Soft glow shell
            local glow = Instance.new("Beam")
            glow.Attachment0 = att0
            glow.Attachment1 = att1
            glow.FaceCamera = true
            glow.LightEmission = 1
            glow.LightInfluence = 0
            glow.Segments = 2
            glow.Width0 = 0.45
            glow.Width1 = 0.35
            glow.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80))
            glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.55)
            glow.Parent = p0
            -- Neon point lights along path
            for _, pos in ipairs({ a, a:Lerp(b, 0.5), b }) do
                local host = Instance.new("Part")
                host.Anchored = true
                host.CanCollide = false
                host.Transparency = 1
                host.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
                host.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
                host.Parent = workspace.Terrain
                local pl = Instance.new("PointLight")
                pl.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                pl.Brightness = 3.5
                pl.Range = 14
                pl.Parent = host
                task.delay(0.55, function() pcall(function() host:Destroy() end) end)
            end
            local t0 = tick()
            local conn
            conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local alpha = math.clamp((tick() - t0) / 0.5, 0, 1)
                local tr = 0.05 + alpha * 0.95
                local trGlow = 0.55 + alpha * 0.45
                pcall(function()
                    beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(tr)
                    glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(trGlow)
                    beam.Width0 = 0.2 * (1 - alpha * 0.4)
                    beam.Width1 = 0.16 * (1 - alpha * 0.4)
                    glow.Width0 = 0.45 * (1 - alpha * 0.4)
                    glow.Width1 = 0.35 * (1 - alpha * 0.4)
                end)
                if alpha >= 1 then
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                    pcall(function() p0:Destroy() end)
                    pcall(function() p1:Destroy() end)
                end
            end)
            return
        end

        if vizType == "Lightning" then
            -- Yellow neon zig-zag bolt (moderate spread, no point lights)
            local segs = 9
            local points = { a }
            local right = dir:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
            if right.Magnitude < 0.05 then right = dir:Cross(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)) end
            right = right.Unit
            local up = dir:Cross(right).Unit
            local spreadScale = 1.5 -- ~1-2 stud lateral jitter
            for i = 1, segs - 1 do
                local t = i / segs
                local base = a + dir * (dist * t)
                local jig = (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 2 * spreadScale
                local jig2 = (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 2 * spreadScale
                points[#points + 1] = base + right * jig + up * jig2
            end
            points[#points + 1] = b
            local boltYellow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 60)
            local boltCore = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 180)
            for i = 1, #points - 1 do
                makeBeam(points[i], points[i + 1], boltYellow, 0.28, 0.12, 0.28, 2)
                makeBeam(points[i], points[i + 1], boltCore, 0.1, 0.04, 0.28, 2)
            end
            return
        end

        -- Bullets: multi-pellet only for shotguns / DB
        local tool = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local tname = tool and tool.Name or ""
        local multi = (tname == "[DoubleBarrel]" or tname == "[Shotgun]" or tname == "[TacticalShotgun]" or tname == "[DrumShotgun]")
        local pelletCount = multi and 5 or 1
        local spreadDeg = multi and (tonumber(Utils.forceHit.visSpread) or 1.8) or 0
        local lifetime = 0.28
        for i = 1, pelletCount do
            local endPos = b
            if pelletCount > 1 then
                local right = dir:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
                if right.Magnitude < 0.05 then right = dir:Cross(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)) end
                right = right.Unit
                local up = dir:Cross(right).Unit
                local ox = (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 2 * math.rad(spreadDeg)
                local oy = (rng:NextNumber() - 0.5) * 2 * math.rad(spreadDeg)
                local pelletDir = (dir + right * math.tan(ox) + up * math.tan(oy)).Unit
                endPos = a + pelletDir * dist
            end
            makeBeam(a, endPos, yellow, 0.085, 0.062, lifetime, 4)
        end
    end)
end

Utils.forceHitFire = function(targetPart)
    if not targetPart then return end
    if Utils.forceHit.tpBusy then return end

    -- ========== Da Hood only: silent getAim path (does not touch HC) ==========
    if selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" then
        Utils.forceHit.current = targetPart
        if silentAim then silentAim.bulletSpread = 0 end
        return
    end

    -- ========== Hood Customs: original Force Hit (pre-DH) ==========
    local function shootAt(part, hrpPos)
        if not part or not part.Parent then return end
        Utils.forceHit.current = part
        local impactPos = part.Position
        local nrm = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        local hits = {
            { Normal = nrm, Instance = part, Position = impactPos },
            { Normal = nrm, Instance = part, Position = impactPos },
            { Normal = nrm, Instance = part, Position = impactPos },
            { Normal = nrm, Instance = part, Position = impactPos },
            { Normal = nrm, Instance = part, Position = impactPos },
        }
        local extras = {
            { thePart = part, theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) },
            { thePart = part, theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) },
            { thePart = part, theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) },
            { thePart = part, theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) },
            { thePart = part, theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) },
        }
        local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        pcall(function()
            local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local ev = RS:FindFirstChild("MainEvent") or RS:FindFirstChild("MAINEVENT")
            if not ev then return end
            pcall(function() ev:FireServer("Shoot", { hits, extras, hrpPos, hrpPos, now }) end)
            pcall(function() ev:FireServer("Shoot", hits, extras, hrpPos, hrpPos, now) end)
        end)
        local barrelPos = Utils.forceHitBarrel()
        if Utils.forceHit.showBullets and barrelPos then
            Utils.forceHitShowBullet(barrelPos, impactPos)
        end
    end

    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not Utils.forceHit.tpMode or not hrp then
        shootAt(targetPart, hrp and hrp.Position or Vector3.zero)
        return
    end
    local origin = hrp.CFrame
    local dest = targetPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
    pcall(function()
        hrp.CFrame = dest
    end)
    shootAt(targetPart, hrp.Position)
    if Utils.forceHit.tpReturn then
        -- HC: wait before return (min ~0.145s) or you can get kicked; increase for long range / walls
        local delay = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.forceHit.tpReturnDelay) or 0.145, 0.145, 10)
        task.delay(delay, function()
            pcall(function()
                local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if me then me.CFrame = origin end
            end)
        end)
    end
end

Utils.forceHitTry = function()
    if not Utils.forceHit.enabled then return false end
    if selectedGameMode ~= "Hood Customs" and selectedGameMode ~= "Da Hood" then return false end
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if not tool or not Utils.forceHit.tools[tool.Name] then return false end
    local part = Utils.forceHitTarget()
    if not part then return false end
    Utils.forceHit.current = part
    local mdl = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    Utils.forceHit.manualTarget = mdl and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(mdl)

    if selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" then
        if silentAim then silentAim.bulletSpread = 0 end
        return false -- normal shot + getAim
    end

    -- Hood Customs (original): fire force hit and sink click
    Utils.forceHitFire(part)
    return true
end

pcall(function()
    local cas = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
    cas:UnbindAction("RaysForceHit")
    cas:BindAction("RaysForceHit", function(_, state)
        if state ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
        end
        if not Utils.forceHit.enabled or (selectedGameMode ~= "Hood Customs" and selectedGameMode ~= "Da Hood") then
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
        end
        local char = Player.Character
        if not char then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
        local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if not tool or not Utils.forceHit.tools[tool.Name] then
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
        end
        Utils.forceHit.lastFire = tick()
        local ok = Utils.forceHitTry()
        -- Sink only when we actually forced a shot so normal shooting still works otherwise
        return ok and Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink or Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
    end, false, Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
end)

Utils.track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        Utils.forceHit.holding = true
    end
end))
Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        Utils.forceHit.holding = false
    end
end))
Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local F = Utils.forceHit
    if not F.enabled then
        F.current = nil
        return
    end
    F.current = Utils.forceHitTarget()
    if F.fullAuto and F.holding then
        local now = tick()
        if now - (F.lastFire or 0) >= (tonumber(F.fireRate) or 0.067) then
            F.lastFire = now
            Utils.forceHitTry()
        end
    end
end))

-- Auto Kill (Hood Customs / rage)
Utils.autoKill = Utils.autoKill or {
    enabled = false,
    target = nil,
    keybind = nil,
    listening = false,
    busy = false,
    lastReload = 0,
    status = "Idle",
    mode = "Double Barrel",
    fullbright = false,
    voidSpam = false,
    spectate = false,
    knifeSpeed = 40,
    orbitDistance = 4.5,
    orbitHeight = 0,
    predict = false,
    predictIntensity = 0.4,
    autoStompLowHp = false,
    resetOnDeath = false,
    randomStomp = false,
    showStatus = false,
    teleportInterval = 0.05,
    aboveHead = false,
    tpMode = "Inside", -- "Above Head" | "Inside" | "Below"
    voidSpeed = 20,
    showHitbox = true,
    _voidHold = false,
    _voidBusy = false,
    _voidSaved = nil,
    _specSaved = nil,
    _knifeAngle = 0,
    _hitboxSaved = nil,
}
-- keep fields if table already existed
do
    local A = Utils.autoKill
    A.mode = A.mode or "Double Barrel"
    A.fullbright = A.fullbright or false
    A.voidSpam = A.voidSpam or false
    A.spectate = A.spectate or false
    A.knifeSpeed = A.knifeSpeed or 40
    A.orbitDistance = A.orbitDistance or 4.5
    A.orbitHeight = A.orbitHeight or 0
    A.predict = A.predict or false
    A.predictIntensity = A.predictIntensity or 0.4
    A.autoStompLowHp = A.autoStompLowHp or false
    A.resetOnDeath = A.resetOnDeath or false
    A.randomStomp = A.randomStomp or false
    A.teleportInterval = A.teleportInterval or 0.05
    A.aboveHead = A.aboveHead or false
    A.tpMode = A.tpMode or "Inside"
    A.targets = A.targets or {}
    A.targetIndex = A.targetIndex or 1
    A.voidSpeed = A.voidSpeed or 20
    if A.showHitbox == nil then A.showHitbox = true end
end

Utils.akIsKnocked = function(plr)
    if not plr or not plr.Character then return false end
    local knocked = false
    pcall(function()
        local be = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
        if be and be:FindFirstChild("K.O") and be["K.O"].Value == true then
            knocked = true
        end
    end)
    if not knocked then
        pcall(function()
            knocked = Utils.isCharacterDowned and Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character) or false
        end)
    end
    return knocked
end

Utils.akHasForceField = function(plr)
    if not plr or not plr.Character then return true end
    if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then return true end
    local ff = false
    pcall(function()
        local be = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
        if be and be:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
            local v = be.ForceField
            if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("BoolValue") then
                ff = v.Value == true
            elseif typeof(v) == "boolean" then
                ff = v
            else
                ff = true
            end
        end
    end)
    return ff
end

Utils.akToolName = function()
    local m = Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel"
    if m == "Shotgun" then return "[Shotgun]" end
    if m == "Knife" then return "[Knife]" end
    return "[DoubleBarrel]"
end

Utils.akEquipTool = function(name)
    name = name or Utils.akToolName()
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChild(name)
    if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then return tool end
    local bp = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        tool = bp:FindFirstChild(name)
        if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then
            pcall(function()
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum:EquipTool(tool) end
            end)
            task.wait(0.08)
            return char:FindFirstChild(name) or tool
        end
    end
    return nil
end

Utils.akEquipDB = function()
    return Utils.akEquipTool("[DoubleBarrel]")
end

Utils.akReload = function()
    local tool = Utils.akEquipTool()
    local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local ev = rep:FindFirstChild("MainEvent") or rep:FindFirstChild("MAINEVENT")
    pcall(function()
        if ev then
            ev:FireServer("Reload")
            ev:FireServer("reload")
            if tool then ev:FireServer("Reload", tool) end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if tool then
            for _, r in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") or r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    local n = string.lower(r.Name)
                    if string.find(n, "reload", 1, true) then
                        pcall(function()
                            if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then r:FireServer() else r:InvokeServer() end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    Utils.autoKill.lastReload = tick()
end

Utils.akUprightAbove = function(worldPos, height, lookFrom)
    height = height or 3
    local pos = Vector3.new(worldPos.X, worldPos.Y + height, worldPos.Z)
    local flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    if lookFrom then
        flatLook = Vector3.new(lookFrom.X - pos.X, 0, lookFrom.Z - pos.Z)
        if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            flatLook = Vector3.new(lookFrom.X, 0, lookFrom.Z)
            if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.05 then flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        end
        flatLook = flatLook.Unit
    end
    return CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + flatLook)
end

Utils.akStandUpright = function(me)
    if not me then return end
    pcall(function()
        me.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        me.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
    local hum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            hum.Sit = false
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end)
    end
end

Utils.akFindDowned = function(prefer)
    if prefer and Utils.akIsKnocked(prefer) then return prefer end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then
            return plr
        end
    end
    return nil
end

Utils.akSetStatusHud = function(on)
    local AK = Utils.autoKill
    if on then
        if not AK._statusLbl or not AK._statusLbl.Parent then
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Name = "RaysAKStatus"
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 28)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -14)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Text = "Idle"
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
            lbl.TextSize = 18
            lbl.TextWrapped = true
            lbl.ZIndex = 80
            lbl.Parent = ScreenGui
            AK._statusLbl = lbl
        end
        AK._statusLbl.Visible = true
        if not AK._statusConn then
            AK._statusConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local hud = Utils.autoKill._statusLbl
                if not hud then return end
                if not Utils.autoKill.showStatus then
                    hud.Visible = false
                    return
                end
                hud.Visible = true
                local plr = Utils.autoKill.target
                local name = plr and plr.Name or "None"
                local hp = 0
                pcall(function()
                    local hum = plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum and hum.MaxHealth > 0 then
                        hp = math.clamp(math.floor((hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) * 100 + 0.5), 0, 100)
                    end
                end)
                if not Utils.autoKill.enabled then
                    hud.Visible = false
                    return
                end
                local st = tostring(Utils.autoKill.status or "Idle")
                if st == "Wait stomp" then st = "Queue Stomp" end
                if st == "Knife orbit" or st == "Low HP stomp" then
                    st = (st == "Knife orbit") and "Knifing" or "Stomping"
                end
                if st == "Teleport + ForceHit" or st == "Reloading" then st = "Shooting" end
                if st == "Idle" or st == "No target" or st == "Waiting spawn/FF" or st == "Waiting respawn" or string.find(st, "Need ", 1, true) then
                    if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam and not Utils.autoKill._voidHold then
                        st = "Hiding (Void)"
                    elseif st == "Waiting spawn/FF" or st == "Waiting respawn" then
                        st = Utils.autoKill.voidSpam and "Hiding (Void)" or "Queue Stomp"
                    else
                        st = Utils.autoKill.voidSpam and "Hiding (Void)" or "Shooting"
                    end
                end
                local mode = tostring(Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel")
                hud.Text = string.format("%s | %s | %d%% Health | Mode: %s", st, name, hp, mode)
            end)
            pcall(function() Utils.track(AK._statusConn) end)
        end
    else
        if AK._statusLbl then AK._statusLbl.Visible = false end
    end
end

Utils.akDropAndStomp = function(plr)
    if not plr or not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then return false end
    local AK = Utils.autoKill
    local char = plr.Character
    if AK._downChar ~= char then
        AK._downChar = char
        AK._downAt = tick()
        AK._stompKey = nil
        AK._stompAt = nil
    end
    -- Do not delay stomp for void — stomp first, then void
    if AK._stompKey == char and AK._stompAt and (tick() - AK._stompAt) < 2.2 then
        if AK.voidSpam then
            AK._voidHold = false
            pcall(Utils.akVoidSpam)
        end
        return false
    end
    AK.status = "Stomping"
    AK._voidHold = true -- pause void while stomping
    local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Head"))
    local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and me then
        pcall(function()
            if Utils.autoKill.randomStomp then
                local ang = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local r = 1.0 + math.random() * 2.0
                local pos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(ang) * r, 2.4, math.sin(ang) * r)
                me.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, hrp.Position)
            else
                me.CFrame = Utils.akUprightAbove(hrp.Position, 2.8, me.Position)
            end
            Utils.akStandUpright(me)
        end)
    end
    local ok = Utils.akStomp(plr)
    AK._stompKey = char
    AK._stompAt = tick()
    AK._voidHold = false
    -- After first stomp registers → void again if enabled
    if AK.voidSpam then
        AK.status = "Hiding (Void)"
        pcall(Utils.akVoidSpam)
    else
        AK.status = "Idle"
    end
    return ok and true or true
end

Utils.akResetSelf = function()
    pcall(function()
        local hum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
    end)
end

Utils.akStomp = function(plr)
    if not plr or not plr.Character then return end
    local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local ev = rep:FindFirstChild("MainEvent") or rep:FindFirstChild("MAINEVENT")
    -- With void on: one short stomp burst then caller voids. Without: longer stomp.
    local once = Utils.autoKill.voidSpam and true or false
    local t0 = tick()
    local stomped = false
    local hits = 0
    local spinAng = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    while tick() - t0 < (once and 0.85 or 4) do
        if not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then break end
        local hrp = plr.Character and (plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
        local me = Player.Character and (Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Player.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
        if not (hrp and me) then break end
        pcall(function()
            if Utils.autoKill.randomStomp then
                spinAng = spinAng + 0.55 + math.random() * 0.35
                local r = 1.1 + math.random() * 1.8
                local pos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(spinAng) * r, 2.2 + math.random() * 0.8, math.sin(spinAng) * r)
                me.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, hrp.Position)
            else
                me.CFrame = Utils.akUprightAbove(hrp.Position, 2.8, me.Position)
            end
            Utils.akStandUpright(me)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if ev then ev:FireServer("Stomp") end
        end)
        stomped = true
        hits = hits + 1
        task.wait(once and 0.05 or 0.08)
    end
    return stomped
end


Utils.akGotoTarget = function(plr)
    if not plr or not plr.Character then return false end
    local th = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not (th and me) then return false end
    local mode = tostring(Utils.autoKill.tpMode or "Inside")
    -- legacy toggle maps to Above Head
    if Utils.autoKill.aboveHead and mode == "Inside" then
        mode = "Above Head"
    end
    local height = 0.4
    local lateral = 0.15
    if mode == "Above Head" then
        height = 3.2 -- close above (houses / interiors)
        lateral = 0.2
    elseif mode == "Below" then
        height = -2.8
        lateral = 0.2
    else -- Inside player
        height = 0.15
        lateral = 0.05
    end
    local dest = th.Position + Vector3.new(0, height, 0)
    if Utils.autoKill.predict then
        local vel = th.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local inten = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.autoKill.predictIntensity) or 0.4, 0, 3)
        dest = dest + Vector3.new(vel.X, vel.Y, vel.Z) * (0.08 + inten * 0.4)
    end
    local toward = me.Position - dest
    local flat = Vector3.new(toward.X, 0, toward.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then
        flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, lateral)
    else
        flat = flat.Unit * lateral
    end
    local stand = dest + flat
    if mode == "Inside" then
        stand = dest -- sit inside their HRP volume
    end
    me.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(stand, th.Position)
    Utils.akStandUpright(me)
    return true
end

Utils.akForceHitTarget = function(plr)
    if not plr or not plr.Character then return false end
    local part = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not part then return false end
    Utils.forceHitFire(part)
    return true
end

Utils.akKnifeSwing = function()
    local tool = Utils.akEquipTool("[Knife]")
    if not tool then return false end
    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
    local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local ev = rep:FindFirstChild("MainEvent") or rep:FindFirstChild("MAINEVENT")
    pcall(function()
        if ev then
            ev:FireServer("Punch")
            ev:FireServer("Knife")
            ev:FireServer("Slash")
        end
    end)
    return true
end

Utils.akOrbitAround = function(plr, dt)
    local th = plr and plr.Character and (plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
    local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not (th and me) then return end
    local spd = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.autoKill.knifeSpeed) or 40, 1, 200)
    local radPerSec = 1.2 + (spd / 200) * 54.8
    Utils.autoKill._knifeAngle = (Utils.autoKill._knifeAngle or 0) + radPerSec * (dt or 0.03)
    local radius = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.autoKill.orbitDistance) or 4.5, 1, 30)
    local height = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.autoKill.orbitHeight) or 0, -10, 10)
    local ang = Utils.autoKill._knifeAngle
    local base = th.Position
    if Utils.autoKill.predict then
        local vel = th.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local inten = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.autoKill.predictIntensity) or 0.4, 0, 3)
        base = base + Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z) * (0.12 + inten * 0.55)
    end
    local pos = base + Vector3.new(math.cos(ang) * radius, 1.5 + height, math.sin(ang) * radius)
    me.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, base)
    Utils.akStandUpright(me)
end

Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox = function(on)
    if on then
        if not Utils.autoKill._hitboxSaved then
            Utils.autoKill._hitboxSaved = {
                active = hitboxActive,
                x = hbXSize, y = hbYSize, z = hbZSize,
            }
        end
        hbXSize, hbYSize, hbZSize = 50, 50, 50
        hitboxActive = true
        hitboxVisible = Utils.autoKill.showHitbox ~= false
        pcall(function() Utils.updateHitboxState(true) end)
    else
        local s = Utils.autoKill._hitboxSaved
        if s then
            hbXSize, hbYSize, hbZSize = s.x or 2, s.y or 2, s.z or 2
            hitboxActive = s.active and true or false
            pcall(function() Utils.updateHitboxState(hitboxActive) end)
            Utils.autoKill._hitboxSaved = nil
        end
    end
end

Utils.akSetSpectate = function(on)
    local AK = Utils.autoKill
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    if on and AK.target and AK.target.Character then
        local hum = AK.target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            if not AK._specSaved then
                AK._specSaved = cam.CameraSubject
            end
            cam.CameraSubject = hum
        end
    else
        if AK._specSaved ~= nil then
            cam.CameraSubject = AK._specSaved
            AK._specSaved = nil
        else
            local myHum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if myHum then cam.CameraSubject = myHum end
        end
    end
end

Utils.akVoidSpam = function()
    local AK = Utils.autoKill
    if AK._voidHold then return end
    local char = Player.Character
    local me = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not me then return end
    if not AK._voidSaved then
        AK._voidSaved = me.CFrame
    end
    pcall(function()
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum.PlatformStand = true
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
        end
        local origin = AK._voidSaved.Position
        AK._voidIdx = (AK._voidIdx or 0) % 6 + 1
        local pts = {
            Vector3.new(0, 2000000, 0),
            Vector3.new(12000, 2800000, 9000),
            Vector3.new(-11000, 2400000, 14000),
            Vector3.new(16000, 3600000, -10000),
            Vector3.new(-15000, 2200000, -13000),
            Vector3.new(7000, 4000000, 5000),
        }
        local off = pts[AK._voidIdx]
        me.CFrame = CFrame.new(origin.X + off.X, off.Y, origin.Z + off.Z)
        me.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        me.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

Utils.akVoidLoop = function()
    local AK = Utils.autoKill
    if AK._voidConn then return end
    AK._voidAcc = 0
    AK._voidConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not AK.enabled or not AK.voidSpam then
            return
        end
        if AK._voidHold then return end
        AK._voidAcc = (AK._voidAcc or 0) + dt
        local spd = math.clamp(tonumber(AK.voidSpeed) or 20, 1, 100)
        local interval = 1 / spd
        if AK._voidAcc >= interval then
            AK._voidAcc = 0
            Utils.akVoidSpam()
        end
    end)
    pcall(function() Utils.track(AK._voidConn) end)
end

Utils.akVoidRestore = function()
    local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local AK = Utils.autoKill
    if me and AK._voidSaved then
        pcall(function()
            me.CFrame = AK._voidSaved
            me.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            local hum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
        end)
    end
    AK._voidSaved = nil
end

Utils.akApplyFullbright = function(on)
    pcall(function()
        if on then
            Lighting.Brightness = 2
            Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
            Lighting.ClockTime = 12
        else
            if forceTimeActive then
                Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue
            end
            if fullbrightActive and Utils.applyFullbright then
                Utils.applyFullbright(true)
            end
        end
    end)
end

Utils.akIsSelected = function(plr)
    if not plr then return false end
    local list = Utils.autoKill.targets or {}
    for i = 1, #list do
        if list[i] == plr then return true, i end
    end
    return Utils.autoKill.target == plr, nil
end
Utils.akToggleTarget = function(plr)
    if not plr then return end
    Utils.autoKill.targets = Utils.autoKill.targets or {}
    for i, p in ipairs(Utils.autoKill.targets) do
        if p == plr then
            table.remove(Utils.autoKill.targets, i)
            if Utils.autoKill.target == plr then
                Utils.autoKill.target = Utils.autoKill.targets[1]
            end
            return
        end
    end
    table.insert(Utils.autoKill.targets, plr)
    if not Utils.autoKill.target then
        Utils.autoKill.target = plr
        Utils.autoKill.targetIndex = #Utils.autoKill.targets
    end
end
Utils.akTargetNames = function()
    local names = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Utils.autoKill.targets or {}) do
        if p and p.Parent then
            table.insert(names, p.Name)
        end
    end
    if #names == 0 then return "None" end
    return table.concat(names, ", ")
end
Utils.akAdvanceTarget = function()
    local live = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Utils.autoKill.targets or {}) do
        if p and p.Parent then
            table.insert(live, p)
        end
    end
    Utils.autoKill.targets = live
    if #live == 0 then
        Utils.autoKill.target = nil
        return nil
    end
    local idx = (Utils.autoKill.targetIndex or 1) + 1
    if idx > #live then idx = 1 end
    Utils.autoKill.targetIndex = idx
    Utils.autoKill.target = live[idx]
    return live[idx]
end
Utils.akCurrentTarget = function()
    local live = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Utils.autoKill.targets or {}) do
        if p and p.Parent then
            table.insert(live, p)
        end
    end
    if #live == 0 and Utils.autoKill.target and Utils.autoKill.target.Parent then
        live = { Utils.autoKill.target }
        Utils.autoKill.targets = live
    end
    Utils.autoKill.targets = live
    if #live == 0 then
        Utils.autoKill.target = nil
        return nil
    end
    local idx = Utils.autoKill.targetIndex or 1
    if idx < 1 or idx > #live then idx = 1 end
    if Utils.autoKill.target then
        for i, p in ipairs(live) do
            if p == Utils.autoKill.target then idx = i break end
        end
    end
    Utils.autoKill.targetIndex = idx
    Utils.autoKill.target = live[idx]
    return live[idx]
end

Utils.akLoop = function()
    if Utils.autoKill.busy then return end
    Utils.autoKill.busy = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while Utils.autoKill.enabled do
            local plr = Utils.akCurrentTarget()
            local mode = Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel"
            local isKnife = (mode == "Knife")

            if Utils.autoKill.fullbright then Utils.akApplyFullbright(true) end
            if Utils.autoKill.spectate then Utils.akSetSpectate(true) end
            if isKnife then Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox(true) else Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox(false) end
            if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then Utils.akVoidLoop() end
            if Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp then
                local myHum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if myHum and myHum.Health < (myHum.MaxHealth * 0.5) then
                    local down = Utils.akFindDowned(plr)
                    if down then
                        Utils.autoKill.status = "Low HP stomp"
                        Utils.autoKill._voidHold = true
                        Utils.akVoidRestore()
                        Utils.akStomp(down)
                        if Utils.autoKill.resetOnDeath then
                            local dhum = down.Character and down.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if (not down.Character) or (not dhum) or dhum.Health <= 0 then
                                Utils.akResetSelf()
                            end
                        end
                        Utils.autoKill._voidHold = false
                    end
                end
            end

            if not plr or not plr.Parent then
                Utils.autoKill.status = "No target"
                if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then Utils.akVoidSpam() end
                task.wait(0.2)
            else
                Utils.autoKill.status = "Waiting spawn/FF"
                local waited = 0
                while Utils.autoKill.enabled and Utils.autoKill.target == plr and waited < 20 do
                    local char = plr.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if char and hum and hum.Health > 0 and not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) and not Utils.akHasForceField(plr) then
                        break
                    end
                    if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam and not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then Utils.akVoidSpam() end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    waited = waited + 0.15
                end
                if not (Utils.autoKill.enabled and Utils.autoKill.target == plr) then break end

                local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local th = plr.Character and (plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
                if me and th then
                    if Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then
                        Utils.akDropAndStomp(plr)
                        if Utils.autoKill.resetOnDeath then
                            local dhum = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if (not plr.Character) or (not dhum) or dhum.Health <= 0 then
                                Utils.akResetSelf()
                            end
                        end
                    elseif isKnife then
                        Utils.autoKill.status = "Knifing"
                        Utils.autoKill._voidHold = true
                        Utils.akVoidRestore()
                        Utils.akEquipTool("[Knife]")
                        local tKnife = tick()
                        while Utils.autoKill.enabled and Utils.autoKill.target == plr and tick() - tKnife < 12 do
                            if Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then break end
                            local hum = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then break end
                            Utils.akOrbitAround(plr, 0.03)
                            Utils.akKnifeSwing()
                            task.wait(0.03)
                        end
                        if Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then
                            Utils.akDropAndStomp(plr)
                            if Utils.autoKill.resetOnDeath then
                                local dhum = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if (not plr.Character) or (not dhum) or dhum.Health <= 0 then
                                    Utils.akResetSelf()
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        Utils.autoKill._voidHold = false
                    else
                        Utils.autoKill.status = "Teleport + ForceHit"
                        Utils.autoKill._voidHold = true
                        if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then
                            local keepAbove = Utils.autoKill.aboveHead
                            Utils.autoKill.aboveHead = true
                            Utils.akGotoTarget(plr)
                            Utils.autoKill.aboveHead = keepAbove
                            local me2 = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if me2 then
                                me2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                me2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end
                            Utils.akStandUpright(me2)
                            task.wait(0.18)
                        else
                            Utils.akVoidRestore()
                            Utils.akGotoTarget(plr)
                        end

                        local toolName = Utils.akToolName()
                        local tool = Utils.akEquipTool(toolName)
                        if not tool then
                            Utils.autoKill.status = "Need " .. toolName
                            task.wait(0.5)
                        else
                            local function burst()
                                local tEnd = tick() + (Utils.autoKill.voidSpam and 0.28 or 0.45)
                                Utils.autoKill.status = "Shooting"
                                if not Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then
                                    Utils.akGotoTarget(plr)
                                end
                                Utils.akForceHitTarget(plr)
                                Utils.autoKill.status = "Reloading"
                                Utils.akReload()
                                while tick() < tEnd and Utils.autoKill.enabled and Utils.autoKill.target == plr and not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) do
                                    if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then
                                        Utils.akForceHitTarget(plr)
                                        task.wait(0.06)
                                    else
                                        Utils.akGotoTarget(plr)
                                        task.wait(math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.autoKill.teleportInterval) or 0.05, 0.01, 0.5))
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            burst()
                            if Utils.autoKill.enabled and not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then
                                burst()
                            end
                            if Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then
                                Utils.akDropAndStomp(plr)
                            end
                            Utils.autoKill._voidHold = false
                        end
                    end

                    if not isKnife then
                        Utils.autoKill.status = "Reloading"
                        Utils.akReload()
                    end
                    Utils.autoKill.status = "Waiting respawn"
                    local t1 = tick()
                    while Utils.autoKill.enabled and Utils.autoKill.target == plr and tick() - t1 < 15 do
                        local char = plr.Character
                        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
                            if Utils.autoKill.resetOnDeath then
                                Utils.akResetSelf()
                                break
                            end
                            task.wait(0.3)
                        elseif not Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) and hum.Health > 0 then
                            Utils.autoKill._stompKey = nil
                            Utils.autoKill._downChar = nil
                            break
                        end
                        if Utils.akIsKnocked(plr) then
                            Utils.akDropAndStomp(plr)
