                        elseif Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then
                            Utils.akVoidSpam()
                        end
                        task.wait(0.15)
                    end
                    if not isKnife then Utils.akReload() end
                    if #(Utils.autoKill.targets or {}) > 1 then
                        Utils.akAdvanceTarget()
                    end
                else
                    if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then Utils.akVoidSpam() end
                    task.wait(0.2)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        Utils.autoKill.status = "Idle"
        Utils.autoKill.busy = false
        Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox(false)
        Utils.akSetSpectate(false)
        Utils.akVoidRestore()
        Utils.akApplyFullbright(false)
        pcall(function() Utils.akSetStatusHud(false) end)
    end)
end

pcall(function() setfflag("AdornShadingAPI", "True") end)
Utils.chams = Utils.chams or {
    enabled = false,
    glow = false,
    playerFF = false,
    itemsFF = false,
    accFF = false,
    glowColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40),
    ffColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40),
    others = false,
    material = "Forcefield",
    saved = {},
}
Utils.chamsClearPart = function(part)
    if not part then return end
    for _, obj in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if obj.Name == "RaysGlow" or obj.Name == "Chams" or obj.Name == "Glow" then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end
Utils.chamsAddGlow = function(part)
    Utils.chamsClearPart(part)
    if not (Utils.chams.enabled and Utils.chams.glow) then return end
    if not part:IsA("BasePart") or part.Transparency >= 1 then return end
    pcall(function()
        local isHead = part.Name == "Head" or part.Name == "FakeHead"
        local ad = Instance.new(isHead and "CylinderHandleAdornment" or "BoxHandleAdornment")
        ad.Name = "RaysGlow"
        ad.AlwaysOnTop = true
        ad.ZIndex = isHead and 10 or 9
        ad.Adornee = part
        local c = Utils.chams.glowColor
        ad.Color3 = Color3.new(c.R * 5, c.G * 5, c.B * 5)
        ad.Transparency = 0.15
        ad.Visible = true
        pcall(function() ad.Shading = Enum.AdornShading.XRayShaded end)
        if isHead then
            ad.Height = part.Size.Y
            ad.Radius = part.Size.X * 0.5
            ad.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
        else
            ad.Size = part.Size + Vector3.new(0.03, 0.03, 0.03)
        end
        ad.Parent = part
        for _, o in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
            if o:IsA("BasePart") and (o.Name == "RaysGlow" or o.Name == "Glow" or o.Name == "Chams") then
                o.Transparency = 1
                o.CanCollide = false
                o.CanQuery = false
                o.CanTouch = false
                o.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            end
        end
    end)
end
Utils.chamsSave = function(inst)
    if not inst or Utils.chams.saved[inst] then return end
    Utils.chams.saved[inst] = {
        Material = inst.Material,
        Color = inst.Color,
        Texture = inst:IsA("MeshPart") and inst.TextureID or nil,
    }
end
Utils.chamsRestore = function(inst)
    local s = Utils.chams.saved[inst]
    if not s or not inst then return end
    pcall(function()
        inst.Material = s.Material
        inst.Color = s.Color
        if inst:IsA("MeshPart") and s.Texture ~= nil then
            inst.TextureID = s.Texture
        end
    end)
end
Utils.chamsApplyClothes = function(char, hide)
    if not char then return end
    Utils.chams.clothes = Utils.chams.clothes or {}
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Shirt") or d:IsA("Pants") or d:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or d:IsA("Clothing") then
            if not Utils.chams.clothes[d] then
                Utils.chams.clothes[d] = {
                    ShirtTemplate = d:IsA("Shirt") and d.ShirtTemplate or nil,
                    PantsTemplate = d:IsA("Pants") and d.PantsTemplate or nil,
                    Graphic = d:IsA("ShirtGraphic") and d.Graphic or nil,
                }
            end
            pcall(function()
                if hide then
                    if d:IsA("Shirt") then d.ShirtTemplate = "" end
                    if d:IsA("Pants") then d.PantsTemplate = "" end
                    if d:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then d.Graphic = "" end
                else
                    local s = Utils.chams.clothes[d]
                    if s then
                        if d:IsA("Shirt") and s.ShirtTemplate then d.ShirtTemplate = s.ShirtTemplate end
                        if d:IsA("Pants") and s.PantsTemplate then d.PantsTemplate = s.PantsTemplate end
                        if d:IsA("ShirtGraphic") and s.Graphic then d.Graphic = s.Graphic end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("WrapLayer") or d:IsA("WrapTarget") then
            pcall(function() d.Enabled = not hide end)
        end
    end
end
Utils.chamsApplyFF = function(inst)
    if not inst or not inst:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    Utils.chamsSave(inst)
    pcall(function()
        local matName = Utils.chams.material or "Forcefield"
        local mat = Enum.Material.ForceField
        if matName == "Neon" then mat = Enum.Material.Neon
        elseif matName == "SmoothPlastic" then mat = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        elseif matName == "Plastic" then mat = Enum.Material.Plastic
        end
        inst.Material = mat
        inst.Color = Utils.chams.ffColor
        if inst:IsA("MeshPart") then inst.TextureID = "" end
        for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("Texture") or d:IsA("Decal") then
                d.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
    end)
end
Utils.chamsStyleChar = function(char)
    if not char then return end
    local owner = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
    if owner and owner ~= Player and not Utils.chams.others then
        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                Utils.chamsClearPart(d)
                Utils.chamsRestore(d)
            end
        end
        Utils.chamsApplyClothes(char, false)
        return
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            Utils.chamsClearPart(d)
            local isAcc = d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory") or d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Hat")
            local isTool = d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
            local isBody = (not isAcc) and (not isTool) and d.Parent == char
            if Utils.chams.enabled and Utils.chams.glow and isBody then
                Utils.chamsAddGlow(d)
            end
            if Utils.chams.enabled and Utils.chams.playerFF and isBody then
                Utils.chamsApplyFF(d)
            elseif Utils.chams.enabled and Utils.chams.accFF and isAcc then
                Utils.chamsApplyFF(d)
            elseif Utils.chams.enabled and Utils.chams.itemsFF and isTool then
                Utils.chamsApplyFF(d)
            else
                Utils.chamsRestore(d)
            end
        end
    end
    local hideClothes = Utils.chams.enabled and Utils.chams.playerFF and (Utils.chams.material or "Forcefield") ~= "Forcefield"
    Utils.chamsApplyClothes(char, hideClothes)
end
Utils.chamsRefresh = function()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr.Character then
            Utils.chamsStyleChar(plr.Character)
        end
    end
end
Utils.chamsStart = function()
    if Utils.chams._started then return end
    Utils.chams._started = true
    pcall(function() setfflag("AdornShadingAPI", "True") end)
    Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Utils.chams.enabled then return end
        Utils.chams._acc = (Utils.chams._acc or 0) + 1
        if Utils.chams._acc < 20 then return end
        Utils.chams._acc = 0
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end))
    Utils.track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.2)
            if Utils.chams.enabled then Utils.chamsStyleChar(char) end
        end)
    end))
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr.Character and Utils.chams.enabled then Utils.chamsStyleChar(plr.Character) end
        Utils.track(plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.2)
            if Utils.chams.enabled then Utils.chamsStyleChar(char) end
        end))
    end
end

function Builders.buildUtilitiesPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Utils.chamsStart()
    local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft or PageScroll, "Chams")
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.chams.enabled, function(t, i)
        Utils.chams.enabled = not Utils.chams.enabled
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.chams.enabled)
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Player", Utils.chams.playerFF, function(t, i)
        Utils.chams.playerFF = not Utils.chams.playerFF
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.chams.playerFF)
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Items", Utils.chams.itemsFF, function(t, i)
        Utils.chams.itemsFF = not Utils.chams.itemsFF
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.chams.itemsFF)
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Accessories", Utils.chams.accFF, function(t, i)
        Utils.chams.accFF = not Utils.chams.accFF
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.chams.accFF)
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Other Players", Utils.chams.others, function(t, i)
        Utils.chams.others = not Utils.chams.others
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.chams.others)
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end)
    do
        local mats = { "Forcefield", "Neon", "SmoothPlastic", "Plastic" }
        local drop = Instance.new("TextButton")
        drop.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        drop.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        drop.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        drop.Text = "  Material: " .. tostring(Utils.chams.material or "Forcefield") .. "   ▼"
        drop.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        drop.TextSize = 11
        drop.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        drop.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        drop.AutoButtonColor = false
        drop.BorderSizePixel = 0
        drop.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(drop, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(drop, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(mats) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.chams.material = n
                drop.Text = "  Material: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                Utils.chamsRefresh()
            end)
        end
        drop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #mats * 22) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        end)
    end
    UI.createColorPicker(box, "Color", Utils.chams.ffColor, function(c)
        Utils.chams.ffColor = c
        Utils.chams.glowColor = c
        Utils.chamsRefresh()
    end)

    if false then
        local cur = UI.groupBox(UILeft or PageScroll, "Crosshair")
        UI.groupToggle(cur, "Enabled", customCursor.enabled, function(t, i)
            Utils.setCustomCursor(not customCursor.enabled)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, customCursor.enabled)
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(cur, "Cursor Color", customCursor.color, function(c)
            customCursor.color = c
            pcall(Utils.applyCursorVisuals)
        end)
    end
end


Utils.trigger = {
    enabled = false,
    visible = false,
    team = false,
    hitboxMul = 1,
    delayMs = 1,
    releaseMs = 10,
    knifeCheck = false,
    keybind = nil,
    busy = false,
}
Utils.triggerSameTeam = function(plr)
    if not Utils.trigger.team then return false end
    if not Player.Team or not plr.Team then return false end
    return Player.Team == plr.Team
end

Utils.triggerVisible = function(part)
    if not Utils.trigger.visible then return true end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not (cam and part) then return false end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Player.Character }
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
    local dir = part.Position - origin
    local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
    if not hit then return true end
    return hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(part.Parent)
end

Utils.triggerOverPart = function(part)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not (cam and part) then return false end
    local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
    if not on or sp.Z < 0 then return false end
    local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local dist = (part.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
    local mul = tonumber(Utils.trigger.hitboxMul) or 1
    local world = part.Size.Magnitude * math.max(mul, 0.1)
    local fov = math.rad(cam.FieldOfView)
    local px = (world / math.max(dist, 0.2)) * (cam.ViewportSize.Y / (2 * math.tan(fov / 2)))
    return (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mouse).Magnitude <= math.max(3, px)
end

Utils.triggerTarget = function()
    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
    local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and plr.Character and not Utils.triggerSameTeam(plr) then
            if not (whitelist and Utils.skipProtected(plr)) then
                local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local downed = false
                    pcall(function() downed = Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character) end)
                    if not downed then
                        if Utils.trigger.knifeCheck then
                            local tool = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                            if not (tool and tool.Name == "[Knife]") then
                                continue
                            end
                        end
                        local part = Utils.getAimPart(plr.Character, silentAim.part or camlock.lockPart or "Head")
                            or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if part and Utils.triggerOverPart(part) and Utils.triggerVisible(part) then
                            local sp = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                            local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mouse).Magnitude
                            if d < bestD then
                                bestD = d
                                best = part
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

Utils.triggerClick = function()
    if Utils.trigger.busy then return end
    Utils.trigger.busy = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local delayS = math.max(0, (tonumber(Utils.trigger.delayMs) or 1) / 1000)
        local holdS = math.max(0.001, (tonumber(Utils.trigger.releaseMs) or 10) / 1000)
        if delayS > 0 then task.wait(delayS) end
        if Utils.trigger.enabled and Utils.triggerTarget() then
            pcall(function()
                if mouse1press then
                    mouse1press()
                    task.wait(holdS)
                    if mouse1release then mouse1release() end
                else
                    local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                    vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                    task.wait(holdS)
                    vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                end
            end)
        end
        task.wait(0.02)
        Utils.trigger.busy = false
    end)
end

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not Utils.trigger.enabled then return end
    if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then return end
    if Utils.triggerTarget() then
        Utils.triggerClick()
    end
end))

Utils.flameIsTool = function(tool)
    if not tool then return false end
    local n = string.lower(tostring(tool.Name or ""))
    return string.find(n, "flame", 1, true) ~= nil
end

Utils.flameLockTick = function()
    local fl = Utils.flameLock
    if not fl.enabled then
        fl.currentTarget = nil
        return
    end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local function flameDisable()
        fl.enabled = false
        fl.currentTarget = nil
        if R.flameToggleBtn and R.flameToggleBtn.Parent then
            UI.applyToggleState(R.flameToggleBtn, R.flameToggleInd, false)
        end
    end
    local function flameAlive(plr)
        if not plr or not plr.Character then return false end
        if Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character) then return false end
        local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
        return true
    end
    if fl.currentTarget and not flameAlive(fl.currentTarget) then
        if fl.releaseOnSwitch then
            flameDisable()
            return
        end
        fl.currentTarget = nil
    end
    if not flameAlive(fl.currentTarget) then
        if fl.sticky then
            -- wait for same player / no auto switch while sticky without release
            return
        end
        fl.currentTarget = Utils.getBestTarget(1000)
    end
    local target = fl.currentTarget
    if not target or not target.Character then return end
    local part = Utils.getAimPart(target.Character, fl.lockPart or "HumanoidRootPart")
        or target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not part then return end
    local pos = part.Position
    local vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local pred = tonumber(fl.prediction) or 0
    if vel and vel.Magnitude > 0.1 and pred ~= 0 then
        pos = pos + Vector3.new(vel.X * pred, 0, vel.Z * pred)
    end
    pos = pos + camera.CFrame.RightVector * (tonumber(fl.horizOffset) or 0)
    pos = pos + Vector3.new(0, tonumber(fl.upOffset) or 0, 0)
    local speed = math.clamp(tonumber(fl.smoothness) or 0, 0, 1)
    local snap = math.clamp(tonumber(fl.snapIntensity) or 0.35, 0, 1)
    local alpha = (0.012 + snap * snap * 0.988) * (0.2 + speed * 0.8)
    if snap >= 0.995 then
        alpha = 1
    else
        alpha = math.clamp(alpha, 0.01, 0.92)
    end
    local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
    local dist = (pos - camPos).Magnitude
    if dist < 0.01 then return end
    local desiredLook = (pos - camPos).Unit
    local newLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector:Lerp(desiredLook, alpha)
    camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, camPos + newLook)
end

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    pcall(Utils.flameLockTick)
end))

Utils.silentRoll = function()
    -- Force Hit on Da Hood: always connect
    if Utils.forceHit and Utils.forceHit.enabled and (selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" or isDaHood) then
        return true
    end
    if silentAim.neverMiss then return true end
    local chance = tonumber(silentAim.hitChance) or 100
    if chance >= 100 then return true end
    if chance <= 0 then return false end
    return (math.random() * 100) <= chance
end
local SILENT_TARGET_MODES = { "Mouse", "Player" }
local SILENT_PART_OPTIONS = {
    "Head", "Body", "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso",
    "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand", "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
    "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot",
    "Closest Part",
}
local silentTracerObj, silentFovCircleObj, silentVisObj, listeningForSilentKey = nil, nil, nil, false
-- Da Hood silent state (GunHandler.getAim — matches open-source silent, no spread hooks)
local dhSilent = { hooked = false, oldGetAim = nil }
-- Hood Customs silent (original getMouseWorldHit script, toggle only)
local hcSilentUtil = {
    target = nil,
    hooked = false,
    RunService = game:GetService("RunService"),
    Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace"),
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService"),
}
pcall(function()
    if cloneref then
        hcSilentUtil.RunService = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
        hcSilentUtil.Workspace = cloneref(game:GetService("Workspace"))
        hcSilentUtil.Players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
        hcSilentUtil.UserInputService = cloneref(game:GetService("UserInputService"))
    end
end)

-- Hood Customs silent — NO filtergc / hookfunction (those hard-freeze Roblox)
hcSilentUtil.GetClosestPlayer = function(self)
    -- Prefer shared silent target logic (no BodyEffects hard-require)
    local okShared, shared = pcall(function()
        if Utils.getSilentTarget then
            return Utils.getSilentTarget()
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if okShared and shared and Utils.silentPartValid(shared) then
        return shared
    end
    local closestDistance = math.huge
    local closest = nil
    local candidates = {}
    local cam = self.Camera or workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil end
    local mouseLoc = (self.UserInputService or UserInputService):GetMouseLocation()
    local myPlr = self.LocalPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
    for _, v in pairs((self.Players or Players):GetPlayers()) do
        local skipWl = false
        pcall(function()
            skipWl = Utils.skipProtected(v)
        end)
        if v ~= myPlr and not skipWl and v.Character then
            local char = v.Character
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hrp then
                local skipDown = false
                if silentAim.healthCheck then
                    local okD, downed = pcall(function() return Utils.isCharacterDowned(char) end)
                    if okD and downed then skipDown = true end
                    if not skipDown then
                        local be = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
                        if be then
                            local koV = be:FindFirstChild("K.O") or be:FindFirstChild("KO") or be:FindFirstChild("Knocked")
                            if koV and koV:IsA("BoolValue") and koV.Value then
                                skipDown = true
                            end
                            local grabbed = be:FindFirstChild("Grabbed")
                            if grabbed and grabbed.Value ~= nil and grabbed.Value ~= false then
                                skipDown = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not skipDown then
                    local part = Utils.getAimPart(char, silentAim.part or camlock.lockPart or "Head")
                    if not part then
                        part = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or hrp
                    end
                    if part then
                        local screenPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                        if onScreen then
                            local distance = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mouseLoc).Magnitude
                            local fovPx = Utils.fovScreenRadius(silentAim.fov, 1000)
                            if silentAim.neverMiss or distance <= fovPx then
                                table.insert(candidates, { part = part, score = distance, plr = v, inFov = distance <= fovPx })
                                if distance < closestDistance then
                                    closestDistance = distance
                                    closest = part
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return Utils.silentPickCandidate(candidates, closest)
end


Utils.findMouseWorldHit = function()
    if hcSilentUtil.cachedFn and typeof(hcSilentUtil.cachedFn) == "function" then
        return hcSilentUtil.cachedFn
    end
    local want = {
        getMouseWorldHit = true,
        GetMouseWorldHit = true,
        getmouseworldhit = true,
        mouseWorldHit = true,
        MouseWorldHit = true,
        getHit = true,
        getAimHit = true,
        worldHit = true,
    }
    local found = nil
    pcall(function()
        if not getgc then return end
        -- getgc(true) is extremely heavy — only plain getgc once
        local list = getgc()
        if type(list) ~= "table" then return end
        local limit = math.min(#list, 25000)
        for i = 1, limit do
            local v = list[i]
            if type(v) == "function" then
                local n
                pcall(function()
                    n = debug.info(v, "n")
                end)
                if n and want[n] then
                    found = v
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    if not found then
        pcall(function()
            if not filtergc then return end
            for name in pairs(want) do
                local f = filtergc("function", { Name = name }, true)
                if typeof(f) == "function" then
                    found = f
                    break
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    if typeof(found) == "function" then
        hcSilentUtil.cachedFn = found
    end
    return found
end

function Utils.hcSilentStart()
    -- Working HC silent (Adis-style): mouse Hit / Target / UnitRay via __index
    hcSilentUtil.Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    hcSilentUtil.LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not hcSilentUtil.RunService then
        pcall(function()
            hcSilentUtil.RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            hcSilentUtil.Players = Players
            hcSilentUtil.UserInputService = UserInputService
        end)
    end

    if not hcSilentUtil.loopStarted then
        hcSilentUtil.loopStarted = true
        hcSilentUtil.RunConn = hcSilentUtil.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not silentAim.enabled then
                hcSilentUtil.target = nil
                return
            end
            hcSilentUtil.Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local okT, t = pcall(function()
                return hcSilentUtil:GetClosestPlayer()
            end)
            if okT and t and Utils.silentPartValid(t) then
                hcSilentUtil.target = t
                silentAim.currentPart = t
            else
                hcSilentUtil.target = nil
            end
        end)
        Utils.track(hcSilentUtil.RunConn)
    end

    if hcSilentUtil.mouseHooked then return end
    if not hookmetamethod then return end

    local mouse = Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
    if not mouse then return end

    local function wrap(f)
        if newcclosure then return newcclosure(f) end
        return f
    end

    -- game __index hook (same pattern as working HC silent)
    local oldIndex
    oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", wrap(function(self, idx)
        local check = true
        pcall(function()
            if checkcaller and checkcaller() then
                check = false
            end
        end)
        if check and silentAim.enabled and self == mouse then
            if idx == "Hit" or idx == "Target" or idx == "UnitRay" then
                -- Always redirect when we have a live target (match working Adis silent).
                -- Hit chance only applies if set below 100.
                local part = hcSilentUtil.target
                if not (part and part.Parent) then
                    local okT, t = pcall(function()
                        return hcSilentUtil:GetClosestPlayer()
                    end)
                    if okT and t and t.Parent then
                        part = t
                        hcSilentUtil.target = t
                    end
                end
                local chance = tonumber(silentAim.hitChance) or 100
                local allowChance = silentAim.neverMiss or chance >= 100 or Utils.silentRoll()
                if part and part.Parent and allowChance then
                    -- Soft validity: must belong to a living player character
                    local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    local hum = model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                        local origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or part.Position
                        local hitPos = part.Position
                        local pred = tonumber(silentAim.prediction) or 0
                        if pred > 0 then
                            local vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            if vel then
                                hitPos = hitPos + vel * pred
                            end
                        end
                        if idx == "UnitRay" then
                            local dir = hitPos - origin
                            if dir.Magnitude < 0.01 then
                                dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
                            end
                            return Ray.new(origin, dir.Unit)
                        elseif idx == "Hit" then
                            return CFrame.new(hitPos)
                        elseif idx == "Target" then
                            return part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return oldIndex(self, idx)
    end))

    hcSilentUtil.mouseHooked = true
    hcSilentUtil.oldMouseIndex = oldIndex
end

function Utils.restoreFollowCamera()
end


-- Lighting FX state (bloom / sunrays / dof)
Utils.pl = {
    bloomOn = false,
    bloomIntensity = 40,
    bloomSize = 24,
    bloomThresh = 95,
    sunraysOn = false,
    sunraysIntensity = 15,
    sunraysSpread = 65,
    dofOn = false,
    dofFar = 75,
    dofNear = 75,
    dofFocus = 50,
    dofRadius = 30,
}
Utils.plFx = function(kind)
    local P = Utils.pl
    if not P then return end
    if kind == "Bloom" then
        local b = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysBloom")
        if not b then
            b = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
            b.Name = "RaysBloom"
            b.Parent = Lighting
        end
        b.Enabled = P.bloomOn == true
        b.Intensity = (P.bloomIntensity or 40) / 100
        b.Size = P.bloomSize or 24
        b.Threshold = (P.bloomThresh or 95) / 100
    elseif kind == "SunRays" then
        local intensity = math.clamp((P.sunraysIntensity or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
        local spread = math.clamp((P.sunraysSpread or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
        local ours = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysSunRays")
        if not P.sunraysOn then
            if ours then pcall(function() ours:Destroy() end) end
            return
        end
        if not ours then
            ours = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect")
            ours.Name = "RaysSunRays"
            ours.Parent = Lighting
        end
        ours.Enabled = intensity > 0
        ours.Intensity = intensity
        ours.Spread = spread
        for _, fx in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if fx:IsA("SunRaysEffect") and fx ~= ours then
                pcall(function() fx.Enabled = false end)
            end
        end
    elseif kind == "DoF" then
        local d = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysDoF")
        if not d then
            d = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
            d.Name = "RaysDoF"
            d.Parent = Lighting
        end
        d.Enabled = P.dofOn == true
        d.FocusDistance = P.dofFocus or 50
        d.InFocusRadius = P.dofRadius or 30
        d.FarIntensity = (P.dofFar or 75) / 100
        d.NearIntensity = (P.dofNear or 75) / 100
    end
end

Utils.silentPickCandidate = function(candidates, fallback)
    if type(candidates) ~= "table" or #candidates == 0 then
        return fallback
    end
    table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
        return (a.score or 0) < (b.score or 0)
    end)
    if silentAim.multiTarget then
        local fovOnly = {}
        for i = 1, #candidates do
            local c = candidates[i]
            local ok = c.inFov
            if ok == nil and c.part then
                local inside = Utils.partInScreenFov(c.part, silentAim.fov, 1000)
                ok = inside
            end
            if ok then
                table.insert(fovOnly, c)
            end
        end
        if #fovOnly > 0 then
            candidates = fovOnly
        else
            return nil
        end
    end
    if not silentAim.multiTarget or #candidates < 2 then
        return candidates[1].part
    end
    if tick() - (silentAim.multiLast or 0) > 0.11 then
        silentAim.multiIndex = (silentAim.multiIndex or 1) + 1
        silentAim.multiLast = tick()
    end
    local i = ((silentAim.multiIndex - 1) % #candidates) + 1
    return candidates[i] and candidates[i].part or fallback
end

-- Da Hood silent = open-source GunHandler silent (exact behavior, no spread hooks)
Utils.dhGetClosest = function()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local mouse = Player:GetMouse()
    if not (cam and mouse) then return nil end
    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local useFov = silentAim.fov
    if Utils.forceHit and Utils.forceHit.enabled and (selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" or isDaHood) then
        useFov = Utils.forceHit.fov or useFov
    end
    local fovPx = Utils.fovScreenRadius(useFov, 1000)
    local aimPartMode = silentAim.part or camlock.lockPart or "Head"
    local candidates = {}
    local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge
    local myRoot = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local usePlayer = silentAim.targetMode == "Player"

    for _, v in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if v ~= Player and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
            local skip = false
            if silentAim.healthCheck then
                local okDown, downed = pcall(function() return Utils.isCharacterDowned(v.Character) end)
                if okDown and downed then skip = true end
                if not skip then
                    local be = v.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
                    if be then
                        for _, n in ipairs({"K.O", "KO", "Knocked", "Downed"}) do
                            local koV = be:FindFirstChild(n)
                            if koV and ((typeof(koV) == "Instance" and koV:IsA("BoolValue") and koV.Value) or koV == true) then
                                skip = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not skip and whitelist and whitelist.enabled then
                local okW, wl = pcall(function() return Utils.isWhitelistedPlayer(v) end)
                if okW and wl then skip = true end
            end
            if not skip then
                local part = nil
                if aimPartMode == "Closest Part" then
                    local shortest = math.huge
                    local parts = {
                        "Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
                        "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot", "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm",
                        "LeftHand", "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso",
                    }
                    for pi = 1, #parts do
                        local p = v.Character:FindFirstChild(parts[pi])
                        if p then
                            local screenPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToScreenPoint(p.Position)
                            if onScreen then
                                local d = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
                                if d < shortest then shortest = d part = p end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    part = part or v.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                elseif aimPartMode == "Body" then
                    part = v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                else
                    part = Utils.getAimPart(v.Character, aimPartMode) or v.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                end

                if part then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToScreenPoint(part.Position)
                    if onScreen then
                        local screenDist = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
                        if silentAim.neverMiss or screenDist <= fovPx then
                            local allow = true
                            if silentAim.wallCheck then
                                allow = false
                                local origin = cam.CFrame.Position
                                local delta = part.Position - origin
                                local mag = delta.Magnitude
                                if mag > 0.01 then
                                    local ray = Ray.new(origin, delta.Unit * math.min(mag, 500))
                                    local hit = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray, { Player.Character, cam })
                                    if hit and hit:IsDescendantOf(v.Character) then
                                        allow = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if allow then
                                local score = screenDist
                                if usePlayer and myRoot then
                                    score = (part.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                                end
                                table.insert(candidates, { part = part, score = score, plr = v, inFov = screenDist <= fovPx })
                                if score < bestScore then
                                    bestScore = score
                                    best = part
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return Utils.silentPickCandidate(candidates, best)
end

pcall(function()
    if Utils._spreadHooked then return end
    if not hookfunction then return end
    Utils._spreadHooked = true
    local oldRandom
    oldRandom = hookfunction(math.random, function(...)
        local args = { ... }
        local result = oldRandom(...)
        local fhOn = Utils.forceHit and Utils.forceHit.enabled and (selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" or isDaHood)
        if not silentAim.enabled and not fhOn then
            return result
        end
        if isHoodCustoms or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID or selectedGameMode == "Hood Customs" then
            return result
        end
        if fhOn then
            -- Force Hit DH: bullet split / spread always 0
            return 0
        end
        local okCaller = true
        pcall(function()
            if checkcaller and checkcaller() then
                okCaller = false
            end
        end)
        if not okCaller then
            return result
        end
        local a1, a2 = args[1], args[2]
        if #args == 0 or (a1 == -0.05 and a2 == 0.05) or a1 == -0.1 or a1 == -0.05 then
            local mul = tonumber(silentAim.bulletSpread)
            if mul == nil then mul = 1 end
            return result * math.clamp(mul, 0, 2)
        end
        return result
    end)
end)

function Utils.dhSilentStart()
    if dhSilent.hooked then return true end
    if game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return false end

    local handler
    local ok = pcall(function()
        handler = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.GunHandler)
    end)
    if not ok or type(handler) ~= "table" or type(handler.getAim) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    local oldFunc = handler.getAim
    dhSilent.oldGetAim = oldFunc

    -- Same pattern as the working open-source Da Hood silent (assignment only)
    handler.getAim = function(origin, maxDist)
        local fhOn = Utils.forceHit and Utils.forceHit.enabled and (selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" or isDaHood)
        if not silentAim.enabled and not fhOn then
            return oldFunc(origin, maxDist)
        end
        if typeof(origin) == "CFrame" then
            origin = origin.Position
        end
        if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or origin
        end
        local target
        pcall(function()
            if fhOn then
                -- Force Hit: FOV target, always hit, spread handled separately
                target = Utils.forceHitTarget and Utils.forceHitTarget() or Utils.dhGetClosest()
            else
                target = Utils.dhGetClosest()
            end
        end)
        -- Force Hit: always redirect (ignore hit chance). Silent: respect silentRoll.
        local allow = target and Utils.silentPartValid(target) and typeof(origin) == "Vector3"
        if allow and not fhOn then
            allow = Utils.silentRoll()
        end
        if allow then
            silentAim.currentPart = target
            if fhOn then
                Utils.forceHit.current = target
            end
            local offset = target.Position - origin
            local dist = offset.Magnitude
            if dist > 0.001 then
                return offset.Unit, math.min(dist, maxDist or 200)
            end
        else
            silentAim.currentPart = nil
        end
        return oldFunc(origin, maxDist)
    end

    dhSilent.hooked = true
    return true
end

function Utils.hcSilentSync()
    if not silentAim.enabled and not (Utils.forceHit and Utils.forceHit.enabled) then
        hcSilentUtil.target = nil
        return
    end
    -- Hood Customs mode / place: dedicated HC silent
    if isHoodCustoms or selectedGameMode == "Hood Customs" or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then
        pcall(Utils.hcSilentStart)
        return
    end
    -- Da Hood first when GunHandler exists
    local hooked = false
    pcall(function()
        hooked = Utils.dhSilentStart() == true
    end)
    if not hooked then
        pcall(Utils.hcSilentStart)
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    local lastUpdate = tick()
    local frames = 0
    Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        frames = frames + 1
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastUpdate >= 1 then
            currentFps = math.floor(frames / (now - lastUpdate))
            FpsLabel.Text = "FPS: " .. tostring(currentFps)
            -- re-assert fps cap each second if executor supports it
            pcall(function()
                if setfpscap then setfpscap(fpsCap) end
            end)
            frames = 0
            lastUpdate = now
        end
    end))
end)

Utils.applyTheme = function(name)
    local preset = ThemePresets[name]
    if not preset then return end
    currentThemeName = name
    Theme.GradTop = nil
    Theme.GradMid = nil
    Theme.GradBottom = nil
    Theme.CardT = 0
    Theme.CardColor = nil
    for k, v in pairs(preset) do
        Theme[k] = v
    end
    if Utils.themeCustom.accent then
        Theme.Accent = Utils.themeCustom.accent
    end
    pcall(function()
        if currentThemeName == "Midnight" or currentThemeName == "Cotton Candy" or currentThemeName == "Lavender" or currentThemeName == "Ash" or currentThemeName == "Bubblegum" then
            MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        else
            MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        end
        MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        if MainGradient then
            MainGradient.Rotation = 45
            MainGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.GradTop or Theme.Primary),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.GradMid or Theme.Primary),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.GradBottom or Theme.Primary),
            })
        end
        TopBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        if TopBarCover then TopBarCover.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
        TitleLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        TitleLabel.Text = "R a y ' s  P a n e l"
        FpsLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        if MainStroke then
            MainStroke.Color = (currentThemeName == "Midnight") and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or (Theme.Border or Color3.fromRGB(99, 18, 22))
        end
        Utils.updatePanelBgImage()
        UI.styleCard(CloseButton)
        CloseButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        UI.styleCard(MinimizeButton)
        MinimizeButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        -- keep avatar preview matched to main panel theme
        if AP and AP.Frame then
            if currentThemeName == "Midnight" or currentThemeName == "Cotton Candy" or currentThemeName == "Lavender" or currentThemeName == "Ash" or currentThemeName == "Bubblegum" then
                AP.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            else
                AP.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            end
            if AP.Grad then
                AP.Grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.GradTop or Theme.Primary),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.GradMid or Theme.Primary),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.GradBottom or Theme.Primary),
                })
            end
            if AP.Stroke then
                AP.Stroke.Color = (currentThemeName == "Midnight") and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or (Theme.Border or Color3.fromRGB(99, 18, 22))
            end
            if AP.Title then
                AP.Title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(Utils.applyAccentEverywhere)
    for _, inst in ipairs(knobRegistry) do
        if inst and inst.Parent then
            pcall(function() inst.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if Utils.updateSidebarSelection then Utils.updateSidebarSelection() end
        if Sidebar then
            for _, d in ipairs(Sidebar:GetChildren()) do
                if d.Name == "SideHead" and d:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    d.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
                end
            end
        end
        if Utils.sideSearch then
            Utils.sideSearch.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            Utils.sideSearch.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp((Theme.CardT or 0.5) + 0.15, 0.2, 0.85)
            Utils.sideSearch.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            Utils.sideSearch.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        for _, inst in ipairs(cardRegistry) do
            if inst and inst.Parent then
                UI.styleCard(inst)
            end
        end
        if MainFrame then
            for _, d in ipairs(MainFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("UIStroke") and (d.Name == "FeatureStroke" or d.Name == "GlassStroke") then
                    d.Enabled = false
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

Utils.movApplyHip = function()
    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid and Utils.getHumanoid() or (Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
    if not hum then return end
    if Utils.mov.hipHeight then
        if not Utils.mov._hipSaved then
            Utils.mov.originalHip = hum.HipHeight
            Utils.mov._hipSaved = true
        end
        hum.HipHeight = tonumber(Utils.mov.hipValue) or 2
    else
        if Utils.mov._hipSaved then
            hum.HipHeight = Utils.mov.originalHip or 2
            Utils.mov._hipSaved = false
        end
    end
end

Utils.movSetFreecam = function(on)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    if on then
        if Utils.mov.freecam then return end
        Utils.mov.freecam = true
        Utils.mov.freecamPos = cam.CFrame
        Utils.mov.freecamFov = cam.FieldOfView
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        if Utils.mov.freecamConn then
            pcall(function() Utils.mov.freecamConn:Disconnect() end)
        end
        Utils.mov.freecamConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not Utils.mov.freecam then return end
            local cam2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam2 then return end

            -- if mouse is over the panel GUI, unlock so user can click toggles
            local overGui = false
            pcall(function()
                local objs = Player.PlayerGui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(
                    UserInputService:GetMouseLocation().X,
                    UserInputService:GetMouseLocation().Y
                )
                for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
                    if o:IsDescendantOf(MainFrame) or (typeof(ScreenGui) == "Instance" and o:IsDescendantOf(ScreenGui)) then
                        overGui = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end)
            -- also check CoreGui-hosted UI (many executors parent there)
            if not overGui then
                pcall(function()
                    local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                    for _, gui in ipairs(game:GetService("CoreGui"):GetChildren()) do
                        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                            for _, o in ipairs(gui:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(mouse.X, mouse.Y)) do
                                if o:IsDescendantOf(MainFrame) or (MainFrame and tostring(o):find("Zenith")) then
                                    overGui = true
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if overGui then break end
                    end
                end)
            end

            if overGui then
                pcall(function()
                    UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
                    UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
                    UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false
                end)
                local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
                if delta.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local cf = cam2.CFrame
                    cf = CFrame.Angles(0, -delta.X * 0.004, 0) * cf * CFrame.Angles(-delta.Y * 0.004, 0, 0)
                    cam2.CFrame = CFrame.new(cf.Position) * (cf - cf.Position)
                end
            end

            local speed = 60
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then speed = 140 end
            local move = Vector3.zero
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + cam2.CFrame.LookVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move - cam2.CFrame.LookVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move - cam2.CFrame.RightVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + cam2.CFrame.RightVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then move = move + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then move = move - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
            if move.Magnitude > 0 then
                cam2.CFrame = cam2.CFrame + move.Unit * speed * dt
            end
        end)
        Utils.track(Utils.mov.freecamConn)
    else
        Utils.mov.freecam = false
        if Utils.mov.freecamConn then
            pcall(function() Utils.mov.freecamConn:Disconnect() end)
            Utils.mov.freecamConn = nil
        end
        pcall(function()
            cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
        end)
    end
end

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    local M = Utils.mov
    if not M then return end
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if M.hipHeight then
        Utils.movApplyHip()
    end
    if M.quickStop and hrp and hum then
        if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.05 and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
        end
    end
    if M.instantFall and hrp and hum then
        if hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air and hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < 2 then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, math.min(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, -180), hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
        end
    end
    -- CFrame-only fly (no BodyMovers / no velocity writes) for lower detection surface
    if M.fly and hrp and not M.freecam then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local speed = tonumber(M.flySpeed) or 50
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
            speed = speed * 2
        end
        local move = Vector3.zero
        local cf = cam and cam.CFrame or hrp.CFrame
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + cf.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move - cf.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move - cf.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + cf.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
            move = move + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
            move = move - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if move.Magnitude > 0 then
            local nextPos = hrp.Position + move.Unit * speed * dt
            -- keep current facing; only translate (no look-at camera)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(nextPos) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
        else
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
        end
        -- zero velocity without movers so physics doesn't drag you down mid-frame
        pcall(function()
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding)
            end)
        end
    end
    
end))


task.spawn(function()
    local hum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then
        local char = Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    end
    if hum then
        originalJumpPower = hum.JumpPower
        if not jumpPowerActive then
            currentJumpPower = hum.JumpPower
        end
    end
end)

-- Waypoint system (Teleport tab)
Utils.waypoints = {
    folderName = "ZenithWaypoints",
    sets = {},          -- name -> list of {name, x,y,z}
    currentSet = nil,
    setNameInput = "",
    showEsp = false,
    openDeathMarkers = false,
    maxDistance = 1000,
    enableIcon = false,
    iconSize = 24,
    iconColor = Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255),
    showText = true,
    textColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 230, 255),
    textSize = 14,
    drawings = {},      -- runtime Drawing objects
    deathMarks = {},    -- {pos, name, time}
    deathConns = {},
}

Utils.wpEnsureFolder = function()
    pcall(function()
        if isfolder and not isfolder(Utils.waypoints.folderName) then
            makefolder(Utils.waypoints.folderName)
        end
    end)
end

Utils.wpListFiles = function()
    Utils.wpEnsureFolder()
    local names = {}
    pcall(function()
        if not listfiles then return end
        for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(Utils.waypoints.folderName) or {}) do
            local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if n then table.insert(names, n) end
        end
    end)
    table.sort(names)
    return names
end

Utils.wpSaveSet = function(name, points)
    if not name or name == "" then return false, "empty name" end
    Utils.wpEnsureFolder()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local payload = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(points or {})
        writefile(Utils.waypoints.folderName .. "/" .. name .. ".json", payload)
    end)
    if ok then
        Utils.waypoints.sets[name] = points or {}
        Utils.waypoints.currentSet = name
    end
    return ok, err
end

Utils.wpLoadSet = function(name)
    if not name or name == "" then return nil end
    local data = nil
    pcall(function()
        local raw = readfile(Utils.waypoints.folderName .. "/" .. name .. ".json")
        data = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw)
    end)
    if type(data) == "table" then
        Utils.waypoints.sets[name] = data
        Utils.waypoints.currentSet = name
        return data
    end
    return nil
end

Utils.wpDeleteSet = function(name)
    if not name or name == "" then return end
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(Utils.waypoints.folderName .. "/" .. name .. ".json") then
            delfile(Utils.waypoints.folderName .. "/" .. name .. ".json")
        end
    end)
    Utils.waypoints.sets[name] = nil
    if Utils.waypoints.currentSet == name then
        Utils.waypoints.currentSet = nil
    end
end

Utils.wpDeleteNearest = function()
    local setName = Utils.waypoints.currentSet
    if not setName or not Utils.waypoints.sets[setName] then return false end
    local list = Utils.waypoints.sets[setName]
    if #list == 0 then return false end
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if not hrp then return false end
    local bestI, bestD = nil, math.huge
    for i, p in ipairs(list) do
        local d = (Vector3.new(p.x, p.y, p.z) - hrp.Position).Magnitude
        if d < bestD then
            bestD = d
            bestI = i
        end
    end
    if bestI then
        table.remove(list, bestI)
        Utils.waypoints.sets[setName] = list
        Utils.wpSaveSet(setName, list)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

Utils.wpAddHere = function(label)
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if not hrp then return end
    local setName = Utils.waypoints.currentSet
    if not setName or setName == "" then
        setName = Utils.waypoints.setNameInput
        if not setName or setName == "" then
            setName = "Set1"
        end
        Utils.waypoints.currentSet = setName
    end
    local list = Utils.waypoints.sets[setName] or {}
    table.insert(list, {
        name = label or ("WP " .. tostring(#list + 1)),
        x = hrp.Position.X,
        y = hrp.Position.Y,
        z = hrp.Position.Z,
    })
    Utils.waypoints.sets[setName] = list
    Utils.wpSaveSet(setName, list)
end

Utils.wpClearDrawings = function()
    for _, d in pairs(Utils.waypoints.drawings) do
        pcall(function()
            if d.Remove then d:Remove() elseif d.Destroy then d:Destroy() end
        end)
    end
    Utils.waypoints.drawings = {}
end

Utils.wpUpdateEsp = function()
    Utils.wpClearDrawings()
    local W = Utils.waypoints
    if not W.showEsp then return end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local myPos = me and me.Position
    local points = {}
    if W.currentSet and W.sets[W.currentSet] then
        for _, p in ipairs(W.sets[W.currentSet]) do
            table.insert(points, p)
        end
    end
    if W.openDeathMarkers then
        for _, p in ipairs(W.deathMarks) do
            table.insert(points, { name = p.name or "Death", x = p.x, y = p.y, z = p.z, death = true })
        end
    end
    local maxD = tonumber(W.maxDistance) or 1000
    for _, p in ipairs(points) do
        local pos = Vector3.new(p.x, p.y, p.z)
        if not myPos or (pos - myPos).Magnitude <= maxD then
            local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
            if on and sp.Z > 0 then
                local x, y = sp.X, sp.Y
                local iconCol = p.death and Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80) or (W.iconColor or Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255))
                local textCol = p.death and Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120) or (W.textColor or Color3.fromRGB(200, 230, 255))
                if W.enableIcon then
                    local size = tonumber(W.iconSize) or 8
                    -- filled icon
                    local sq = Drawing.new("Square")
                    sq.Size = Vector2.new(size, size)
                    sq.Position = Vector2.new(x - size / 2, y - size / 2)
                    sq.Filled = true
                    sq.Color = iconCol
                    sq.Transparency = 1
                    sq.Visible = true
                    table.insert(W.drawings, sq)
                    -- outline matching ESP text color
                    local ol = Drawing.new("Square")
                    ol.Size = Vector2.new(size + 2, size + 2)
                    ol.Position = Vector2.new(x - (size + 2) / 2, y - (size + 2) / 2)
                    ol.Filled = false
                    ol.Thickness = 1
                    ol.Color = textCol
                    ol.Transparency = 1
                    ol.Visible = true
                    table.insert(W.drawings, ol)
                end
                if W.showText ~= false then
                    local txt = Drawing.new("Text")
                    txt.Text = tostring(p.name or "WP")
                    txt.Size = tonumber(W.textSize) or 14
                    txt.Center = true
                    txt.Outline = true
                    txt.Color = textCol
                    txt.Position = Vector2.new(x, y + ((W.enableIcon and ((tonumber(W.iconSize) or 8) / 2 + 2)) or 0))
                    txt.Visible = true
                    table.insert(W.drawings, txt)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

Utils.wpHookDeaths = function()
    local W = Utils.waypoints
    for _, c in pairs(W.deathConns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
    W.deathConns = {}
    if not W.logDeath then return end
    local function track(plr)
        if plr == Player then return end
        local function onChar(char)
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not hum then return end
            local c = hum.Died:Connect(function()
                if not Utils.waypoints.logDeath then return end
                local part = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if part then
                    table.insert(Utils.waypoints.deathMarks, {
                        name = plr.Name,
                        x = part.Position.X,
                        y = part.Position.Y,
                        z = part.Position.Z,
                        t = tick(),
                    })
                    -- keep last 40
                    while #Utils.waypoints.deathMarks > 40 do
                        table.remove(Utils.waypoints.deathMarks, 1)
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.insert(Utils.waypoints.deathConns, c)
        end
        if plr.Character then onChar(plr.Character) end
        table.insert(W.deathConns, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(onChar))
    end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do track(plr) end
    table.insert(W.deathConns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(track))
end

Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    pcall(Utils.wpUpdateEsp)
end))


Utils.tpApply = function(dt)
    local T = Utils.tp
    local target = T.selected
    if not target then return end
    local dest = target.Character and (target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    local me = Player.Character and (Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if not (dest and me) then return end
    local dist = math.clamp(tonumber(T.distance) or 3, 0, 30)
    if T.orbit then
        T.angle = (T.angle or 0) + (tonumber(T.orbitSpeed) or 2) * (dt or 0.03)
        local off = Vector3.new(math.sin(T.angle) * dist, 0, math.cos(T.angle) * dist)
        me.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest.Position + off, dest.Position)
    else
        me.CFrame = dest.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, dist)
    end
end

Utils.tpClosestLook = function()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge
    local center = Vector2.new(cam.ViewportSize.X * 0.5, cam.ViewportSize.Y * 0.5)
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
            local part = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if part then
                local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                if onScreen and sp.Z > 0 then
                    local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
                    if d < bestScore then
                        bestScore = d
                        best = plr
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not best then return end
    Utils.tp.selected = best
    Utils.tpApply(0)
end

Utils.tpTrigger = function(isDown)
    local T = Utils.tp
    if not T.selected then return end
    if T.mode == "Hold" then
        T.holding = isDown and true or false
        if isDown then
            T.lastTp = 0
            Utils.tpApply(0)
        end
    elseif T.mode == "Toggle" then
        if isDown then
            T.active = not T.active
            if T.active then
                T.lastTp = 0
                Utils.tpApply(0)
            end
        end
    else -- Press
        if isDown then
            Utils.tpApply(0)
        end
    end
end

Utils.mm2 = Utils.mm2 or {
    autoGrab = false,
    silent = false,
    murdererEsp = false,
    sheriffEsp = false,
    gunDropEsp = false,

    sheriffHl = {},
    gunHl = nil,

}

Utils.mm2HasKnife = function(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    if not char then return false end
    if char:FindFirstChild("Knife") then return true end
    local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    return bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Knife") ~= nil
end

Utils.mm2HasGun = function(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    if not char then return false end
    if char:FindFirstChild("Gun") or char:FindFirstChild("Revolver") then return true end
    local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    return bp and (bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bp:FindFirstChild("Revolver")) ~= nil
end

Utils.mm2GrabGun = function()
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    if Utils.mm2HasKnife(Player) then return end
    local gunDrop = workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop", true)
    if not gunDrop then return end
    for _, part in ipairs(gunDrop:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.Transparency = 1
        elseif string.find(string.lower(part.Name), "fire", 1, true) or string.find(string.lower(part.Name), "flame", 1, true) then
            pcall(function() part:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then
        pcall(function() gunDrop:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame) end)
    end
end

Utils.mm2SetAutoGrab = function(on)
    Utils.mm2.autoGrab = on and true or false
    if Utils.mm2.conns.autoGrab then
        pcall(function() Utils.mm2.conns.autoGrab:Disconnect() end)
        Utils.mm2.conns.autoGrab = nil
    end
    if Utils.mm2.autoGrab then
        Utils.mm2.conns.autoGrab = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if obj.Name == "GunDrop" then
                task.defer(Utils.mm2GrabGun)
            end
        end)
        Utils.track(Utils.mm2.conns.autoGrab)
    end
end

Utils.mm2TpLobby = function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    local spawnsModel
    for _, name in ipairs({ "SummerLobby", "RegularLobby" }) do
        local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
        if lobby then
            spawnsModel = lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
            if spawnsModel then break end
        end
    end
    if not spawnsModel then return end
    local pts = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(spawnsModel:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("SpawnLocation") or (obj.Name == "Spawn" and obj:IsA("BasePart")) then
            table.insert(pts, obj)
        end
    end
    if #pts > 0 then
        hrp.CFrame = pts[math.random(1, #pts)].CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    end
end

Utils.mm2TpMap = function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    local spawnsModel
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj.Name == "Spawns" and obj:IsA("Model") then
            local hasSL = false
            for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("SpawnLocation") then hasSL = true break end
            end
            if not hasSL then
                spawnsModel = obj
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not spawnsModel then return end
    local pts = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(spawnsModel:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj.Name == "Spawn" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            table.insert(pts, obj)
        end
    end
    if #pts > 0 then
        hrp.CFrame = pts[math.random(1, #pts)].CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    end
end

Utils.mm2SilentFire = function()
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local gun = char:FindFirstChild("Gun") or char:FindFirstChild("Revolver")
    if not gun then return end
    local originCF
    local gunServer = gun:FindFirstChild("GunServer")
    if gunServer then
        local att = gunServer:FindFirstChild("GunRaycastAttachment1") or gunServer:FindFirstChild("RaycastAttachment")
        if att then originCF = att.WorldCFrame end
    end
    if not originCF then
        local handle = gun:FindFirstChild("Handle") or gun:FindFirstChild("Gun")
        if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then originCF = handle.CFrame end
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not originCF and hrp then originCF = hrp.CFrame end
    if not originCF then return end
    local targetHRP
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and Utils.mm2HasKnife(plr) then
            local th = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if th then targetHRP = th break end
        end
    end
    if not targetHRP then return end
    local shootRemote = gun:FindFirstChild("Shoot") or gun:FindFirstChild("Fire")
    if shootRemote then
        pcall(function()
            shootRemote:FireServer(CFrame.new(originCF.Position, targetHRP.Position), CFrame.new(targetHRP.Position))
        end)
    end
end

Utils.mm2IsInRound = function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return false end
    for _, name in ipairs({ "SummerLobby", "RegularLobby" }) do
        local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
        if lobby and hrp:IsDescendantOf(lobby) then
            return false
        end
    end
    return true
end
Utils.mm2SetNoclip = function(on)
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.CanCollide = not on
        end
    end
end
Utils.mm2FlyToCoin = function(hrp, hum, part)
    if not part or not part.Parent or not hrp then return end
    local target = part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
    local done = false
    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Utils.mm2.coins or not hrp.Parent or not part.Parent then
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            done = true
            return
        end
        local dir = target - hrp.Position
        if dir.Magnitude < 1.5 then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(target)
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            done = true
            return
        end
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir.Unit * (hum and hum.WalkSpeed or 16)
    end)
    local t0 = tick()
    while not done and Utils.mm2.coins and tick() - t0 < 8 do
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    pcall(function() if conn then conn:Disconnect() end end)
    if hrp and hrp.Parent then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
end
Utils.mm2SetCoins = function(on)
    Utils.mm2.coins = on and true or false
    if Utils.mm2.coinTask then
        pcall(function() task.cancel(Utils.mm2.coinTask) end)
        Utils.mm2.coinTask = nil
    end
    local hrp = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
    if not Utils.mm2.coins then
        Utils.mm2SetNoclip(false)
        return
    end
    Utils.mm2SetNoclip(true)
    Utils.mm2.coinTask = task.spawn(function()
        while Utils.mm2.coins do
            if Utils.mm2IsInRound() then
                local char = Player.Character
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if root and hum then
                    Utils.mm2SetNoclip(true)
                    local coins = {}
                    for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                        if model:IsA("Model") then
                            local box = model:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer")
                            if box and box:IsA("Model") then
                                for _, p in ipairs(box:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(coins, p) end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    for _, p in ipairs(coins) do
                        if not Utils.mm2.coins then break end
                        if p and p.Parent then
                            Utils.mm2FlyToCoin(root, hum, p)
                            task.wait(0.12)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(1.1)
        end
        Utils.mm2SetNoclip(false)
    end)
end
Utils.mm2SetSilent = function(on)
    Utils.mm2.silent = on and true or false
    if Utils.mm2.conns.silent then
        pcall(function() Utils.mm2.conns.silent:Disconnect() end)
        Utils.mm2.conns.silent = nil
    end
    if Utils.mm2.silent then
        Utils.mm2.conns.silent = Player:GetMouse().Button1Down:Connect(function()
            if Utils.mm2.silent then Utils.mm2SilentFire() end
        end)
        Utils.track(Utils.mm2.conns.silent)
    end
end

Utils.mm2ClearHl = function(tbl)
    for plr, hl in pairs(tbl) do
        pcall(function() if hl then hl:Destroy() end end)
        tbl[plr] = nil
    end
end

Utils.mm2MakeHl = function(plr, color)
    if not plr.Character then return nil end
    local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
    hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    hl.FillTransparency = 0.6
    hl.OutlineTransparency = 0.2
    hl.FillColor = color
    hl.OutlineColor = color
    hl.Parent = plr.Character
    return hl
end

Utils.mm2UpdateRoles = function()
    local M = Utils.mm2
    if M.murdererEsp then
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                if Utils.mm2HasKnife(plr) then
                    if not M.murdHl[plr] or not M.murdHl[plr].Parent then
                        if M.murdHl[plr] then pcall(function() M.murdHl[plr]:Destroy() end) end
                        M.murdHl[plr] = Utils.mm2MakeHl(plr, Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40))
                    end
                elseif M.murdHl[plr] then
                    pcall(function() M.murdHl[plr]:Destroy() end)
                    M.murdHl[plr] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    else
        Utils.mm2ClearHl(M.murdHl)
    end
    if M.sheriffEsp then
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                if Utils.mm2HasGun(plr) then
                    if not M.sheriffHl[plr] or not M.sheriffHl[plr].Parent then
                        if M.sheriffHl[plr] then pcall(function() M.sheriffHl[plr]:Destroy() end) end
                        M.sheriffHl[plr] = Utils.mm2MakeHl(plr, Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 255))
                    end
                elseif M.sheriffHl[plr] then
                    pcall(function() M.sheriffHl[plr]:Destroy() end)
                    M.sheriffHl[plr] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    else
        Utils.mm2ClearHl(M.sheriffHl)
    end
end

Utils.mm2SetGunDropEsp = function(on)
    Utils.mm2.gunDropEsp = on and true or false
    if Utils.mm2.conns.gunDrop then
        pcall(function() Utils.mm2.conns.gunDrop:Disconnect() end)
        Utils.mm2.conns.gunDrop = nil
    end
    if Utils.mm2.gunHl then
        pcall(function() Utils.mm2.gunHl:Destroy() end)
        Utils.mm2.gunHl = nil
    end
    local function mark(obj)
