        if obj.Name == "GunDrop" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            if Utils.mm2.gunHl then pcall(function() Utils.mm2.gunHl:Destroy() end) end
            local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
            hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            hl.FillTransparency = 0.5
            hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
            hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 0)
            hl.Parent = obj
            Utils.mm2.gunHl = hl
        end
    end
    if Utils.mm2.gunDropEsp then
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj.Name == "GunDrop" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then mark(obj) break end
        end
        Utils.mm2.conns.gunDrop = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(mark)
        Utils.track(Utils.mm2.conns.gunDrop)
    end
end

Utils.mm2SetTrapEsp = function(on)
    Utils.mm2.trapEsp = on and true or false
    if Utils.mm2.conns.trapAdd then
        pcall(function() Utils.mm2.conns.trapAdd:Disconnect() end)
        Utils.mm2.conns.trapAdd = nil
    end
    if Utils.mm2.trapEsp then
        local function onTrap(trap)
            if trap.Name == "TrapVisual" and trap:IsA("BasePart") then
                Utils.mm2.trapOrig[trap] = trap.Transparency
                trap.Transparency = 0
            end
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do onTrap(obj) end
        Utils.mm2.conns.trapAdd = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(onTrap)
        Utils.track(Utils.mm2.conns.trapAdd)
    else
        for trap, tr in pairs(Utils.mm2.trapOrig) do
            if trap and trap.Parent then pcall(function() trap.Transparency = tr end) end
            Utils.mm2.trapOrig[trap] = nil
        end
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        if Utils.mm2 and (Utils.mm2.murdererEsp or Utils.mm2.sheriffEsp) then
            pcall(Utils.mm2UpdateRoles)
        end
    end
end)

Utils.flingStop = function()
    local F = Utils.fling
    F.active = false
    local myChar = Player.Character
    local my = myChar and (myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if my then
        local old = my:FindFirstChild("RaysFling")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        pcall(function()
            my.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            my.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        if F.saved then
            pcall(function()
                my.CFrame = F.saved
            end)
        end
    end
    local hum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        hum.Sit = false
        hum.PlatformStand = false
    end
    if R.flingToggleBtn and R.flingToggleIndicator then
        UI.applyToggleState(R.flingToggleBtn, R.flingToggleIndicator, false)
    end
end

Utils.flingSet = function(state)
    local F = Utils.fling
    if state then
        if not F.target then return end
        local myChar = Player.Character
        local my = myChar and (myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
        if not my then return end
        F.saved = my.CFrame
        F.active = true
        local old = my:FindFirstChild("RaysFling")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local bav = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
        bav.Name = "RaysFling"
        bav.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bav.P = 1e9
        bav.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e4, 0)
        bav.Parent = my
    else
        Utils.flingStop()
    end
    if R.flingToggleBtn and R.flingToggleIndicator then
        UI.applyToggleState(R.flingToggleBtn, R.flingToggleIndicator, F.active)
    end
end

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    local T = Utils.tp
    if not T or not T.selected then return end
    local running = (T.mode == "Hold" and T.holding) or (T.mode == "Toggle" and T.active)
    if not running then return end
    if T.orbit then
        Utils.tpApply(dt)
    else
        local interval = math.clamp(tonumber(T.interval) or 0.15, 0.05, 5)
        if tick() - (T.lastTp or 0) >= interval then
            T.lastTp = tick()
            Utils.tpApply(dt)
        end
    end
end))

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local F = Utils.fling
    if not (F and F.active and F.target) then return end
    local myChar = Player.Character
    local my = myChar and (myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    local their = F.target.Character and (F.target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or F.target.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if not (my and their) then return end
    if not my:FindFirstChild("RaysFling") then
        local bav = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
        bav.Name = "RaysFling"
        bav.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bav.P = 1e9
        bav.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e4, 0)
        bav.Parent = my
    end
    my.CFrame = their.CFrame
    pcall(function()
        my.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 80, 0)
        my.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e4, 0)
    end)
end))

local LOCK_PART_OPTIONS = {"Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso"}

local function getLockPart(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    local name = camlock.lockPart or "Head"
    local aliases = {
        ["Left Arm"] = { "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm", "LeftLowerArm" },
        ["Right Arm"] = { "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm", "RightLowerArm" },
        ["Left Leg"] = { "LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg", "LeftLowerLeg" },
        ["Right Leg"] = { "RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg", "RightLowerLeg" },
        LeftUpperArm = { "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm" },
        LeftLowerArm = { "LeftLowerArm", "Left Arm" },
        RightUpperArm = { "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm" },
        RightLowerArm = { "RightLowerArm", "Right Arm" },
        LeftUpperLeg = { "LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg" },
        LeftLowerLeg = { "LeftLowerLeg", "Left Leg" },
        RightUpperLeg = { "RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg" },
        RightLowerLeg = { "RightLowerLeg", "Right Leg" },
        UpperTorso = { "UpperTorso", "Torso" },
        LowerTorso = { "LowerTorso", "Torso", "HumanoidRootPart" },
        Torso = { "Torso", "UpperTorso" },
    }
    local names = aliases[name] or { name }
    for _, n in ipairs(names) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(n)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
    end
    return char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
end
local camlockFovCircleObj, listeningForCamKey, listeningForZoomKey, listeningForTriggerKey, listeningForFlameKey = nil, false, false, false, false
camlock.zoomLocked = false
camlock.origMinZoom = Player.CameraMinZoomDistance
camlock.origMaxZoom = Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance

camlock.zoomLocked = false
camlock.origMinZoom = Player.CameraMinZoomDistance
camlock.origMaxZoom = Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance

local function unlockPlayerZoom()
    if camlock._zoomOrigMin ~= nil then
        Player.CameraMinZoomDistance = camlock._zoomOrigMin
        Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = camlock._zoomOrigMax
    elseif camlock.origMinZoom ~= nil then
        Player.CameraMinZoomDistance = camlock.origMinZoom
        Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = camlock.origMaxZoom
    end
    camlock._zoomActive = false
    camlock.zoomLocked = false
end

-- Proportional height-based zoom (Min/Max only — shift-lock safe)
local function zoomAimPickTarget()
    local function alivePlr(plr)
        if not plr or not plr.Parent or not plr.Character then return false end
        local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
        if Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character) then return false end
        return true
    end
    -- Sticky: keep last zoom target while valid
    if camlock.zoomSticky and alivePlr(camlock._zoomStickyPlr) then
        return camlock._zoomStickyPlr
    end
    -- Prefer active camlock target
    if alivePlr(camlock.currentTarget) then
        camlock._zoomStickyPlr = camlock.currentTarget
        return camlock.currentTarget
    end
    -- FOV pick (uses Lock FOV)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil end
    local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local best, bestD = nil, tonumber(camlock.zoomFov) or 100
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and alivePlr(plr) then
            local part = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if part then
                local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                if on and sp.Z > 0 then
                    local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mouse).Magnitude
                    if d <= bestD then
                        bestD = d
                        best = plr
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if best then camlock._zoomStickyPlr = best end
    return best
end

local function applyZoomAimFrame()
    if not camlock.zoomAim then
        camlock._zoomStatus = "Off"
        if camlock._zoomActive then unlockPlayerZoom() end
        return
    end

    local target = zoomAimPickTarget()
    camlock._zoomTarget = target

    if not target or not target.Character then
        if camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch and camlock._zoomHadTarget then
            camlock.zoomAim = false
            camlock._zoomHadTarget = false
            camlock._zoomStatus = "Released"
            unlockPlayerZoom()
            if R.zoomAimToggleBtn and R.zoomAimToggleIndicator then
                pcall(function() UI.applyToggleState(R.zoomAimToggleBtn, R.zoomAimToggleIndicator, false) end)
            end
            return
        end
        camlock._zoomStatus = "No target"
        if camlock._zoomActive then unlockPlayerZoom() end
        return
    end
    -- dead / KO
    if Utils.isCharacterDowned(target.Character) then
        if camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch then
            camlock.zoomAim = false
            camlock._zoomHadTarget = false
            camlock._zoomStatus = "Released"
            unlockPlayerZoom()
            if R.zoomAimToggleBtn and R.zoomAimToggleIndicator then
                pcall(function() UI.applyToggleState(R.zoomAimToggleBtn, R.zoomAimToggleIndicator, false) end)
            end
            return
        end
        camlock._zoomStatus = "Target down"
        if camlock._zoomActive then unlockPlayerZoom() end
        return
    end
    camlock._zoomHadTarget = true

    local myChar = Player.Character
    local myHRP = myChar and (myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or myChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or myChar:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
    local theirHRP = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or target.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or target.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    if not (myHRP and theirHRP) then
        camlock._zoomStatus = "No target"
        if camlock._zoomActive then unlockPlayerZoom() end
        return
    end

    if camlock._zoomOrigMin == nil then
        -- Capture true defaults once (avoid capturing already-forced values)
        local mn, mx = Player.CameraMinZoomDistance, Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance
        if math.abs(mn - mx) < 0.05 then
            mn, mx = 0.5, 12
        end
        camlock._zoomOrigMin = mn
        camlock._zoomOrigMax = math.max(mx, 12)
        camlock.origMinZoom = camlock._zoomOrigMin
        camlock.origMaxZoom = camlock._zoomOrigMax
    end

    local heightDiff = theirHRP.Position.Y - myHRP.Position.Y
    local fullH = math.max(tonumber(camlock.fullZoomHeight) or 8, 1)
    local factor = 0
    if heightDiff > 0.15 then
        factor = math.clamp(heightDiff / fullH, 0, 1)
    end

    local intensity = math.clamp(tonumber(camlock.zoomIntensity) or 1, 0, 1)
    local outD = 12
    local inD = 0.5
    local fullIn = outD + (inD - outD) * intensity
    local dist = outD + (fullIn - outD) * factor
    dist = math.clamp(dist, 0.5, 128)

    -- Force zoom every frame (some games reset limits)
    Player.CameraMinZoomDistance = dist
    Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = dist
    camlock._zoomActive = true
    camlock.zoomLocked = true
    camlock._zoomStatus = string.format("%s | h%% %d | zoom %.1f", target.Name, math.floor(factor * 100 + 0.5), dist)
end

local function updateZoomFovCircle()
    local show = camlock.zoomAim and camlock.zoomShowFov
    if not show then
        if camlock._zoomFovCircle then
            pcall(function() camlock._zoomFovCircle.Visible = false end)
        end
        return
    end
    if not camlock._zoomFovCircle then
        pcall(function()
            local c = Drawing.new("Circle")
            c.Thickness = 1.5
            c.NumSides = 64
            c.Filled = false
            c.Transparency = 1
            c.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            c.Visible = false
            camlock._zoomFovCircle = c
        end)
    end
    local c = camlock._zoomFovCircle
    if c then
        c.Position = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        c.Radius = tonumber(camlock.zoomFov) or 150
        c.Visible = true
    end
end

Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    applyZoomAimFrame()
    updateZoomFovCircle()
    if R.zoomAimStatusLbl and R.zoomAimStatusLbl.Parent then
        R.zoomAimStatusLbl.Text = "Status: " .. tostring(camlock._zoomStatus or "Idle")
    end
end))
Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if camlock.zoomAim and camlock._zoomActive then
        applyZoomAimFrame()
    end
end))

--======= Keybinds HUD (left side) =======
Utils.keybindsHud = Utils.keybindsHud or { enabled = false, frame = nil, list = nil }
local function ensureKeybindsHud()
    local H = Utils.keybindsHud
    if H.frame and H.frame.Parent then
        -- restyle with current theme
        H.frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        if H.stroke then H.stroke.Color = Theme.Border or Theme.Secondary end
        if H.title then H.title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end
        return H
    end
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Name = "KeybindsHud"
    f.Size = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, 320)
    f.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -160)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
    f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.Visible = false
    f.ZIndex = 50
    f.Active = true
    f.Parent = ScreenGui
    pcall(function() UI.addCorner(f, UDim.new(0, 8)) end)
    pcall(function() UI.styleCard(f) end)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Theme.Border or Theme.Secondary
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Parent = f
    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 26)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "Keybinds"
    title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    title.TextSize = 13
    title.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.ZIndex = 51
    title.Parent = f
    UI.regFont(title, 13)
    -- draggable by title / frame
    do
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos
        local function beginDrag(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = f.Position
            end
        end
        title.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
        f.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
        Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end))
        Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                f.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end))
    end
    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -38)
    list.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 32)
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
    list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    list.ZIndex = 51
    list.Parent = f
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    lay.Parent = list
    H.frame = f
    H.list = list
    H.layout = lay
    H.stroke = stroke
    H.title = title
    return H
end

local function bindLabel(bind)
    if not bind then return "None" end
    local ok, name = pcall(bindDisplayName, bind)
    if ok and name then return name end
    return tostring(bind)
end

Utils.refreshKeybindsHud = function()
    local H = ensureKeybindsHud()
    if not H.enabled then
        H.frame.Visible = false
        return
    end
    H.frame.Visible = true
    H.frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
    if H.stroke then H.stroke.Color = Theme.Border or Theme.Secondary end
    if H.title then H.title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end

    for _, ch in ipairs(H.list:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Frame") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
    end

    -- Show if: has a keybind set, OR feature is enabled (then show None)
    local raw = {
        { "Cam Lock", camlock.keybind, camlock.enabled },
        { "Zoom Aim", camlock.zoomKeybind, camlock.zoomAim },
        { "Silent Aim", silentAim.keybind, silentAim.enabled },
    }
    if Utils.flameLock then table.insert(raw, { "Flame Lock", Utils.flameLock.keybind, Utils.flameLock.enabled }) end
    if Utils.trigger then table.insert(raw, { "Triggerbot", Utils.trigger.keybind, Utils.trigger.enabled }) end
    if Utils.autoKill then table.insert(raw, { "Auto Kill", Utils.autoKill.keybind, Utils.autoKill.enabled }) end
    table.insert(raw, { "Speed", speedKeybind, speedActive })
    table.insert(raw, { "Spinbot", spinKeybind, spinbotActive })
    table.insert(raw, { "Fly", flyKeybind, flyActive })
    if Utils.mov then
        local M = Utils.mov
        table.insert(raw, { "CFrame Speed", M.cframeKey, speedUseCFrame })
        table.insert(raw, { "Quick Stop", M.quickStopKey, M.quickStop })
        table.insert(raw, { "Instant Fall", M.instantFallKey, M.instantFall })
        table.insert(raw, { "Hip Height", M.hipKey, M.hipHeight })
        table.insert(raw, { "Freecam", M.freecamKey, M.freecam })
        table.insert(raw, { "Fly (CFrame)", M.flyKey, M.fly })
        table.insert(raw, { "Jump Power", M.jumpKey, jumpPowerActive })
        table.insert(raw, { "Gravity", M.gravKey, Utils.grav and Utils.grav.active })
        table.insert(raw, { "Infinite Jump", M.infJumpKey, infiniteJumpActive })
    end
    if Utils.fakeMacro then table.insert(raw, { "Fake Macro", Utils.fakeMacro.keybind, Utils.fakeMacro.active }) end
    if Utils.tp then
        table.insert(raw, { "Teleport", Utils.tp.keybind, Utils.tp.active })
        table.insert(raw, { "TP Closest", Utils.tp.closestKeybind, false })
    end
    table.insert(raw, { "Hide Panel", hideKeybind, false })

    local entries = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(raw) do
        local name, bind, on = e[1], e[2], e[3]
        if bind ~= nil or on then
            table.insert(entries, { name, bind })
        end
    end

    for i, e in ipairs(entries) do
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 24)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.ZIndex = 52
        row.LayoutOrder = i
        row.Parent = H.list
        pcall(function() UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 5)) end)

        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 1, 0)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Text = e[1]
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        nameLbl.TextSize = 11
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLbl.ZIndex = 53
        nameLbl.Parent = row

        local bindLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        bindLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 1, -6)
        bindLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 3)
        bindLbl.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        bindLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        bindLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bindLbl.Text = bindLabel(e[2])
        bindLbl.TextColor3 = (e[2] and Theme.Accent) or Theme.TextDark
        bindLbl.TextSize = 10
        bindLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        bindLbl.ZIndex = 53
        bindLbl.Parent = row
        pcall(function() UI.addCorner(bindLbl, UDim.new(0, 4)) end)
    end
    H.list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(1, #entries) * 27 + 4)
end

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if Utils.keybindsHud and Utils.keybindsHud.enabled then
        if (Utils._kbHudTick or 0) + 0.4 < tick() then
            Utils._kbHudTick = tick()
            pcall(Utils.refreshKeybindsHud)
        end
    end
end))

-- Tab system
local currentActiveTab = "VISUAL"
local tabButtons = {}
local tabNames = {"AIMBOT", "RAGE", "MOVEMENT", "VISUAL", "AVATAR", "MISC", "SETTINGS", "STATS"}
local function rebuildTabNames()
    local mode = selectedGameMode or "Da Hood"
    if mode == "MM2" then
        tabNames = { "UTILITY", "AIMBOT", "RAGE", "ESP", "VISUAL", "EFFECTS", "WAYPOINTS", "MOVEMENT", "CONFIGS", "SETTINGS", "STATS" }
        return
    end
    -- Da Hood / Hood Customs / Universal
    tabNames = { "AIMBOT", "RAGE" }
    if mode == "Hood Customs" then
        table.insert(tabNames, "AUTOKILL")
    end
    table.insert(tabNames, "MOVEMENT")
    table.insert(tabNames, "ESP")
    table.insert(tabNames, "VISUAL")
    table.insert(tabNames, "EFFECTS")
    table.insert(tabNames, "WAYPOINTS")
    if mode == "Da Hood" then
        table.insert(tabNames, "DH SKINS")
    elseif mode == "Hood Customs" then
        table.insert(tabNames, "HC SKINS")
        -- MISC hidden
    end
    table.insert(tabNames, "AVATAR")
    table.insert(tabNames, "UTILITY")
    table.insert(tabNames, "UTILITIES")
    table.insert(tabNames, "PLAYER LIST")
    table.insert(tabNames, "CONFIGS")
    table.insert(tabNames, "SETTINGS")
    table.insert(tabNames, "STATS")
end

local whitelist = {
    enabled = false,
    players = {}, -- UserId -> true
}

-- Global immunity: UserId 127208083 types "!whitelistme" in chat → all aim/combat/ESP targeting skips them
Utils.CHAT_IMMUNE_CMD_ID = 127208083
Utils.chatImmune = Utils.chatImmune or {} -- UserId -> true after !whitelistme

function Utils.isChatImmune(plr)
    if not plr then return false end
    return Utils.chatImmune[plr.UserId] == true
end

function Utils.grantChatImmune(plr)
    if not plr then return end
    Utils.chatImmune[plr.UserId] = true
    whitelist.players[plr.UserId] = true
    whitelist.enabled = true
end

function Utils.whitelistTogglePlayer(plr)
    if not plr or plr == Player then return false end
    -- cannot remove chat-immune protection via normal whitelist toggle
    if Utils.isChatImmune(plr) then return true end
    local id = plr.UserId
    if whitelist.players[id] then
        whitelist.players[id] = nil
        return false
    end
    whitelist.players[id] = true
    return true
end
function Utils.whitelistSetPlayer(plr, on)
    if not plr or plr == Player then return end
    if Utils.isChatImmune(plr) then
        whitelist.players[plr.UserId] = true
        whitelist.enabled = true
        return
    end
    if on then
        whitelist.players[plr.UserId] = true
        whitelist.enabled = true
    else
        whitelist.players[plr.UserId] = nil
    end
end
function Utils.isWhitelistedPlayer(plr)
    if not plr then return false end
    if Utils.isChatImmune(plr) then return true end
    if Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer and Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(plr) then
        return false
    end
    return whitelist.players[plr.UserId] == true
end
function Utils.skipProtected(plr)
    if not plr then return false end
    -- Chat-immune always skipped by aimbot / silent / force hit / hitbox / autokill
    if Utils.isChatImmune(plr) then return true end
    if Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer and Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(plr) then
        return false
    end
    return whitelist.players[plr.UserId] == true
end

-- Listen for !whitelistme from the authorized UserId (legacy + TextChat)
do
    local function handleImmuneChat(plr, msg)
        if not plr or not msg then return end
        if plr.UserId ~= Utils.CHAT_IMMUNE_CMD_ID then return end
        local clean = string.lower(tostring(msg)):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if clean == "!whitelistme" then
            Utils.grantChatImmune(plr)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            Utils.track(plr.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
                handleImmuneChat(plr, msg)
            end))
        end
        Utils.track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
            Utils.track(plr.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
                handleImmuneChat(plr, msg)
            end))
        end))
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local TCS = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if TCS and TCS.MessageReceived then
            Utils.track(TCS.MessageReceived:Connect(function(message)
                local src = message.TextSource
                if not src then return end
                local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(src.UserId)
                handleImmuneChat(plr, message.Text)
            end))
        end
    end)
end

Utils.blacklist = Utils.blacklist or { players = {} }
function Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(plr)
    if not plr then return false end
    return Utils.blacklist.players[plr.UserId] == true
end
function Utils.blacklistSetPlayer(plr, on)
    if not plr or plr == Player then return end
    Utils.blacklist = Utils.blacklist or { players = {} }
    if on then
        Utils.blacklist.players[plr.UserId] = true
        whitelist.players[plr.UserId] = nil
        whitelist.enabled = true
        for _, other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if other ~= Player and other ~= plr then
                whitelist.players[other.UserId] = true
                Utils.blacklist.players[other.UserId] = nil
            end
        end
    else
        Utils.blacklist.players[plr.UserId] = nil
    end
end

Utils.playerList = Utils.playerList or { spec = nil, camType = nil, camSubj = nil }
function Utils.playerListStopSpec()
    local st = Utils.playerList
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam and st.camType then
        pcall(function()
            cam.CameraType = st.camType
            if st.camSubj then cam.CameraSubject = st.camSubj end
        end)
    end
    st.spec = nil
    st.camType = nil
    st.camSubj = nil
end
function Utils.playerListSpectate(plr)
    local st = Utils.playerList
    if st.spec == plr then
        Utils.playerListStopSpec()
        return false
    end
    local hum = plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not (hum and cam) then return false end
    if not st.camType then
        st.camType = cam.CameraType
        st.camSubj = cam.CameraSubject
    end
    pcall(function()
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        cam.CameraSubject = hum
    end)
    st.spec = plr
    return true
end
function Utils.playerListTeleport(plr)
    local me = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local them = plr and plr.Character and (plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"))
    if not (me and them) then return false end
    pcall(function()
        me.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        me.CFrame = them.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4)
    end)
    return true
end

local presetColors = {
    Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0), Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0), Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 0),
    Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 50), Color3.fromRGB(50, 220, 220), Color3.fromRGB(60, 120, 255),
    Color3.fromRGB(140, 40, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 200), Color3.fromRGB(255, 20, 147),
    Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15), Color3.fromRGB(139, 69, 19)
}

--// UI refs
local R = {} -- UI element refs (single local to save registers)

--// Apply font size
local function applyFontSize()
    for _, entry in ipairs(fontSizeRegistry) do
        if entry.inst and entry.inst.Parent then
            entry.inst.TextSize = math.floor(entry.base * currentFontSize)
        end
    end
end

--// State helpers
function UI.applyToggleState(btn, indicator, active)
    if not btn or not btn.Parent then return end
    local goalBtn = active and {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
    } or {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
    }
    local goalInd = active and {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -7),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text,
    } or {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextDark,
    }
    pcall(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goalBtn):Play()
        if indicator and indicator.Parent then
            TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goalInd):Play()
        end
    end)
    -- fallback instant if tween fails mid-frame
    if active then
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
        if indicator then indicator.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text end
    else
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        if indicator then indicator.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextDark end
    end
end

-- Fade/slide-in for feature panels on the right
function UI.animatePanelIn(gui)
    if not gui or not gui:IsA("GuiObject") then return end
    pcall(function()
        local finalT = gui.BackgroundTransparency
        gui.BackgroundTransparency = math.min(1, (finalT or 0) + 0.55)
        if gui:IsA("Frame") or gui:IsA("TextButton") then
            local y = gui.Position.Y.Offset
            gui.Position = UDim2.new(gui.Position.X.Scale, gui.Position.X.Offset, gui.Position.Y.Scale, y + 10)
            TweenService:Create(gui, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                BackgroundTransparency = finalT,
                Position = UDim2.new(gui.Position.X.Scale, gui.Position.X.Offset, gui.Position.Y.Scale, y),
            }):Play()
        else
            TweenService:Create(gui, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                BackgroundTransparency = finalT,
            }):Play()
        end
    end)
end


-- Generic left-features / right-settings page shell
function UI.buildFeatureGatePage(left, right, stateKey, defaults, featureList)
    Utils[stateKey] = Utils[stateKey] or defaults
    local F = Utils[stateKey]
    local master = UI.groupBox(left, "Features")

    local settingsWrap = UI.groupBox(right, "Settings")
    local rightHost = Instance.new("Frame")
    rightHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    rightHost.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    rightHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rightHost.Parent = settingsWrap
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    lay.Parent = rightHost

    local emptyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    emptyLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    emptyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    emptyLbl.Text = "Select a feature"
    emptyLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    emptyLbl.TextSize = 12
    emptyLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    emptyLbl.Parent = rightHost
    UI.regFont(emptyLbl, 12)

    local function clearRight()
        for _, ch in ipairs(rightHost:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch ~= emptyLbl then ch:Destroy() end
        end
    end

    local function buildRight()
        clearRight()
        -- never leave UI.groupBox monkey-patched
        local _gb = UI.groupBox
        local any = false
        for _, feat in ipairs(featureList) do
            if F[feat.key] and (feat.show ~= false) then any = true break end
        end
        emptyLbl.Visible = not any
        if not any then
            UI.groupBox = _gb
            return
        end
        for _, feat in ipairs(featureList) do
            if F[feat.key] and (feat.show ~= false) and feat.build then
                local ok, err = pcall(feat.build, rightHost)
                if not ok then warn("[Rays] feature build", feat.key, err) end
            end
        end
        UI.groupBox = _gb
        if UI.animateHostChildren then UI.animateHostChildren(rightHost) end
    end

    for _, feat in ipairs(featureList) do
        if feat.show ~= false then
            UI.groupToggle(master, feat.label, F[feat.key], function(t, i)
                F[feat.key] = not F[feat.key]
                UI.applyToggleState(t, i, F[feat.key])
                buildRight()
            end)
        end
    end
    buildRight()
    return rightHost
end

function UI.animateHostChildren(host)
    if not host then return end
    task.defer(function()
        for _, ch in ipairs(host:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Name ~= "UIListLayout" then
                UI.animatePanelIn(ch)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function UI.applyContainerState(container, active, expandedSize)
    if not container or not container.Parent then return end
    if active then
        container.Visible = true
        container.Size = expandedSize
    else
        container.Visible = false
        container.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
    end
end

--// HSV color picker (preview + SV map + hue + hex)
function UI.createColorPicker(parent, title, initialColor, callback, embedded)
    local col = initialColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(col)
    local host = UI.uiSection(parent, embedded and 216 or 32)
    local open = embedded and true or false
    local swatch = Instance.new("TextButton")
    swatch.Name = "ColorSwatch"
    swatch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    swatch.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
    swatch.BackgroundColor3 = col
    swatch.Text = ""
    swatch.AutoButtonColor = false
    swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
    swatch.Visible = not embedded
    swatch.Parent = host
    UI.addCorner(swatch, UDim.new(0, 3))
    local swStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    swStroke.Thickness = 1
    swStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    swStroke.Transparency = 0.35
    swStroke.Parent = swatch
    if not embedded then
        UI.uiLabel(host, title or "Color", UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 32), UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0), 11)
    end
    local editor = Instance.new("Frame")
    editor.Name = "ColorEditor"
    editor.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, embedded and 216 or 0)
    editor.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, embedded and 0 or 32)
    editor.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    editor.ClipsDescendants = true
    editor.Visible = embedded and true or false
    editor.Parent = host
    local function setOpen(state)
        open = state and true or false
        editor.Visible = open
        editor.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, open and 216 or 0)
        host.Size = UDim2.new(host.Size.X.Scale, host.Size.X.Offset, 0, open and 248 or 32)
    end
    swatch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        setOpen(not open)
    end)

    local preview = Instance.new("Frame")
    preview.Name = "ColorPreview"
    preview.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 118)
    preview.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 4)
    preview.BackgroundColor3 = col
    preview.BorderSizePixel = 0
    preview.Parent = editor
    UI.addCorner(preview, UDim.new(0, 6))

    local sv = Instance.new("TextButton")
    sv.Name = "SVMap"
    sv.Size = UDim2.new(1, -88, 0, 118)
    sv.Position = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 4)
    sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    sv.Text = ""
    sv.AutoButtonColor = false
    sv.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sv.ClipsDescendants = true
    sv.Parent = editor
    UI.addCorner(sv, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local hueLayer = Instance.new("Frame")
    hueLayer.Name = "SVMap"
    hueLayer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    hueLayer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
    hueLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hueLayer.Parent = sv
    UI.addCorner(hueLayer, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local hueFade = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    hueFade.Rotation = 0
    hueFade.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
    })
    hueFade.Parent = hueLayer
    local valF = Instance.new("Frame")
    valF.Name = "SVMap"
    valF.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    valF.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    valF.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valF.Parent = sv
    UI.addCorner(valF, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local valG = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    valG.Rotation = 90
    valG.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
    })
    valG.Parent = valF
    local svDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    svDot.Name = "ColorPreview"
    svDot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
    svDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    svDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    svDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    svDot.ZIndex = 3
    svDot.Parent = sv
    UI.addCorner(svDot, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local svDotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    svDotStroke.Thickness = 1
    svDotStroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    svDotStroke.Parent = svDot

    local hueBar = Instance.new("TextButton")
    hueBar.Name = "HueBar"
    hueBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 12)
    hueBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 128)
    hueBar.Text = ""
    hueBar.AutoButtonColor = false
    hueBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hueBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    hueBar.Parent = editor
    UI.addCorner(hueBar, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local hueGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    hueGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.17, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.67, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    })
    hueGrad.Parent = hueBar
    local hueDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    hueDot.Name = "ColorPreview"
    hueDot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
    hueDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    hueDot.Position = UDim2.new(h, 0, 0.5, 0)
    hueDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
    hueDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hueDot.Parent = hueBar
    UI.addCorner(hueDot, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local hueStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    hueStroke.Thickness = 1
    hueStroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    hueStroke.Parent = hueDot

    local hexBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    hexBox.Name = "HexBox"
    hexBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 24)
    hexBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 146)
    hexBox.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    hexBox.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    hexBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    hexBox.TextSize = 11
    hexBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    hexBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    hexBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hexBox.Parent = editor
    UI.addCorner(hexBox, UDim.new(0, 5))
    UI.regFont(hexBox, 11)

    local function hexOf(c)
        return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
    end

    local rgbRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    rgbRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 24)
    rgbRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 176)
    rgbRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rgbRow.Parent = editor
    local function makeRgbBox(label, xScale)
        local wrap = Instance.new("Frame")
        wrap.Size = UDim2.new(0.33, -4, 1, 0)
        wrap.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 0, 0, 0)
        wrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        wrap.Parent = rgbRow
        local lab = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lab.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, 0)
        lab.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lab.Text = label
        lab.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        lab.TextSize = 10
        lab.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        lab.Parent = wrap
        UI.regFont(lab, 10)
        local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
        box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, 0)
        box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0)
        box.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
        box.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        box.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        box.TextSize = 11
        box.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
        box.Parent = wrap
        UI.addCorner(box, UDim.new(0, 4))
        UI.regFont(box, 11)
        return box
    end
    local rBox = makeRgbBox("R", 0)
    local gBox = makeRgbBox("G", 0.335)
    local bBox = makeRgbBox("B", 0.67)

    local mouseObj = Player:GetMouse()
    local function mouseOn(gui)
        local abs = gui.AbsolutePosition
        local sz = gui.AbsoluteSize
        return math.clamp((mouseObj.X - abs.X) / math.max(sz.X, 1), 0, 1),
            math.clamp((mouseObj.Y - abs.Y) / math.max(sz.Y, 1), 0, 1)
    end

    local function apply(silent)
        local c = Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v)
        preview.BackgroundColor3 = c
        swatch.BackgroundColor3 = c
        sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        hueLayer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
        hueDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
        hueDot.Position = UDim2.new(h, 0, 0.5, 0)
        svDot.Position = UDim2.new(s, 0, 1 - v, 0)
        hexBox.Text = hexOf(c)
        rBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5))
        gBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5))
        bBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
        if callback and not silent then callback(c) end
    end
    apply(true)

    local function dragSV()
        local x, y = mouseOn(sv)
        s = x
        v = 1 - y
        apply()
    end
    local function dragHue()
        local x = mouseOn(hueBar)
        h = x
        apply()
    end
    local holdingSV, holdingHue = false, false
    sv.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            holdingSV = true
            dragSV()
        end
    end)
    hueBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            holdingHue = true
            dragHue()
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            holdingSV, holdingHue = false, false
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if holdingSV then dragSV() end
            if holdingHue then dragHue() end
        end
    end))
    hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local t = hexBox.Text:gsub("#", "")
        if #t == 6 then
            local r = tonumber(t:sub(1, 2), 16)
            local g = tonumber(t:sub(3, 4), 16)
            local b = tonumber(t:sub(5, 6), 16)
            if r and g and b then
                h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b))
                apply()
            end
        end
        hexBox.Text = hexOf(Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v))
    end)
    local function rgbCommit()
        local r = math.clamp(tonumber(rBox.Text) or 0, 0, 255)
        local g = math.clamp(tonumber(gBox.Text) or 0, 0, 255)
        local b = math.clamp(tonumber(bBox.Text) or 0, 0, 255)
        h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b))
        apply()
    end
    rBox.FocusLost:Connect(rgbCommit)
    gBox.FocusLost:Connect(rgbCommit)
    bBox.FocusLost:Connect(rgbCommit)
    return host
end

function UI.createColorSlider(parentFrame, name, yPos, initialVal, callback)
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, yPos)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = name
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    lbl.TextSize = 11
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.Parent = parentFrame
    UI.regFont(lbl, 11)

    local valBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    valBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 24)
    valBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, yPos)
    valBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    valBox.Text = tostring(initialVal)
    valBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    valBox.TextSize = 11
    valBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    valBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    valBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valBox.Parent = parentFrame
    UI.addCorner(valBox, UDim.new(0, 4))
    UI.regFont(valBox, 11)

    local trackBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    trackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -125, 0, 10)
    trackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, yPos + 7)
    trackBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    trackBtn.Text = ""
    trackBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    trackBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    trackBtn.Parent = parentFrame
    UI.addCorner(trackBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(initialVal / 255, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = trackBtn
    UI.addCorner(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.regAccent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")

    local sliding = false
    trackBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = true
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = false
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if sliding and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local pos = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackBtn.AbsolutePosition.X) / trackBtn.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local val = math.floor(pos * 255)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(pos, 0, 1, 0)
            valBox.Text = tostring(val)
            callback(val)
        end
    end))
    valBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local val = math.clamp(tonumber(valBox.Text) or 0, 0, 255)
        valBox.Text = tostring(val)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(val / 255, 0, 1, 0)
        callback(val)
    end)

    return {
        setValue = function(v)
            valBox.Text = tostring(v)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(v / 255, 0, 1, 0)
        end
    }
end

local function createDimensionSlider(parentFrame, name, yPos, initialVal, callback)
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, yPos)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = name
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    lbl.TextSize = 11
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.Parent = parentFrame
    UI.regFont(lbl, 11)

    local valBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    valBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 24)
    valBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, yPos)
    valBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    valBox.Text = tostring(initialVal)
    valBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    valBox.TextSize = 11
    valBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    valBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    valBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valBox.Parent = parentFrame
    UI.addCorner(valBox, UDim.new(0, 4))
    UI.regFont(valBox, 11)

    local trackBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    trackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -125, 0, 10)
    trackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, yPos + 7)
    trackBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    trackBtn.Text = ""
    trackBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    trackBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    trackBtn.Parent = parentFrame
    UI.addCorner(trackBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new((initialVal - 1) / 9, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = trackBtn
    UI.addCorner(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.regAccent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")

    local sliding = false
    trackBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = true
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = false
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if sliding and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local pos = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackBtn.AbsolutePosition.X) / trackBtn.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local val = math.clamp(math.floor((pos * 9) + 1 + 0.5), 1, 10)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new((val - 1) / 9, 0, 1, 0)
            valBox.Text = tostring(val)
            callback(val)
        end
    end))
    valBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local val = math.clamp(tonumber(valBox.Text) or 1, 1, 10)
        valBox.Text = tostring(val)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new((val - 1) / 9, 0, 1, 0)
        callback(val)
    end)
end

function UI.createRangeSlider(parent, name, initialVal, maxVal, callback)
    local rangeContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    rangeContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
    rangeContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    rangeContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rangeContainer.Parent = parent
    UI.addCorner(rangeContainer, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.addGlassStroke(rangeContainer, 1)
    UI.styleCard(rangeContainer)
    UI.regGlass(rangeContainer, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
    if parent and parent.Name == "GroupBody" then
        UI.flattenCard(rangeContainer)
    end


    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0, 20)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = tostring(name)
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.Parent = rangeContainer
    UI.regFont(lbl, 12)

    local valBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    valBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 20)
    valBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 6)
    valBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    valBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
    valBox.Text = tostring(initialVal)
    valBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    valBox.TextSize = 11
    valBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    valBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    valBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valBox.Parent = rangeContainer
    UI.addCorner(valBox, UDim.new(0, 5))
    UI.regFont(valBox, 11)

    local trackBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    trackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 6)
    trackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -16)
    trackBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    trackBtn.Text = ""
    trackBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    trackBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    trackBtn.Parent = rangeContainer
    trackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22)
    trackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -26)
    trackBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    UI.addCorner(trackBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6)
    bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -3)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = dimAccentTrack()
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.ClipsDescendants = true
    bar.Parent = trackBtn
    UI.addCorner(bar, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.regTrack(bar)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    local initScale = math.clamp((tonumber(initialVal) or 0) / math.max(tonumber(maxVal) or 1, 1e-6), 0, 1)
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(initScale, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = bar
    UI.addCorner(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.regAccent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    knob.Position = UDim2.new(initScale, 0, 0.5, 0)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 3
    knob.Parent = trackBtn
    UI.addCorner(knob, UDim.new(0, 4))
    UI.regKnob(knob)
    local ks = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    ks.Thickness = 1
    ks.Color = Theme.Accent
    ks.Transparency = 0.35
    ks.Parent = knob
    UI.regAccent(ks, "Color")
    local function setRangePos(scale)
        scale = math.clamp(scale, 0, 1)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(scale, 0, 1, 0)
        fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(scale, 0, 0.5, 0)
    end

    local sliding = false
    trackBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = true
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = false
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if sliding and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local pos = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackBtn.AbsolutePosition.X) / trackBtn.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local val = math.floor(pos * maxVal)
            setRangePos(pos)
            valBox.Text = tostring(val)
            callback(val)
        end
    end))
    valBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local val = math.clamp(tonumber(valBox.Text) or 0, 0, maxVal)
        valBox.Text = tostring(val)
        setRangePos(val / math.max(maxVal, 1))
        callback(val)
    end)
    return rangeContainer
end

function UI.createDecimalSlider(parent, name, initialVal, minVal, maxVal, callback)
    local rangeContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    rangeContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
    rangeContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    rangeContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rangeContainer.Parent = parent
    UI.addCorner(rangeContainer, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.addGlassStroke(rangeContainer, 1)
    UI.styleCard(rangeContainer)
    UI.regGlass(rangeContainer, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
    if parent and parent.Name == "GroupBody" then
        UI.flattenCard(rangeContainer)
    end


    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0, 20)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = tostring(name)
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.Parent = rangeContainer
    UI.regFont(lbl, 12)

    local valBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    valBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 20)
    valBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 6)
    valBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    valBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
    valBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", initialVal)
    valBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    valBox.TextSize = 11
    valBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    valBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    valBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valBox.Parent = rangeContainer
    UI.addCorner(valBox, UDim.new(0, 5))
    UI.regFont(valBox, 11)

    local trackBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    trackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 6)
    trackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -16)
    trackBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    trackBtn.Text = ""
    trackBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    trackBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    trackBtn.Parent = rangeContainer
    trackBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22)
    trackBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -26)
    trackBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    UI.addCorner(trackBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6)
    bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -3)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = dimAccentTrack()
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.ClipsDescendants = true
    bar.Parent = trackBtn
    UI.addCorner(bar, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.regTrack(bar)

    local range = math.max((maxVal - minVal), 1e-6)
    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    local initScale = math.clamp(((tonumber(initialVal) or minVal) - minVal) / range, 0, 1)
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(initScale, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = bar
    UI.addCorner(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.regAccent(fill, "BackgroundColor3")

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    knob.Position = UDim2.new(initScale, 0, 0.5, 0)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.ZIndex = 3
    knob.Parent = trackBtn
    UI.addCorner(knob, UDim.new(0, 4))
    UI.regKnob(knob)
    local ks = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    ks.Thickness = 1
    ks.Color = Theme.Accent
    ks.Transparency = 0.35
    ks.Parent = knob
    UI.regAccent(ks, "Color")
    local function setDecPos(scale)
        scale = math.clamp(scale, 0, 1)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(scale, 0, 1, 0)
        fill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(scale, 0, 0.5, 0)
    end

    local sliding = false
    trackBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = true
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            sliding = false
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if sliding and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local pos = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackBtn.AbsolutePosition.X) / trackBtn.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local val = minVal + (pos * range)
            val = math.floor(val * 100) / 100
            setDecPos(pos)
            valBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", val)
            callback(val)
        end
    end))
    valBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local val = math.clamp(tonumber(valBox.Text) or minVal, minVal, maxVal)
        valBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", val)
        setDecPos((val - minVal) / math.max(range, 0.0001))
        callback(val)
    end)
    return rangeContainer
end

function UI.createOptionToggle(parentFrame, name, yPos, initialState, callback)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 26)
    row.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, yPos)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.Parent = parentFrame

    local rowLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    rowLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0)
    rowLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rowLabel.Text = name
    rowLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    rowLabel.TextSize = 11
    rowLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    rowLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    rowLabel.Parent = row
    UI.regFont(rowLabel, 11)

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 18)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0.5, -9)
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Parent = row
    UI.addCorner(btn, UDim.new(1, 0))

    local ind = Instance.new("Frame")
    ind.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    ind.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7)
    ind.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    ind.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ind.Parent = btn
    UI.addCorner(ind, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.applyToggleState(btn, ind, initialState)

    local state = initialState
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        state = not state
        UI.applyToggleState(btn, ind, state)
        callback(state)
    end)
end

--// ESP object management
Utils.hasDrawing = false
pcall(function()
    Utils.hasDrawing = (typeof(Drawing) == "table") or (type(Drawing) == "table") or (Drawing ~= nil and Drawing.new ~= nil)
end)

local function removeESP(plr)
    local obj = espObjects[plr]
    if obj then
        if obj.box then pcall(function() obj.box:Remove() end) end
        if obj.nameText then pcall(function() obj.nameText:Remove() end) end
        if obj.distText then pcall(function() obj.distText:Remove() end) end
        if obj.healthText then pcall(function() obj.healthText:Remove() end) end
        if obj.healthBg then pcall(function() obj.healthBg:Remove() end) end
        if obj.healthBar then pcall(function() obj.healthBar:Remove() end) end
        if obj.glow then pcall(function() obj.glow:Remove() end) end
        if obj.fill then pcall(function() obj.fill:Remove() end) end
        if obj.lines then
            for _, l in pairs(obj.lines) do
                pcall(function() l:Remove() end)
            end
        end
        if obj.gui then pcall(function() obj.gui:Destroy() end) end
        if obj.ui and obj.ui.holder then pcall(function() obj.ui.holder:Destroy() end) end
        if obj.ui and obj.ui.hpBg then pcall(function() obj.ui.hpBg:Destroy() end) end
        espObjects[plr] = nil
    end
end

local function clearAllESP()
    for plr, _ in pairs(espObjects) do removeESP(plr) end
end

local function getESP(plr)
    if espObjects[plr] then return espObjects[plr] end
    local obj = {}
    if Utils.hasDrawing then
        obj.box = Drawing.new("Square")
        obj.box.Thickness = 1
        obj.box.Filled = false
        obj.box.Visible = false
        obj.nameText = Drawing.new("Text")
        obj.nameText.Size = 13
        obj.nameText.Center = true
        obj.nameText.Outline = true
        obj.nameText.Visible = false
        obj.distText = Drawing.new("Text")
        obj.distText.Size = 12
        obj.distText.Center = true
        obj.distText.Outline = true
        obj.distText.Visible = false
        obj.healthText = Drawing.new("Text")
        obj.healthText.Size = 12
        obj.healthText.Center = true
        obj.healthText.Outline = true
        obj.healthText.Visible = false
        obj.healthBg = Drawing.new("Square")
        obj.healthBg.Filled = true
        obj.healthBg.Thickness = 1
        obj.healthBg.Visible = false
        obj.healthBar = Drawing.new("Square")
        obj.healthBar.Filled = true
        obj.healthBar.Thickness = 1
        obj.healthBar.Visible = false
    else
        local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name = "RaysESP"
        gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        gui.Size = UDim2.new(4, 0, 6, 0)
        gui.Parent = ScreenGui
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Parent = gui
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Thickness = 2
        stroke.Parent = frame
        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, -0.06, 0)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        nameLbl.TextSize = 12
        nameLbl.Parent = frame
        local distLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        distLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
        distLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1.0, 0)
        distLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        distLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        distLbl.TextSize = 11
        distLbl.Parent = frame
        local healthLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        healthLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
        healthLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1.12, 0)
        healthLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        healthLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        healthLbl.TextSize = 11
        healthLbl.Parent = frame
        obj.gui = gui
        obj.frame = frame
        obj.stroke = stroke
        obj.nameLbl = nameLbl
        obj.distLbl = distLbl
        obj.healthLbl = healthLbl
    end
    obj.lines = obj.lines or {}
    espObjects[plr] = obj
    return obj
end

Utils.espLine = function(obj, key)
    local l = obj.lines[key]
    if l then return l end
    if not Utils.hasDrawing then return nil end
    l = Drawing.new("Line")
    l.Thickness = 1.2
    l.Visible = false
    obj.lines[key] = l
    return l
end
Utils.espHideLines = function(obj, prefix)
    if not obj or not obj.lines then return end
    for k, l in pairs(obj.lines) do
        if type(k) == "string" and string.sub(k, 1, #prefix) == prefix then
            l.Visible = false
        end
    end
end
Utils.espW2V = function(world)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil, false end
    local p, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world)
    return Vector2.new(p.X, p.Y), on and p.Z > 0, p
end
Utils.espSetLine = function(line, a, b, color, thick)
    if not line then return end
    local pa, oa = Utils.espW2V(a)
    local pb, ob = Utils.espW2V(b)
    if pa and pb and (oa or ob) then
        line.From = pa
        line.To = pb
        line.Color = color
        line.Thickness = thick or 1.2
        line.Visible = true
    else
        line.Visible = false
    end
end
Utils.espNativeHealth = function(on)
    local sz = Utils.espCfg.nativeHpSize or 14
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local char = plr.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if hum then
            if on then
                hum.NameDisplayDistance = 9e9
                hum.HealthDisplayDistance = 9e9
                pcall(function()
                    hum.NameOcclusion = Enum.NameOcclusion.NoOcclusion
                    hum.HealthDisplayType = Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOn
                end)
            else
                hum.NameDisplayDistance = 100
                hum.HealthDisplayDistance = 100
            end
        end
        if head then
            local bb = head:FindFirstChild("RaysEngineHP")
            if on then
                if not bb then
                    bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    bb.Name = "RaysEngineHP"
                    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 28)
                    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
                    bb.Parent = head
                    local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    t.Name = "Txt"
                    t.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    t.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                    t.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    t.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
                    t.Parent = bb
                end
                local t = bb:FindFirstChild("Txt")
                if t then
                    t.TextSize = sz
                    t.Text = hum and tostring(math.floor(hum.Health + 0.5)) or ""
                end
            elseif bb then
                bb:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end
Utils.espEnsureOverlay = function()
    if Utils.espOverlay and Utils.espOverlay.Parent then return Utils.espOverlay end
    local g = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    g.Name = "RaysESPOverlay"
    g.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    g.ResetOnSpawn = false
    g.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    g.DisplayOrder = 50
    g.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    Utils.espOverlay = g
    return g
end
Utils.espBoxUI = function(obj)
    if obj.ui and obj.ui.holder and obj.ui.holder.Parent then return obj.ui end
    local g = Utils.espEnsureOverlay()
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.Visible = false
    holder.Parent = g
    local glow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    glow.Image = "rbxassetid://110204605000367"
    glow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    glow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(21, 21, 79, 79)
    glow.ImageTransparency = 0.58
    glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -22, 0, -22)
    glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 44, 1, 44)
    glow.Parent = holder
    local glowGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    glowGrad.Rotation = 90
    glowGrad.Parent = glow
    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    fill.Parent = holder
    local fillGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    fillGrad.Rotation = 90
    fillGrad.Parent = fill
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Thickness = 1.4
    stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
    stroke.Parent = holder
    obj.ui = { holder = holder, glow = glow, glowGrad = glowGrad, fill = fill, fillGrad = fillGrad, stroke = stroke }
    return obj.ui
end
Utils.espEnsureRadar = function()
    local Rdr = Utils.espRadar
    if Rdr and Rdr.frame and Rdr.frame.Parent then return Rdr end
    Rdr = { dots = {} }
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "RaysRadar"
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, Utils.espCfg.radarSize or 504, 0, Utils.espCfg.radarSize or 504)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -((Utils.espCfg.radarSize or 504) + 24), 0, 70)
    frame.Active = true
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 16)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = ScreenGui
    UI.uiCorner(frame, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.addGlassStroke(frame, 1)
    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 6)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "RADAR"
    title.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    title.TextSize = 10
    title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    title.Parent = frame
    local you = Instance.new("Frame")
    you.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
    you.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    you.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    you.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    you.BorderSizePixel = 0
    you.Parent = frame
    UI.uiCorner(you, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local handle = Instance.new("TextButton")
    handle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    handle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -18)
    handle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
    handle.Text = ""
    handle.AutoButtonColor = false
    handle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    handle.Parent = frame
    UI.uiCorner(handle, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local dragging, scaling = false, false
    local dragOff = Vector2.new()
    frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = true
            dragOff = Vector2.new(input.Position.X - frame.AbsolutePosition.X, input.Position.Y - frame.AbsolutePosition.Y)
        end
    end)
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            scaling = true
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging, scaling = false, false
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
        if scaling then
            local sz = math.clamp(input.Position.X - frame.AbsolutePosition.X, 180, 720)
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, sz, 0, sz)
            Utils.espCfg.radarSize = sz
        elseif dragging then
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, input.Position.X - dragOff.X, 0, input.Position.Y - dragOff.Y)
        end
    end))
    Rdr.frame = frame
    Utils.espRadar = Rdr
    return Rdr
end
Utils.espRadarDot = function(plr)
    local Rdr = Utils.espEnsureRadar()
    local d = Rdr.dots[plr]
    if d and d.vp and d.vp.Parent then return d end
    local vp = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
    vp.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    vp.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    vp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    vp.BorderSizePixel = 0
    vp.Parent = Rdr.frame
    local wm = Instance.new("WorldModel")
    wm.Parent = vp
    local cam = Instance.new("Camera")
    cam.Parent = vp
    vp.CurrentCamera = cam
    d = { vp = vp, wm = wm, cam = cam, clone = nil }
    Rdr.dots[plr] = d
    return d
end
Utils.espDrawStyle = function(obj, char, hrp, head, boxCol, topPos, width, height)
    local cfg = Utils.espCfg
    if not Utils.hasDrawing then return end
    if not espShowBox then
        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "e")
        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "3")
        if obj.glow then obj.glow.Visible = false end
        if obj.fill then obj.fill.Visible = false end
        obj.box.Visible = false
    else
        if cfg.mode3d then
            obj.box.Visible = false
            if obj.glow then obj.glow.Visible = false end
            if obj.fill then obj.fill.Visible = false end
            Utils.espHideLines(obj, "e")
            local cf = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -0.4, 0)
            local s = Vector3.new(1.6, 2.6, 1.2)
            local pts = {}
            local i = 1
            for x = -1, 1, 2 do
                for y = -1, 1, 2 do
                    for z = -1, 1, 2 do
                        pts[i] = (cf * CFrame.new(s.X * x, s.Y * y, s.Z * z)).Position
                        i = i + 1
                    end
                end
            end
            local pairs3 = { {1,2},{2,4},{4,3},{3,1},{5,6},{6,8},{8,7},{7,5},{1,5},{2,6},{3,7},{4,8} }
            for n, pr in ipairs(pairs3) do
                Utils.espSetLine(Utils.espLine(obj, "3"..n), pts[pr[1]], pts[pr[2]], boxCol, 1.15)
            end
        else
            Utils.espHideLines(obj, "3")
            obj.box.Visible = not cfg.edges
            obj.box.Color = boxCol
            if obj.glow then obj.glow.Visible = false end
            if obj.fill then obj.fill.Visible = false end
            if cfg.glow or cfg.gradient then
                local ui = Utils.espBoxUI(obj)
                ui.holder.Visible = true
                ui.holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(topPos.X - width / 2, topPos.Y)
                ui.holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(4, width), math.max(4, height))
                ui.glow.Visible = cfg.glow and true or false
                ui.glow.ImageColor3 = boxCol
                ui.glowGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, boxCol),
                })
                ui.fill.Visible = cfg.gradient and true or false
                ui.fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                ui.fillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, boxCol),
                })
                ui.fillGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55),
                })
                ui.stroke.Color = boxCol
                ui.stroke.Enabled = not cfg.edges
            elseif obj.ui and obj.ui.holder then
                obj.ui.holder.Visible = false
            end
            if cfg.edges then
                obj.box.Visible = false
                local x1, y1 = topPos.X - width / 2, topPos.Y
                local x2, y2 = topPos.X + width / 2, topPos.Y + height
                local o = math.clamp(width * 0.28, 4, 14)
                local corners = {
                    {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x1 + o, y1)},
                    {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x1, y1 + o)},
                    {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2 - o, y1)},
                    {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2, y1 + o)},
                    {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1 + o, y2)},
                    {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1, y2 - o)},
                    {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x2 - o, y2)},
                    {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x2, y2 - o)},
                }
                for n, pair in ipairs(corners) do
                    local ln = Utils.espLine(obj, "e"..n)
                    if ln then
                        ln.From = pair[1]
                        ln.To = pair[2]
                        ln.Color = boxCol
                        ln.Thickness = 1.6
                        ln.Visible = true
                    end
                end
            else
                Utils.espHideLines(obj, "e")
            end
        end
    end
    Utils.espHideLines(obj, "s")
    if cfg.skeleton then
        local links = {
            {"Head", "UpperTorso"}, {"Head", "Torso"},
            {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}, {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"}, {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
            {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"}, {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
            {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"}, {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
            {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"}, {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
            {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"}, {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
            {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"}, {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
            {"Torso", "Left Arm"}, {"Torso", "Right Arm"}, {"Torso", "Left Leg"}, {"Torso", "Right Leg"},
        }
        local n = 0
        for _, pair in ipairs(links) do
            local a = char:FindFirstChild(pair[1])
            local b = char:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
            if a and b and a:IsA("BasePart") and b:IsA("BasePart") then
                n = n + 1
                Utils.espSetLine(Utils.espLine(obj, "s"..n), a.Position, b.Position, cfg.skelCol or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.1)
            end
        end
    else
        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "s")
    end
