    if cfg.aimview and head then
        local origin = head.Position
        local dir = head.CFrame.LookVector * 400
        local hitPos = origin + dir
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char, Player.Character }
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
        if hit then hitPos = hit.Position end
        Utils.espSetLine(Utils.espLine(obj, "av"), origin, hitPos, Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90), 1.4)
    else
        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "av")
    end
end
Utils.espUpdateRadar = function()
    local cfg = Utils.espCfg
    if not cfg.radar then
        if Utils.espRadar and Utils.espRadar.frame then
            Utils.espRadar.frame.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end
    local Rdr = Utils.espEnsureRadar()
    Rdr.frame.Visible = true
    local my = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not my then return end
    local look = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector or my.CFrame.LookVector
    local yaw = math.atan2(look.X, look.Z)
    local radius = math.max(40, (Rdr.frame.AbsoluteSize.X * 0.42))
    local seen = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
            local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if hrp and head then
                seen[plr] = true
                local rel = hrp.Position - my.Position
                local rx = rel.X * math.cos(yaw) - rel.Z * math.sin(yaw)
                local rz = rel.X * math.sin(yaw) + rel.Z * math.cos(yaw)
                local scale = 0.35
                local x = math.clamp(rx * scale, -radius, radius)
                local y = math.clamp(-rz * scale, -radius, radius)
                local d = Utils.espRadarDot(plr)
                d.vp.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, y)
                if not d.clone or not d.clone.Parent then
                    local cl = head:Clone()
                    for _, ch in ipairs(cl:GetDescendants()) do
                        if ch:IsA("BaseScript") then ch:Destroy() end
                    end
                    cl.Anchored = true
                    cl.CFrame = CFrame.new()
                    cl.Parent = d.wm
                    d.clone = cl
                    d.cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, 0, 2.2), Vector3.new())
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if Utils.espRadar and Utils.espRadar.dots then
        for plr, d in pairs(Utils.espRadar.dots) do
            if not seen[plr] then
                if d.vp then d.vp.Visible = false end
            else
                d.vp.Visible = true
            end
        end
    end
end

Utils.track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    removeESP(plr)
end))

--// ESP render loop
Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not Utils.espAnyOn() then
        if Utils.espRadar and Utils.espRadar.frame then Utils.espRadar.frame.Visible = false end
        return
    end
    pcall(Utils.espUpdateRadar)
    if Utils.espCfg.nativeHp and (tick() - (Utils.espCfg._hpT or 0) > 1) then
        Utils.espCfg._hpT = tick()
        pcall(Utils.espNativeHealth, true)
    end
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local espColor = Color3.fromRGB(espR, espG, espB)
    local cols = espObjects.cols or {}
    local nameCol, distCol, boxCol, hpCol = cols.name or espColor, cols.dist or espColor, cols.box or espColor, cols.health or espColor

    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player then
            if Utils.skipProtected(plr) then
                if espObjects[plr] then removeESP(plr) end
            else
            local char = plr.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local alive = humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0

            if hrp and head and alive then
                local topPos, topOnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
                local botPos = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                local distance = (Camera.CFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                local obj = getESP(plr)

                if topOnScreen and topPos.Z > 0 then
                    local height = math.abs(topPos.Y - botPos.Y)
                    local width = height * 0.5

                    if Utils.hasDrawing then
                        if obj.nameText then pcall(function() obj.nameText.Font = Drawing.Fonts and Drawing.Fonts.UI or obj.nameText.Font end) end
                        if espShowBox or Utils.espCfg.skeleton or Utils.espCfg.aimview then
                            obj.box.Size = Vector2.new(width, height)
                            obj.box.Position = Vector2.new(topPos.X - width / 2, topPos.Y)
                            obj.box.Color = boxCol
                        end
                        Utils.espDrawStyle(obj, char, hrp, head, boxCol, topPos, width, height)
                        if espShowNames then
                            obj.nameText.Text = plr.Name
                            obj.nameText.Position = Vector2.new(topPos.X, topPos.Y - 16)
                            obj.nameText.Color = nameCol
                            obj.nameText.Visible = true
                        else
                            obj.nameText.Visible = false
                        end
                        if espShowDistance then
                            obj.distText.Text = string.format("%dm", math.floor(distance))
                            obj.distText.Position = Vector2.new(topPos.X, topPos.Y + height + 2)
                            obj.distText.Color = distCol
                            obj.distText.Visible = true
                        else
                            obj.distText.Visible = false
                        end
                        if espShowHealth then
                            local maxH = humanoid.MaxHealth
                            if maxH <= 0 then maxH = 100 end
                            local hp = math.clamp(humanoid.Health / maxH, 0, 1)
                            local barW = 7
                            local barX = topPos.X - width / 2 - barW - 5
                            local barY = topPos.Y
                            local fullH = height
                            local fillH = fullH * hp
                            obj.healthText.Visible = false
                            if obj.healthBg then obj.healthBg.Visible = false end
                            if obj.healthBar then obj.healthBar.Visible = false end
                            local ui = Utils.espBoxUI(obj)
                            if not ui.hpBg then
                                ui.hpBg = Instance.new("Frame")
                                ui.hpBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                ui.hpBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
                                ui.hpBg.Parent = Utils.espEnsureOverlay()
                                ui.hpFill = Instance.new("Frame")
                                ui.hpFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                ui.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                                ui.hpFill.Parent = ui.hpBg
                                local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                                g.Rotation = 90
                                g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 80)),
                                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)),
                                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)),
                                })
                                g.Parent = ui.hpFill
                            end
                            ui.hpBg.Visible = true
                            ui.hpBg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(barX, barY)
                            ui.hpBg.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(barW, fullH)
                            ui.hpFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, hp, 0)
                            ui.hpFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1 - hp, 0)
                            ui.hpFill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                        else
                            obj.healthText.Visible = false
                            if obj.healthBg then obj.healthBg.Visible = false end
                            if obj.healthBar then obj.healthBar.Visible = false end
                            if obj.ui and obj.ui.hpBg then obj.ui.hpBg.Visible = false end
                        end
                    else
                        obj.gui.Adornee = hrp
                        obj.gui.Enabled = true
                        obj.stroke.Color = boxCol
                        obj.stroke.Visible = espShowBox
                        obj.stroke.Thickness = 2
                        if espShowNames then
                            obj.nameLbl.Text = plr.Name
                            obj.nameLbl.TextColor3 = nameCol
                            obj.nameLbl.Visible = true
                        else
                            obj.nameLbl.Visible = false
                        end
                        if espShowDistance then
                            obj.distLbl.Text = string.format("%dm", math.floor(distance))
                            obj.distLbl.TextColor3 = distCol
                            obj.distLbl.Visible = true
                        else
                            obj.distLbl.Visible = false
                        end
                        if espShowHealth then
                            local maxH = humanoid.MaxHealth
                            if maxH <= 0 then maxH = 100 end
                            local hp = math.clamp(humanoid.Health / maxH, 0, 1)
                            if obj.healthBarFrame then
                                obj.healthBarFrame.Visible = true
                                obj.healthBarFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, hp, 0)
                                obj.healthBarFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1 - hp, 0)
                                obj.healthBarFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255 * (1 - hp), 255 * hp, 40)
                            end
                            if obj.healthLbl then obj.healthLbl.Visible = false end
                        else
                            if obj.healthLbl then obj.healthLbl.Visible = false end
                            if obj.healthBarFrame then obj.healthBarFrame.Visible = false end
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if Utils.hasDrawing then
                        obj.box.Visible = false
                        obj.nameText.Visible = false
                        obj.distText.Visible = false
                        obj.healthText.Visible = false
                        if obj.healthBg then obj.healthBg.Visible = false end
                        if obj.healthBar then obj.healthBar.Visible = false end
                        if obj.glow then obj.glow.Visible = false end
                        if obj.fill then obj.fill.Visible = false end
                        if obj.ui and obj.ui.holder then obj.ui.holder.Visible = false end
                        if obj.ui and obj.ui.hpBg then obj.ui.hpBg.Visible = false end
                        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "e")
                        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "3")
                        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "s")
                        Utils.espHideLines(obj, "av")
                    else
                        obj.gui.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
            else
                if espObjects[plr] then removeESP(plr) end
            end
            end
        end
    end
end))


-- Downed / KO check (DaHood, Hood Customs, generic)
-- Downed lock: 0 HP starts a 13s window; cleared if their character resets
local downedUntil = {} -- [Player] = tick when targetable again
local downedChar, downedUntil = {}, {}  -- [Player] = Character / until tick for floor KO
local DOWNED_LOCK_SEC = 13

function Utils.clearDownedState(plr)
    if plr then
        downedUntil[plr] = nil
        downedChar[plr] = nil
    end
end

function Utils.isCharacterDowned(char)
    -- DaHood / Hood Customs / floor KO detection
    if not char then return true end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return true end

    local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
    if plr and downedChar[plr] and downedChar[plr] ~= char then
        Utils.clearDownedState(plr)
    end

    -- sticky downed window so flicker doesn't re-target floor bodies
    if plr and downedUntil and downedUntil[plr] and tick() < downedUntil[plr] then
        if downedChar[plr] == char then
            return true
        end
    end

    local function truthyVal(v)
        if v == nil then return false end
        if typeof(v) == "boolean" then return v end
        if typeof(v) == "number" then return v ~= 0 end
        if typeof(v) == "string" then
            local s = string.lower(v)
            return s == "true" or s == "1" or s == "yes"
        end
        if typeof(v) == "Instance" then
            if v:IsA("BoolValue") then return v.Value end
            if v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("IntValue") then return v.Value ~= 0 end
            if v:IsA("StringValue") then
                local s = string.lower(tostring(v.Value))
                return s == "true" or s == "1" or s == "yes"
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local ko = false
    if hum.Health <= 0 then
        ko = true
    else
        local stOk, st = pcall(function() return hum:GetState() end)
        if stOk and (st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics) then
            -- Physics often used while ragdolled / on floor in hood games
            if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
                ko = true
            end
        end
    end

    -- BodyEffects flags (Da Hood / Hood Customs)
    if not ko then
        local be = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
        if be then
            for _, n in ipairs({"K.O", "KO", "Knocked", "Downed", "Dead", "SDeath", "Cuffed"}) do
                local v = be:FindFirstChild(n)
                if truthyVal(v) then ko = true break end
            end
            -- nested paths some builds use
            if not ko then
                for _, d in ipairs(be:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d.Name == "K.O" or d.Name == "Knocked" or d.Name == "Downed" then
                        if truthyVal(d) then ko = true break end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Common constraints / attributes while on floor
    if not ko then
        if char:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT") or char:FindFirstChild("Grabbed") then
            -- grabbed is not always downed; only if also KO-like posture
        end
        local attr = nil
        pcall(function() attr = char:GetAttribute("Downed") or char:GetAttribute("Knocked") or char:GetAttribute("KO") end)
        if truthyVal(attr) then ko = true end
    end

    -- Floor posture heuristic: very low torso vs HRP height spread is unreliable;
    -- if MoveDirection ~0 and state FallingDown / getting up after damage
    if not ko then
        local stOk, st = pcall(function() return hum:GetState() end)
        if stOk and (st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll) then
            ko = true
        end
    end

    if ko then
        if plr then
            downedChar[plr] = char
            downedUntil = downedUntil or {}
            downedUntil[plr] = tick() + 1.25 -- hold downed at least 1.25s
        end
        return true
    end
    if plr then Utils.clearDownedState(plr) end
    return false
end


-- Clear downed lock when their character is removed (reset / leave)
Utils.track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    Utils.clearDownedState(plr)
end))
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    Utils.track(plr.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        Utils.clearDownedState(plr)
    end))
end
Utils.track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
    Utils.track(plr.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        Utils.clearDownedState(plr)
    end))
end))

Utils.silentPartValid = function(part)
    if not part or not part.Parent then return false end
    local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not model then return false end
    local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    if silentAim and silentAim.healthCheck then
        if Utils.isCharacterDowned(model) then return false end
        local be = model:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
        if be then
            for _, n in ipairs({"K.O", "KO", "Knocked", "Downed", "Dead"}) do
                local v = be:FindFirstChild(n)
                if v and typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value then
                    return false
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
    if not plr or not plr.Parent then return false end
    -- reject corpse models that aren't the player's current character
    if plr.Character ~= model then return false end
    if Utils.skipProtected and Utils.skipProtected(plr) then return false end
    return true
end

function Utils.isPlayerTargetable(plr)
    if not plr or plr == Player then return false end
    if Utils.skipProtected(plr) then return false end
    local char = plr.Character
    if not char then return false end
    return not Utils.isCharacterDowned(char)
end

--// Hitbox render loop
Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not hitboxActive then return end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= Player and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not (Utils.skipProtected(p)) then
            local rootPart = p.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local humanoid = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local downed = Utils.isCharacterDowned(p.Character)
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not downed then
                if not originalSizes[rootPart] then
                    originalSizes[rootPart] = rootPart.Size
                    originalColors[rootPart] = rootPart.Color
                    originalMaterials[rootPart] = rootPart.Material
                end
                rootPart.Size = Vector3.new(hbXSize, hbYSize, hbZSize)
                rootPart.CanCollide = false
                if hitboxVisible then
                    rootPart.Transparency = 0.85
                    rootPart.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                    rootPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                else
                    rootPart.Transparency = 1
                end
            else
                -- downed / dead: restore normal hitbox
                if originalSizes[rootPart] then
                    rootPart.Size = originalSizes[rootPart]
                    if originalColors[rootPart] then rootPart.Color = originalColors[rootPart] end
                    if originalMaterials[rootPart] then rootPart.Material = originalMaterials[rootPart] end
                    rootPart.Transparency = 1
                    originalSizes[rootPart] = nil
                    originalColors[rootPart] = nil
                    originalMaterials[rootPart] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end))

--// Hitbox state changer
function Utils.updateHitboxState(newState)
    hitboxActive = newState
    if hitboxActive then
        if R.hitboxExtra and R.hitboxExtra.Parent then
            R.hitboxExtra.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(R.hitboxExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 192)}):Play()
        end
        if R.hitboxToggleBtn and R.hitboxToggleIndicator and R.hitboxToggleBtn.Parent then
            UI.applyToggleState(R.hitboxToggleBtn, R.hitboxToggleIndicator, true)
        end
    else
        if R.hitboxToggleBtn and R.hitboxToggleIndicator and R.hitboxToggleBtn.Parent then
            UI.applyToggleState(R.hitboxToggleBtn, R.hitboxToggleIndicator, false)
        end
        if R.hitboxExtra and R.hitboxExtra.Parent then
            local tweenOut = TweenService:Create(R.hitboxExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
            tweenOut:Play()
            tweenOut.Completed:Connect(function()
                if not hitboxActive and R.hitboxExtra and R.hitboxExtra.Parent then
                    R.hitboxExtra.Visible = false
                end
            end)
        end
        for part, origSize in pairs(originalSizes) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                part.Size = origSize
                part.Transparency = 1
                if originalColors[part] then part.Color = originalColors[part] end
                if originalMaterials[part] then part.Material = originalMaterials[part] end
            end
        end
        originalSizes = {}
        originalColors = {}
        originalMaterials = {}
    end
end

--// MOVEMENT
function Utils.getHumanoid()
    local char = Player.Character
    return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

Utils.applyMovementStats = function(hum)
    hum = hum or Utils.getHumanoid()
    if not hum then return end
    if speedActive and not speedUseCFrame then
        pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = currentSpeedValue end)
    end
    if jumpPowerActive then
        pcall(function()
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            hum.JumpPower = currentJumpPower
            hum.JumpHeight = math.max(7.2, currentJumpPower * 0.2)
        end)
    end
end

local function hookMovementHumanoid(hum)
    if not hum or hum:GetAttribute("RaysMoveHook") then return end
    hum:SetAttribute("RaysMoveHook", true)
    Utils.track(hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
        if speedActive and not speedUseCFrame and math.abs(hum.WalkSpeed - currentSpeedValue) > 0.05 then
            hum.WalkSpeed = currentSpeedValue
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower"):Connect(function()
        if jumpPowerActive and math.abs(hum.JumpPower - currentJumpPower) > 0.05 then
            hum.JumpPower = currentJumpPower
        end
    end))
end

local function hookMovementChar(char)
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 8)
    if hum then hookMovementHumanoid(hum) Utils.applyMovementStats(hum) end
    Utils.track(char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(ch)
        if ch and ch:IsA("ForceField") then
            task.defer(function() Utils.applyMovementStats() end)
            task.delay(0.15, Utils.applyMovementStats)
            task.delay(0.6, Utils.applyMovementStats)
        end
    end))
end

if Player.Character then hookMovementChar(Player.Character) end
Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.defer(function() hookMovementChar(char) end)
end))
Utils.track(RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
    Utils.applyMovementStats()
end))

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    local char = Player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if hum then
        if speedActive and not speedUseCFrame then
            pcall(function()
                hum.WalkSpeed = currentSpeedValue
            end)
        end
        if jumpPowerActive then
            pcall(function()
                hum.UseJumpPower = true
                hum.JumpPower = currentJumpPower
                hum.JumpHeight = math.max(7.2, currentJumpPower * 0.2)
            end)
        end
    end
    if Utils.grav and Utils.grav.active then
        pcall(function()
            workspace.Gravity = tonumber(Utils.grav.value) or Utils.grav.original or 196.2
        end)
    end

    -- Force time every frame so the game's day/night loop cannot override it
    if forceTimeActive then
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue
        end)
    end

    -- Force sunrays every frame (games scripts re-enable them otherwise)
    if Utils.pl and Utils.pl.sunraysOn then
        pcall(function()
            local P = Utils.pl
            local intensity = math.clamp((P.sunraysIntensity or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
            local spread = math.clamp((P.sunraysSpread or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
            if intensity <= 0 then
                for _, fx in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if fx:IsA("SunRaysEffect") then
                        fx.Enabled = false
                        fx.Intensity = 0
                    end
                end
            else
                local ours = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysSunRays")
                if not ours then
                    ours = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect")
                    ours.Name = "RaysSunRays"
                    ours.Parent = Lighting
                end
                ours.Enabled = true
                ours.Intensity = intensity
                ours.Spread = spread
                for _, fx in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if fx:IsA("SunRaysEffect") and fx ~= ours then
                        fx.Enabled = false
                        fx.Intensity = 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- CFrame speed (TranslateBy while moving) — same idea as Cian
    if speedActive and speedUseCFrame and hum and hrp and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
        local step = dt
        if step <= 0 or step > 0.1 then step = 1/60 end
        char:TranslateBy(hum.MoveDirection * currentSpeedValue * step * 0.35)
    end
    if Utils.fakeMacro and Utils.fakeMacro.active and speedActive and hrp then
        local moving = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W)
            or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A)
            or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S)
            or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D)
        if moving then
            local spd = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.fakeMacro.speed) or 40, 1, 120)
            local intense = math.clamp(tonumber(Utils.fakeMacro.intensity) or 50, 1, 100) / 100
            local osc = math.sin(tick() * spd) * math.rad(8 + intense * 70)
            local delta = osc - (Utils.fakeMacro.last or 0)
            Utils.fakeMacro.last = osc
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, delta, 0)
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + hrp.CFrame.RightVector * math.sin(tick() * spd) * (0.04 + intense * 0.28)
        else
            Utils.fakeMacro.last = 0
        end
    end

    -- Noclip: force CanCollide off every frame (more reliable than one-shot)
    if noclipActive and char then
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end
end))

-- Spinbot
function Utils.setSpinbot(on)
    spinbotActive = on and true or false
    if spinbotConn then
        pcall(function() spinbotConn:Disconnect() end)
        spinbotConn = nil
    end
    if spinbotActive then
        spinbotConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local char = Player.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not hrp then return end
            if flyActive then return end
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(spinbotSpeed), 0)
        end)
        table.insert(trackedConns, spinbotConn)
    else
        local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
        if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
    end
end

Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.3)
    if spinbotActive then Utils.setSpinbot(true) end
    if trail.active then applyTrail(true) end
    if aura.enabled then applyAura(aura.selected) end
    if headless.active then task.defer(function() Utils.applyHeadless(true) end) end
    if korblox.active then task.defer(function() Utils.applyKorblox(true) end) end
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        if Utils.refreshAvatarPreview then Utils.refreshAvatarPreview() end
    end)
end))

-- Trail
function Utils.clearTrail(hrp)
    if not hrp then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "RaysTrail" or child.Name == "RaysTrailAtt0" or child.Name == "RaysTrailAtt1" then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

function applyTrail(on)
    trail.active = on and true or false
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    Utils.clearTrail(hrp)
    if not trail.active then return end

    local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
    att0.Name = "RaysTrailAtt0"
    att0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
    att0.Parent = hrp

    local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
    att1.Name = "RaysTrailAtt1"
    att1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.9, 0)
    att1.Parent = hrp

    local t = Instance.new("Trail")
    t.Name = "RaysTrail"
    t.Attachment0 = att0
    t.Attachment1 = att1
    t.Color = ColorSequence.new(trail.color1, trail.color2)
    t.Lifetime = trail.lifetime
    t.LightEmission = trail.lightEmission
    t.Brightness = trail.brightness
    t.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1)
    t.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, trail.width),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
    })
    t.Parent = hrp
end

function Utils.loadAuraModel(assetId)
    local ok, objs = pcall(function()
        return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(assetId))
    end)
    if ok and type(objs) == "table" and objs[1] then
        return objs[1]
    end
    ok, objs = pcall(function()
        local is = game:GetService("InsertService")
        local container = is:LoadAsset(assetId)
        local child = container:GetChildren()[1]
        if child then
            child.Parent = nil
            container:Destroy()
            return child
        end
        return container
    end)
    if ok and objs then return objs end
    return nil
end

function Utils.getAuraParent(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

function Utils.clearActiveAura()
    local char = Player.Character
    local parent = Utils.getAuraParent(char)
    if not parent then
        aura.activeFolder = nil
        return
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Attachment") and (child:GetAttribute("RaysAura") or string.sub(child.Name, 1, 9) == "RaysAura_") then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        elseif child.Name == "RaysAuraFX" then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    -- also clear leftover on HRP from older versions
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and hrp ~= parent then
        for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Attachment") and (child:GetAttribute("RaysAura") or string.sub(child.Name, 1, 9) == "RaysAura_") then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            elseif child.Name == "RaysAuraFX" then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end
    aura.activeFolder = nil
end

-- Extract particles/attachments/lights from model parts onto HRP (no part follows the player)
Utils.extractAuraContents = function(source, body)
    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "RaysAuraFX"
    folder.Parent = body
    aura.activeFolder = folder

    local attachments = {}
    local loose = {}
    for _, d in ipairs(source:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Attachment") then
            table.insert(attachments, d)
        elseif d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail")
            or d:IsA("PointLight") or d:IsA("SpotLight") or d:IsA("SurfaceLight")
            or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Sparkles") then
            table.insert(loose, d)
        end
    end

    for _, att in ipairs(attachments) do
        pcall(function()
            att.Name = "RaysAura_" .. att.Name
            att:SetAttribute("RaysAura", true)
            att.Parent = body
        end)
    end

    for _, fx in ipairs(loose) do
        if fx.Parent and not (fx.Parent:IsA("Attachment") and fx.Parent:GetAttribute("RaysAura")) then
            if fx.Parent:IsA("BasePart") or fx.Parent:IsA("Model") or fx.Parent == source then
                pcall(function()
                    local holder = Instance.new("Attachment")
                    holder.Name = "RaysAura_Hold"
                    holder:SetAttribute("RaysAura", true)
                    holder.Parent = body
                    fx.Parent = holder
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    pcall(function() source:Destroy() end)
end

function applyAura(name)
    if name then aura.selected = name end
    Utils.clearActiveAura()
    if not aura.enabled then return end

    local char = Player.Character
    local body = Utils.getAuraParent(char)
    if not body then return end

    local assetId = aura.catalog[aura.selected]
    if not assetId then return end

    local model = Utils.loadAuraModel(assetId)
    if not model then
        warn("[Zenith] Failed to load aura:", aura.selected, assetId)
        return
    end
    Utils.extractAuraContents(model, body)
end

function setAuraEnabled(on)
    aura.enabled = on and true or false
    if aura.enabled then
        applyAura(aura.selected)
    else
        Utils.clearActiveAura()
    end
end

Utils.applyKorblox = function(on)
    korblox.active = on and true or false
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    -- Prefer HumanoidDescription so the leg attaches under the torso correctly
    if hum then
        local okDesc = pcall(function()
            local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
            if korblox.active then
                if not korblox.savedRightLeg then
                    korblox.savedRightLeg = desc.RightLeg
                end
                -- Korblox Deathspeaker right leg package
                desc.RightLeg = 139607673
                hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
            else
                desc.RightLeg = korblox.savedRightLeg or 0
                hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
                korblox.savedRightLeg = nil
            end
        end)
        if okDesc then
            task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
            return
        end
    end
    -- Fallback: mesh swap (preserve Size so it doesn't sink to the floor)
    if korblox.active then
        korblox.saved = {}
        for partName, data in pairs(KORBLOX_RIGHT) do
            local part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
            if part and part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                korblox.saved[partName] = {
                    MeshId = part.MeshId,
                    TextureID = part.TextureID,
                    Size = part.Size,
                }
                pcall(function()
                    local sz = part.Size
                    part.MeshId = data.MeshId
                    part.TextureID = data.TextureID
                    part.Size = sz
                end)
            end
        end
    else
        for partName, data in pairs(korblox.saved or {}) do
            local part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
            if part and part:IsA("MeshPart") and data then
                pcall(function()
                    part.MeshId = data.MeshId or ""
                    part.TextureID = data.TextureID or ""
                    if data.Size then part.Size = data.Size end
                end)
            end
        end
        korblox.saved = {}
    end
    task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
end

Utils.clearAvatarPreview = function()
    if AP.clone then
        pcall(function() AP.clone:Destroy() end)
        AP.clone = nil
    end
    if AP.World then
        for _, ch in ipairs(AP.World:GetChildren()) do
            pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

Utils.refreshAvatarPreview = function()
    if not AP.open then return end
    if not (AP.World and AP.Cam and AP.Viewport) then return end
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then
        Utils.clearAvatarPreview()
        return
    end
    Utils.clearAvatarPreview()
    -- Characters are often non-Archivable; force clone
    local wasArch = char.Archivable
    char.Archivable = true
    local ok, clone = pcall(function()
        return char:Clone()
    end)
    char.Archivable = wasArch
    if not ok or not clone then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("ModuleScript") or d:IsA("Sound") then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
            d.Anchored = true
            d.CanCollide = false
            pcall(function()
                d.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                d.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    end
    local hum = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
            hum.HealthDisplayType = Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff
            hum.PlatformStand = true
        end)
    end
    -- Parent first, then aim camera using the clone's real facing direction
    clone.Parent = AP.World
    AP.clone = clone
    local hrp = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or clone:FindFirstChild("Torso") or clone:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or clone.PrimaryPart
    if hrp then
        -- reset upright at origin (keep original yaw from live char via clone)
        pcall(function()
            local yaw = select(2, hrp.CFrame:ToOrientation())
            clone:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0))
        end)
        hrp = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or hrp
        local look = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
        if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then
            look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        end
        look = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if look.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        else
            look = look.Unit
        end
        -- level camera; aim lower so avatar sits higher in the panel
        local focus = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, -0.15, 0)
        local dist = 8.6
        local camPos = focus + look * dist + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
        AP.baseFocus = focus
        AP.baseLook = look
        AP.baseDist = dist
        AP.Cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(camPos, focus)
        AP.Cam.FieldOfView = 37
    else
        AP.Cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 4, 9, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1)
    end
    AP.Viewport.CurrentCamera = AP.Cam
end

Utils.setAvatarPreviewVisible = function(visible)
    AP.open = visible and true or false
    if not AP.Frame then return end
    local mainPos = MainFrame.Position
    AP.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(mainPos.X.Scale, mainPos.X.Offset + PANEL_W + 12, mainPos.Y.Scale, mainPos.Y.Offset)
    if Theme then
        if currentThemeName == "Midnight" or currentThemeName == "Cotton Candy" or currentThemeName == "Lavender" or currentThemeName == "Ash" or currentThemeName == "Bubblegum" then
            AP.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        else
            AP.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        end
        if AP.Viewport then
            AP.Viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end
        if AP.Grad and Theme.GradTop then
            AP.Grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.GradTop),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.GradMid or Theme.GradTop),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.GradBottom or Theme.GradTop),
            })
        end
        if AP.Stroke then
            AP.Stroke.Color = (currentThemeName == "Midnight") and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or (Theme.Border or Color3.fromRGB(99, 18, 22))
            AP.Stroke.Thickness = 2
            AP.Stroke.Transparency = 0.05
        end
        if AP.Title then
            AP.Title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        end
        pcall(Utils.updatePanelBgImage)
    end
    if visible then
        AP.Frame.Visible = true
        AP.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, AP.H)
        TweenService:Create(AP.Frame, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, AP.W, 0, AP.H),
        }):Play()
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(0.05)
            Utils.refreshAvatarPreview()
        end)
    else
        local tw = TweenService:Create(AP.Frame, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, AP.H),
        })
        tw:Play()
        tw.Completed:Connect(function()
            if not AP.open then
                AP.Frame.Visible = false
                Utils.clearAvatarPreview()
            end
        end)
    end
end

Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not AP.open or not AP.Frame or not AP.Frame.Visible then return end
    local mainPos = MainFrame.Position
    AP.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(mainPos.X.Scale, mainPos.X.Offset + PANEL_W + 12, mainPos.Y.Scale, mainPos.Y.Offset)
    -- continuous orbit when Live Preview is on
    if AP.liveSync then
        local now = tick()
        local gap = AP.liveNoDelay and 0 or 0.12
        if now - (AP.lastSync or 0) >= gap then
            AP.lastSync = now
            pcall(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
        end
    end
    if AP.liveSpin and AP.Cam and AP.baseFocus and AP.baseLook and AP.baseDist then
        AP.spinAngle = (AP.spinAngle or 0) + (dt or 0.016) * 1.25
        local look = AP.baseLook
        local right = Vector3.new(-look.Z, 0, look.X)
        if right.Magnitude < 0.05 then right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) else right = right.Unit end
        local ang = AP.spinAngle
        local orbit = (look * math.cos(ang) + right * math.sin(ang)) * AP.baseDist
        local camPos = AP.baseFocus + orbit + Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0)
        AP.Cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(camPos, AP.baseFocus)
    end
end))

Utils.applyHeadless = function(on)
    headless.active = on and true or false
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end

    if not headless.active then
        -- restore
        for inst, trans in pairs(headless.saved) do
            if inst and inst.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    if inst:IsA("BasePart") or inst:IsA("Decal") or inst:IsA("Texture") then
                        inst.Transparency = trans
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        headless.saved = {}
        task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
        return
    end

    headless.saved = {}
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
        headless.saved[head] = head.Transparency
        head.Transparency = 1
        for _, d in ipairs(head:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                headless.saved[d] = d.Transparency
                d.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
    end
    -- R15 face accessories / MeshParts named Face
    for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Decal") and (obj.Name == "face" or obj.Name == "Face") then
            if headless.saved[obj] == nil then
                headless.saved[obj] = obj.Transparency
                obj.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
    end
    task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
end


-- Infinite Jump
Utils.track(UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if not infiniteJumpActive then return end
    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
    if hum then
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
    end
end))

--// FLY
Utils.stopFly = function()
    flyActive = false
    if flyConn then
        pcall(function() flyConn:Disconnect() end)
        flyConn = nil
    end
    local char = Player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hum then
        hum.PlatformStand = false
    end
    if hrp then
        for _, obj in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("BodyVelocity") and obj.Name == "RaysFlyBV" then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end

Utils.startFly = function()
    Utils.stopFly()
    flyActive = true
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not hrp then return end

    hum.PlatformStand = true

    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bv.Name = "RaysFlyBV"
    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
    bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    bv.Parent = hrp

    flyConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not flyActive or not bv.Parent then
            Utils.stopFly()
            return
        end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local dir = Vector3.zero
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            dir = dir + cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            dir = dir - cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            dir = dir - cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            dir = dir + cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
            dir = dir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
            dir = dir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if dir.Magnitude > 0 then
            bv.Velocity = dir.Unit * currentFlySpeed
        else
            bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

Utils.toggleFly = function(state)
    if state == nil then
        state = not flyActive
    end
    if state then
        Utils.startFly()
    else
        Utils.stopFly()
    end
    if R.flyToggleBtn and R.flyToggleBtn.Parent then
        UI.applyToggleState(R.flyToggleBtn, R.flyToggleIndicator, flyActive)
        if flyActive then
            if R.flyExtra then
                R.flyExtra.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(R.flyExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)}):Play()
            end
        else
            if R.flyExtra then
                local tweenOut = TweenService:Create(R.flyExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
                tweenOut:Play()
                tweenOut.Completed:Connect(function()
                    if not flyActive and R.flyExtra then
                        R.flyExtra.Visible = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if flyActive then
        task.wait(0.3)
        if flyActive then
            Utils.startFly()
        end
    end
end))

--// GOD MODE
Utils.godCleanup = function()
    if godTrack then
        pcall(function() godTrack:Stop() end)
        pcall(function() godTrack:Destroy() end)
        godTrack = nil
    end
    if godHeartbeat then
        godHeartbeat:Disconnect()
        godHeartbeat = nil
    end
    if godAnimConn then
        godAnimConn:Disconnect()
        godAnimConn = nil
    end
end

Utils.godStart = function()
    Utils.godCleanup()
    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
    if not hum then return end

    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim.AnimationId = godEmoteId
    godTrack = hum:LoadAnimation(anim)
    godTrack:Play(0, 1, 1)

    godHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if godTrack and godModeActive then
            godTrack.TimePosition = godFreezeTime
            godTrack:AdjustSpeed(0)
        end
    end)

    godAnimConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(newtrack)
        if godModeActive and godTrack and newtrack ~= godTrack then
            task.delay(0.02 + math.random() * 0.03, function()
                if godModeActive then Utils.godStart() end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

Utils.godStop = function()
    Utils.godCleanup()
end

Utils.toggleGodMode = function()
    godModeActive = not godModeActive
    if godModeActive then
        Utils.godStart()
    else
        Utils.godStop()
    end
    if R.godToggleBtn and R.godToggleBtn.Parent then
        UI.applyToggleState(R.godToggleBtn, R.godToggleIndicator, godModeActive)
    end
end

Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.25)
    if godModeActive then
        Utils.godStart()
    end
end))


--// AFK Spoofer (Hood Customs)
Utils.afkSpoofActive = false
Utils.afkSpoofThread = nil

Utils.setAfkSpoof = function(state)
    if state == nil then
        state = not Utils.afkSpoofActive
    end
    local wasOn = Utils.afkSpoofActive
    Utils.afkSpoofActive = state and true or false
    if Utils.afkSpoofActive then
        if Utils.afkSpoofThread then return end
        Utils.afkSpoofThread = task.spawn(function()
            while Utils.afkSpoofActive do
                pcall(function()
                    local ev = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("MainEvent")
                    if ev then
                        ev:FireServer("RequestAFKDisplay", true)
                    end
                end)
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
            Utils.afkSpoofThread = nil
        end)
    elseif wasOn then
        -- turn AFK off once on the server
        pcall(function()
            local ev = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("MainEvent")
            if ev then
                ev:FireServer("RequestAFKDisplay", false)
            end
        end)
    end
    if R.afkToggleBtn and R.afkToggleIndicator then
        UI.applyToggleState(R.afkToggleBtn, R.afkToggleIndicator, Utils.afkSpoofActive)
    end
end

Utils.toggleAfkSpoof = function()
    Utils.setAfkSpoof(not Utils.afkSpoofActive)
end


--// Crosshair Cursor (Drawing API) - Visual
local customCursor = {
    enabled = false,
    text = "Ray's Panel",
    refreshrate = 0,
    mode = "mouse", -- mouse | center
    width = 1.5,
    length = 10,
    radius = 11,
    color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 110),
    spin = true,
    spin_speed = 150,
    spin_max = 340,
    resize = true,
    resize_speed = 150,
    resize_min = 5,
    resize_max = 22,
    hideDefault = true,
}
customCursor.drawings = nil
customCursor.conn = nil
customCursor.lastRender = 0

Utils.destroyCustomCursor = function()
    if customCursor.conn then
        pcall(function() customCursor.conn:Disconnect() end)
        customCursor.conn = nil
    end
    if customCursor.drawings then
        for _, d in pairs(customCursor.drawings.crosshair or {}) do
            pcall(function() d.Visible = false d:Remove() end)
        end
        for _, d in pairs(customCursor.drawings.text or {}) do
            pcall(function() d.Visible = false d:Remove() end)
        end
        customCursor.drawings = nil
    end
    pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true end)
end

Utils.solveAngle = function(angle, radius)
    return Vector2.new(
        math.sin(math.rad(angle)) * radius,
        math.cos(math.rad(angle)) * radius
    )
end

Utils.startCustomCursor = function()
    Utils.destroyCustomCursor()
    if not customCursor.enabled then return end
    if not Utils.hasDrawing then
        warn("[Zenith] Drawing API not available on this executor")
        customCursor.enabled = false
        return
    end

    local ok, drawings = pcall(function()
        local t = {
            crosshair = {},
            text = {
                Drawing.new("Text"),
                Drawing.new("Text"),
            },
        }
        for i = 1, 8 do
            t.crosshair[i] = Drawing.new("Line")
        end
        -- text setup
        t.text[1].Size = 13
        t.text[1].Font = Drawing.Fonts and Drawing.Fonts.Plex or 2
        t.text[1].Outline = true
        t.text[1].Center = false
        t.text[1].Text = "."
        t.text[1].Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

        t.text[2].Size = 13
        t.text[2].Font = Drawing.Fonts and Drawing.Fonts.Plex or 2
        t.text[2].Outline = true
        t.text[2].Center = false
        t.text[2].Text = tostring(customCursor.text or "Ray's Panel")
        t.text[2].Color = customCursor.color
        return t
    end)
    if not ok or not drawings then
        warn("[Zenith] Crosshair create failed:", drawings)
        return
    end
    customCursor.drawings = drawings

    if customCursor.hideDefault then
        pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false end)
    end

    customCursor.conn = RunService.PostSimulation:Connect(function()
        if not customCursor.enabled or not customCursor.drawings then return end
        local _tick = tick()
        if _tick - customCursor.lastRender <= (customCursor.refreshrate or 0) then return end
        customCursor.lastRender = _tick

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local position
        if customCursor.mode == "center" and cam then
            position = cam.ViewportSize / 2
        else
            position = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        end

        local t1 = customCursor.drawings.text[1]
        local t2 = customCursor.drawings.text[2]
        t2.Text = tostring(customCursor.text or "Ray's Panel")
        t2.Color = customCursor.color

        local bounds1 = t1.TextBounds or Vector2.new(0, 0)
        local bounds2 = t2.TextBounds or Vector2.new(0, 0)
        local text_x = bounds1.X + bounds2.X
        local yOff = customCursor.radius + ((customCursor.resize and customCursor.resize_max) or customCursor.length) + 15

        t1.Visible = true
        t2.Visible = true
        t1.Position = position + Vector2.new(-text_x / 2, yOff)
        t2.Position = t1.Position + Vector2.new(bounds1.X, 0)

        for idx = 1, 4 do
            local outline = customCursor.drawings.crosshair[idx]
            local inline = customCursor.drawings.crosshair[idx + 4]
            local angle = (idx - 1) * 90
            local length = customCursor.length

            if customCursor.spin then
                local spin_angle = -_tick * customCursor.spin_speed % customCursor.spin_max
                local tv = 0
                pcall(function()
                    tv = TweenService:GetValue(spin_angle / 360, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
                end)
                angle = angle + tv * 360
            end

            if customCursor.resize then
                local resize_length = _tick * customCursor.resize_speed % 180
                length = customCursor.resize_min + math.sin(math.rad(resize_length)) * customCursor.resize_max
            end

            inline.Visible = true
            inline.Color = customCursor.color
            inline.From = position + Utils.solveAngle(angle, customCursor.radius)
            inline.To = position + Utils.solveAngle(angle, customCursor.radius + length)
            inline.Thickness = customCursor.width

            outline.Visible = true
            outline.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            outline.From = position + Utils.solveAngle(angle, customCursor.radius - 1)
            outline.To = position + Utils.solveAngle(angle, customCursor.radius + length + 1)
            outline.Thickness = customCursor.width + 1.5
        end
    end)
    Utils.track(customCursor.conn)
end

Utils.applyCursorVisuals = function()
    -- live updates applied each frame from customCursor table
    if customCursor.drawings and customCursor.drawings.text and customCursor.drawings.text[2] then
        pcall(function()
            customCursor.drawings.text[2].Text = tostring(customCursor.text or "Ray's Panel")
            customCursor.drawings.text[2].Color = customCursor.color
        end)
    end
end

Utils.setCustomCursor = function(on)
    customCursor.enabled = on and true or false
    if customCursor.enabled then
        Utils.startCustomCursor()
    else
        Utils.destroyCustomCursor()
    end
    if R.cursorToggleBtn and R.cursorToggleInd then
        UI.applyToggleState(R.cursorToggleBtn, R.cursorToggleInd, customCursor.enabled)
    end
end

--// Accessories (local visual add/remove)
local accessoryState = {
    added = {}, -- {id=, inst=, name=} live instances
    savedIds = {}, -- asset ids to re-apply on respawn (order preserved)
}

Utils.getCharacter = function()
    return Player.Character
end

Utils.loadAccessoryAsset = function(id)
    local url = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
    local model = nil

    local function pickFrom(res)
        if res == nil then return nil end
        if typeof(res) == "table" then
            return res[1] or res[0]
        end
        if typeof(res) == "Instance" then
            return res
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function tryLoad(fn)
        local ok, res = pcall(fn)
        if not ok then return nil end
        return pickFrom(res)
    end

    -- 1) game:GetObjects (Potassium / many executors)
    model = tryLoad(function()
        return game:GetObjects(url)
    end)

    -- 2) global GetObjects
    if not model and typeof(GetObjects) == "function" then
        model = tryLoad(function()
            return GetObjects(url)
        end)
    end

    -- 3) Xeno-style LoadLocalAsset (string url or id)
    if not model then
        local ins = game:GetService("InsertService")
        model = tryLoad(function()
            return ins:LoadLocalAsset(url)
        end)
        if not model then
            model = tryLoad(function()
                return ins:LoadLocalAsset(tostring(id))
            end)
        end
    end

    -- 4) InsertService:LoadAsset (classic)
    if not model then
        model = tryLoad(function()
            return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(tonumber(id))
        end)
    end

    -- 5) some executors expose getcustomasset / loadasset aliases
    if not model and typeof(loadasset) == "function" then
        model = tryLoad(function()
            return loadasset(id)
        end)
    end
    if not model and typeof(getobjects) == "function" then
        model = tryLoad(function()
            return getobjects(url)
        end)
    end

    if not model then
        return nil
    end

    -- Normalize: prefer Accessory/Hat instance
    if not (model:IsA("Accessory") or model:IsA("Hat")) then
        local found = model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Accessory", true)
            or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Hat", true)
        if found then
            local cl = found:Clone()
            pcall(function()
                if model.Parent == nil then
                    model:Destroy()
                end
            end)
            model = cl
        end
    else
        -- ensure we don't keep a locked asset reference
        model = model:Clone()
    end
    return model
end

Utils.sanitizeAccessoryParts = function(acc)
    for _, d in ipairs(acc:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            d.Anchored = false
            d.CanCollide = false
            d.Massless = true
            d.CanQuery = false
            d.CanTouch = false
            pcall(function()
                d.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                d.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    end
end

Utils.findCharacterAttachment = function(char, name)
    if not name or name == "" then return nil end
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name == name then
            return d
        end
    end
    return nil
end

Utils.attachAccessoryToCharacter = function(acc, char)
    if not acc or not char then return nil end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil end

    local clone = acc:Clone()
    Utils.sanitizeAccessoryParts(clone)

    local handle = clone:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not handle or not handle:IsA("BasePart") then
        handle = clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end
    if not handle then
        pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end)
        return nil
    end

    -- Move handle to character BEFORE welding so relative offset is not "world origin"
    -- Prefer matching attachment (hat, hair, face, etc.)
    local handleAtt = handle:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Attachment")
    local charAtt = handleAtt and Utils.findCharacterAttachment(char, handleAtt.Name) or nil
    local weldParent = hrp

    if charAtt and handleAtt then
        weldParent = charAtt.Parent
        if weldParent and weldParent:IsA("BasePart") then
            handle.CFrame = charAtt.WorldCFrame * handleAtt.CFrame:Inverse()
        else
            weldParent = hrp
            handle.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
        end
    else
        -- fallback: sit near head / HRP, do NOT leave at 0,0,0
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
            handle.CFrame = head.CFrame
            weldParent = head
        else
            handle.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
            weldParent = hrp
        end
    end

    clone.Parent = char

    -- Remove any existing welds that might fight
    for _, w in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint") or w:IsA("Motor6D") then
            pcall(function() w:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    if charAtt and handleAtt and weldParent and weldParent:IsA("BasePart") then
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Name = "RaysAccessoryWeld"
        w.Part0 = weldParent
        w.Part1 = handle
        w.C0 = charAtt.CFrame
        w.C1 = handleAtt.CFrame
        w.Parent = handle
    else
        local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
        w.Name = "RaysAccessoryWeld"
        w.Part0 = weldParent
        w.Part1 = handle
        w.Parent = handle
    end

    -- Do NOT call Humanoid:AddAccessory on client for foreign assets — can fling/teleport
    return clone
end

Utils.addAccessoryById = function(rawId, fromRespawn)
    local id = tonumber(tostring(rawId):match("%d+"))
    if not id then return false, "Invalid ID" end
    local char = Utils.getCharacter()
    if not char then return false, "No character" end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not hrp then return false, "No humanoid/root" end

    -- Remember where we are so a bad attach can never leave us at origin
    local safeCF = hrp.CFrame

    local model = Utils.loadAccessoryAsset(id)
    if not model then
        return false, "Failed to load asset"
    end

    local finalInst = Utils.attachAccessoryToCharacter(model, char)
    pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)

    -- Restore CFrame if something yanked the character
    task.defer(function()
        local ch = Utils.getCharacter()
        local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            local pos = root.Position
            if pos.Magnitude < 5 or (pos - safeCF.Position).Magnitude > 80 then
                root.CFrame = safeCF
            end
        end
    end)

    if not finalInst or not finalInst.Parent then
        return false, "Could not attach"
    end

    table.insert(accessoryState.added, { id = id, inst = finalInst, name = finalInst.Name })
    if not fromRespawn then
        local already = false
        for _, sid in ipairs(accessoryState.savedIds) do
            if sid == id then already = true break end
        end
        if not already then
            table.insert(accessoryState.savedIds, id)
        end
    end
    task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
    return true, finalInst.Name
end

Utils.reapplySavedAccessories = function()
    local char = Utils.getCharacter()
    if not char then return end
    accessoryState.added = {}
    for _, id in ipairs(accessoryState.savedIds) do
        pcall(function()
            Utils.addAccessoryById(id, true)
        end)
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end

Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.6)
    Utils.reapplySavedAccessories()
end))

Utils.listCharacterAccessories = function()
    local char = Utils.getCharacter()
    local out = {}
    local seen = {}
    if char then
        for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Accessory") or ch:IsA("Hat") then
                table.insert(out, ch)
                seen[ch] = true
            end
        end
    end
    for _, entry in ipairs(accessoryState.added) do
        local inst = entry.inst
        if inst and inst.Parent and not seen[inst] then
            table.insert(out, inst)
            seen[inst] = true
        end
    end
    return out
end

Utils.removeAccessoryInstance = function(inst)
    local removeId = nil
    for i = #accessoryState.added, 1, -1 do
        if accessoryState.added[i].inst == inst then
            removeId = accessoryState.added[i].id
            table.remove(accessoryState.added, i)
        end
    end
    if removeId then
        for i = #accessoryState.savedIds, 1, -1 do
            if accessoryState.savedIds[i] == removeId then
                -- only remove one matching id entry if no other live instance uses it
                local still = false
                for _, e in ipairs(accessoryState.added) do
                    if e.id == removeId then still = true break end
                end
                if not still then
                    table.remove(accessoryState.savedIds, i)
                end
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if inst and inst.Parent then
        pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
    end
    task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
end

--// Cam Lock functions
--// Cam Lock targeting + loop (fixed)
Utils.isTargetValid = function(player)
    if not player then return false end
    if Utils.skipProtected(player) then return false end
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return false end
    if Utils.isCharacterDowned(char) then return false end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return false end
    local part = getLockPart(char)
    if not part then return false end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false end

    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local pos = part.Position
    local toTarget = pos - origin
    local dist = toTarget.Magnitude
    if dist < 0.1 or dist > 2000 then return false end

    -- Sticky mode: stay locked even if camera looks away (until camlock toggled off)
    if camlock.sticky then
        return true
    end

    -- Non-sticky: must stay inside the on-screen FOV circle
    local inFov = Utils.partInScreenFov(part, fovOverride or camlock.fov)
    return inFov
end

Utils.getBestTarget = function(fovOverride)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return nil end
    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local lookVec = camera.CFrame.LookVector

    local bestTarget = nil
    local bestScore = math.huge

    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local filterList = {}
    if Player.Character then
        table.insert(filterList, Player.Character)
    end
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterList

    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and not (Utils.skipProtected(plr)) then
            local targetChar = plr.Character
            if targetChar then
                local humanoid = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not Utils.isCharacterDowned(targetChar) then
                    local part = getLockPart(targetChar)
                    if part then
                        local pos = part.Position
                        local toTarget = pos - origin
                        local dist = toTarget.Magnitude
                        if dist > 0.5 and dist < 2000 then
                            local inFov, screenDist = Utils.partInScreenFov(part, fovOverride or camlock.fov)
                            if inFov then
                                local vec = toTarget.Unit
                                local score = screenDist + dist * 0.05
                                local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, vec * dist, params)
                                if result and result.Instance and not result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetChar) then
                                    score = score + 500
                                end
                                if score < bestScore then
                                    bestScore = score
                                    bestTarget = plr
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return bestTarget
end


function Utils.fovScreenRadius(slider, maxV)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return 8 end
    local vp = camera.ViewportSize
    local half = math.min(vp.X, vp.Y) * 0.5
    maxV = tonumber(maxV) or 1000
    if maxV < 1 then maxV = 1000 end
    local visualScale = 400
    return math.max(4, half * (math.clamp(tonumber(slider) or 1, 1, maxV) / visualScale))
end

function Utils.getAimOrigin2D()
    local ok, loc = pcall(function()
        return UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    end)
    if ok and loc then return loc end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        return Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X * 0.5, camera.ViewportSize.Y * 0.5)
    end
    return Vector2.new(0, 0)
end

function Utils.partInScreenFov(part, slider, maxV)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not (camera and part) then return false, math.huge end
    local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
    if (not onScreen) or sp.Z <= 0 then return false, math.huge end
    local center = Utils.getAimOrigin2D()
    local dist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
    return dist <= Utils.fovScreenRadius(slider, maxV), dist
end

Utils.AIM_PART_NAMES = {
    "Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso",
    "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand", "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
    "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot",
    "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
}

function Utils.getAimPart(char, mode)
    if not char then return nil end
    mode = mode or "Head"
    if mode == "Closest Part" then
        local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, name in ipairs(Utils.AIM_PART_NAMES) do
            local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
            if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
                local _, d = Utils.partInScreenFov(p, 200)
                if d < bestDist then
                    bestDist = d
                    best = p
                end
            end
        end
        return best or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    end
    if mode == "Body" then
        return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    end
    if mode == "Left Leg" then
        return char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or char:FindFirstChild("LeftLeg")
    end
    if mode == "Right Leg" then
        return char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") or char:FindFirstChild("RightLeg")
    end
    if mode == "Left Arm" then
        return char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm") or char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or char:FindFirstChild("LeftArm")
    end
    if mode == "Right Arm" then
        return char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or char:FindFirstChild("RightArm")
    end
    local aliases = {
        LeftUpperArm = { "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm" },
        LeftLowerArm = { "LeftLowerArm", "Left Arm" },
        LeftHand = { "LeftHand", "Left Arm" },
        RightUpperArm = { "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm" },
        RightLowerArm = { "RightLowerArm", "Right Arm" },
        RightHand = { "RightHand", "Right Arm" },
        LeftUpperLeg = { "LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg" },
        LeftLowerLeg = { "LeftLowerLeg", "Left Leg" },
        LeftFoot = { "LeftFoot", "Left Leg" },
        RightUpperLeg = { "RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg" },
        RightLowerLeg = { "RightLowerLeg", "Right Leg" },
        RightFoot = { "RightFoot", "Right Leg" },
        UpperTorso = { "UpperTorso", "Torso" },
        LowerTorso = { "LowerTorso", "Torso", "HumanoidRootPart" },
        Head = { "Head" },
    }
    for _, n in ipairs(aliases[mode] or { mode }) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(n)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
    end
    return char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

--// Silent Aim (redirects mouse.Hit toward target part when enabled)
Utils.getSilentTarget = function()
    if (silentAim.bindMode or "") == "Always" then
        silentAim.enabled = true
    end
    if not silentAim.enabled then return nil end
    -- Prefer DH-native closest (same as GunHandler silent)
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or isDaHood then
        return Utils.dhGetClosest()
    end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return nil end
    local myRoot = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local usePlayerDist = (silentAim.targetMode == "Player")
    local candidates = {}
    local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
            if not (Utils.skipProtected(plr)) then
                local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 and not (silentAim.healthCheck and Utils.isCharacterDowned(plr.Character)) then
                    local part = Utils.getAimPart(plr.Character, silentAim.part or camlock.lockPart or "Head")
                    if part then
                        local inFov, screenDist = Utils.partInScreenFov(part, silentAim.fov, 1000)
                        if silentAim.neverMiss or inFov then
                            local score = screenDist
                            if usePlayerDist and myRoot then
                                score = (part.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            end
                            table.insert(candidates, { part = part, score = score, plr = plr, inFov = inFov })
                            if score < bestScore then
                                bestScore = score
                                best = part
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return Utils.silentPickCandidate(candidates, best)
end

pcall(function()
    -- Da Hood uses GunHandler.getAim only. HC uses the light mouse.Hit hook in hcSilentStart.
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID then return end
    local hasWraps = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Wraps") or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BulletBeams")
    if hasWraps then return end -- HC handled in Utils.hcSilentStart

    local mouse = Player:GetMouse()
    if not mouse then return end
    local function wrap(fn)
        if newcclosure then return newcclosure(fn) end
        return fn
    end
    local function hitAt(part)
        return CFrame.new(part.Position)
    end

    local function shouldSilent()
        if not silentAim.enabled then return false end
        -- Never stack with HC / DH dedicated silent
        if game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID then return false end
        if isDaHood or isHoodCustoms then return false end
        if checkcaller and checkcaller() then return false end
        return true
    end

    local hooked = false
    if hookmetamethod then
        local oldIndex
        oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", wrap(function(self, key)
            if shouldSilent() and self == mouse then
                if key == "Hit" or key == "hit" then
                    local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
                    if part then
                        silentAim.currentPart = part
                        return hitAt(part)
                    end
                elseif key == "Target" or key == "target" then
                    local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
                    if part then
                        silentAim.currentPart = part
                        return part
                    end
                elseif key == "UnitRay" then
                    local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
                    if part then
                        local origin = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position or mouse.Origin.Position
                        local dir = (part.Position - origin).Unit
                        return Ray.new(origin, dir * 1000)
                    end
                end
            end
            return oldIndex(self, key)
        end))
        hooked = true
        -- NOTE: do NOT hook CurrentCamera ScreenPointToRay / ViewportPointToRay —
        -- that breaks the camera popper and leaves the camera stuck outside the body.
    end

    local MOUSE_POS_KEYS = {
        UpdateMousePos = true,
        UpdateMousePosI = true,
        UpdateMousePosII = true,
        MousePosUpdate = true,
        MousePos = true,
        MOUSE = true,
    }
    local function silentWorldPos()
        local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
        if not part then return nil end
        silentAim.currentPart = part
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or part.Position
        local pos = part.Position
        local vel = Vector3.zero
        pcall(function() vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
        if vel.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            pcall(function()
