                local root = part.Parent and part.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end
            end)
        end
        -- Distance-based lead (open-source Da Hood / FPS style)
        local bspeed = math.max(tonumber(silentAim.bulletSpeed) or 3584, 1)
        local dist = (origin - pos).Magnitude
        local lead = Vector3.zero
        if silentAim.movementPredict ~= false then
            local t = dist / bspeed
            lead = vel * t
        end
        -- Optional vertical ballistics offset
        local drop = 0
        local boost = tonumber(silentAim.ballisticsBoost) or 0
        if boost ~= 0 then
            local minused = (boost + 50) / 50
            drop = dist - (dist / minused)
        end
        return pos + lead + Vector3.new(0, drop, 0)
    end
    local function trySpoofRemote(self, args)
        if not silentAim.enabled or typeof(self) ~= "Instance" then return end
        -- Da Hood uses GunHandler.getAim — leave remotes alone there
        if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or isDaHood then return end
        -- Hood Customs: allow mouse-pos spoof (post-patch server may validate these)
        local n = self.Name
        if n ~= "MainEvent" and n ~= "MAINEVENT" and n ~= "MainEvents" then return end
        local key = args[1]
        if type(key) ~= "string" then return end
        if not (MOUSE_POS_KEYS[key] or string.find(string.lower(key), "mouse", 1, true)) then return end
        local pos = silentWorldPos()
        if pos then args[2] = pos end
    end
    if hookmetamethod then
        local oldNc
        oldNc = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(self, ...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod and getnamecallmethod() or ""
            if method == "FireServer" and silentAim.enabled then
                local args = {...}
                trySpoofRemote(self, args)
                return oldNc(self, unpack(args))
            end
            return oldNc(self, ...)
        end))
    end
    pcall(function()
        local ev = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("MainEvent")
            or game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("MAINEVENT")
        if ev and ev:IsA("RemoteEvent") and hookfunction then
            local oldFire
            oldFire = hookfunction(ev.FireServer, wrap(function(self, ...)
                local args = {...}
                trySpoofRemote(self, args)
                return oldFire(self, unpack(args))
            end))
        end
    end)

    if not hooked and getrawmetatable then
        local mt = getrawmetatable(mouse)
        if mt then
            local oldIndex = mt.__index
            pcall(function() setreadonly(mt, false) end)
            mt.__index = wrap(function(self, key)
                if silentAim.enabled and (key == "Hit" or key == "hit" or key == "Target" or key == "target") then
                    local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
                    if part then
                        silentAim.currentPart = part
                        if key == "Hit" or key == "hit" then
                            return hitAt(part)
                        end
                        return part
                    end
                end
                if type(oldIndex) == "function" then
                    return oldIndex(self, key)
                end
                return oldIndex[key]
            end)
            pcall(function() setreadonly(mt, true) end)
        end
    end
end)

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
silentAim.lastShoot = 0

function Utils.silentPredictedPos(part)
    if not part then return nil end
    local dest = part.Position
    pcall(function()
        local vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if vel.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            local root = part.Parent and part.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end
        end
        local pred = 0.135
        if camlock.predictionEnabled then
            pred = tonumber(camlock.predictionX) or tonumber(camlock.prediction) or pred
        end
        dest = part.Position + vel * pred
    end)
    return dest
end

function Utils.silentMainEvent()
    return ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MainEvent") or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MAINEVENT")
end

function Utils.silentSpreadDest(dest, origin)
    if not dest then return dest end
    local spread = tonumber(silentAim.bulletSpread)
    if spread == nil then spread = 1 end
    spread = math.clamp(spread, 0, 2)
    if spread <= 0 then return dest end
    if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        origin = cam and cam.CFrame.Position or dest
    end
    local dir = dest - origin
    local dist = dir.Magnitude
    if dist < 0.05 then return dest end
    dir = dir.Unit
    local right = dir:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    if right.Magnitude < 0.05 then
        right = dir:Cross(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0))
    end
    right = right.Unit
    local up = right:Cross(dir).Unit
    local radius = dist * (0.028 * spread)
    local ang = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    local r = math.sqrt(math.random()) * radius
    return dest + right * math.cos(ang) * r + up * math.sin(ang) * r
end

Utils.silentUpdateMousePos = function(part, withSpread)
    -- DH uses GunHandler only; HC uses getMouseWorldHit only
    if isHoodCustoms or isDaHood then return end
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return end
    local ev = Utils.silentMainEvent()
    local dest = Utils.silentPredictedPos(part)
    if not (ev and dest) then return end
    if withSpread then
        local tool = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        dest = Utils.silentSpreadDest(dest, tool and tool.Name)
    end
    silentAim.lastDest = dest
    pcall(function() ev:FireServer("UpdateMousePosI", dest) end)
    pcall(function() ev:FireServer("UpdateMousePos", dest) end)
end

Utils.silentFireAtTarget = function(force)
    if isHoodCustoms or isDaHood then return end
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return end
    if not silentAim.enabled then return end
    local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
    if not part then return end
    silentAim.currentPart = part
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    local handle = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not (tool and handle) then return end
    if tool.Name == "[Knife]" or tool.Name == "[Bat]" then return end
    local ev = Utils.silentMainEvent()
    local dest = Utils.silentPredictedPos(part)
    if not (ev and dest) then return end
    dest = Utils.silentSpreadDest(dest, tool.Name)
    silentAim.lastDest = dest
    local now = tick()
    if (not force) and (now - (silentAim.lastShoot or 0) < 0.12) then return end
    silentAim.lastShoot = now
    pcall(function() ev:FireServer("UpdateMousePosI", dest) end)
    pcall(function()
        ev:FireServer("ShootGun", handle, handle.CFrame.Position, dest, part, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
    end)
end

function Utils.silentRedirectBullet(obj)
    if isHoodCustoms or isDaHood then return end
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return end
    local part = silentAim.currentPart
    if not (part and obj) then return end
    local dest = silentAim.lastDest or Utils.silentPredictedPos(part)
    if not dest then return end
    dest = Utils.silentSpreadDest(dest, nil)
    local function aimPart(p)
        if not (p and p:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
        pcall(function()
            local pos = p.Position
            p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, dest)
            p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (dest - pos).Unit * math.max(p.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude, 400)
        end)
    end
    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
        aimPart(obj)
    elseif obj:IsA("Model") then
        aimPart(obj.PrimaryPart or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
    end
end

pcall(function()
    -- Never install bullet-redirect / ShootGun silent on DH or HC
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return end
    local ignored = workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
    if ignored then
        Utils.track(ignored.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if isDaHood or isHoodCustoms then return end
            if not silentAim.enabled or not silentAim.currentPart then return end
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model") then
                local n = string.lower(obj.Name)
                if n:find("bullet", 1, true) or n:find("gun", 1, true) or n == "part" then
                    task.defer(function() Utils.silentRedirectBullet(obj) end)
                end
            end
        end))
    end
end)

Utils.track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if isHoodCustoms or isDaHood then return end
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return end
    if not silentAim.enabled then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        Utils.silentFireAtTarget(true)
    end
end))

Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if isHoodCustoms or isDaHood then return end
    if game.PlaceId == DAHOOD_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == HOOD_CUSTOMS_PLACE_ID then return end
    if not silentAim.enabled then return end
    local part = Utils.getSilentTarget()
    if part then
        silentAim.currentPart = part
        Utils.silentUpdateMousePos(part, false)
    end
    if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
        Utils.silentFireAtTarget(false)
    end
end))

Utils.camlockLoop = function()
    if (camlock.bindMode or "") == "Always" then
        camlock.enabled = true
    end
    if not camlock.enabled then
        camlock.currentTarget = nil
        if camlock._zoomActive then unlockPlayerZoom() end
        return
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end

    -- Drop target immediately when they are downed (0 HP / KO)
    if camlock.currentTarget then
        local tchar = camlock.currentTarget.Character
        local dead = (not tchar) or Utils.isCharacterDowned(tchar)
        if not dead then
            local hum = tchar and tchar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then dead = true end
        end
        if dead then
            if camlock.releaseOnSwitch then
                camlock.enabled = false
                camlock.currentTarget = nil
                if R.camlockToggleBtn and R.camlockToggleIndicator then
                    pcall(function() UI.applyToggleState(R.camlockToggleBtn, R.camlockToggleIndicator, false) end)
                end
                if camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch and camlock.zoomAim then
                    camlock.zoomAim = false
                    unlockPlayerZoom()
                    if R.zoomAimToggleBtn and R.zoomAimToggleIndicator then
                        pcall(function() UI.applyToggleState(R.zoomAimToggleBtn, R.zoomAimToggleIndicator, false) end)
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            camlock.currentTarget = nil
        end
    end

    if camlock.sticky and camlock.currentTarget and Utils.isTargetValid(camlock.currentTarget) then
        -- keep same target
    elseif not (camlock.currentTarget and Utils.isTargetValid(camlock.currentTarget)) then
        camlock.currentTarget = Utils.getBestTarget()
    end

    local target = camlock.currentTarget
    if not target then return end

    local char = target.Character
    if not char then return end
    if camlock.aimAssist then
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Player.Character, camera }
        params.IgnoreWater = true
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, camera.CFrame.LookVector * 800, params)
        if hit and hit.Instance and hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(char) then
            return
        end
        local vp = camera.ViewportSize
        local center = Vector2.new(vp.X * 0.5, vp.Y * 0.5)
        for _, p in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                local sp, on = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(p.Position)
                if on and sp.Z > 0 and (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude <= 34 then
                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local part = getLockPart(char)
    if not part then return end

    local pos = part.Position

    -- Prediction (horizontal / vertical)
    if camlock.predictionEnabled then
        local vel = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if vel.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local px = camlock.predictionX or camlock.prediction or 0
            local py = camlock.predictionY or camlock.prediction or 0
            pos = pos + Vector3.new(vel.X * px, vel.Y * py, vel.Z * px)
        end
    end

    local speed = math.clamp(camlock.smoothness or 0, 0, 1)
    local ease = math.clamp(camlock.ease or 0, 0, 1)
    local toVec = pos - camera.CFrame.Position
    local remaining = 0
    if toVec.Magnitude > 0.01 then
        remaining = math.acos(math.clamp(camera.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(toVec.Unit), -1, 1))
    end
    if camlock._trackId ~= camlock.currentTarget then
        camlock._trackId = camlock.currentTarget
        camlock._startAng = math.max(remaining, 0.08)
    end
    local startAng = math.max(camlock._startAng or remaining, remaining, 0.08)
    local t = 1 - math.clamp(remaining / startAng, 0, 1)
    local s = speed ^ 2.7
    local maxStep = math.rad(0.28 + s * 24)
    local linearStep = maxStep
    local easeOutStep = remaining * (0.035 + s * 0.5)
    local tClose = 1 - math.clamp(remaining / math.max(startAng, 0.08), 0, 1)
    local easeInStep = maxStep * math.clamp(0.05 + tClose * tClose * 1.4, 0.05, 1)
    local step
    if ease >= 0 then
        step = linearStep * (1 - ease) + easeOutStep * ease
    else
        local u = math.clamp(-ease, 0, 1)
        step = linearStep * (1 - u) + easeInStep * u
    end
    if speed >= 0.995 then
        step = remaining
    end
    local alpha = 1
    if remaining > 1e-4 then
        alpha = math.clamp(step / remaining, 0.004, 1)
    end

    if camlock.freehand then
        local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
        if onScreen and sp.Z > 0 then
            local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local dx = (sp.X - mouse.X) * alpha
            local dy = (sp.Y - mouse.Y) * alpha
            if math.abs(dx) > 0.2 or math.abs(dy) > 0.2 then
                if mousemoverel then
                    pcall(mousemoverel, dx, dy)
                elseif mousemoveabs then
                    pcall(mousemoveabs, mouse.X + dx, mouse.Y + dy)
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end

    local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
    local toTarget = pos - camPos
    local dist = toTarget.Magnitude
    if dist < 0.01 then return end
    local desiredLook = toTarget.Unit
    local currentLook = camera.CFrame.LookVector
    local newLook = currentLook:Lerp(desiredLook, alpha)
    camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, camPos + newLook)
end

skinChanger.allowedOrder = {
    "[Revolver]",
    "[Double-Barrel SG]",
    "[TacticalShotgun]",
    "[Shotgun]",
    "[DrumShotgun]",
    "[Silencer]",
    "[Glock]",
    "[AK-47]",

    "[Silencer AR]",
    "[Drum Gun]",
    "[AUG]",
    "[P90]",
    "[Rifle]",
    "[Knife]",
    "[Bat]",
}
skinChanger.allowedGuns = {}
;(function()
    for _, gunName in ipairs(skinChanger.allowedOrder) do
        skinChanger.allowedGuns[gunName] = true
        skinChanger.selected[gunName] = ""
    end
end)()

function Utils.skinLog(...)
end

function Utils.skinLower(v)
    return string.lower(tostring(v or ""))
end

function Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(name)
    local raw = tostring(name or "")
    local plain = raw:gsub("^%[", ""):gsub("%]$", "")
    local compact = plain:gsub("[%s%-_]", ""):lower()
    local aliases = {
        ["doublebarrelsg"] = "[Double-Barrel SG]",
        ["doublebarrel"] = "[Double-Barrel SG]",
        ["db"] = "[Double-Barrel SG]",
        ["tacticalshotgun"] = "[TacticalShotgun]",
        ["tact"] = "[TacticalShotgun]",
        ["shotgun"] = "[Shotgun]",
        ["drumshotgun"] = "[DrumShotgun]",
        ["revolver"] = "[Revolver]",
        ["silencer"] = "[Silencer]",
        ["glock"] = "[Glock]",
        ["ak47"] = "[AK-47]",
        ["ak"] = "[AK-47]",
        ["ar"] = "[AR]",
        ["silencerar"] = "[Silencer AR]",
        ["drumgun"] = "[Drum Gun]",
        ["aug"] = "[AUG]",
        ["p90"] = "[P90]",
        ["rifle"] = "[Rifle]",
        ["knife"] = "[Knife]",
        ["bat"] = "[Bat]",
    }
    if aliases[compact] then return aliases[compact] end
    if raw:sub(1, 1) == "[" then return raw end
    if plain ~= "" then return "[" .. plain .. "]" end
    return raw
end

function Utils.skinAllowedGun(name)
    return skinChanger.allowedGuns[Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(name)] == true
end

function Utils.skinExcludedCategory(name)
    local s = Utils.skinLower(name)
    return s:find("mask", 1, true)
        or s:find("house", 1, true)
        or s:find("vehicle", 1, true)
        or s:find("car", 1, true)
        or s:find("bike", 1, true)
        or s:find("boat", 1, true)
end

function Utils.skinSortCI(t)
    table.sort(t, function(a, b)
        return string.lower(tostring(a)) < string.lower(tostring(b))
    end)
    return t
end

function Utils.skinCollectParts(root)
    local out = {}
    if not root then return out end
    if root:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(out, root) end
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(out, d) end
    end
    return out
end

function Utils.skinFindTool(gunName)
    gunName = Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(gunName)
    local char = Player.Character
    if char then
        local t = char:FindFirstChild(gunName)
        if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
    end
    local bp = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        local t = bp:FindFirstChild(gunName)
        if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
    end
    return nil
end

function Utils.skinWaitForReady(timeout)
    local started = tick()
    timeout = timeout or 8
    while tick() - started < timeout do
        local char = Player.Character
        if char and (char:FindFirstChild("FULLY_LOADED_CHAR") or char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")) then
            return true
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return Player.Character ~= nil
end

function Utils.skinRequireModule(force)
    if skinChanger.moduleData and not force then
        return skinChanger.moduleData
    end

    local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local obj = rs:FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
    if not obj then
        skinChanger.moduleSource = "SkinModules missing"
        Utils.skinLog("FAIL require: ReplicatedStorage.SkinModules not found")
        return nil, "ReplicatedStorage.SkinModules not found"
    end
    Utils.skinLog("found SkinModules", obj.ClassName, obj:GetFullName())

    -- SkinModules is often a ModuleScript that also has Knives/Bats children.
    local function tryRequire(target)
        if not target then return false, nil end
        local ok, result = pcall(require, target)
        if (not ok or type(result) ~= "table") and getrenv then
            local gameRequire
            pcall(function() gameRequire = getrenv().require end)
            if type(gameRequire) == "function" then
                ok, result = pcall(gameRequire, target)
            end
        end
        return ok, result
    end

    local ok, result = tryRequire(obj)
    if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
        local nested = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ModuleScript")
        if nested and nested ~= obj then
            ok, result = tryRequire(nested)
            obj = nested
        end
    end

    if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
        skinChanger.moduleSource = "require failed"
        Utils.skinLog("FAIL require SkinModules:", tostring(result))
        return nil, "SkinModules require failed: " .. tostring(result)
    end

    skinChanger.moduleObject = obj
    skinChanger.moduleData = result
    skinChanger.moduleSource = "ReplicatedStorage.SkinModules"
    local keys = 0
    for _ in pairs(result) do keys = keys + 1 end
    Utils.skinLog("require OK, table keys:", keys)
    return result
end

function Utils.skinFindFolderAsset(folder, skinName)
    if not folder or not skinName then return nil end
    local hit = folder:FindFirstChild(skinName)
    if hit then return hit end
    for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
        if d.Name == skinName then return d end
    end
    return nil
end

function Utils.skinGetData(gunName, skinName)
    gunName = Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(gunName)
    local data = skinChanger.moduleData
    if type(data) == "table" then
        local gunTable = data[gunName]
        if type(gunTable) ~= "table" then
            local plain = gunName:gsub("^%[", ""):gsub("%]$", "")
            gunTable = data[plain] or data["[" .. plain .. "]"]
        end
        if type(gunTable) == "table" and gunTable[skinName] ~= nil then
            Utils.skinLog("getData hit module", gunName, skinName, typeof(gunTable[skinName]))
            return gunTable[skinName]
        end
        Utils.skinLog("getData no module entry", gunName, skinName, "gunTable=", type(gunTable))
    else
        Utils.skinLog("getData moduleData not a table", type(data))
    end
    local sm = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
    if not sm then return nil end
    if gunName == "[Knife]" then
        return Utils.skinFindFolderAsset(sm:FindFirstChild("Knives") or sm:FindFirstChild("Knife"), skinName)
    end
    if gunName == "[Bat]" then
        return Utils.skinFindFolderAsset(sm:FindFirstChild("Bats") or sm:FindFirstChild("Bat"), skinName)
    end
    local gm = sm:FindFirstChild("GunModels")
    if gm then
        local plain = gunName:gsub("^%[", ""):gsub("%]$", "")
        local gunFolder = gm:FindFirstChild(gunName) or gm:FindFirstChild(plain)
        local hit = (gunFolder and Utils.skinFindFolderAsset(gunFolder, skinName)) or Utils.skinFindFolderAsset(gm, skinName)
        if hit then return hit end
    end
    return nil
end

function Utils.skinScanInventory()
    Utils.skinWaitForReady(6)
    local moduleData = Utils.skinRequireModule(true)
    if type(moduleData) ~= "table" then
        moduleData = {}
    end

    local scanned = {}
    local count = 0
    local order = {}

    -- Prefer fixed order, but also pick up any extra gun keys from the live module
    local seen = {}
    local function scanGun(gunName)
        gunName = Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(gunName)
        if seen[gunName] then return end
        seen[gunName] = true
        local gunTable = moduleData[gunName]
        if type(gunTable) ~= "table" then
            local plain = gunName:gsub("^%[", ""):gsub("%]$", "")
            gunTable = moduleData[plain] or moduleData["[" .. plain .. "]"]
        end
        if type(gunTable) ~= "table" then return end
        local names = {}
        for skinName, skinData in pairs(gunTable) do
            if type(skinName) == "string"
                and skinName ~= ""
                and not Utils.skinExcludedCategory(skinName)
                and (type(skinData) == "table" or typeof(skinData) == "Instance")
            then
                table.insert(names, skinName)
            end
        end
        if #names == 0 then return end
        Utils.skinSortCI(names)
        scanned[gunName] = names
        table.insert(order, gunName)
        count = count + #names
        skinChanger.allowedGuns[gunName] = true
        if skinChanger.selected[gunName] == nil then
            skinChanger.selected[gunName] = ""
        end
    end

    for _, gunName in ipairs(skinChanger.allowedOrder) do
        scanGun(gunName)
    end

    -- Knife / Bat skins often live as instances under SkinModules folders
    local function scanMeleeFolder(gunName)
        local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local sm = rs:FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
        if not sm then return end
        local plain = gunName:gsub("^%[", ""):gsub("%]$", "")
        local folder = sm:FindFirstChild(gunName) or sm:FindFirstChild(plain)
        if not folder then
            local childMod = sm:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ModuleScript")
            if childMod then
                folder = childMod:FindFirstChild(gunName) or childMod:FindFirstChild(plain)
            end
        end
        if not folder then return end
        local names = scanned[gunName] or {}
        local have = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do have[n] = true end
        local added = false
        for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if ch.Name ~= "" and not have[ch.Name] and not Utils.skinExcludedCategory(ch.Name) then
                table.insert(names, ch.Name)
                have[ch.Name] = true
                added = true
            end
        end
        if #names == 0 then return end
        Utils.skinSortCI(names)
        scanned[gunName] = names
        if not seen[gunName] then
            seen[gunName] = true
            table.insert(order, gunName)
        end
        count = 0
        for _, list in pairs(scanned) do count = count + #list end
        skinChanger.allowedGuns[gunName] = true
        if skinChanger.selected[gunName] == nil then
            skinChanger.selected[gunName] = ""
        end
    end
    scanMeleeFolder("[Bat]")

    local function addFolderSkins(gunName, folder)
        if not folder then return end
        local names = scanned[gunName] or {}
        local have = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do have[n] = true end
        for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if ch.Name ~= "" and not have[ch.Name] then
                table.insert(names, ch.Name)
                have[ch.Name] = true
            end
        end
        if gunName == "[Knife]" then
            for n in pairs(skinChanger.knifeskins or {}) do
                if not have[n] then
                    table.insert(names, n)
                    have[n] = true
                end
            end
        end
        if #names == 0 then return end
        Utils.skinSortCI(names)
        scanned[gunName] = names
        if not seen[gunName] then
            seen[gunName] = true
            table.insert(order, gunName)
        end
        skinChanger.allowedGuns[gunName] = true
        if skinChanger.selected[gunName] == nil then
            skinChanger.selected[gunName] = ""
        end
    end
    do
        local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local sm = rs:FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
        if sm then
            addFolderSkins("[Bat]", sm:FindFirstChild("Bats") or sm:FindFirstChild("Bat"))
        end
        do
            local names = {}
            for n in pairs(skinChanger.knifeskins or {}) do table.insert(names, n) end
            Utils.skinSortCI(names)
            scanned["[Knife]"] = names
            seen["[Knife]"] = true
            if not table.find(order, "[Knife]") then
                table.insert(order, "[Knife]")
            end
            skinChanger.allowedGuns["[Knife]"] = true
            if skinChanger.selected["[Knife]"] == nil then
                skinChanger.selected["[Knife]"] = ""
            end
        end
    end
    count = 0
    for _, list in pairs(scanned) do count = count + #list end

    -- discover extra weapons present in SkinModules
    for key, gunTable in pairs(moduleData) do
        if type(key) == "string" and type(gunTable) == "table" and not Utils.skinExcludedCategory(key) then
            if key:sub(1, 1) == "[" or skinChanger.allowedGuns[Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(key)] then
                scanGun(key)
            end
        end
    end

    skinChanger.scanned = scanned
    skinChanger.weaponOrder = order
    skinChanger.lastScanCount = count

    if not order[1] then
        skinChanger.lastStatus = "No skins found in SkinModules"
        Utils.skinLog("scan FAIL: no weapons/skins found")
        if R.skinRefreshBrowser then pcall(R.skinRefreshBrowser) end
        return false, skinChanger.lastStatus
    end

    if not skinChanger.scanned[skinChanger.uiWeapon] then
        skinChanger.uiWeapon = order[1]
    end
    local currentList = scanned[skinChanger.uiWeapon] or {}
    local selected = skinChanger.selected[skinChanger.uiWeapon]
    if selected and selected ~= "" and table.find(currentList, selected) then
        skinChanger.uiSkin = selected
    else
        skinChanger.uiSkin = currentList[1]
    end

    skinChanger.lastStatus = string.format("Scanned %d skins across %d weapons", count, #order)
    Utils.skinLog("scan OK", skinChanger.lastStatus)
    if R.skinRefreshBrowser then pcall(R.skinRefreshBrowser) end
    return true, skinChanger.lastStatus
end

function Utils.skinGetRenderPart(tool)
    if not tool then return nil end
    local default = tool:FindFirstChild("Default")
    if default and default:IsA("BasePart") then return default end

    for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("MeshPart") and d.Name ~= "Handle" and d.Transparency < 1 then
            return d
        end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "Handle" and d.Transparency < 1 then
            return d
        end
    end
    return tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
end

function Utils.skinSanitizeVisual(root)
    if not root then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("ModuleScript")
            or d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("RemoteFunction")
            or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or d:IsA("Sound")
        then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(Utils.skinCollectParts(root)) do
        p.Anchored = false
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.CanQuery = false
        p.Massless = true
        p.CastShadow = false
    end
end

function Utils.skinDestroyHolder(tool)
    if not tool then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if d.Name == "RaysSkinHolder" then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

function Utils.skinRestoreTint(tool)
    local state = skinChanger.toolStates[tool]
    if not state or not state.tintOriginals then return end
    for part, col in pairs(state.tintOriginals) do
        if part and part.Parent then
            pcall(function() part.Color = col end)
        end
    end
    state.tintOriginals = {}
end

function Utils.skinCleanKnife(tool)
    if tool then
        pcall(function() tool:SetAttribute("CurrentKnifeSkin", nil) end)
    end
    local data = skinChanger.knifedata[tool]
    if data then
        if data.track then
            pcall(function() data.track:Stop() data.track:Destroy() end)
            data.track = nil
        end
        if data.welds then
            for _, w in ipairs(data.welds) do
                if w then pcall(function() w:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end
        if data.sounds then
            for _, s in ipairs(data.sounds) do
                if s then pcall(function() s:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end
        skinChanger.knifedata[tool] = nil
    end
    local mesh = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Default")
    if mesh then
        for _, v in ipairs(mesh:GetChildren()) do
            if v.Name == "Handle.R" or v:IsA("Model") or (v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "Default") then
                pcall(function() v:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        pcall(function() mesh.Transparency = 0 end)
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(tool and tool:GetDescendants() or {}) do
        if d.Name == "CurrentSkin" then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

function Utils.skinHideBatHitbox(tool)
    if not tool then return end
    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
        handle.Transparency = 1
        pcall(function() handle.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end)
    end
end

function Utils.skinApplyBat(tool, name)
    if not tool or not name or name == "" then return false end
    for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if d.Name == "CurrentSkin" then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    local mesh = nil
    for _, v in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("MeshPart") and v.Transparency < 1 and v.Name ~= "Handle" then
            mesh = v
            break
        end
    end
    mesh = mesh or tool:FindFirstChild("Default")
    if not (mesh and mesh:IsA("BasePart")) then
        mesh = tool:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("MeshPart", true)
    end
    if not mesh then return false end

    local batskins = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
    batskins = batskins and (batskins:FindFirstChild("Bats") or batskins:FindFirstChild("Bat"))
    local skin = batskins and batskins:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not skin then return false end

    local clone = skin:Clone()
    clone.Name = "CurrentSkin"
    clone.Parent = tool
    if clone:IsA("BasePart") then
        clone.Anchored = false
        clone.CanCollide = false
        clone.Massless = true
        clone.CFrame = mesh.CFrame
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Part0 = clone
        w.Part1 = mesh
        w.Parent = clone
    elseif clone:IsA("Model") then
        local root = clone.PrimaryPart or clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if root then
            pcall(function() clone:PivotTo(mesh.CFrame) end)
            root.Anchored = false
            root.CanCollide = false
            root.Massless = true
            local w = Instance.new("Weld")
            w.Part0 = root
            w.Part1 = mesh
            w.Parent = root
        end
    end
    mesh.Transparency = 1
    Utils.skinHideBatHitbox(tool)
    return true
end

function Utils.skinApplyKnife(char, tool, skin)
    local cfg = skinChanger.knifeskins[skin]
    if not cfg then return false end
    char = char or Player.Character
    if not char or not tool then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local rhand = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand") or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
    if not hum or not rhand then return false end
    if tool:GetAttribute("CurrentKnifeSkin") == skin then
        local meshChk = tool:FindFirstChild("Default")
        if meshChk and meshChk:FindFirstChild("Handle.R") then
            return true
        end
    end
    Utils.skinCleanKnife(tool)
    skinChanger.knifedata[tool] = {track = nil, welds = {}, sounds = {}}
    local data = skinChanger.knifedata[tool]
    local mesh = tool:FindFirstChild("Default") or tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not mesh then return false end
    mesh.Transparency = 1
    local skinmodules = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
    local knives = skinmodules and (skinmodules:FindFirstChild("Knives") or skinmodules:FindFirstChild("Knife"))
    local skinmodel = knives and knives:FindFirstChild(skin)
    if not skinmodel then return false end
    local clone = skinmodel:Clone()
    clone.Name = skin

    local handr = Instance.new("Part")
    handr.Name = "Handle.R"
    handr.Transparency = 1
    handr.CanCollide = false
    handr.Anchored = false
    handr.Size = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
    handr.Massless = true
    handr.Parent = mesh

    local m6d = Instance.new("Motor6D")
    m6d.Name = "Handle.R"
    m6d.Part0 = rhand
    m6d.Part1 = handr
    m6d.Parent = handr

    local offset = CFrame.new(cfg.positionoffset) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(cfg.rotationoffset.X), math.rad(cfg.rotationoffset.Y), math.rad(cfg.rotationoffset.Z))

    if clone:IsA("Model") then
        if not clone.PrimaryPart then
            for _, c in ipairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("BasePart") then
                    clone.PrimaryPart = c
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if clone.PrimaryPart then
            for _, p in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                    p.CanCollide = false
                    p.Massless = true
                    p.Anchored = false
                    local w = Instance.new("Weld")
                    w.Part0 = handr
                    w.Part1 = p
                    w.C0 = offset
                    w.C1 = p.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(clone.PrimaryPart.CFrame)
                    w.Parent = p
                    table.insert(data.welds, w)
                end
            end
        end
        clone.Parent = mesh
    elseif clone:IsA("BasePart") then
        clone.CanCollide = false
        clone.Massless = true
        clone.Anchored = false
        if clone:IsA("MeshPart") and cfg.textureid then
            clone.TextureID = cfg.textureid
        end
        if cfg.particle then
            local skinassets = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("SkinAssets")
            local particlefolder = skinassets and skinassets:FindFirstChild("GunHandleParticle")
            local particlesource = particlefolder and particlefolder:FindFirstChild(skin)
            local pe = particlesource and particlesource:FindFirstChild("ParticleEmitter")
            if pe then pe:Clone().Parent = clone end
        end
        clone.Parent = mesh
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Part0 = handr
        w.Part1 = clone
        w.C0 = offset
        w.Parent = clone
        table.insert(data.welds, w)
    end

    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then
        animator = Instance.new("Animator")
        animator.Parent = hum
    end
    if cfg.animationid and cfg.animationid ~= "" then
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = cfg.animationid
        local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
        track.Looped = false
        track:Play()
        data.track = track
        anim:Destroy()
        track.Ended:Once(function()
            if data.track == track then data.track = nil end
            track:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    if cfg.soundid and cfg.soundid ~= "" then
        local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
        snd.SoundId = cfg.soundid
        snd.Parent = workspace
        snd:Play()
        table.insert(data.sounds, snd)
        snd.Ended:Connect(function()
            snd:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    pcall(function() tool:SetAttribute("CurrentKnifeSkin", skin) end)
    return true
end

function Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
    if not tool then return end
    Utils.skinCleanKnife(tool)
    local state = skinChanger.toolStates[tool]
    Utils.skinDestroyHolder(tool)

    if state then
        local default = state.default
        if default and default.Parent then
            if state.defaultTexture ~= nil and default:IsA("MeshPart") then
                pcall(function() default.TextureID = state.defaultTexture end)
            end
            if state.defaultTransparency ~= nil and default.Name ~= "Handle" then
                pcall(function() default.Transparency = state.defaultTransparency end)
            end
            if state.defaultLTM ~= nil then
                pcall(function() default.LocalTransparencyModifier = state.defaultLTM end)
            end
            if state.defaultColor then
                pcall(function() default.Color = state.defaultColor end)
            end
        end
        if state.handle and state.handle.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                state.handle:SetAttribute("SkinName", state.oldSkinAttribute)
            end)
        end
        if state.shoot and state.shoot.Parent and state.oldShootSound then
            pcall(function() state.shoot.SoundId = state.oldShootSound end)
        end
        if state.hiddenParts then
            for part, info in pairs(state.hiddenParts) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        if info.Transparency ~= nil then part.Transparency = info.Transparency end
                        if info.LTM ~= nil then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = info.LTM end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
        Utils.skinRestoreTint(tool)
        skinChanger.toolStates[tool] = nil
    else
        -- Bat/knife None with no saved state: unhide default meshes
        for _, p in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "CurrentSkin" then
                pcall(function()
                    if p.LocalTransparencyModifier >= 1 then
                        p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                    end
                    if p.Transparency >= 1 and (p.Name == "Default" or p:IsA("MeshPart")) then
                        p.Transparency = 0
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if d.Name == "CurrentSkin" then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function() Utils.skinResetGunFx(tool) end)
    pcall(function() Utils.skinHideBatHitbox(tool) end)
    local saved = skinChanger.batVisuals[tool]
    if saved then
        for part, info in pairs(saved) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    part.Transparency = info.Transparency
                    part.LocalTransparencyModifier = info.LTM
                end)
            end
        end
        skinChanger.batVisuals[tool] = nil
    end
end

function Utils.skinTintParts(tool, root)
    if not skinChanger.paintEnabled or not root then return end
    local state = skinChanger.toolStates[tool]
    if not state then return end
    state.tintOriginals = state.tintOriginals or {}
    for _, part in ipairs(Utils.skinCollectParts(root)) do
        if part.Transparency < 1 then
            if state.tintOriginals[part] == nil then
                state.tintOriginals[part] = part.Color
            end
            pcall(function() part.Color = skinChanger.paintColor end)
        end
    end
end

function Utils.skinAttachSpecialVisual(tool, default, visual, skinData)
    if typeof(visual) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
    local clone = visual:Clone()
    clone.Name = "RaysSkinHolder"
    Utils.skinSanitizeVisual(clone)

    local offset = CFrame.new()
    if type(skinData) == "table" then
        if typeof(skinData.CFrame) == "CFrame" then offset = skinData.CFrame
        elseif typeof(skinData.C0) == "CFrame" then offset = skinData.C0 end
    end

    local parts = Utils.skinCollectParts(clone)
    if #parts == 0 then
        pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end)
        return nil
    end

    local root = clone:IsA("BasePart") and clone
        or (clone:IsA("Model") and clone.PrimaryPart)
        or parts[1]

    if clone:IsA("Model") and not clone.PrimaryPart then
        pcall(function() clone.PrimaryPart = root end)
    end

    local rootCF = root.CFrame
    local rel = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
        rel[p] = rootCF:ToObjectSpace(p.CFrame)
    end

    clone.Parent = default
    root.CFrame = default.CFrame * offset

    for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
        p.Anchored = false
        if p ~= root then
            p.CFrame = root.CFrame * rel[p]
            local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            w.Name = "RaysSkinInternalWeld"
            w.Part0 = root
            w.Part1 = p
            w.Parent = p
        end
    end

    local rootWeld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    rootWeld.Name = "RaysSkinRootWeld"
    rootWeld.Part0 = default
    rootWeld.Part1 = root
    rootWeld.Parent = root
    return clone
end

function Utils.skinEnsureGunFx(tool)
    if not tool then return nil end
    if skinChanger.gunFx[tool] then return skinChanger.gunFx[tool] end
    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    local fx = { origSound = nil, origParticles = {} }
    if handle then
        local shoot = handle:FindFirstChild("ShootSound")
        if shoot and shoot:IsA("Sound") then
            fx.origSound = shoot.SoundId
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or c:IsA("Beam") or c:IsA("Trail") then
                table.insert(fx.origParticles, c:Clone())
            end
        end
    end
    skinChanger.gunFx[tool] = fx
    return fx
end

function Utils.skinResetGunFx(tool)
    local handle = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not handle then return end
    local fx = Utils.skinEnsureGunFx(tool)
    local shoot = handle:FindFirstChild("ShootSound")
    if shoot and shoot:IsA("Sound") and fx.origSound then
        pcall(function() shoot.SoundId = fx.origSound end)
    end
    for _, c in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if c:GetAttribute("RaysSkinFX") or c:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or c:IsA("Beam") or c:IsA("Trail") then
            -- keep only originals we will restore
            pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    for _, template in ipairs(fx.origParticles) do
        local clone = template:Clone()
        clone.Parent = handle
    end
end

function Utils.skinApplyGunFx(tool, skinName)
    if not tool then return end
    Utils.skinEnsureGunFx(tool)
    Utils.skinResetGunFx(tool)
    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not handle or not skinName or skinName == "" then return end

    local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local assets = rs:FindFirstChild("SkinAssets")
    local shoot = handle:FindFirstChild("ShootSound")
    if shoot and shoot:IsA("Sound") and assets then
        local sounds = assets:FindFirstChild("GunShootSounds")
        local gunSounds = sounds and sounds:FindFirstChild(tool.Name)
        local skinSound = gunSounds and gunSounds:FindFirstChild(skinName)
        if skinSound then
            local val = skinSound:IsA("StringValue") and skinSound.Value or (skinSound:IsA("Sound") and skinSound.SoundId)
            if type(val) == "string" and val ~= "" then
                pcall(function() shoot.SoundId = val end)
            end
        end
    end

    if assets then
        local particlefolder = assets:FindFirstChild("GunHandleParticle")
        local particlesource = particlefolder and particlefolder:FindFirstChild(skinName)
        if particlesource then
            for _, d in ipairs(particlesource:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") then
                    local pe = d:Clone()
                    pe:SetAttribute("RaysSkinFX", true)
                    pe.Parent = handle
                end
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function() handle:SetAttribute("SkinName", skinName) end)
end

function Utils.skinApplyShootSound(tool, skinName, state)
    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not handle then return end
    if state then
        state.handle = handle
        state.oldSkinAttribute = handle:GetAttribute("SkinName")
    end
    Utils.skinApplyGunFx(tool, skinName)
end

function Utils.skinApplyModuleVisual(tool, skinName)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") or not skinName or skinName == "" then
        return false, "invalid tool/skin"
    end

    local skinData = Utils.skinGetData(tool.Name, skinName)
    if not skinData then return false, "skin not present in live SkinModules table" end

    local default = Utils.skinGetRenderPart(tool)
    if not default then return false, "weapon render part not found" end

    Utils.skinClearTool(tool)

    local state = {
        default = default,
        defaultTransparency = default.Transparency,
        defaultLTM = default.LocalTransparencyModifier,
        defaultColor = default.Color,
        defaultTexture = default:IsA("MeshPart") and default.TextureID or nil,
        tintOriginals = {},
    }
    skinChanger.toolStates[tool] = state
    Utils.skinApplyShootSound(tool, skinName, state)

    local visual = nil
    if type(skinData) == "table" then
        visual = skinData.TextureID or skinData.TextureId or skinData.Texture or skinData.Mesh
    elseif typeof(skinData) == "Instance" then
        visual = skinData
    end

    local applied = false
    local holder = nil

    if typeof(visual) == "number" then
        visual = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(visual)
    end

    if type(visual) == "string" then
        if default:IsA("MeshPart") then
            pcall(function()
                default.TextureID = visual
                default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
            end)
            applied = true
        end
    elseif typeof(visual) == "Instance" then
        if visual:IsA("MeshPart") and default:IsA("MeshPart") then
            if visual.TextureID and visual.TextureID ~= "" then
                pcall(function()
                    default.TextureID = visual.TextureID
                    default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                end)
                applied = true
            end
            local needsHolder = false
            pcall(function()
                needsHolder = visual.MeshId ~= "" and default.MeshId ~= "" and visual.MeshId ~= default.MeshId
            end)
            if needsHolder then
                holder = Utils.skinAttachSpecialVisual(tool, default, visual, skinData)
                if holder then
                    default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    applied = true
                end
            end
        else
            holder = Utils.skinAttachSpecialVisual(tool, default, visual, skinData)
            if holder then
                default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                applied = true
            end
        end
    end

    if not applied and type(skinData) == "table" then
        for _, candidate in pairs(skinData) do
            if typeof(candidate) == "Instance" and (candidate:IsA("MeshPart") or candidate:IsA("Model") or candidate:IsA("BasePart")) then
                holder = Utils.skinAttachSpecialVisual(tool, default, candidate, skinData)
                if holder then
                    default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    applied = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not applied then
        Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
        return false, "skin data found, but no usable TextureID/mesh"
    end

    if skinChanger.paintEnabled then
        Utils.skinTintParts(tool, holder or default)
    end
    return true, "live module skin applied"
end

function Utils.skinFindMeleeAsset(weaponName, skinName)
    if not skinName or skinName == "" then return nil, nil end
    local data = Utils.skinGetData(weaponName, skinName)
    if typeof(data) == "Instance" then
        return data, data
    end
    if type(data) == "table" then
        for _, key in ipairs({"Mesh", "Model", "TextureID", "Texture", "Handle"}) do
            local v = data[key]
            if typeof(v) == "Instance" then return v, data end
        end
        for _, v in pairs(data) do
            if typeof(v) == "Instance" and (v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("Model") or v:IsA("MeshPart") or v:IsA("Accessory")) then
                return v, data
            end
        end
    end
    local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local sm = rs:FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
    if not sm then return nil, data end
    local plain = tostring(weaponName):gsub("^%[", ""):gsub("%]$", "")
    local folders = {sm:FindFirstChild(weaponName), sm:FindFirstChild(plain), sm:FindFirstChild("Knife"), sm:FindFirstChild("Bat")}
    local childMod = sm:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ModuleScript")
    if childMod then
        table.insert(folders, childMod:FindFirstChild(weaponName))
        table.insert(folders, childMod:FindFirstChild(plain))
    end
    for _, folder in ipairs(folders) do
        if folder then
            local hit = folder:FindFirstChild(skinName)
            if hit then return hit, data end
        end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(sm:GetDescendants()) do
        if d.Name == skinName and (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("MeshPart") or d:IsA("Accessory")) then
            return d, data
        end
    end
    return nil, data
end

function Utils.skinApplyMelee(tool, skinName)
    if not tool or not skinName or skinName == "" then return false, "invalid" end
    local visual, skinData = Utils.skinFindMeleeAsset(tool.Name, skinName)
    if not visual then
        -- last try: module visual path
        return Utils.skinApplyModuleVisual(tool, skinName)
    end
    local default = Utils.skinGetRenderPart(tool) or tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not default then return false, "no handle" end

    Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
    local state = {
        default = default,
        defaultTransparency = default.Transparency,
        defaultLTM = default.LocalTransparencyModifier,
        defaultColor = default.Color,
        defaultTexture = default:IsA("MeshPart") and default.TextureID or nil,
        tintOriginals = {},
    }
    skinChanger.toolStates[tool] = state

    if typeof(visual) == "Instance" and visual:IsA("MeshPart") and default:IsA("MeshPart") then
        if visual.TextureID and visual.TextureID ~= "" and (visual.MeshId == "" or visual.MeshId == default.MeshId) then
            pcall(function()
                default.TextureID = visual.TextureID
                default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
            end)
            return true, "melee texture"
        end
    end

    local holder = Utils.skinAttachSpecialVisual(tool, default, visual, skinData)
    if not holder then
        Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
        return false, "could not attach melee skin"
    end
    pcall(function() default.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end)
    for _, p in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "Handle" and not p:IsDescendantOf(holder) and p.Name ~= "RaysSkinHolder" then
            pcall(function() p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end)
        end
    end
    return true, "melee skin applied"
end

function Utils.skinApplyGun(tool, name)
    Utils.skinLog("applyGun start", tool and tool.Name, name)
    if not tool or not name or name == "" then
        Utils.skinLog("applyGun abort: missing tool/name")
        return false
    end
    local orig = tool:FindFirstChildOfClass("MeshPart")
    if not orig then orig = Utils.skinGetRenderPart(tool) end
    if not orig then
        Utils.skinLog("applyGun FAIL: no render part on", tool.Name)
        return false
    end
    Utils.skinLog("applyGun render part", orig.ClassName, orig.Name)

    local skinmodules = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("SkinModules")
    if not skinmodules then
        Utils.skinLog("applyGun FAIL: SkinModules missing")
        return false
    end
    local okReq, skinmodulesreq = pcall(require, skinmodules)
    Utils.skinLog("applyGun require", okReq, type(skinmodulesreq))
    if not okReq or type(skinmodulesreq) ~= "table" then
        skinmodulesreq = skinChanger.moduleData
    end
    if type(skinmodulesreq) ~= "table" then
        Utils.skinLog("applyGun FAIL: module table missing")
        return false
    end
    local info = skinmodulesreq[tool.Name] and skinmodulesreq[tool.Name][name]
    if not info then
        Utils.skinLog("applyGun FAIL: no info for", tool.Name, name)
        return false
    end
    Utils.skinLog("applyGun info type", typeof(info), type(info))

    for _, child in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "CurrentSkin" or (child:IsA("MeshPart") and child ~= orig) then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    local skinpart = type(info) == "table" and (info.TextureID or info.TextureId) or info
    if typeof(skinpart) == "Instance" then
        local clone = skinpart:Clone()
        clone.Parent = tool
        pcall(function() clone.CFrame = orig.CFrame end)
        clone.Name = "CurrentSkin"
        local w = Instance.new("Weld")
        w.Part0 = clone
        w.Part1 = orig
        if type(info) == "table" and typeof(info.CFrame) == "CFrame" then
            pcall(function() w.C0 = info.CFrame:Inverse() end)
        end
        w.Parent = clone
        orig.Transparency = 1
    elseif type(skinpart) == "string" then
        orig.TextureID = skinpart
        orig.Transparency = 0
    else
        return false
    end

    Utils.skinApplyGunFx(tool, name)
    Utils.skinLog("applyGun OK", tool.Name, name)
    return true
end

function Utils.skinApplyTool(tool)
    if not isDaHood then return end
    if not skinChanger.enabled or not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
        Utils.skinLog("applyTool skip: enabled=", skinChanger.enabled, "tool=", tool and tool.Name)
        return
    end
    if not Utils.skinAllowedGun(tool.Name) then
        Utils.skinLog("applyTool skip: gun not allowed", tool.Name)
        return
    end

    local skinName = skinChanger.selected[Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(tool.Name)]
    Utils.skinLog("applyTool", tool.Name, "selected=", skinName, "parent=", tool.Parent and tool.Parent.Name)
    if skinName and skinName ~= "" then
        local ok
        if tool.Name == "[Knife]" then
            ok = Utils.skinApplyKnife(Player.Character, tool, skinName)
            Utils.skinLog("applyKnife result", ok)
        elseif tool.Name == "[Bat]" then
            ok = Utils.skinApplyBat(tool, skinName)
            Utils.skinLog("applyBat result", ok)
        else
            ok = Utils.skinApplyGun(tool, skinName)
            Utils.skinLog("applyGun result", ok)
            if not ok then
                ok = Utils.skinApplyModuleVisual(tool, skinName)
                Utils.skinLog("applyModuleVisual result", ok)
            end
        end
        if not ok then
            Utils.skinLog("applyTool FAIL, clearing", tool.Name, skinName)
            Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
        end
    else
        Utils.skinLog("applyTool no skin selected, clearing", tool.Name)
        Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
    end
    if tool.Name == "[Bat]" then
        Utils.skinHideBatHitbox(tool)
    end
end

function Utils.skinApplyToolStable(tool)
    if not tool or tool.Parent ~= Player.Character then return end
    Utils.skinApplyTool(tool)
    if tool.Name == "[Knife]" or tool.Name == "[Bat]" then
        return
    end
    task.delay(0.12, function()
        if skinChanger.enabled and tool.Parent == Player.Character then Utils.skinApplyTool(tool) end
    end)
    task.delay(0.45, function()
        if skinChanger.enabled and tool.Parent == Player.Character then Utils.skinApplyTool(tool) end
    end)
end

function Utils.skinRefreshEquipped()
    if not skinChanger.enabled then return end
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") and Utils.skinAllowedGun(tool.Name) then
            Utils.skinApplyToolStable(tool)
        end
    end
end

function Utils.skinSetupTool(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") or not Utils.skinAllowedGun(tool.Name) then return end
    if skinChanger.setupTools[tool] then return end
    skinChanger.setupTools[tool] = true

    Utils.track(tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
        if skinChanger.enabled then
            task.defer(function()
                if tool.Parent == Player.Character then Utils.skinApplyToolStable(tool) end
            end)
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
        Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
    end))
    Utils.track(tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if not tool.Parent then
            skinChanger.toolStates[tool] = nil
        end
    end))
end

function Utils.skinWatchContainer(container)
    if not container then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then Utils.skinSetupTool(child) end
    end
    Utils.track(container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            Utils.skinSetupTool(child)
            if skinChanger.enabled and child.Parent == Player.Character then
                task.defer(function() Utils.skinApplyToolStable(child) end)
            end
        end
    end))
end

function Utils.skinStart()
    if not isDaHood then
        Utils.skinLog("start skipped: not Da Hood")
        return
    end
    if skinChanger.started then
        Utils.skinLog("start already running")
        return
    end
    Utils.skinLog("start watching backpack/character")
    skinChanger.started = true
    Utils.skinWatchContainer(Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack"))
    Utils.skinWatchContainer(Player.Character)
    Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.25)
        Utils.skinWatchContainer(char)
        if skinChanger.enabled then
            Utils.skinScanInventory()
            task.wait(0.15)
            Utils.skinRefreshEquipped()
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local function hideIn(container)
            if not container then return end
            local bat = container:FindFirstChild("[Bat]")
            if bat then Utils.skinHideBatHitbox(bat) end
        end
        hideIn(Player.Character)
        hideIn(Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack"))
    end))
end

function Utils.skinSelect(gunName, skinName)
    Utils.skinLog("select", gunName, skinName)
    gunName = Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(gunName)
    if not Utils.skinAllowedGun(gunName) then
        Utils.skinLog("select FAIL: weapon not allowed", gunName)
        return false, "weapon not allowed"
    end
    skinName = tostring(skinName or "")

    local list = skinChanger.scanned[gunName] or {}
    if skinName ~= "" and not table.find(list, skinName) then
        -- still allow selecting if module has it even if list stale
        if not Utils.skinGetData(gunName, skinName) then
            return false, "skin is not in the live SkinModules scan"
        end
    end

    skinChanger.selected[gunName] = skinName
    skinChanger.uiWeapon = gunName
    skinChanger.uiSkin = skinName ~= "" and skinName or nil

    local tool = Utils.skinFindTool(gunName)
    if tool and tool.Parent == Player.Character and skinChanger.enabled then
        Utils.skinApplyToolStable(tool)
    end

    if R.skinRefreshBrowser then pcall(R.skinRefreshBrowser) end
    return true, skinName == "" and "default restored" or "selected"
end

function Utils.skinSetEnabled(on)
    Utils.skinLog("setEnabled", on)
    if on and not isDaHood then
        Utils.skinLog("setEnabled blocked: not Da Hood")
        on = false
    end
    skinChanger.enabled = on and true or false
    if skinChanger.enabled then
        Utils.skinStart()
        local ok, msg = Utils.skinScanInventory()
        if ok then
            Utils.skinRefreshEquipped()
        else
            skinChanger.lastStatus = msg
        end
    else
        local char = Player.Character
        local bp = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        for _, container in ipairs({char, bp}) do
            if container then
                for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") and Utils.skinAllowedGun(tool.Name) then
                        Utils.skinClearTool(tool)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        skinChanger.lastStatus = "Skin Changer disabled"
    end
    if R.skinRefreshBrowser then pcall(R.skinRefreshBrowser) end
end

function Utils.skinGetGunSkinList(toolName)
    toolName = Utils.skinNormalizeGunName(toolName)
    return skinChanger.scanned[toolName] or {}
end

--// Hood Customs skin changer (Wraps + Knives)
Utils.hcSkins = Utils.hcSkins or {
    enabled = false,
    started = false,
    selected = {},
    scanned = {},
    processed = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    order = { "DoubleBarrel", "Revolver", "TacticalShotgun", "SMG", "Shotgun", "Silencer", "Knife" },
    bulletGuns = { "DoubleBarrel", "Revolver", "TacticalShotgun", "SMG", "Shotgun", "Silencer" },
    handleMap = { DB_HANDLE = "DoubleBarrel", REV_HANDLE = "Revolver" },
    lastStatus = "Hood Customs only",
    bullets = {},
    bulletTypes = { "Beta" },
    beamBackup = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
}
;(function()
    for _, n in ipairs(Utils.hcSkins.order) do
        Utils.hcSkins.selected[n] = ""
        Utils.hcSkins.scanned[n] = {}
    end
    for _, n in ipairs(Utils.hcSkins.bulletGuns) do
        Utils.hcSkins.bullets[n] = ""
    end
end)()

function Utils.hcIsBasePart(x)
    return typeof(x) == "Instance" and x:IsA("BasePart")
end

function Utils.hcEnsurePrimary(m)
    if m and m:IsA("Model") then
        if not Utils.hcIsBasePart(m.PrimaryPart) then
            m.PrimaryPart = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        end
        return m.PrimaryPart
    end
end

function Utils.hcPrepParts(model)
    for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.CanQuery = false
            p.CanTouch = false
            p.Anchored = false
            p.Massless = true
            p.CastShadow = false
        end
    end
end

function Utils.hcRestoreGunVisuals()
    local function restore(container)
        if not container then return end
        for _, t in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if t:IsA("Tool") or t:IsA("Model") then
                for _, p in ipairs(t:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                            if p.Name == "Handle" then
                                p.Transparency = 0
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    restore(Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack"))
    restore(Player.Character)
end

function Utils.hcHideOriginals(holder, hide)
    if not holder then return end
    local clone = holder:FindFirstChild("HCSkinClone")
    for _, p in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            local n = string.lower(p.Name)
            local hideThis = (n == "handle") or (n == "hitbox") or n:find("hitbox", 1, true)
            if hideThis then
                if hide then
                    p.Transparency = 1
                    p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    p.CanCollide = false
                else
                    p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                    if n == "handle" then
                        p.Transparency = 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if clone then
        for _, p in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                local n = string.lower(p.Name)
                if n == "hitbox" or n:find("hitbox", 1, true) then
                    p.Transparency = 1
                    p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    p.CanCollide = false
                else
                    p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function Utils.hcClearHolder(holder)
    if not holder then return end
    local clone = holder:FindFirstChild("HCSkinClone")
    if clone then
        pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end)
    end
    for _, ch in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("WeldConstraint") and ch.Name == "HCSkinWeld" then
            pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function() holder:SetAttribute("HCSkin", nil) end)
    Utils.hcHideOriginals(holder, false)
end

function Utils.hcApplyModel(holder, skinModel, skinName)
    if not holder or not skinModel then return false end
    local handle = holder:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not Utils.hcIsBasePart(handle) then return false end
    Utils.hcClearHolder(holder)
    local clone = skinModel:Clone()
    local pp = Utils.hcEnsurePrimary(clone)
    if not Utils.hcIsBasePart(pp) then
        pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end)
        return false
    end
    Utils.hcPrepParts(clone)
    for _, p in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            local n = string.lower(p.Name)
            if n == "hitbox" or n:find("hitbox", 1, true) then
                p.Transparency = 1
                p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                p.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end
    clone.Name = "HCSkinClone"
    clone.Parent = holder
    pcall(function()
        clone:PivotTo(handle.CFrame)
    end)
    -- Weld every part to the primary part so nothing falls off on equip
    for _, p in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            p.Anchored = false
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.Massless = true
            if p ~= pp then
                -- remove loose constraints that don't keep parts on the skin
                for _, c in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("WeldConstraint") and c.Name == "HCSkinInternalWeld" then
                        pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
                local iw = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
                iw.Name = "HCSkinInternalWeld"
                iw.Part0 = pp
                iw.Part1 = p
                iw.Parent = pp
            end
        end
    end
    local w = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    w.Name = "HCSkinWeld"
    w.Part0 = handle
    w.Part1 = pp
    w.Parent = handle
    -- re-assert weld each equip in case the tool rebuilds
    pcall(function()
        holder:SetAttribute("HCSkin", skinName or "")
    end)
    Utils.hcHideOriginals(holder, true)
    return true
end

function Utils.hcGetWrapModel(weaponName, skinName)
    if not skinName or skinName == "" then return nil end
    local wraps = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Wraps")
    if not wraps then return nil end
    local folder = wraps:FindFirstChild("[" .. weaponName .. "]") or wraps:FindFirstChild(weaponName)
    if not folder then return nil end
    return folder:FindFirstChild(skinName)
end

function Utils.hcGetKnifeModel(skinName)
    if not skinName or skinName == "" then return nil end
    local knives = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Knives")
    if not knives then return nil end
    return knives:FindFirstChild(skinName)
end

function Utils.hcWeaponFromTool(tool)
    if not tool then return nil end
    local n = tool.Name
    local plain = n:match("^%[(.+)%]$") or n
    for _, w in ipairs(Utils.hcSkins.order) do
        if plain:lower() == w:lower() or n:lower() == ("[" .. w .. "]"):lower() then
            return w
        end
    end
    -- common aliases
    local compact = plain:gsub("[%s%-_]", ""):lower()
    if compact == "doublebarrel" or compact == "db" then return "DoubleBarrel" end
    if compact == "tacticalshotgun" or compact == "tact" then return "TacticalShotgun" end
    if compact == "silencer" then return "Silencer" end
    if compact == "smg" then return "SMG" end
    return nil
end

function Utils.hcScan()
    local wraps = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Wraps")
    local total = 0
    for _, weaponName in ipairs(Utils.hcSkins.order) do
        local list = {}
        if weaponName == "Knife" then
            local knives = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Knives")
            if knives then
                for _, ch in ipairs(knives:GetChildren()) do
                    table.insert(list, ch.Name)
                end
            end
        elseif wraps then
            local folder = wraps:FindFirstChild("[" .. weaponName .. "]") or wraps:FindFirstChild(weaponName)
            if folder then
                for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    table.insert(list, ch.Name)
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(list)
        Utils.hcSkins.scanned[weaponName] = list
        total = total + #list
    end
    Utils.hcSkins.lastStatus = string.format("Scanned %d HC skins", total)
    local types = {}
    local seen = {}
    local function addType(name)
        name = tostring(name or "")
        if name == "" or string.lower(name) == "none" then return end
        local k = string.lower(name)
        if seen[k] then return end
        seen[k] = true
        table.insert(types, name)
    end
    local beamsFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BulletBeams")
    if beamsFolder then
        for _, ch in ipairs(beamsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            addType(ch.Name)
        end
    end
    table.sort(types)
    Utils.hcSkins.bulletTypes = types
    return total > 0, Utils.hcSkins.lastStatus
end

function Utils.hcFindTemplateBeam(skinName)
    local folder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BulletBeams")
    if not folder or not skinName or skinName == "" then return nil end
    local pack = folder:FindFirstChild(skinName)
    if not pack then
        for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if string.lower(ch.Name) == string.lower(skinName) then
                pack = ch
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not pack then return nil end
    if pack:IsA("Beam") then return pack end
    return pack:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Beam", true)
end

function Utils.hcDesiredBulletName()
    local char = Player.Character
    local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    local weaponName = Utils.hcWeaponFromTool(tool)
    if not weaponName then return "" end
    return Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName] or ""
end

function Utils.hcCopyBeam(dst, src)
    if not dst or not src then return end
    pcall(function()
        dst.Texture = src.Texture
        dst.Color = src.Color
        dst.TextureSpeed = src.TextureSpeed
        dst.LightEmission = math.max(tonumber(src.LightEmission) or 0, 0.6)
        dst.LightInfluence = src.LightInfluence
        dst.FaceCamera = true
        -- force visible transparency (never fully invisible)
        local t0 = 0.15
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(src.Transparency) == "NumberSequence" then
                local kps = src.Transparency.Keypoints
                if kps and #kps > 0 then
                    t0 = math.min(kps[1].Value, 0.35)
                end
            elseif type(src.Transparency) == "number" then
                t0 = math.min(src.Transparency, 0.35)
            end
        end)
        dst.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(math.clamp(t0, 0, 0.4))
        dst.TextureLength = (tonumber(src.TextureLength) and src.TextureLength > 0) and src.TextureLength or 2
        dst.Segments = math.max(tonumber(dst.Segments) or 1, tonumber(src.Segments) or 1, 12)
        dst.CurveSize0 = src.CurveSize0
        dst.CurveSize1 = src.CurveSize1
        -- force usable width so the beam is not a single pixel
        local srcW0 = tonumber(src.Width0) or 0
        local srcW1 = tonumber(src.Width1) or 0
        local w0 = srcW0 > 0.05 and srcW0 or 0.45
        local w1 = srcW1 > 0.05 and srcW1 or 0.35
        dst.Width0 = math.max(w0, 0.35)
        dst.Width1 = math.max(w1, 0.25)
        dst.Enabled = true
    end)
end

function Utils.hcFindBulletPack(skinName)
    local folder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BulletBeams")
    if not folder or not skinName or skinName == "" then return nil end
    local pack = folder:FindFirstChild(skinName)
    if pack then return pack end
    for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
        if string.lower(ch.Name) == string.lower(skinName) then
            return ch
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function Utils.hcCollectClass(root, className)
    local out = {}
    if not root then return out end
    if root:IsA(className) then table.insert(out, root) end
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA(className) then table.insert(out, d) end
    end
    return out
end

function Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(obj)
    if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled or not obj then return end
    if obj.Name ~= "BULLET_RAYS" then return end
    local desired = Utils.hcDesiredBulletName()
    if desired == "" or string.lower(desired) == "none" then return end
    local pack = Utils.hcFindBulletPack(desired)
    if not pack then
        -- fallback: template beam only
        local template = Utils.hcFindTemplateBeam(desired)
        if not template then return end
        pack = template
    end
    local srcBeams = Utils.hcCollectClass(pack, "Beam")
    if #srcBeams == 0 and pack:IsA("Beam") then
        srcBeams = { pack }
    end
    local dstBeams = Utils.hcCollectClass(obj, "Beam")
    if #srcBeams > 0 then
        for i, dst in ipairs(dstBeams) do
            Utils.hcCopyBeam(dst, srcBeams[((i - 1) % #srcBeams) + 1])
            -- if the live beam is essentially a point (attachments almost overlapping), stretch width more
            pcall(function()
                dst.Width0 = math.max(dst.Width0, 0.4)
                dst.Width1 = math.max(dst.Width1, 0.3)
                dst.FaceCamera = true
                dst.Enabled = true
            end)
        end
        if #dstBeams == 0 then
            local host = obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if not host then
                host = Instance.new("Part")
                host.Name = "RaysBulletHost"
                host.Anchored = true
                host.CanCollide = false
                host.Transparency = 1
                host.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
                host.CFrame = CFrame.new(obj:GetPivot().Position)
                host.Parent = obj
            end
            local src = srcBeams[1]
            local clone = src:Clone()
            clone.Parent = host
            Utils.hcCopyBeam(clone, src)
            pcall(function()
                if not clone.Attachment0 then
                    local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                    a0.Parent = host
                    clone.Attachment0 = a0
                end
                if not clone.Attachment1 then
                    local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                    a1.Parent = host
                    a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, -8)
                    clone.Attachment1 = a1
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    local srcParts = Utils.hcCollectClass(pack, "BasePart")
    local dstParts = Utils.hcCollectClass(obj, "BasePart")
    local srcPart = srcParts[1]
    for _, dst in ipairs(dstParts) do
        if srcPart then
            pcall(function()
                dst.Color = srcPart.Color
                dst.Material = srcPart.Material
                if srcPart.Transparency < 1 then
                    dst.Transparency = srcPart.Transparency
                end
            end)
        end
        for _, className in ipairs({ "ParticleEmitter", "Trail", "PointLight", "SpotLight", "SurfaceLight" }) do
            if #Utils.hcCollectClass(dst, className) == 0 then
                for _, srcFx in ipairs(Utils.hcCollectClass(pack, className)) do
                    pcall(function()
