                        srcFx:Clone().Parent = dst
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function Utils.hcStyleLiveBeam(beam)
    if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled or not beam or not beam:IsA("Beam") then return end
    local okRS, RS = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
    if okRS and RS and beam:IsDescendantOf(RS) then return end
    local parent = beam
    while parent and parent ~= workspace do
        if parent.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then
            Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(parent)
            return
        end
        parent = parent.Parent
    end
    local desired = Utils.hcDesiredBulletName()
    if desired == "" or string.lower(desired) == "none" then return end
    local template = Utils.hcFindTemplateBeam(desired)
    if template then
        Utils.hcCopyBeam(beam, template)
        pcall(function()
            beam.Width0 = math.max(beam.Width0, 0.4)
            beam.Width1 = math.max(beam.Width1, 0.3)
            beam.FaceCamera = true
            beam.Enabled = true
        end)
    end
end

function Utils.hcHookIgnoredFolder(folder)
    if not folder or Utils.hcSkins.ignoredHooked == folder then return end
    Utils.hcSkins.ignoredHooked = folder
    Utils.track(folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
        if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled then return end
        task.defer(function()
            if ch.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then
                Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(ch)
            else
                local hit = ch:FindFirstChild("BULLET_RAYS", true)
                if hit then Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(hit) end
                if ch:IsA("Beam") then Utils.hcStyleLiveBeam(ch) end
            end
        end)
    end))
    Utils.track(folder.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled then return end
        if d.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then
            task.defer(function() Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(d) end)
        elseif d:IsA("Beam") then
            task.defer(function() Utils.hcStyleLiveBeam(d) end)
        end
    end))
end

function Utils.hcWatchLiveBullets()
    if Utils.hcSkins.bulletWatch then return end
    Utils.hcSkins.bulletWatch = true
    local ignored = workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
    if ignored then Utils.hcHookIgnoredFolder(ignored) end
    Utils.track(workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
        if ch.Name == "Ignored" then
            Utils.hcHookIgnoredFolder(ch)
        elseif ch.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then
            task.defer(function() Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(ch) end)
        end
    end))
    Utils.track(workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled then return end
        if obj.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then
            task.defer(function() Utils.hcStyleBulletRay(obj) end)
        elseif obj:IsA("Beam") then
            task.defer(function() Utils.hcStyleLiveBeam(obj) end)
        end
    end))
end

function Utils.hcApplyBullets(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return end
    local weaponName = Utils.hcWeaponFromTool(tool)
    if not weaponName then return end
    local desired = Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName] or ""
    local template = nil
    if Utils.hcSkins.enabled and desired ~= "" then
        template = Utils.hcFindTemplateBeam(desired)
    end
    for _, b in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if b:IsA("Beam") then
            if not Utils.hcSkins.beamBackup[b] then
                Utils.hcSkins.beamBackup[b] = {
                    Texture = b.Texture,
                    Color = b.Color,
                    Width0 = b.Width0,
                    Width1 = b.Width1,
                    TextureSpeed = b.TextureSpeed,
                    LightEmission = b.LightEmission,
                    LightInfluence = b.LightInfluence,
                    FaceCamera = b.FaceCamera,
                    Transparency = b.Transparency,
                }
            end
            if template then
                Utils.hcCopyBeam(b, template)
            else
                local bak = Utils.hcSkins.beamBackup[b]
                if bak then
                    pcall(function()
                        b.Texture = bak.Texture
                        b.Color = bak.Color
                        b.Width0 = bak.Width0
                        b.Width1 = bak.Width1
                        b.TextureSpeed = bak.TextureSpeed
                        b.LightEmission = bak.LightEmission
                        b.LightInfluence = bak.LightInfluence
                        b.FaceCamera = bak.FaceCamera
                        b.Transparency = bak.Transparency
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function Utils.hcApplyTool(tool)
    if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled or not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return end
    local weaponName = Utils.hcWeaponFromTool(tool)
    if not weaponName then return end
    local skinName = Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName] or ""
    if skinName == "" then
        Utils.hcClearHolder(tool)
    else
        local model
        if weaponName == "Knife" then
            model = Utils.hcGetKnifeModel(skinName)
        else
            model = Utils.hcGetWrapModel(weaponName, skinName)
        end
        if model then
            Utils.hcApplyModel(tool, model, skinName)
        end
    end
    Utils.hcApplyBullets(tool)
end

function Utils.hcApplyHandles(char)
    if not Utils.hcSkins.enabled or not char then return end
    for handleName, weaponName in pairs(Utils.hcSkins.handleMap) do
        local skinName = Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName]
        if skinName and skinName ~= "" then
            local holder = char:FindFirstChild(handleName)
            if holder then
                local model = Utils.hcGetWrapModel(weaponName, skinName)
                if model then
                    Utils.hcApplyModel(holder, model, skinName)
                end
            end
        else
            local holder = char:FindFirstChild(handleName)
            if holder then Utils.hcClearHolder(holder) end
        end
    end
end

function Utils.hcOnTool(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return end
    if not Utils.hcSkins.processed[tool] then
        Utils.hcSkins.processed[tool] = true
        Utils.track(tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
            task.defer(function()
                if Utils.hcSkins.enabled then Utils.hcApplyTool(tool) end
            end)
        end))
    end
    Utils.hcApplyTool(tool)
end

function Utils.hcRefreshAll()
    local char = Player.Character
    local bp = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    for _, container in ipairs({ char, bp }) do
        if container then
            for _, t in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if t:IsA("Tool") then
                    Utils.hcOnTool(t)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if char then Utils.hcApplyHandles(char) end
end

function Utils.hcConnectChar(char)
    if not char then return end
    Utils.track(char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            Utils.hcOnTool(child)
        elseif Utils.hcSkins.handleMap[child.Name] then
            Utils.hcApplyHandles(char)
        end
    end))
    Utils.hcApplyHandles(char)
    for _, t in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if t:IsA("Tool") then Utils.hcOnTool(t) end
    end
end

function Utils.hcStart()
    if Utils.hcSkins.started then return end
    Utils.hcSkins.started = true
    Utils.hcSkins.loopId = (Utils.hcSkins.loopId or 0) + 1
    local loopId = Utils.hcSkins.loopId
    Utils.track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if loopId ~= Utils.hcSkins.loopId or not Utils.hcSkins.enabled then return end
        local char = Player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if tool and tool:FindFirstChild("HCSkinClone") then
            Utils.hcHideOriginals(tool, true)
        end
    end))
    local bp = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or Player:WaitForChild("Backpack", 5)
    if bp then
        Utils.track(bp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if ch:IsA("Tool") then Utils.hcOnTool(ch) end
        end))
        for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if t:IsA("Tool") then Utils.hcOnTool(t) end
        end
    end
    if Player.Character then Utils.hcConnectChar(Player.Character) end
    Utils.track(Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.2)
        Utils.hcConnectChar(char)
        if Utils.hcSkins.enabled then Utils.hcRefreshAll() end
    end))
end

function Utils.hcSetEnabled(on)
    Utils.hcSkins.enabled = on and true or false
    if Utils.hcSkins.enabled then
        Utils.hcStart()
        Utils.hcScan()
        Utils.hcWatchLiveBullets()
        Utils.hcRefreshAll()
    else
        local char = Player.Character
        local bp = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        for _, container in ipairs({ char, bp }) do
            if container then
                for _, t in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if t:IsA("Tool") then Utils.hcClearHolder(t) end
                end
            end
        end
        if char then
            for handleName in pairs(Utils.hcSkins.handleMap) do
                local h = char:FindFirstChild(handleName)
                if h then Utils.hcClearHolder(h) end
            end
        end
        Utils.hcSkins.lastStatus = "HC Skin Changer off"
        Utils.hcRestoreGunVisuals()
    end
end

pcall(Utils.hcRestoreGunVisuals)

--// Fullbright
Utils.applyFullbright = function(on)
    if on then
        if not fullbrightActive then
            fbClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime
            fbBrightness = Lighting.Brightness
            fbFogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
            fbGlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
            fbAmbient = Lighting.Ambient
        end
        fullbrightActive = true
        if not forceTimeActive then
            Lighting.ClockTime = 14
        end
        Lighting.Brightness = 2
        Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
    else
        fullbrightActive = false
        if not forceTimeActive and fbClockTime ~= nil then Lighting.ClockTime = fbClockTime end
        if fbBrightness ~= nil then Lighting.Brightness = fbBrightness end
        if fbFogEnd ~= nil then Lighting.FogEnd = fbFogEnd end
        if fbGlobalShadows ~= nil then Lighting.GlobalShadows = fbGlobalShadows end
        if fbAmbient ~= nil then Lighting.Ambient = fbAmbient end
        if forceTimeActive then Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue end
        -- respect other lighting toggles if active
        if brightnessActive then Lighting.Brightness = currentBrightness end
        if fogActive then
            Lighting.FogEnd = currentFogEnd
            Lighting.FogStart = currentFogStart
            Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(currentR, currentG, currentB)
        end
        if ambienceActive then
            Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(ambR, ambG, ambB)
        end
    end
end

Utils.realLight = Utils.realLight or { used = false }
Utils.applyRealisticLighting = function()
    if Utils.realLight.used then return false end
    Utils.realLight.used = true
    Lighting.Brightness = 2.35
    Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(92, 96, 108)
    Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 132, 140)
    Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 180)
    Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(70, 80, 95)
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 1
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 1
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
    Lighting.ClockTime = 15.4
    Lighting.GeographicLatitude = 25
    local function fx(className, name, props)
        local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
        if not e then
            e = Instance.new(className)
            e.Name = name
            e.Parent = Lighting
        end
        for k, v in pairs(props) do
            pcall(function() e[k] = v end)
        end
        e.Enabled = true
    end
    fx("ColorCorrectionEffect", "RaysRealisticCC", {
        Brightness = 0.02, Contrast = 0.18, Saturation = 0.12,
        TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 248, 240),
    })
    fx("BloomEffect", "RaysRealisticBloom", { Intensity = 0.35, Size = 22, Threshold = 0.92 })
    fx("SunRaysEffect", "RaysRealisticSun", { Intensity = 0.12, Spread = 0.65 })
    local atmo = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
    if not atmo then
        atmo = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atmo.Name = "RaysRealisticAtmo"
        atmo.Parent = Lighting
    end
    atmo.Density = 0.28
    atmo.Offset = 0.12
    atmo.Glare = 0.35
    atmo.Haze = 1.1
    atmo.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 210, 220)
    return true
end

--// Eject script
Utils.ejectScript = function()
    Utils.afkSpoofActive = false
    skinChanger.enabled = false
    Utils.hcSkins.enabled = false
    Utils.hcSkins.loopId = (Utils.hcSkins.loopId or 0) + 1
    pcall(Utils.hcRestoreGunVisuals)
    pcall(destroyCustomCursor)

    Utils.playSound(SoundIds.Eject, 0.5)
    Utils.updateHitboxState(false)
    Utils.godStop()
    if camlockFovCircleObj then
        pcall(function() camlockFovCircleObj:Remove() end)
        camlockFovCircleObj = nil
    end
    if fullbrightActive then Utils.applyFullbright(false) end
    -- realistic lighting is one-shot until rejoin; do not undo on eject
    if worldBlur then
        pcall(function() worldBlur:Destroy() end)
        worldBlur = nil
    end
    forceTimeActive = false
    pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = originalClockTime end)
    Lighting.FogEnd = originalFogEnd
    Lighting.FogStart = originalFogStart
    Lighting.FogColor = originalFogColor
    Lighting.Ambient = originalAmbient
    Lighting.Brightness = originalBrightness
    workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = originalFov
    unlockPlayerZoom()
    camlock.zoomAim = false
    clearAllESP()
    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
    if hum then
        hum.WalkSpeed = originalWalkSpeed
        hum.JumpPower = originalJumpPower
    end
    jumpPowerActive = false
    infiniteJumpActive = false
    speedActive = false
    if Utils.grav then
        Utils.grav.active = false
        pcall(function()
            workspace.Gravity = Utils.grav.original or 196.2
        end)
    end
    Utils.setSpinbot(false)
    noclipActive = false
    applyTrail(false)
    setAuraEnabled(false)
    Utils.clearActiveAura()
    Utils.applyHeadless(false)
    Utils.stopFly()
    for _, c in ipairs(trackedConns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
    trackedConns = {}
    task.delay(0.3, function()
        pcall(function() ScreenGui:Destroy() end)
    end)
end

--// CONFIG SYSTEM (named profiles)
local Config = {
    folder = "RaysPanel",
    configsFolder = "RaysPanel/configs",
    legacyFile = "RaysPanel/config.json",
    lastProfileFile = "RaysPanel/last_profile.txt",
    activeName = "default",
    version = 3,
    lastStatus = nil,
    lastStatusGood = false,
    runtimeFullbright = false,
}

function Config.sanitizeName(name)
    name = tostring(name or "")
    name = name:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    name = name:gsub("[^%w_%-%s]", "")
    name = name:gsub("%s+", "_")
    if name == "" then name = "default" end
    return name:sub(1, 40)
end

function Config.pathFor(name)
    return Config.configsFolder .. "/" .. Config.sanitizeName(name) .. ".json"
end

function Config.ensureFolders()
    if type(makefolder) ~= "function" then return end
    pcall(function() makefolder(Config.folder) end)
    pcall(function() makefolder(Config.configsFolder) end)
end

function Config.rememberProfile(name)
    name = Config.sanitizeName(name)
    Config.activeName = name
    if type(writefile) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            Config.ensureFolders()
            writefile(Config.lastProfileFile, name)
        end)
    end
    return name
end

function Config.getRememberedProfile()
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return Config.activeName end
    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        if type(isfile) == "function" then
            if not isfile(Config.lastProfileFile) then return nil end
        end
        return readfile(Config.lastProfileFile)
    end)
    if ok and type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
        return Config.sanitizeName(value)
    end
    return Config.activeName
end

function Config.listProfiles()
    local names, seen = {}, {}
    local function addName(name)
        name = Config.sanitizeName(name)
        if not seen[name] then
            seen[name] = true
            table.insert(names, name)
        end
    end
    if type(isfile) == "function" then
        local okLegacy, hasLegacy = pcall(isfile, Config.legacyFile)
        local okDefault, hasDefault = pcall(isfile, Config.pathFor("default"))
        if okLegacy and hasLegacy and not (okDefault and hasDefault) then
            addName("default")
        end
    end
    if type(listfiles) ~= "function" then
        table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        return names, "Executor does not support listfiles"
    end
    Config.ensureFolders()
    local ok, filesOrErr = pcall(function()
        return listfiles(Config.configsFolder)
    end)
    if not ok or type(filesOrErr) ~= "table" then
        table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
        return names, tostring(filesOrErr or "Could not list configs")
    end
    for _, path in ipairs(filesOrErr) do
        if type(path) == "string" then
            local fileName = path:gsub("\\", "/"):match("([^/]+)$")
            if fileName and fileName:lower():sub(-5) == ".json" then
                addName(fileName:sub(1, -6))
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(names, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
    return names
end

function Config.pick(value, fallback)
    if value == nil then return fallback end
    return value
end

function Config.colorToTable(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return {255, 255, 255} end
    return {
        math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5),
        math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5),
        math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5),
    }
end

function Config.tableToColor(value, fallback)
    if type(value) ~= "table" then return fallback end
    return Color3.fromRGB(
        math.clamp(tonumber(value[1]) or 255, 0, 255),
        math.clamp(tonumber(value[2]) or 255, 0, 255),
        math.clamp(tonumber(value[3]) or 255, 0, 255)
    )
end

function Config.bindToTable(bind)
    if not bind then return nil end
    local ok, enumType = pcall(function() return bind.EnumType end)
    if not ok or not enumType then return nil end
    if enumType == Enum.KeyCode then
        return {kind = "KeyCode", name = bind.Name}
    end
    if enumType == Enum.UserInputType then
        return {kind = "UserInputType", name = bind.Name}
    end
    return nil
end

function Config.tableToBind(value, fallback)
    if type(value) ~= "table" or type(value.name) ~= "string" then return fallback end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        if value.kind == "KeyCode" then
            return Enum.KeyCode[value.name]
        elseif value.kind == "UserInputType" then
            return Enum.UserInputType[value.name]
        end
    end)
    if ok and result then return result end
    return fallback
end

function Config.deepCopy(value)
    if type(value) ~= "table" then return value end
    local out = {}
    for k, v in pairs(value) do
        out[Config.deepCopy(k)] = Config.deepCopy(v)
    end
    return out
end

function Config.supported()
    return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function"
end

function Config.setStatus(text, good)
    Config.lastStatus = tostring(text)
    Config.lastStatusGood = good and true or false
    if R.configStatusLbl and R.configStatusLbl.Parent then
        R.configStatusLbl.Text = Config.lastStatus
        R.configStatusLbl.TextColor3 = Config.lastStatusGood and Theme.Accent or Theme.TextDark
    end
end

function Config.capture()
    return {
        version = Config.version,
        fpsCap = fpsCap,
        settings = {
            fontSize = currentFontSize,
            hideKeybind = Config.bindToTable(hideKeybind),
            theme = currentThemeName,
            panelBgVisible = panelBgVisible,
        },
        aim = {
            silent = {
                enabled = silentAim.enabled,
                fov = silentAim.fov,
                part = silentAim.part,
                keybind = Config.bindToTable(silentAim.keybind),
                holdMode = silentAim.holdMode,
                bindMode = silentAim.bindMode,
                fovCircle = silentAim.fovCircle,
                tracer = silentAim.tracer,
                predictionEnabled = silentAim.predictionEnabled,
                prediction = silentAim.prediction,
                healthCheck = silentAim.healthCheck,
                neverMiss = silentAim.neverMiss,
                hitChance = silentAim.hitChance,
                bulletSpread = silentAim.bulletSpread,
                wallCheck = silentAim.wallCheck,
                visualizer = silentAim.visualizer,
                visualizerColor = Config.colorToTable(silentAim.visualizerColor),
                circleColor = Config.colorToTable(silentAim.circleColor),
            },
            forceHit = {
                enabled = Utils.forceHit.enabled,
                fov = Utils.forceHit.fov,
                tpMode = Utils.forceHit.tpMode,
                tpReturn = Utils.forceHit.tpReturn,
                tpReturnDelay = Utils.forceHit.tpReturnDelay,
                bulletVizType = Utils.forceHit.bulletVizType,
            },
            camlock = {
                enabled = camlock.enabled,
                fov = camlock.fov,
                smoothness = camlock.smoothness,
                ease = camlock.ease,
                smoothMode = camlock.smoothMode,
                prediction = camlock.prediction,
                predictionEnabled = camlock.predictionEnabled,
                predictionX = camlock.predictionX,
                predictionY = camlock.predictionY,
                fovCircle = camlock.fovCircle,
                circleColor = Config.colorToTable(camlock.circleColor),
                keybind = Config.bindToTable(camlock.keybind),
                sticky = camlock.sticky,
                holdMode = camlock.holdMode,
                bindMode = camlock.bindMode,
                lockPart = camlock.lockPart,
            },
        },
        visual = {
            esp = {
                enabled = espActive,
                names = espShowNames,
                distance = espShowDistance,
                box = espShowBox,
                health = espShowHealth,
                color = {espR, espG, espB},
            },
            hitbox = {
                enabled = hitboxActive,
                visible = hitboxVisible,
                x = hbXSize,
                y = hbYSize,
                z = hbZSize,
                keybind = Config.bindToTable(selectedKeybind),
            },
            fog = {
                enabled = fogActive,
                start = currentFogStart,
                finish = currentFogEnd,
                color = {currentR, currentG, currentB},
            },
            ambience = {
                enabled = ambienceActive,
                color = {ambR, ambG, ambB},
            },
            brightness = {
                enabled = brightnessActive,
                value = currentBrightness,
            },
            fullbright = fullbrightActive,
            fov = {
                enabled = fovActive,
                value = currentFovValue,
            },
            trail = {
                enabled = trail.active,
                color1 = Config.colorToTable(trail.color1),
                color2 = Config.colorToTable(trail.color2),
                lifetime = trail.lifetime,
                width = trail.width,
                lightEmission = trail.lightEmission,
                brightness = trail.brightness,
            },
            aura = {
                enabled = aura.enabled,
                selected = aura.selected,
            },
            headless = headless.active,
            cursor = {
                enabled = customCursor.enabled,
                text = customCursor.text,
                refreshrate = customCursor.refreshrate,
                mode = customCursor.mode,
                width = customCursor.width,
                length = customCursor.length,
                radius = customCursor.radius,
                color = Config.colorToTable(customCursor.color),
                spin = customCursor.spin,
                spin_speed = customCursor.spin_speed,
                spin_max = customCursor.spin_max,
                resize = customCursor.resize,
                resize_speed = customCursor.resize_speed,
                resize_min = customCursor.resize_min,
                resize_max = customCursor.resize_max,
                hideDefault = customCursor.hideDefault,
            },
        },
        movement = {
            speed = {
                enabled = speedActive,
                value = currentSpeedValue,
                keybind = Config.bindToTable(speedKeybind),
                cframe = speedUseCFrame,
            },
            spinbot = {
                enabled = spinbotActive,
                speed = spinbotSpeed,
                keybind = Config.bindToTable(spinKeybind),
            },
            noclip = noclipActive,
            jump = {
                enabled = jumpPowerActive,
                power = currentJumpPower,
                infinite = infiniteJumpActive,
            },
            fly = {
                enabled = flyActive,
                speed = currentFlySpeed,
                keybind = Config.bindToTable(flyKeybind),
            },
        },
        misc = {
            godMode = godModeActive,
            afkSpoof = Utils.afkSpoofActive,
            accessories = accessoryState and Config.deepCopy(accessoryState.savedIds) or {},
        },
        skins = {
            enabled = skinChanger.enabled,
            selected = Config.deepCopy(skinChanger.selected),
        },
    }
end

function Config.applyData(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return false, "Invalid config" end
    fpsCap = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(data.fpsCap) or fpsCap), 1, 2000)
    local settings = data.settings or {}
    currentFontSize = math.clamp(tonumber(settings.fontSize) or currentFontSize, 0.5, 2.0)
    hideKeybind = Config.tableToBind(settings.hideKeybind, hideKeybind)
    if settings.panelBgVisible ~= nil then
        panelBgVisible = settings.panelBgVisible and true or false
    end
    if type(settings.theme) == "string" and ThemePresets[settings.theme] then
        pcall(function() Utils.applyTheme(settings.theme) end)
    end
    pcall(Utils.updatePanelBgImage)

    local aim = data.aim or {}
    local silent = aim.silent or {}
    silentAim.enabled = Config.pick(silent.enabled, false)
    silentAim.fov = math.clamp(tonumber(silent.fov) or silentAim.fov, 1, 1000)
    silentAim.part = Config.pick(silent.part, silentAim.part)
    silentAim.keybind = Config.tableToBind(silent.keybind, silentAim.keybind)
    silentAim.holdMode = Config.pick(silent.holdMode, silentAim.holdMode)
    silentAim.bindMode = Config.pick(silent.bindMode, silentAim.bindMode)
    silentAim.fovCircle = Config.pick(silent.fovCircle, silentAim.fovCircle)
    silentAim.tracer = Config.pick(silent.tracer, silentAim.tracer)
    silentAim.predictionEnabled = Config.pick(silent.predictionEnabled, silentAim.predictionEnabled)
    silentAim.prediction = tonumber(silent.prediction) or silentAim.prediction
    silentAim.healthCheck = Config.pick(silent.healthCheck, silentAim.healthCheck)
    silentAim.neverMiss = Config.pick(silent.neverMiss, silentAim.neverMiss)
    silentAim.hitChance = math.clamp(tonumber(silent.hitChance) or silentAim.hitChance or 100, 0, 100)
    silentAim.bulletSpread = tonumber(silent.bulletSpread) or silentAim.bulletSpread
    silentAim.wallCheck = Config.pick(silent.wallCheck, silentAim.wallCheck)
    silentAim.visualizer = Config.pick(silent.visualizer, silentAim.visualizer)
    silentAim.visualizerColor = Config.tableToColor(silent.visualizerColor, silentAim.visualizerColor)
    silentAim.circleColor = Config.tableToColor(silent.circleColor, silentAim.circleColor)

    local fh = aim.forceHit or {}
    if Utils.forceHit then
        Utils.forceHit.enabled = Config.pick(fh.enabled, false)
        Utils.forceHit.fov = math.clamp(tonumber(fh.fov) or Utils.forceHit.fov or 100, 10, 1000)
        Utils.forceHit.tpMode = Config.pick(fh.tpMode, Utils.forceHit.tpMode)
        Utils.forceHit.tpReturn = Config.pick(fh.tpReturn, Utils.forceHit.tpReturn)
        Utils.forceHit.tpReturnDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(fh.tpReturnDelay) or Utils.forceHit.tpReturnDelay or 0.145, 0.145, 10)
        if fh.bulletVizType then Utils.forceHit.bulletVizType = tostring(fh.bulletVizType) end
    end

    local c = aim.camlock or {}
    camlock.enabled = Config.pick(c.enabled, camlock.enabled)
    camlock.fov = math.clamp(tonumber(c.fov) or camlock.fov, 1, 1000)
    camlock.smoothness = tonumber(c.smoothness) or camlock.smoothness
    camlock.ease = tonumber(c.ease) or camlock.ease
    camlock.smoothMode = Config.pick(c.smoothMode, camlock.smoothMode)
    camlock.prediction = tonumber(c.prediction) or camlock.prediction
    camlock.predictionEnabled = Config.pick(c.predictionEnabled, camlock.predictionEnabled)
    camlock.predictionX = tonumber(c.predictionX) or camlock.predictionX
    camlock.predictionY = tonumber(c.predictionY) or camlock.predictionY
    camlock.fovCircle = Config.pick(c.fovCircle, camlock.fovCircle)
    camlock.circleColor = Config.tableToColor(c.circleColor, camlock.circleColor)
    camlock.keybind = Config.tableToBind(c.keybind, camlock.keybind)
    -- never keep accidental default Z
    if camlock.keybind == Enum.KeyCode.Z then
        camlock.keybind = nil
    end
    camlock.sticky = Config.pick(c.sticky, camlock.sticky)
    camlock.holdMode = Config.pick(c.holdMode, camlock.holdMode)
    camlock.bindMode = Config.pick(c.bindMode, camlock.bindMode)
    camlock.lockPart = Config.pick(c.lockPart, camlock.lockPart)

    local visual = data.visual or {}
    local esp = visual.esp or {}
    espActive = false
    espShowNames = false
    espShowDistance = false
    espShowBox = false
    espShowHealth = false
    if type(esp.color) == "table" then
        espR = math.clamp(tonumber(esp.color[1]) or espR, 0, 255)
        espG = math.clamp(tonumber(esp.color[2]) or espG, 0, 255)
        espB = math.clamp(tonumber(esp.color[3]) or espB, 0, 255)
    end

    local hb = visual.hitbox or {}
    hitboxActive = Config.pick(hb.enabled, hitboxActive)
    hitboxVisible = Config.pick(hb.visible, hitboxVisible)
    hbXSize = math.clamp(tonumber(hb.x) or hbXSize, 1, 25)
    hbYSize = math.clamp(tonumber(hb.y) or hbYSize, 1, 25)
    hbZSize = math.clamp(tonumber(hb.z) or hbZSize, 1, 25)
    selectedKeybind = Config.tableToBind(hb.keybind, selectedKeybind)

    local fog = visual.fog or {}
    -- Never auto-enable fog from configs (prevents pink fog on load / game select)
    fogActive = false
    currentFogStart = math.clamp(tonumber(fog.start) or currentFogStart, 0, 2000)
    currentFogEnd = math.clamp(tonumber(fog.finish) or currentFogEnd, 0, 100000)
    if type(fog.color) == "table" then
        currentR = math.clamp(tonumber(fog.color[1]) or currentR, 0, 255)
        currentG = math.clamp(tonumber(fog.color[2]) or currentG, 0, 255)
        currentB = math.clamp(tonumber(fog.color[3]) or currentB, 0, 255)
    end

    local ambience = visual.ambience or {}
    ambienceActive = Config.pick(ambience.enabled, ambienceActive)
    if type(ambience.color) == "table" then
        ambR = math.clamp(tonumber(ambience.color[1]) or ambR, 0, 255)
        ambG = math.clamp(tonumber(ambience.color[2]) or ambG, 0, 255)
        ambB = math.clamp(tonumber(ambience.color[3]) or ambB, 0, 255)
    end

    local brightness = visual.brightness or {}
    brightnessActive = Config.pick(brightness.enabled, brightnessActive)
    currentBrightness = tonumber(brightness.value) or currentBrightness
    fullbrightActive = false -- never auto from config

    local fov = visual.fov or {}
    fovActive = Config.pick(fov.enabled, fovActive)
    currentFovValue = tonumber(fov.value) or currentFovValue

    local tr = visual.trail or {}
    trail.active = Config.pick(tr.enabled, trail.active)
    trail.color1 = Config.tableToColor(tr.color1, trail.color1)
    trail.color2 = Config.tableToColor(tr.color2, trail.color2)
    trail.lifetime = tonumber(tr.lifetime) or trail.lifetime
    trail.width = tonumber(tr.width) or trail.width
    trail.lightEmission = tonumber(tr.lightEmission) or trail.lightEmission
    trail.brightness = tonumber(tr.brightness) or trail.brightness

    local au = visual.aura or {}
    aura.enabled = Config.pick(au.enabled, aura.enabled)
    aura.selected = Config.pick(au.selected, aura.selected)
    headless.active = Config.pick(visual.headless, headless.active)

    local cursor = visual.cursor or {}
    customCursor.enabled = Config.pick(cursor.enabled, customCursor.enabled)
    customCursor.text = Config.pick(cursor.text, customCursor.text)
    customCursor.refreshrate = tonumber(cursor.refreshrate) or customCursor.refreshrate
    customCursor.mode = Config.pick(cursor.mode, customCursor.mode)
    customCursor.width = tonumber(cursor.width) or customCursor.width
    customCursor.length = tonumber(cursor.length) or customCursor.length
    customCursor.radius = tonumber(cursor.radius) or customCursor.radius
    customCursor.color = Config.tableToColor(cursor.color, customCursor.color)
    customCursor.spin = Config.pick(cursor.spin, customCursor.spin)
    customCursor.spin_speed = tonumber(cursor.spin_speed) or customCursor.spin_speed
    customCursor.spin_max = tonumber(cursor.spin_max) or customCursor.spin_max
    customCursor.resize = Config.pick(cursor.resize, customCursor.resize)
    customCursor.resize_speed = tonumber(cursor.resize_speed) or customCursor.resize_speed
    customCursor.resize_min = tonumber(cursor.resize_min) or customCursor.resize_min
    customCursor.resize_max = tonumber(cursor.resize_max) or customCursor.resize_max
    customCursor.hideDefault = Config.pick(cursor.hideDefault, customCursor.hideDefault)

    local movement = data.movement or {}
    local speed = movement.speed or {}
    speedActive = Config.pick(speed.enabled, speedActive)
    currentSpeedValue = tonumber(speed.value) or currentSpeedValue
    speedKeybind = Config.tableToBind(speed.keybind, speedKeybind)
    speedUseCFrame = Config.pick(speed.cframe, speedUseCFrame)

    local spin = movement.spinbot or {}
    spinbotActive = Config.pick(spin.enabled, spinbotActive)
    spinbotSpeed = tonumber(spin.speed) or spinbotSpeed
    spinKeybind = Config.tableToBind(spin.keybind, spinKeybind)
    noclipActive = Config.pick(movement.noclip, noclipActive)

    local jump = movement.jump or {}
    jumpPowerActive = Config.pick(jump.enabled, jumpPowerActive)
    currentJumpPower = tonumber(jump.power) or currentJumpPower
    infiniteJumpActive = Config.pick(jump.infinite, infiniteJumpActive)

    local fly = movement.fly or {}
    flyActive = Config.pick(fly.enabled, flyActive)
    currentFlySpeed = tonumber(fly.speed) or currentFlySpeed
    flyKeybind = Config.tableToBind(fly.keybind, flyKeybind)

    local misc = data.misc or {}
    godModeActive = Config.pick(misc.godMode, godModeActive)
    Utils.afkSpoofActive = Config.pick(misc.afkSpoof, Utils.afkSpoofActive)
    if type(misc.accessories) == "table" and accessoryState then
        accessoryState.savedIds = Config.deepCopy(misc.accessories)
    end

    local skins = data.skins or {}
    if skinChanger then
        skinChanger.enabled = Config.pick(skins.enabled, skinChanger.enabled)
        if type(skins.selected) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(skins.selected) do
                skinChanger.selected[k] = v
            end
        end
    end
    return true
end

function Config.applyRuntime()
    pcall(function() if setfpscap then setfpscap(fpsCap) end end)
    pcall(function() Utils.updateHitboxState(hitboxActive) end)
    pcall(function() Utils.setSpinbot(spinbotActive) end)
    pcall(function() applyTrail(trail.active) end)
    pcall(function() setAuraEnabled(aura.enabled) end)
    pcall(function() if Utils.applyHeadless then Utils.applyHeadless(headless.active) end end)
    pcall(function()
        if flyActive then Utils.startFly() else Utils.stopFly() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if godModeActive then Utils.godStart() else Utils.godStop() end
    end)
    pcall(function() Utils.setAfkSpoof(Utils.afkSpoofActive) end)
    pcall(function() Utils.setCustomCursor(customCursor.enabled) end)
    pcall(function()
        if fogActive then
            Lighting.FogStart = currentFogStart
            Lighting.FogEnd = currentFogEnd
            Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(currentR, currentG, currentB)
        else
            if Utils.restoreGameLighting then
                Utils.restoreGameLighting()
            else
                Lighting.FogStart = originalFogStart
                Lighting.FogEnd = originalFogEnd
                Lighting.FogColor = originalFogColor
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.Ambient = ambienceActive and Color3.fromRGB(ambR, ambG, ambB) or originalAmbient
    end)
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.Brightness = brightnessActive and currentBrightness or originalBrightness
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = fovActive and currentFovValue or originalFov
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Utils.applyFullbright then
            local desired = fullbrightActive and true or false
            if desired and not Config.runtimeFullbright then
                fullbrightActive = false
                Utils.applyFullbright(true)
            elseif (not desired) and Config.runtimeFullbright then
                fullbrightActive = true
                Utils.applyFullbright(false)
            else
                Utils.applyFullbright(desired)
            end
            Config.runtimeFullbright = desired
        end
    end)
    pcall(applyFontSize)
    pcall(function()
        if skinChanger.enabled and isDaHood then
            Utils.skinSetEnabled(true)
        else
            Utils.skinSetEnabled(false)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if accessoryState and accessoryState.savedIds and #accessoryState.savedIds > 0 and Utils.reapplySavedAccessories then
            Utils.reapplySavedAccessories()
        end
    end)
end

function Config.save(name)
    if not Config.supported() then
        Config.setStatus("File API unsupported", false)
        return false, "Executor does not support readfile/writefile"
    end
    name = Config.sanitizeName(name or Config.activeName)
    local path = Config.pathFor(name)
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        Config.ensureFolders()
        writefile(path, Utils.HttpService:JSONEncode(Config.capture()))
        Config.rememberProfile(name)
    end)
    if ok then
        Config.setStatus("Saved: " .. name, true)
        return true
    end
    Config.setStatus("Save failed", false)
    return false, tostring(err)
end

function Config.load(name, refreshUI)
    if type(name) == "boolean" and refreshUI == nil then
        refreshUI = name
        name = nil
    end
    if not Config.supported() then
        Config.setStatus("File API unsupported", false)
        return false, "Executor does not support readfile/writefile"
    end
    name = Config.sanitizeName(name or Config.activeName)
    local path = Config.pathFor(name)
    local selectedPath = path
    local exists = true
    if type(isfile) == "function" then
        local okExists, result = pcall(isfile, path)
        exists = okExists and result or false
        if not exists and name == "default" then
            local okLegacy, legacyExists = pcall(isfile, Config.legacyFile)
            if okLegacy and legacyExists then
                selectedPath = Config.legacyFile
                exists = true
            end
        end
    end
    if not exists then
        Config.setStatus("Not found: " .. name, false)
        return false, "No saved config named " .. name
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local decoded = Utils.HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(selectedPath))
        local applied, applyErr = Config.applyData(decoded)
        if not applied then error(applyErr or "Invalid config") end
        Config.applyRuntime()
        Config.rememberProfile(name)
        if selectedPath == Config.legacyFile then
            Config.ensureFolders()
            writefile(path, Utils.HttpService:JSONEncode(Config.capture()))
        end
    end)
    if not ok then
        Config.setStatus("Load failed", false)
        return false, tostring(err)
    end
    Config.setStatus("Loaded: " .. name, true)
    if refreshUI and Builders and Builders.rebuildPageUI then
        task.defer(function()
            Builders.rebuildPageUI(currentActiveTab or "SETTINGS")
        end)
    end
    return true
end

function Config.delete(name)
    name = Config.sanitizeName(name or Config.activeName)
    if type(delfile) ~= "function" then
        Config.setStatus("Delete unsupported", false)
        return false, "Executor does not support delfile"
    end
    local path = Config.pathFor(name)
    if type(isfile) == "function" then
        local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, path)
        if okExists and not exists then
            Config.setStatus("Not found: " .. name, false)
            return false, "No saved config named " .. name
        end
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(function() delfile(path) end)
    if not ok then
        Config.setStatus("Delete failed", false)
        return false, tostring(err)
    end
    Config.setStatus("Deleted: " .. name, true)
    return true
end

pcall(function()
    Config.defaults = Config.deepCopy(Config.capture())
end)

function Config.reset(refreshUI)
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        Config.applyData(Config.deepCopy(Config.defaults or {}))
        Config.applyRuntime()
    end)
    if not ok then
        Config.setStatus("Reset failed", false)
        return false, tostring(err)
    end
    if Config.supported() then
        Config.save(Config.activeName)
    end
    Config.setStatus("Defaults restored", true)
    if refreshUI and Builders and Builders.rebuildPageUI then
        task.defer(function()
            Builders.rebuildPageUI(currentActiveTab or "SETTINGS")
        end)
    end
    return true
end


--// FIXED Keybind listener (keyboard + mouse)
Utils.track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    -- Ignore the click that opened "press a key..." so M1 can be bound on the next click
    if tick() < listenIgnoreUntil then
        local movListening = Utils.mov and (
            Utils.mov.cframeListening or Utils.mov.gravListening or Utils.mov.jumpListening or Utils.mov.infJumpListening
            or Utils.mov.quickStopListening or Utils.mov.instantFallListening or Utils.mov.hipListening
            or Utils.mov.freecamListening or Utils.mov.flyListening or (Utils.autoKill and Utils.autoKill.listening) or Utils.fakeMacro.listening
        )
        if listeningForKey or listeningForSpeedKey or listeningForHideKey or listeningForSpinKey or listeningForCamKey or listeningForZoomKey or listeningForFlyKey or listeningForSilentKey or listeningForTriggerKey or listeningForFlameKey or movListening or (Utils.tp and (Utils.tp.listening or Utils.tp.listeningClosest)) or (Utils.fling and Utils.fling.listening) then
            return
        end
    end
    if listeningForFlameKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            Utils.flameLock.keybind = bind
            if R.flameKeybindBtn and R.flameKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.flameKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.flameKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForFlameKey = false
        end
        return
    end
    if listeningForTriggerKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            Utils.trigger.keybind = bind
            if R.triggerKeybindBtn and R.triggerKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.triggerKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.triggerKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForTriggerKey = false
        end
        return
    end
    -- Listening for Hitbox keybind
    if listeningForKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            selectedKeybind = bind
            if R.hitboxKeybindBtn and R.hitboxKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.hitboxKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.hitboxKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    -- Listening for Speed keybind
    if listeningForSpeedKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            speedKeybind = bind
            if R.speedKeybindBtn and R.speedKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.speedKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.speedKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForSpeedKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    -- Movement feature keybind listeners
    do
        local pairsList = {
            { "cframeListening", "cframeKey", "cframeKeyBtn" },
            { "gravListening", "gravKey", "gravKeyBtn" },
            { "jumpListening", "jumpKey", "jumpKeyBtn" },
            { "infJumpListening", "infJumpKey", "infJumpKeyBtn" },
            { "quickStopListening", "quickStopKey", "quickStopKeyBtn" },
            { "instantFallListening", "instantFallKey", "instantFallKeyBtn" },
            { "hipListening", "hipKey", "hipKeyBtn" },
            { "freecamListening", "freecamKey", "freecamKeyBtn" },
            { "flyListening", "flyKey", "flyKeyBtn" },
        }
        for _, it in ipairs(pairsList) do
            local listenKey, bindKey, btnKey = it[1], it[2], it[3]
            if Utils.mov and Utils.mov[listenKey] then
                local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
                if bind ~= nil or cleared then
                    Utils.mov[bindKey] = bind
                    local btn = R[btnKey]
                    if btn and btn.Parent then
                        btn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                        btn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
                    end
                    Utils.mov[listenKey] = false
                end
                return
            end
        end
        if Utils.autoKill and Utils.autoKill.listening then
            local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
            if bind ~= nil or cleared then
                Utils.autoKill.keybind = bind
                if R.autoKillKeyBtn and R.autoKillKeyBtn.Parent then
                    R.autoKillKeyBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                    R.autoKillKeyBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
                end
                Utils.autoKill.listening = false
            end
            return
        end
        if Utils.fakeMacro and Utils.fakeMacro.listening then
            local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
            if bind ~= nil or cleared then
                Utils.fakeMacro.keybind = bind
                if R.macroKeyBtn and R.macroKeyBtn.Parent then
                    R.macroKeyBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                    R.macroKeyBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
                end
                Utils.fakeMacro.listening = false
            end
            return
        end
    end

    -- Listening for Hide keybind
    if listeningForHideKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            hideKeybind = bind
            if R.hideKeybindBtn and R.hideKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.hideKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.hideKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForHideKey = false
        end
        return
    end


    -- Listening for Spinbot keybind
    if listeningForSpinKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            spinKeybind = bind
            if R.spinKeybindBtn and R.spinKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.spinKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.spinKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForSpinKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    if listeningForSilentKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            silentAim.keybind = bind
            if R.silentKeybindBtn and R.silentKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.silentKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.silentKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForSilentKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    -- Listening for Cam Lock keybind
    if listeningForCamKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            camlock.keybind = bind
            if R.camlockKeybindBtn and R.camlockKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.camlockKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.camlockKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForCamKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    -- Listening for Zoom Aim keybind
    if listeningForZoomKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            camlock.zoomKeybind = bind
            if R.zoomAimKeybindBtn and R.zoomAimKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.zoomAimKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.zoomAimKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForZoomKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    if Utils.fling and Utils.fling.listening then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            Utils.fling.keybind = bind
            if R.flingKeybindBtn and R.flingKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.flingKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.flingKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            Utils.fling.listening = false
        end
        return
    end

    if Utils.tp and Utils.tp.listeningClosest then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            Utils.tp.closestKeybind = bind
            if R.tpClosestKeyBtn and R.tpClosestKeyBtn.Parent then
                R.tpClosestKeyBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.tpClosestKeyBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            Utils.tp.listeningClosest = false
        end
        return
    end

    if Utils.tp and Utils.tp.listening then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            Utils.tp.keybind = bind
            if R.tpKeybindBtn and R.tpKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.tpKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.tpKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            Utils.tp.listening = false
        end
        return
    end

    -- Listening for Fly keybind
    if listeningForFlyKey then
        local bind, cleared = captureBindFromInput(input)
        if bind ~= nil or cleared then
            flyKeybind = bind
            if R.flyKeybindBtn and R.flyKeybindBtn.Parent then
                R.flyKeybindBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(bind)
                R.flyKeybindBtn.TextColor3 = bind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
            end
            listeningForFlyKey = false
        end
        return
    end

    if not gameProcessed then
        -- Hitbox keybind
        if inputMatchesBind(input, selectedKeybind) then
            Utils.updateHitboxState(not hitboxActive)
        end
        -- Speed keybind
        if inputMatchesBind(input, speedKeybind) then
            speedActive = not speedActive
            if R.speedToggleBtn and R.speedToggleBtn.Parent then
                UI.applyToggleState(R.speedToggleBtn, R.speedToggleIndicator, speedActive)
                if speedActive then
                    R.speedExtra.Visible = true
                    TweenService:Create(R.speedExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 86)}):Play()
                else
                    local tweenOut = TweenService:Create(R.speedExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
                    tweenOut:Play()
                    tweenOut.Completed:Connect(function()
                        if not speedActive then
                            R.speedExtra.Visible = false
                        end
                    end)
                    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
                    if hum then
                        hum.WalkSpeed = originalWalkSpeed
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        -- Hide keybind
        if inputMatchesBind(input, hideKeybind) then
            panelHidden = not panelHidden
            MainFrame.Visible = not panelHidden
            if panelHidden then
                Utils.playSound(SoundIds.Hide, 1.4)
            else
                Utils.playSound(SoundIds.Unhide, 1.4)
            end
        end
        if inputMatchesBind(input, camlock.keybind) then
            local mode = camlock.bindMode or (camlock.holdMode and "Hold" or "Toggle")
            if mode ~= "Always" then
                if mode == "Hold" or mode == "Click" then
                    camlock.enabled = true
                else
                    camlock.enabled = not camlock.enabled
                    if not camlock.enabled then camlock.currentTarget = nil end
                end
                if R.camlockToggleBtn and R.camlockToggleBtn.Parent then
                    UI.applyToggleState(R.camlockToggleBtn, R.camlockToggleIndicator, camlock.enabled)
                end
            end
        end
        if inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.flameLock.keybind) then
            Utils.flameLock.enabled = not Utils.flameLock.enabled
            if Utils.flameLock.enabled then
                Utils.flameLock._hadTarget = false
                Utils.flameLock.currentTarget = nil
            end
            if R.flameToggleBtn and R.flameToggleBtn.Parent then
                UI.applyToggleState(R.flameToggleBtn, R.flameToggleInd, Utils.flameLock.enabled)
            end
        end
        if inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.trigger.keybind) then
            Utils.trigger.enabled = not Utils.trigger.enabled
            if R.triggerToggleBtn and R.triggerToggleBtn.Parent then
                UI.applyToggleState(R.triggerToggleBtn, R.triggerToggleInd, Utils.trigger.enabled)
            end
        end
        if inputMatchesBind(input, silentAim.keybind) then
            local mode = silentAim.bindMode or (silentAim.holdMode and "Hold" or "Toggle")
            if mode ~= "Always" then
                if mode == "Hold" or mode == "Click" then
                    silentAim.enabled = true
                else
                    silentAim.enabled = not silentAim.enabled
                end
                if R.silentToggleBtn and R.silentToggleBtn.Parent then
                    UI.applyToggleState(R.silentToggleBtn, R.silentToggleIndicator, silentAim.enabled)
                end
                pcall(Utils.hcSilentSync)
            end
        end
        -- Zoom Aim keybind
        if inputMatchesBind(input, camlock.zoomKeybind) then
            camlock.zoomAim = not camlock.zoomAim
            if R.zoomAimToggleBtn and R.zoomAimToggleIndicator then
                UI.applyToggleState(R.zoomAimToggleBtn, R.zoomAimToggleIndicator, camlock.zoomAim)
            end
            if not camlock.zoomAim then
                unlockPlayerZoom()
            end
        end
        -- Spinbot keybind
        if inputMatchesBind(input, spinKeybind) then
            Utils.setSpinbot(not spinbotActive)
        end
        -- Fly keybind
        if inputMatchesBind(input, flyKeybind) then
            Utils.toggleFly()
        end
        -- Movement extras keybinds
        if Utils.mov then
            local M = Utils.mov
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.cframeKey) then
                speedUseCFrame = not speedUseCFrame
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.gravKey) then
                Utils.grav.active = not Utils.grav.active
                if not Utils.grav.active then
                    pcall(function() workspace.Gravity = Utils.grav.original or 196.2 end)
                else
                    pcall(function() workspace.Gravity = Utils.grav.value end)
                end
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.jumpKey) then
                jumpPowerActive = not jumpPowerActive
                if not jumpPowerActive then
                    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
                    if hum then hum.JumpPower = originalJumpPower end
                end
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.infJumpKey) then
                infiniteJumpActive = not infiniteJumpActive
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.quickStopKey) then
                M.quickStop = not M.quickStop
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.instantFallKey) then
                M.instantFall = not M.instantFall
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.hipKey) then
                M.hipHeight = not M.hipHeight
                Utils.movApplyHip()
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.freecamKey) then
                Utils.movSetFreecam(not M.freecam)
            end
            if inputMatchesBind(input, M.flyKey) then
                M.fly = not M.fly
                if not M.fly then
                    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid and Utils.getHumanoid() or nil
                    if hum then pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end) end
                end
            end
        end
        if Utils.autoKill and inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.autoKill.keybind) then
            Utils.autoKill.enabled = not Utils.autoKill.enabled
            if R.autoKillToggle and R.autoKillInd then
                UI.applyToggleState(R.autoKillToggle, R.autoKillInd, Utils.autoKill.enabled)
            end
            if Utils.autoKill.enabled then
                Utils.akLoop()
            else
                Utils.autoKill.status = "Idle"
            end
        end
        if Utils.fakeMacro and inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.fakeMacro.keybind) then
            Utils.fakeMacro.active = not Utils.fakeMacro.active
            Utils.fakeMacro.last = 0
        end
        -- Teleport keybind
        if Utils.tp and inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.tp.keybind) then
            Utils.tpTrigger(true)
        end
        if Utils.tp and inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.tp.closestKeybind) then
            Utils.tpClosestLook()
        end
        if Utils.fling and inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.fling.keybind) then
            if Utils.fling.active then
                Utils.flingSet(false)
            else
                Utils.flingSet(true)
            end
        end
    end
end))

--// Cam Lock hold-mode release
Utils.track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    local camMode = camlock.bindMode or (camlock.holdMode and "Hold" or "Toggle")
    if (camMode == "Hold" or camMode == "Click") and inputMatchesBind(input, camlock.keybind) then
        camlock.enabled = false
        camlock.currentTarget = nil
        if R.camlockToggleBtn and R.camlockToggleBtn.Parent then
            UI.applyToggleState(R.camlockToggleBtn, R.camlockToggleIndicator, false)
        end
    end
    local silMode = silentAim.bindMode or (silentAim.holdMode and "Hold" or "Toggle")
    if (silMode == "Hold" or silMode == "Click") and inputMatchesBind(input, silentAim.keybind) then
        silentAim.enabled = false
        if R.silentToggleBtn and R.silentToggleBtn.Parent then
            UI.applyToggleState(R.silentToggleBtn, R.silentToggleIndicator, false)
        end
        pcall(Utils.hcSilentSync)
    end
    if Utils.tp and Utils.tp.mode == "Hold" and inputMatchesBind(input, Utils.tp.keybind) then
        Utils.tpTrigger(false)
    end
end))

--// Cam Lock render loop (after camera so it actually overrides look)
pcall(function()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RaysCamLock")
end)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RaysCamLock", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
    Utils.camlockLoop()
end)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RaysFlameLock", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, function()
    Utils.flameLockTick()
end)
table.insert(trackedConns, {
    Disconnect = function()
        pcall(function()
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RaysCamLock")
        end)
    end
})

--// Cam Lock FOV circle
Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not Utils.hasDrawing then return end
    if camlock.fovCircle then
        if not camlockFovCircleObj then
            camlockFovCircleObj = Drawing.new("Circle")
            camlockFovCircleObj.Thickness = 1
            camlockFovCircleObj.NumSides = 64
            camlockFovCircleObj.Filled = false
            camlockFovCircleObj.Transparency = 0.7
        end
        camlockFovCircleObj.Position = Utils.getAimOrigin2D()
        camlockFovCircleObj.Radius = Utils.fovScreenRadius(camlock.fov)
        camlockFovCircleObj.Color = camlock.circleColor
        camlockFovCircleObj.Visible = true
    else
        if camlockFovCircleObj then
            camlockFovCircleObj.Visible = false
        end
    end
    if silentAim.fovCircle then
        if not silentFovCircleObj then
            silentFovCircleObj = Drawing.new("Circle")
            silentFovCircleObj.Thickness = 1
            silentFovCircleObj.NumSides = 64
            silentFovCircleObj.Filled = false
            silentFovCircleObj.Transparency = 0.7
        end
        silentFovCircleObj.Position = Utils.getAimOrigin2D()
        silentFovCircleObj.Radius = Utils.fovScreenRadius(silentAim.fov, 1000)
        silentFovCircleObj.Color = silentAim.circleColor or camlock.circleColor
        silentFovCircleObj.Visible = true
    else
        if silentFovCircleObj then
            silentFovCircleObj.Visible = false
        end
    end
    if silentAim.tracer and silentAim.enabled then
        local part = silentAim.currentPart or Utils.getSilentTarget()
        silentAim.currentPart = part
        if part then
            if not silentTracerObj then
                silentTracerObj = Drawing.new("Line")
                silentTracerObj.Thickness = 1.5
                silentTracerObj.Transparency = 0.85
            end
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            silentTracerObj.From = Utils.getAimOrigin2D()
            silentTracerObj.To = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
            silentTracerObj.Color = camlock.circleColor
            silentTracerObj.Visible = onScreen and sp.Z > 0
        elseif silentTracerObj then
            silentTracerObj.Visible = false
        end
    else
        if silentTracerObj then
            silentTracerObj.Visible = false
        end
    end
    local visPart, visCol = nil, silentAim.visualizerColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
    if silentAim.visualizer and silentAim.enabled then
        visPart = silentAim.currentPart or Utils.getSilentTarget()
        silentAim.currentPart = visPart
    elseif Utils.forceHit.enabled and (Utils.forceHit.visualizer or Utils.forceHit.targetTracer) then
        -- keep current FOV target live for visualizer / target tracer (DH + HC)
        local live = Utils.forceHitTarget and Utils.forceHitTarget()
        if live then Utils.forceHit.current = live end
        visPart = Utils.forceHit.current
        visCol = Utils.forceHit.visualizerColor or visCol
        if not Utils.forceHit.visualizer then
            visPart = nil -- circle only when visualizer on; tracer separate
        end
    end
    -- Force Hit target tracer: mouse (aim origin) -> target
    if Utils.forceHit.enabled and Utils.forceHit.targetTracer then
        local part = Utils.forceHit.current or (Utils.forceHitTarget and Utils.forceHitTarget())
        if part then
            Utils.forceHit.current = part
            if not Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj then
                Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj = Drawing.new("Line")
                Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj.Thickness = 1.5
                Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj.Transparency = 0.9
            end
            local line = Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            local from = Utils.getAimOrigin2D and Utils.getAimOrigin2D() or UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            line.From = from
            line.To = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
            line.Color = Utils.forceHit.visualizerColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
            line.Visible = onScreen and sp.Z > 0
        elseif Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj then
            Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj.Visible = false
        end
    elseif Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj then
        Utils.forceHitTargetTracerObj.Visible = false
    end
    if visPart then
        if not silentVisObj then
            silentVisObj = Drawing.new("Circle")
            silentVisObj.Filled = true
            silentVisObj.Thickness = 1
            silentVisObj.NumSides = 16
            silentVisObj.Transparency = 1
        end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(visPart.Position)
        silentVisObj.Position = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y)
        silentVisObj.Filled = true
        silentVisObj.Transparency = 1
        silentVisObj.Color = visCol
        silentVisObj.Radius = 6
        silentVisObj.Visible = onScreen and sp.Z > 0
    elseif silentVisObj then
        silentVisObj.Visible = false
    end
end))

--// Stats live update
Utils.track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if currentActiveTab ~= "STATS" then return end
    if R.statsPlayerCountLbl and R.statsPlayerCountLbl.Parent then
        R.statsPlayerCountLbl.Text = tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
    end
    if R.statsMaxPlayersLbl and R.statsMaxPlayersLbl.Parent then
        R.statsMaxPlayersLbl.Text = tostring(Players.MaxPlayers)
    end
    if R.statsFpsLbl and R.statsFpsLbl.Parent then
        R.statsFpsLbl.Text = tostring(currentFps)
    end
end))

Utils.updateSidebarSelection = function()
    for name, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
        local stroke = btn:FindFirstChild("TabStroke")
        local meta = Utils.tabMeta and Utils.tabMeta[name]
        local label = (meta and meta.label) or name
        local icon = (meta and meta.icon) or "•"
        btn.Text = "  " .. label
        btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        if name == currentActiveTab then
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
            btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        else
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            btn.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        end
        if stroke then stroke.Enabled = false end
        UI.applyPanelFont(btn)
        pcall(function()
            btn.FontFace = Font.fromName("BuilderSans", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
        end)
    end
end


--// Compact UI helpers (keeps section builders under register limit)
function UI.uiCorner(inst, rad)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
    c.Parent = inst
    return inst
end

function UI.uiLabel(parent, text, size, pos, textSize, xAlign)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Size = size or UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
    l.Position = pos or UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    l.TextSize = textSize or 12
    l.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    l.TextXAlignment = xAlign or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.Parent = parent
    UI.regFont(l, textSize or 12)
    return l
end

function UI.uiToggleSwitch(parent, pos)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 18)
    btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    btn.Position = pos or UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0)
    btn.Name = "RaysToggle"
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Parent = parent
    UI.uiCorner(btn, UDim.new(1, 0))
    UI.addGlassStroke(btn, 1)
    UI.regGlass(btn, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.45)
    local ind = Instance.new("Frame")
    ind.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    ind.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -7)
    ind.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    ind.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ind.Parent = btn
    UI.uiCorner(ind, UDim.new(1, 0))
    return btn, ind
end

function UI.openBindModeMenu(anchor, getMode, setMode)
    if R.bindModeMenu then
        R.bindModeMenu:Destroy()
        R.bindModeMenu = nil
    end
    local menu = Instance.new("Frame")
    menu.Name = "BindModeMenu"
    menu.Size = UDim2.new(0, 108, 0, 132)
    menu.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    menu.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    menu.BorderSizePixel = 0
    menu.ZIndex = 80
    menu.Parent = MainFrame
    UI.addCorner(menu, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.styleCard(menu)
    R.bindModeMenu = menu
    local pos = anchor.AbsolutePosition
    local par = MainFrame.AbsolutePosition
    menu.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.clamp(pos.X - par.X - 8, 8, 520), 0, math.clamp(pos.Y - par.Y + 22, 8, 420))
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    lay.Parent = menu
    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.Parent = menu
    local current = getMode() or "Toggle"
    for _, name in ipairs({ "Hold", "Toggle", "Always", "Click" }) do
        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = (name == current) and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Theme.Secondary
        row.BackgroundTransparency = (name == current) and 0.72 or 1
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Text = "  " .. name
        row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        row.TextSize = 12
        row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        row.ZIndex = 81
        row.AutoButtonColor = false
        row.Parent = menu
        UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(row, 12)
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            setMode(name)
            if menu.Parent then menu:Destroy() end
            R.bindModeMenu = nil
        end)
    end
    task.delay(0.05, function()
        local c
        c = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function()
            if R.bindModeMenu == menu and menu.Parent then
                menu:Destroy()
                R.bindModeMenu = nil
            end
            if c then c:Disconnect() end
        end)
    end)
end

function UI.attachBindMode(btn, getMode, setMode)
    if not btn then return end
    btn.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        UI.openBindModeMenu(btn, getMode, setMode)
    end)
end

function UI.uiKeybindBtn(parent, pos, keybind)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 18)
    b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    b.Position = pos or UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, 0)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    b.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    UI.styleCard(b)
    b.Text = bindDisplayName(keybind)
    b.TextColor3 = keybind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
    b.TextSize = 10
    b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Parent = parent
    UI.uiCorner(b, UDim.new(0, 4))
    UI.addGlassStroke(b, 1)
    UI.regGlass(b, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.5)
    UI.regFont(b, 10)
    return b
end

function UI.uiSection(parent, height)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Size = UDim2.new(1, (parent and parent.Name == "GroupBody") and 0 or -10, 0, height or 44)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    f.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.Parent = parent
    UI.uiCorner(f, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.addGlassStroke(f, 1)
    UI.styleCard(f)
    UI.regGlass(f, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
    if parent and parent.Name == "GroupBody" then
        UI.flattenCard(f)
    end
    return f
end

function UI.uiExtra(parent)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
    f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    f.ClipsDescendants = true
    f.Visible = false
    f.Parent = parent
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    lay.Parent = f
    return f
end

function UI.flattenCard(inst)
    if not inst then return end
    inst.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pcall(function()
        for _, c in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
            if c:IsA("UIStroke") and c.Parent == inst then
                c.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("UIStroke") then c.Transparency = 1 end
        end
    end)
end

function UI.groupBox(parent, title)
    local wrap = Instance.new("Frame")
    wrap.Name = "GroupBox"
    wrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 40)
    wrap.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    wrap.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp((Theme.CardT or 0.55) + 0.08, 0.2, 0.75)
    wrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
    wrap.Parent = parent
    UI.addCorner(wrap, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.styleCard(wrap)

    local head = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    head.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
    head.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6)
    head.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    head.Text = tostring(title or "")
    head.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    head.TextSize = 12
    head.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    head.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    head.Parent = wrap
    UI.regFont(head, 12)

    local body = Instance.new("Frame")
    body.Name = "GroupBody"
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
    body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 28)
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    body.Visible = true
    body.Parent = wrap
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    lay.Parent = body
    local function fit()
        local h = lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
        body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, h)
        wrap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, h + 36)
    end
    lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fit)
    task.defer(fit)
    return body, wrap
end

function UI.groupToggle(parent, name, initial, callback, colorInit, colorCb)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
    row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    row.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    row.Parent = parent
    UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.addGlassStroke(row, 1)
    UI.styleCard(row)
    UI.regGlass(row, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
    if parent and parent.Name == "GroupBody" then
        UI.flattenCard(row)
    end
    local labelX = 8
    if colorInit ~= nil and colorCb then
        local sw = Instance.new("TextButton")
        sw.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        sw.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -8)
        sw.BackgroundColor3 = colorInit
        sw.Text = ""
        sw.AutoButtonColor = false
        sw.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sw.Parent = row
        UI.addCorner(sw, UDim.new(0, 3))
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Thickness = 1
        st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        st.Transparency = 0.35
        st.Parent = sw
        labelX = 28
        local picker
        sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            if picker and picker.Parent then
                picker.Visible = not picker.Visible
                if picker.Visible then
                    picker.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 216)
                else
                    picker.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                end
            else
                picker = UI.createColorPicker(parent, "", sw.BackgroundColor3, function(c)
                    sw.BackgroundColor3 = c
                    colorCb(c)
                end, true)
            end
        end)
    end
    UI.uiLabel(row, name, UDim2.new(1, -52, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, labelX, 0, 0), 11)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0))
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, initial)
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        callback(tog, ind)
    end)
    return tog, ind, row
end

function Builders.buildHitboxSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local extra = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Hitbox Expander")
    R.hitboxExtra = extra
    local row = UI.uiSection(extra, 32)
    UI.uiLabel(row, "Enabled", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), selectedKeybind)
    R.hitboxKeybindBtn = keyBtn
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        listeningForKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
    R.hitboxToggleBtn = tog
    R.hitboxToggleIndicator = ind
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, hitboxActive)
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.updateHitboxState(not hitboxActive)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, hitboxActive)
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(extra, "X Size", hbXSize, 50, function(v) hbXSize = v if hitboxActive then Utils.updateHitboxState(true) end end)
