    UI.createRangeSlider(extra, "Y Size", hbYSize, 50, function(v) hbYSize = v if hitboxActive then Utils.updateHitboxState(true) end end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(extra, "Z Size", hbZSize, 50, function(v) hbZSize = v if hitboxActive then Utils.updateHitboxState(true) end end)
    UI.groupToggle(extra, "Visible Hitbox", hitboxVisible, function(vt, vi)
        hitboxVisible = not hitboxVisible
        UI.applyToggleState(vt, vi, hitboxVisible)
        if hitboxActive then Utils.updateHitboxState(true) end
    end)
end

function Builders.buildCamlockMainSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    UILeft = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Cam Lock")
    local box = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 32)
    UI.uiLabel(box, "Enabled", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(box, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), camlock.keybind)
    R.camlockKeybindBtn = keyBtn
    UI.attachBindMode(keyBtn, function()
        return camlock.bindMode or "Toggle"
    end, function(mode)
        camlock.bindMode = mode
        camlock.holdMode = (mode == "Hold" or mode == "Click")
        if mode == "Always" then
            camlock.enabled = true
            if R.camlockToggleBtn then UI.applyToggleState(R.camlockToggleBtn, R.camlockToggleIndicator, true) end
        end
    end)
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        listeningForCamKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
    R.camlockToggleBtn = tog
    R.camlockToggleIndicator = ind
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, camlock.enabled)
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        camlock.enabled = not camlock.enabled
        if not camlock.enabled then
            camlock.currentTarget = nil
        end
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, camlock.enabled)
    end)

    UI.createRangeSlider(UILeft, "FOV Size", camlock.fov, 1000, function(v) camlock.fov = math.clamp(v, 1, 1000) end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(UILeft, "Smoothness", camlock.smoothness, 0, 1.0, function(v)
        camlock.smoothness = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(UILeft, "Ease", camlock.ease or 1, -1, 1, function(v)
        camlock.ease = v
        if R.refreshEaseGraph then pcall(R.refreshEaseGraph) end
    end)
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Prediction", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.predictionEnabled)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.predictionEnabled = not camlock.predictionEnabled
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.predictionEnabled)
        end)
    end
    UI.createDecimalSlider(UILeft, "Predict Horizontal", camlock.predictionX, 0, 0.5, function(v)
        camlock.predictionX = v
        camlock.prediction = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(UILeft, "Predict Vertical", camlock.predictionY, 0, 0.5, function(v)
        camlock.predictionY = v
    end)

    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Sticky Lock", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.sticky)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.sticky = not camlock.sticky
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.sticky)
        end)
    end
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Release On Switch", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.releaseOnSwitch)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.releaseOnSwitch = not camlock.releaseOnSwitch
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.releaseOnSwitch)
        end)
    end
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Aim Assist", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.aimAssist)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.aimAssist = not camlock.aimAssist
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.aimAssist)
        end)
    end

    if false then
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Freehand", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.freehand)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.freehand = not camlock.freehand
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.freehand)
        end)
    end

    if false then
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Hold Mode (off = toggle)", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.holdMode)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.holdMode = not camlock.holdMode
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.holdMode)
        end)
    end

    if false then
        local dropOpen = false
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Lock Part: " .. tostring(camlock.lockPart) .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = UILeft
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)

        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = UILeft
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        listLay.Parent = list

        local rowH = 32
        for _, n in ipairs(LOCK_PART_OPTIONS) do
            local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, rowH)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = (camlock.lockPart == n) and Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Text = "  " .. n
            row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            row.TextSize = 11
            row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            row.AutoButtonColor = false
            row.Parent = list
            UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
            UI.regFont(row, 11)
            row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                camlock.lockPart = n
                dropBtn.Text = "  Lock Part: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                for _, child in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local on = child.Text:find(camlock.lockPart, 1, true) ~= nil
                        child.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
                    end
                end
                dropOpen = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        local listFullH = #LOCK_PART_OPTIONS * (rowH + 2) + 6
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            dropOpen = not dropOpen
            if dropOpen then
                list.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(list, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, listFullH)}):Play()
                dropBtn.Text = "  Lock Part: " .. tostring(camlock.lockPart) .. "   ▲"
            else
                local tw = TweenService:Create(list, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
                tw:Play()
                tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                    if not dropOpen then list.Visible = false end
                end)
                dropBtn.Text = "  Lock Part: " .. tostring(camlock.lockPart) .. "   ▼"
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Show FOV Circle", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.fovCircle)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.fovCircle = not camlock.fovCircle
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.fovCircle)
        end)
    end

    UI.createColorPicker(UILeft, "Circle Color", camlock.circleColor, function(c)
        camlock.circleColor = c
    end)

end

-- Silent Aim on right column
function Builders.buildSilentAimSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    UIRight = UI.groupBox(UIRight or UILeft, "Silent Aim")
    local box = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 32)
    UI.uiLabel(box, "Enabled", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local sKey = UI.uiKeybindBtn(box, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), silentAim.keybind)
    R.silentKeybindBtn = sKey
    UI.attachBindMode(sKey, function()
        return silentAim.bindMode or "Toggle"
    end, function(mode)
        silentAim.bindMode = mode
        silentAim.holdMode = (mode == "Hold" or mode == "Click")
        if mode == "Always" then
            silentAim.enabled = true
            if R.silentToggleBtn then UI.applyToggleState(R.silentToggleBtn, R.silentToggleIndicator, true) end
            pcall(Utils.hcSilentSync)
        end
    end)
    sKey.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        listeningForSilentKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        sKey.Text = "..."
        sKey.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
    R.silentToggleBtn = t
    R.silentToggleIndicator = i
    UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.enabled)
    t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        silentAim.enabled = not silentAim.enabled
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.enabled)
        pcall(Utils.hcSilentSync)
    end)

    UI.createRangeSlider(UIRight, "Silent FOV", silentAim.fov, 1000, function(v)
        silentAim.fov = math.clamp(v, 1, 1000)
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(UIRight, "Hit Chance", silentAim.hitChance or 100, 100, function(v)
        silentAim.hitChance = math.clamp(v, 0, 100)
    end)
    if selectedGameMode == "Da Hood" or isDaHood then
        UI.createDecimalSlider(UIRight, "Bullet Spread", silentAim.bulletSpread or 1, 0, 2, function(v)
            silentAim.bulletSpread = math.clamp(v, 0, 2)
        end)
    end
    -- Target mode: closest to mouse vs closest to you
    do
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local modeLabel = (silentAim.targetMode == "Player") and "Closest to You" or "Closest to Mouse"
        dropBtn.Text = "  Target: " .. modeLabel .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = UIRight
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.Visible = false
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        list.Parent = UIRight
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        local labels = { Mouse = "Closest to Mouse", Player = "Closest to You" }
        for _, mode in ipairs(SILENT_TARGET_MODES) do
            local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Text = "  " .. labels[mode]
            row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            row.TextSize = 11
            row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            row.AutoButtonColor = false
            row.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(row, 11)
            row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                silentAim.targetMode = mode
                dropBtn.Text = "  Target: " .. labels[mode] .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 56) or UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 52)
            dropBtn.Text = "  Target: " .. (labels[silentAim.targetMode] or "Closest to Mouse") .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
    end

    -- Hit part dropdown
    if false then
    do
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Hit Part: " .. tostring(silentAim.part) .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = UIRight
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.Visible = false
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        list.Parent = UIRight
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, name in ipairs(SILENT_PART_OPTIONS) do
            local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Text = "  " .. name
            row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            row.TextSize = 11
            row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            row.AutoButtonColor = false
            row.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(row, 11)
            row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                silentAim.part = name
                dropBtn.Text = "  Hit Part: " .. name .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, math.min(#SILENT_PART_OPTIONS * 26, 130)) or UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #SILENT_PART_OPTIONS * 26)
            dropBtn.Text = "  Hit Part: " .. tostring(silentAim.part) .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
    end
    end

    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Show FOV Circle", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local ct, ci = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(ct, ci, silentAim.fovCircle)
        ct.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            silentAim.fovCircle = not silentAim.fovCircle
            UI.applyToggleState(ct, ci, silentAim.fovCircle)
        end)
    end
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Health Check", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local ht, hi = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(ht, hi, silentAim.healthCheck)
        ht.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            silentAim.healthCheck = not silentAim.healthCheck
            UI.applyToggleState(ht, hi, silentAim.healthCheck)
        end)
    end
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Never Miss", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.neverMiss)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            silentAim.neverMiss = not silentAim.neverMiss
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.neverMiss)
        end)
    end
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Multi Target", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.multiTarget)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            silentAim.multiTarget = not silentAim.multiTarget
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.multiTarget)
        end)
    end
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Target Visualizer", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.visualizer)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            silentAim.visualizer = not silentAim.visualizer
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.visualizer)
        end)
    end
    UI.createColorPicker(UIRight, "FOV Color", silentAim.circleColor, function(c)
        silentAim.circleColor = c
        if silentFovCircleObj then silentFovCircleObj.Color = c end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(UIRight, "Visualizer Color", silentAim.visualizerColor, function(c)
        silentAim.visualizerColor = c
    end)
end

function Builders.buildFlameLockSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    if selectedGameMode ~= "Da Hood" and not isDaHood then return end
    local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Flame Lock")
    local row = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
    UI.uiLabel(row, "Enabled", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), Utils.flameLock.keybind)
    R.flameKeybindBtn = keyBtn
    UI.attachBindMode(keyBtn, function()
        return Utils.flameLock.bindMode or "Toggle"
    end, function(mode)
        Utils.flameLock.bindMode = mode
    end)
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        listeningForFlameKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
    R.flameToggleBtn = tog
    R.flameToggleInd = ind
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.flameLock.enabled)
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.flameLock.enabled = not Utils.flameLock.enabled
        if Utils.flameLock.enabled then
            Utils.flameLock._hadTarget = false
            Utils.flameLock.currentTarget = nil
        end
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.flameLock.enabled)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Sticky", Utils.flameLock.sticky, function(t, i)
        Utils.flameLock.sticky = not Utils.flameLock.sticky
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.flameLock.sticky)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Release On Switch", Utils.flameLock.releaseOnSwitch, function(t, i)
        Utils.flameLock.releaseOnSwitch = not Utils.flameLock.releaseOnSwitch
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.flameLock.releaseOnSwitch)
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Smoothness", Utils.flameLock.smoothness, 0, 1, function(v)
        Utils.flameLock.smoothness = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Snap Intensity", Utils.flameLock.snapIntensity, 0, 1, function(v)
        Utils.flameLock.snapIntensity = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Prediction", Utils.flameLock.prediction, 0, 1, function(v)
        Utils.flameLock.prediction = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Horizontal Offset", Utils.flameLock.horizOffset, -8, 8, function(v)
        Utils.flameLock.horizOffset = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Up Offset", Utils.flameLock.upOffset, -8, 8, function(v)
        Utils.flameLock.upOffset = v
    end)
    do
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        dropBtn.Text = "  Hit Part: " .. tostring(Utils.flameLock.lockPart) .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local names = { "Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso", "Torso" }
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.flameLock.lockPart = n
                dropBtn.Text = "  Hit Part: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #names * 22) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            dropBtn.Text = "  Hit Part: " .. tostring(Utils.flameLock.lockPart) .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
    end
end

function Builders.buildTriggerSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Trigger Bot")
    local row = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
    UI.uiLabel(row, "Enabled", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), Utils.trigger.keybind)
    R.triggerKeybindBtn = keyBtn
    UI.attachBindMode(keyBtn, function()
        return Utils.trigger.bindMode or "Toggle"
    end, function(mode)
        Utils.trigger.bindMode = mode
    end)
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        listeningForTriggerKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
    R.triggerToggleBtn = tog
    R.triggerToggleInd = ind
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.trigger.enabled)
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.trigger.enabled = not Utils.trigger.enabled
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.trigger.enabled)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Visible Check", Utils.trigger.visible, function(t, i)
        Utils.trigger.visible = not Utils.trigger.visible
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.trigger.visible)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Team Check", Utils.trigger.team, function(t, i)
        Utils.trigger.team = not Utils.trigger.team
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.trigger.team)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Knife Check", Utils.trigger.knifeCheck, function(t, i)
        Utils.trigger.knifeCheck = not Utils.trigger.knifeCheck
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.trigger.knifeCheck)
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Hitbox Mul", Utils.trigger.hitboxMul, 0.1, 5, function(v)
        Utils.trigger.hitboxMul = math.clamp(v, 0.1, 5)
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Delay (ms)", Utils.trigger.delayMs, 200, function(v)
        Utils.trigger.delayMs = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 0, 200)
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Release (ms)", Utils.trigger.releaseMs, 200, function(v)
        Utils.trigger.releaseMs = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 1, 200)
    end)
end

function Builders.buildAutoKillSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local only = Builders._akSection
    local host = UILeft or PageScroll
    if only == "fullbright" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Fullbright")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.autoKill.fullbright, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.fullbright = not Utils.autoKill.fullbright
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.fullbright)
            if Utils.autoKill.fullbright then Utils.akApplyFullbright(true) end
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "voidspam" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Void Spam")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.autoKill.voidSpam, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.voidSpam = not Utils.autoKill.voidSpam
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.voidSpam)
            if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then
                if Utils.autoKill.enabled then Utils.akVoidLoop() end
            else
                Utils.akVoidRestore()
            end
        end)
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Void Speed", Utils.autoKill.voidSpeed or 20, 100, function(v)
            Utils.autoKill.voidSpeed = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 1, 100)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "spectate" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Spectate Target")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.autoKill.spectate, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.spectate = not Utils.autoKill.spectate
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.spectate)
            Utils.akSetSpectate(Utils.autoKill.spectate and Utils.autoKill.enabled)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "predict" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Predict")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.autoKill.predict, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.predict = not Utils.autoKill.predict
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.predict)
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Predict Intensity", Utils.autoKill.predictIntensity or 0.4, 0, 3, function(v)
            Utils.autoKill.predictIntensity = v
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "teleport" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Teleport Settings")
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Teleport Interval", Utils.autoKill.teleportInterval or 0.05, 0.01, 0.5, function(v)
            Utils.autoKill.teleportInterval = v
        end)
        local modes = { "Above Head", "Inside", "Below" }
        local cur = tostring(Utils.autoKill.tpMode or "Inside")
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Position: " .. cur .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(modes) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.autoKill.tpMode = n
                Utils.autoKill.aboveHead = (n == "Above Head")
                dropBtn.Text = "  Position: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #modes * 22) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "lowhp" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Low HP Auto Stomp")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp = not Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "status" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Status")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Show Status", Utils.autoKill.showStatus, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.showStatus = not Utils.autoKill.showStatus
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.showStatus)
            pcall(function() Utils.akSetStatusHud(Utils.autoKill.showStatus) end)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "abovehead" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Teleport Mode")
        local modes = { "Above Head", "Inside", "Below" }
        local cur = tostring(Utils.autoKill.tpMode or "Inside")
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Position: " .. cur .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(modes) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.autoKill.tpMode = n
                Utils.autoKill.aboveHead = (n == "Above Head")
                dropBtn.Text = "  Position: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #modes * 22) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            dropBtn.Text = "  Position: " .. tostring(Utils.autoKill.tpMode or "Inside") .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "Above Head / Inside / Below — use Inside or Below indoors so you are not stuck in the ceiling."
        hint.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        hint.TextSize = 10
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        hint.TextWrapped = true
        hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hint.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(hint, 10)
        return
    end
    if only == "orbit" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Orbit Settings")
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Orbit Distance", Utils.autoKill.orbitDistance or 4.5, 1, 30, function(v)
            Utils.autoKill.orbitDistance = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Orbit Height", Utils.autoKill.orbitHeight or 0, -10, 10, function(v)
            Utils.autoKill.orbitHeight = v
        end)
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Orbit Speed", Utils.autoKill.orbitSpeed or 50, 100, function(v)
            Utils.autoKill.orbitSpeed = v
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "target" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Target")
        local note = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        note.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
        note.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        note.Text = "Targets: " .. (Utils.akTargetNames and Utils.akTargetNames() or "None")
        note.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        note.TextSize = 12
        note.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        note.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        note.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(note, 12)
        local countLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        countLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
        countLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        countLbl.Text = "Players in server: 0"
        countLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        countLbl.TextSize = 10
        countLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        countLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        countLbl.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(countLbl, 10)
        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 380)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 6
        list.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        list.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        lay.Parent = list
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pad.Parent = list
        local function rebuildTargets()
            for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
            end
            local others = {}
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= Player then
                    table.insert(others, plr)
                end
            end
            table.sort(others, function(a, b)
                return string.lower(a.DisplayName or a.Name) < string.lower(b.DisplayName or b.Name)
            end)
            countLbl.Text = string.format("Players in server: %d  (showing %d)", #Players:GetPlayers(), #others)
            note.Text = "Targets: " .. (Utils.akTargetNames and Utils.akTargetNames() or "None")
            for i, plr in ipairs(others) do
                local selected = Utils.akIsSelected and Utils.akIsSelected(plr)
                local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 26)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Primary
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.15 or 0.35
                btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                btn.Text = "  " .. (plr.DisplayName or plr.Name) .. "  (@" .. plr.Name .. ")"
                btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                btn.TextSize = 11
                btn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                btn.LayoutOrder = i
                btn.Parent = list
                UI.addCorner(btn, UDim.new(0, 4))
                UI.regFont(btn, 11)
                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    if Utils.akToggleTarget then Utils.akToggleTarget(plr) end
                    note.Text = "Targets: " .. (Utils.akTargetNames and Utils.akTargetNames() or "None")
                    local sel = Utils.akIsSelected and Utils.akIsSelected(plr)
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = sel and Theme.Accent or Theme.Primary
                    btn.BackgroundTransparency = sel and 0.15 or 0.35
                end)
            end
            task.defer(function()
                list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 12, 1))
            end)
        end
        rebuildTargets()
        Utils.track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
            task.defer(rebuildTargets)
        end))
        Utils.track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
            task.defer(rebuildTargets)
        end))
        return
    end
    if only == "enabled" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Enabled")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enable Auto Kill", Utils.autoKill.enabled, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.enabled = not Utils.autoKill.enabled
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.enabled)
            if Utils.autoKill.enabled then
                pcall(Utils.akLoop)
            end
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "mode" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Kill Mode")
        local modes = { "Double Barrel", "Shotgun", "Knife" }
        local cur = tostring(Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel")
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Mode: " .. cur .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(modes) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.autoKill.mode = n
                dropBtn.Text = "  Mode: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                if n ~= "Knife" then
                    pcall(function() Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox(false) end)
                end
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #modes * 24) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            dropBtn.Text = "  Mode: " .. tostring(Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel") .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "Double Barrel / Shotgun use Force Hit. Knife orbits and spams melee."
        hint.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        hint.TextSize = 10
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        hint.TextWrapped = true
        hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hint.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(hint, 10)
        return
    end
    if only == "randomstomp" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Random Stomp")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.autoKill.randomStomp, function(t, i)
            Utils.autoKill.randomStomp = not Utils.autoKill.randomStomp
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.randomStomp)
        end)
        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "When stomping, spin around the target at random positions instead of standing still on top."
        hint.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        hint.TextSize = 10
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        hint.TextWrapped = true
        hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hint.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(hint, 10)
        return
    end
    local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft or PageScroll, "Auto Kill")
    local row = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
    UI.uiLabel(row, "Enable", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), Utils.autoKill.keybind)
    R.autoKillKeyBtn = keyBtn
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.autoKill.listening = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
    R.autoKillToggle = tog
    R.autoKillInd = ind
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.autoKill.enabled)
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.autoKill.enabled = not Utils.autoKill.enabled
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.autoKill.enabled)
        if Utils.autoKill.enabled then
            if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then Utils.akVoidLoop() end
            if Utils.autoKill.showStatus then Utils.akSetStatusHud(true) end
            Utils.akLoop()
        else
            Utils.autoKill.status = "Idle"
            Utils.autoKill._voidHold = false
            Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox(false)
            Utils.akSetSpectate(false)
            Utils.akVoidRestore()
            Utils.akApplyFullbright(false)
            Utils.akSetStatusHud(false)
        end
    end)

    local status = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    status.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    status.Text = "Status: " .. tostring(Utils.autoKill.status or "Idle")
    status.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    status.TextSize = 11
    status.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    status.Parent = box
    UI.regFont(status, 11)
    R.autoKillStatus = status
    task.spawn(function()
        while status.Parent do
            status.Text = "Status: " .. tostring(Utils.autoKill.status or "Idle")
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)

    -- Mode dropdown
    do
        local modes = { "Double Barrel", "Shotgun", "Knife" }
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        dropBtn.Text = "  Mode: " .. tostring(Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel") .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(modes) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.autoKill.mode = n
                dropBtn.Text = "  Mode: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                if n ~= "Knife" then
                    Utils.akApplyKnifeHitbox(false)
                end
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #modes * 22) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            dropBtn.Text = "  Mode: " .. tostring(Utils.autoKill.mode or "Double Barrel") .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
    end

    UI.groupToggle(box, "Fullbright", Utils.autoKill.fullbright, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.fullbright = not Utils.autoKill.fullbright
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.fullbright)
        if Utils.autoKill.fullbright then
            Utils.akApplyFullbright(true)
        end
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Void Spam", Utils.autoKill.voidSpam, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.voidSpam = not Utils.autoKill.voidSpam
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.voidSpam)
        if Utils.autoKill.voidSpam then
            if Utils.autoKill.enabled then Utils.akVoidLoop() end
        else
            Utils.akVoidRestore()
        end
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Spectate Target", Utils.autoKill.spectate, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.spectate = not Utils.autoKill.spectate
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.spectate)
        Utils.akSetSpectate(Utils.autoKill.spectate and Utils.autoKill.enabled)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Predict", Utils.autoKill.predict, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.predict = not Utils.autoKill.predict
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.predict)
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Void Speed", Utils.autoKill.voidSpeed or 20, 100, function(v)
        Utils.autoKill.voidSpeed = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 1, 100)
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Teleport Interval", Utils.autoKill.teleportInterval or 0.05, 0.01, 0.5, function(v)
        Utils.autoKill.teleportInterval = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Predict Intensity", Utils.autoKill.predictIntensity or 0.4, 0, 3, function(v)
        Utils.autoKill.predictIntensity = v
    end)

    local right = UI.groupBox(UIRight or PageScroll, "Options")
    UI.groupToggle(right, "Low HP Auto Stomp", Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp = not Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.autoStompLowHp)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(right, "Status", Utils.autoKill.showStatus, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.showStatus = not Utils.autoKill.showStatus
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.showStatus)
        Utils.akSetStatusHud(Utils.autoKill.showStatus)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(right, "Above Head", Utils.autoKill.aboveHead, function(t, i)
        Utils.autoKill.aboveHead = not Utils.autoKill.aboveHead
        if Utils.autoKill.aboveHead then
            Utils.autoKill.tpMode = "Above Head"
        elseif Utils.autoKill.tpMode == "Above Head" then
            Utils.autoKill.tpMode = "Inside"
        end
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.autoKill.aboveHead)
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(right, "Knife Orbit Speed", Utils.autoKill.knifeSpeed or 40, 200, function(v)
        Utils.autoKill.knifeSpeed = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 1, 200)
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(right, "Orbit Distance", Utils.autoKill.orbitDistance or 4.5, 1, 30, function(v)
        Utils.autoKill.orbitDistance = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(right, "Orbit Height", Utils.autoKill.orbitHeight or 0, -10, 10, function(v)
        Utils.autoKill.orbitHeight = v
    end)

    local selectedLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    selectedLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    selectedLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    selectedLbl.Text = "Targets: " .. Utils.akTargetNames()
    selectedLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    selectedLbl.TextSize = 12
    selectedLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    selectedLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    selectedLbl.Parent = right
    UI.regFont(selectedLbl, 12)

    local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
    search.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
    search.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    search.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    search.BorderSizePixel = 0
    search.PlaceholderText = "  Search player..."
    search.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    search.Text = ""
    search.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    search.TextSize = 12
    search.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    search.Parent = right
    UI.addCorner(search, UDim.new(0, 6))
    UI.regFont(search, 12)

    local listFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 380)
    listFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    listFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    listFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    listFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 6
    listFrame.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    listFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    listFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    listFrame.Parent = right
    UI.addCorner(listFrame, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    listLay.Parent = listFrame

    local function refresh()
        for _, ch in ipairs(listFrame:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
        end
        local q = string.lower(search.Text or "")
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player and (q == "" or string.find(string.lower(plr.Name), q, 1, true)) then
                local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 24)
                local on = Utils.akIsSelected(plr)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Accent or Theme.Primary
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.4 or 0.55
                btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                btn.Text = "  " .. plr.Name
                btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                btn.TextSize = 11
                btn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                btn.Parent = listFrame
                UI.addCorner(btn, UDim.new(0, 4))
                UI.regFont(btn, 11)
                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    Utils.akToggleTarget(plr)
                    selectedLbl.Text = "Targets: " .. Utils.akTargetNames()
                    refresh()
                    if Utils.autoKill.spectate and Utils.autoKill.enabled then
                        Utils.akSetSpectate(true)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        listFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, listLay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 8)
    end
    refresh()
    search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        refresh()
    end)
    Utils.track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() task.defer(refresh) end))
    Utils.track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
        local list = Utils.autoKill.targets or {}
        for i = #list, 1, -1 do
            if list[i] == plr then table.remove(list, i) end
        end
        if Utils.autoKill.target == plr then
            Utils.autoKill.target = list[1]
        end
        selectedLbl.Text = "Targets: " .. Utils.akTargetNames()
        task.defer(refresh)
    end))
end


function Builders.buildForceHitSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local only = Builders._fhSection
    local host = UILeft or PageScroll
    local isDH = (selectedGameMode == "Da Hood") or isDaHood
    local isHC = (selectedGameMode == "Hood Customs") or isHoodCustoms

    local function enableToggle(parent)
        UI.groupToggle(parent, "Enabled", Utils.forceHit.enabled, function(t, i)
            Utils.forceHit.enabled = not Utils.forceHit.enabled
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.forceHit.enabled)
            if Utils.forceHit.enabled then
                if isDH then
                    if silentAim then
                        Utils.forceHit._prevBulletSpread = silentAim.bulletSpread
                        silentAim.bulletSpread = 0
                    end
                    pcall(function() Utils.dhSilentStart() end)
                    pcall(function() Utils.hcSilentSync() end)
                end
            else
                if Utils.forceHit._prevBulletSpread ~= nil and silentAim then
                    silentAim.bulletSpread = Utils.forceHit._prevBulletSpread
                    Utils.forceHit._prevBulletSpread = nil
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if only == "enabled" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Enabled")
        enableToggle(box)
        return
    end
    if only == "fov" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "FOV")
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Force FOV", Utils.forceHit.fov or 100, 1000, function(v)
            Utils.forceHit.fov = math.clamp(v, 10, 1000)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "bulletviz" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Bullet Visualizer")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.forceHit.showBullets, function(t, i)
            Utils.forceHit.showBullets = not Utils.forceHit.showBullets
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.forceHit.showBullets)
        end)
        local types = { "Bullets", "Lightning", "Laser" }
        local cur = tostring(Utils.forceHit.bulletVizType or "Bullets")
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Type: " .. cur .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.Parent = box
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = box
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(types) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            b.Text = "  " .. n
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(b, 11)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.forceHit.bulletVizType = n
                dropBtn.Text = "  Type: " .. n .. "   ▼"
                open = false
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #types * 22) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Visualizer Spread", Utils.forceHit.visSpread or 1.8, 0, 8, function(v)
            Utils.forceHit.visSpread = v
        end)
        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "Bullets: multi-pellet only on DoubleBarrel / Shotgun / TacticalShotgun. Other guns show 1 trail. Lightning = bolt. Laser = red line (0.5s fade)."
        hint.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        hint.TextSize = 10
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        hint.TextWrapped = true
        hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hint.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(hint, 10)
        return
    end
    if only == "targetviz" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Target Visualizer")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.forceHit.visualizer, function(t, i)
            Utils.forceHit.visualizer = not Utils.forceHit.visualizer
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.forceHit.visualizer)
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(box, "Visualizer Color", Utils.forceHit.visualizerColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0), function(c)
            Utils.forceHit.visualizerColor = c
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "tpmode" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Teleport Mode")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.forceHit.tpMode, function(t, i)
            Utils.forceHit.tpMode = not Utils.forceHit.tpMode
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.forceHit.tpMode)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "tpreturn" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Return to Origin")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", Utils.forceHit.tpReturn, function(t, i)
            Utils.forceHit.tpReturn = not Utils.forceHit.tpReturn
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.forceHit.tpReturn)
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Return Delay (s)", Utils.forceHit.tpReturnDelay or 0.145, 0.145, 10, function(v)
            Utils.forceHit.tpReturnDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 0.145, 0.145, 10)
        end)
        return
    end

    -- Legacy full section (if called without only)
    local box = UI.groupBox(host, "Force Hit")
    enableToggle(box)
    UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Force FOV", Utils.forceHit.fov or 100, 1000, function(v)
        Utils.forceHit.fov = math.clamp(v, 10, 1000)
    end)
end

function Builders.buildCamlockZoomSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local box = UI.groupBox(UIRight or UILeft, "Zoom Aim [Experimental]")
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Zoom Aim [Experimental]", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), camlock.zoomKeybind)
        R.zoomAimKeybindBtn = keyBtn
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            listeningForZoomKey = true
            listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
        R.zoomAimToggleBtn = tog
        R.zoomAimToggleIndicator = ind
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, camlock.zoomAim)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.zoomAim = not camlock.zoomAim
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, camlock.zoomAim)
            if not camlock.zoomAim then
                unlockPlayerZoom()
                if camlock._zoomFovCircle then pcall(function() camlock._zoomFovCircle.Visible = false end) end
            end
        end)
    end
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Sticky Aim", camlock.zoomSticky ~= false, function(t, i)
        camlock.zoomSticky = not (camlock.zoomSticky ~= false)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.zoomSticky)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Release On Switch", camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch, function(t, i)
        camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch = not camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.zoomReleaseOnSwitch)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(box, "Show FOV", camlock.zoomShowFov, function(t, i)
        camlock.zoomShowFov = not camlock.zoomShowFov
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, camlock.zoomShowFov)
        if not camlock.zoomShowFov and camlock._zoomFovCircle then
            pcall(function() camlock._zoomFovCircle.Visible = false end)
        end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Lock FOV", camlock.zoomFov or 150, 400, function(v)
        camlock.zoomFov = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 40, 400)
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Zoom Intensity", camlock.zoomIntensity or 1, 0.05, 1, function(v)
        camlock.zoomIntensity = v
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Full Zoom Height", camlock.fullZoomHeight or 8, 3, 25, function(v)
        camlock.fullZoomHeight = v
    end)
    local status = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    status.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    status.Text = "Status: " .. tostring(camlock._zoomStatus or "Idle")
    status.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    status.TextSize = 11
    status.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    status.Parent = box
    UI.regFont(status, 11)
    R.zoomAimStatusLbl = status
end




function Builders.buildEaseGraphSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local box = UI.groupBox(UIRight or UILeft, "Ease Graph")
    UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Ease", camlock.ease or 1, -1, 1, function(v)
        camlock.ease = v
        if R.refreshEaseGraph then pcall(R.refreshEaseGraph) end
    end)
    local graphBox = Instance.new("Frame")
    graphBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 128)
    graphBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)
    graphBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    graphBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    graphBox.Parent = box
    UI.addCorner(graphBox, UDim.new(0, 8))
    local gTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    gTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 16)
    gTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 4)
    gTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    gTitle.Text = "Ease Graph"
    gTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    gTitle.TextSize = 10
    gTitle.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    gTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    gTitle.Parent = graphBox
    UI.regFont(gTitle, 10)
    local plot = Instance.new("Frame")
    plot.Name = "EasePlot"
    plot.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 96)
    plot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 22)
    plot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32)
    plot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    plot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    plot.ClipsDescendants = true
    plot.Parent = graphBox
    UI.addCorner(plot, UDim.new(0, 4))
    local segs = {}
    for i = 1, 36 do
        local s = Instance.new("Frame")
        s.Name = "EaseSeg"
        s.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        s.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
        s.BorderSizePixel = 0
        s.ZIndex = 3
        s.Parent = plot
        segs[i] = s
    end
    local function refreshGraph()
        local ease = math.clamp(tonumber(camlock.ease) or 1, -1, 1)
        local n = #segs
        local w = plot.AbsoluteSize.X
        local h = plot.AbsoluteSize.Y
        if w < 4 or h < 4 then return end
        for i = 1, n do
            local x0 = (i - 1) / n
            local x1 = i / n
            local function curve(x)
                if ease >= 0 then
                    local lin = x
                    local curved = 1 - (1 - x) ^ (1 + ease * 3.5)
                    return lin * (1 - ease) + curved * ease
                else
                    local e = -ease
                    local lin = x
                    local curved = x ^ (1 + e * 3.5)
                    return lin * (1 - e) + curved * e
                end
            end
            local y0 = curve(x0)
            local y1 = curve(x1)
            local s = segs[i]
            local px0, px1 = x0 * w, x1 * w
            local py0, py1 = (1 - y0) * h, (1 - y1) * h
            local dx, dy = px1 - px0, py1 - py0
            local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
            s.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(2, len), 0, 2)
            s.Position = UDim2.new(0, px0, 0, py0)
            s.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        end
    end
    R.refreshEaseGraph = refreshGraph
    plot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(refreshGraph)
    task.defer(refreshGraph)
end

function Builders.buildTargetSelectSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local body = UI.groupBox(UIRight or UILeft, "Target Selection")
    UI.groupToggle(body, "Closest Part", silentAim.part == "Closest Part", function(t, i)
        if silentAim.part == "Closest Part" then
            local last = camlock.lastLockPart or "Head"
            silentAim.part = last
            camlock.lockPart = last
        else
            camlock.lastLockPart = camlock.lockPart or "Head"
            silentAim.part = "Closest Part"
            camlock.lockPart = "Closest Part"
        end
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, silentAim.part == "Closest Part")
    end)
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 300)
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.Parent = body
    local stage = Instance.new("Frame")
    stage.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 290)
    stage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
    stage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stage.Parent = holder
    local idle = Color3.fromRGB(214, 214, 220)
    local cells = {
        { part = "Head", x = 0.50, y = 0.08, w = 32, h = 32 },
        { part = "LeftUpperArm", x = 0.26, y = 0.26, w = 22, h = 32 },
        { part = "UpperTorso", x = 0.50, y = 0.28, w = 64, h = 44 },
        { part = "RightUpperArm", x = 0.74, y = 0.26, w = 22, h = 32 },
        { part = "LeftLowerArm", x = 0.26, y = 0.40, w = 22, h = 24 },
        { part = "RightLowerArm", x = 0.74, y = 0.40, w = 22, h = 24 },
        { part = "LeftHand", x = 0.26, y = 0.50, w = 22, h = 16 },
        { part = "RightHand", x = 0.74, y = 0.50, w = 22, h = 16 },
        { part = "LowerTorso", x = 0.50, y = 0.46, w = 56, h = 18 },
        { part = "LeftUpperLeg", x = 0.40, y = 0.62, w = 22, h = 36 },
        { part = "RightUpperLeg", x = 0.60, y = 0.62, w = 22, h = 36 },
        { part = "LeftLowerLeg", x = 0.40, y = 0.78, w = 22, h = 28 },
        { part = "RightLowerLeg", x = 0.60, y = 0.78, w = 22, h = 28 },
        { part = "LeftFoot", x = 0.40, y = 0.91, w = 22, h = 16 },
        { part = "RightFoot", x = 0.60, y = 0.91, w = 22, h = 16 },
    }
    local btns = {}
    local function paint()
        local cur = camlock.lockPart or "Head"
        for _, b in ipairs(btns) do
            local on = b:GetAttribute("PartName") == cur
            b:SetAttribute("PartOn", on)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Accent or idle
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        end
    end
    for _, c in ipairs(cells) do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name = "RaysPartPick"
        b:SetAttribute("PartName", c.part)
        b:SetAttribute("RaysPartPick", true)
        b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, c.w, 0, c.h)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(c.x, 0, c.y, 0)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = idle
        b.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = ""
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Parent = stage
        UI.addCorner(b, UDim.new(0, 3))
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            camlock.lockPart = c.part
            camlock.lastLockPart = c.part
            silentAim.part = c.part
            paint()
        end)
        table.insert(btns, b)
    end
    paint()

end

function Builders.buildCamlockSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Builders.buildCamlockMainSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
end

function Builders.buildAimbotPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Utils.uiFeatAim = Utils.uiFeatAim or {
        targets = false,
        camlock = false,
        easegraph = false,
        zoom = false,
        silent = false,
        flame = false,
        trigger = false,
        hitbox = false,
    }
    local F = Utils.uiFeatAim
    local left = UILeft or PageScroll
    local right = UIRight or PageScroll
    local isHC = (selectedGameMode == "Hood Customs")

    local master = UI.groupBox(left, "Features")
    local settingsWrap = UI.groupBox(right, "Settings")
    local rightHost = Instance.new("Frame")
    rightHost.Name = "AimFeatureSettings"
    rightHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    rightHost.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    rightHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rightHost.Parent = settingsWrap
    local rightLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    rightLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    rightLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    rightLay.Parent = rightHost

    local emptyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    emptyLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    emptyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    emptyLbl.Text = "Select a feature"
    emptyLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    emptyLbl.TextSize = 12
    emptyLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    emptyLbl.Parent = rightHost
    UI.regFont(emptyLbl, 12)

    local function clearRight()
        for _, ch in ipairs(rightHost:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch ~= emptyLbl then
                ch:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    local function buildRight()
        clearRight()
        if isHC then F.flame = false end
        local any = F.targets or F.camlock or F.easegraph or F.zoom or F.silent or F.flame or F.trigger or F.hitbox
        emptyLbl.Visible = not any
        if not any then return end

        if F.targets then
            pcall(function() Builders.buildTargetSelectSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
        end
        if F.easegraph then
            pcall(function()
                Builders.buildEaseGraphSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost)
            end)
        end
        if F.camlock then
            pcall(function() Builders.buildCamlockMainSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
        end
        if F.zoom and selectedGameMode ~= "MM2" then
            pcall(function() Builders.buildCamlockZoomSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
        end
        if F.silent then
            if selectedGameMode == "MM2" then
                local box = UI.groupBox(rightHost, "Silent Aim (BETA)")
                UI.groupToggle(box, "Silent Aim (Click)", Utils.mm2.silent, function(t, i)
                    Utils.mm2SetSilent(not Utils.mm2.silent)
                    UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.silent)
                end)
            elseif selectedGameMode ~= "Universal" then
                pcall(function() Builders.buildSilentAimSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
            end
        end
        if F.flame and not isHC then
            pcall(function() Builders.buildFlameLockSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
        end
        if F.trigger then
            pcall(function() Builders.buildTriggerSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
        end
        if F.hitbox and not isHC then
            pcall(function() Builders.buildHitboxSection(PageScroll, rightHost, rightHost) end)
        end
        if UI.animateHostChildren then UI.animateHostChildren(rightHost) end
    end

    local function addMaster(label, key, show)
        if show == false then return end
        UI.groupToggle(master, label, F[key], function(t, i)
            F[key] = not F[key]
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, F[key])
            buildRight()
        end)
    end

    addMaster("Target Select", "targets", true)
    addMaster("Cam Lock", "camlock", true)
    addMaster("Ease Graph", "easegraph", true)
    addMaster("Zoom Aim", "zoom", selectedGameMode ~= "MM2")
    addMaster("Silent Aim", "silent", selectedGameMode ~= "Universal" or selectedGameMode == "MM2")
    addMaster("Flame Lock", "flame", not isHC)
    addMaster("Triggerbot", "trigger", true)
    addMaster("Hitbox Expander", "hitbox", not isHC)

    buildRight()
end

    -- kept for compatibility; movement is built as group boxes

function Builders._unusedMovementStart(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local box = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 44)
    UI.uiLabel(box, "Speed", UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0))
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(box, UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, 0), speedKeybind)
    R.speedKeybindBtn = keyBtn
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        listeningForSpeedKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
    R.speedToggleBtn = tog
    R.speedToggleIndicator = ind
    local extra = UI.uiExtra(UILeft)
    R.speedExtra = extra
    UI.createRangeSlider(extra, "Walkspeed", currentSpeedValue, 500, function(v) currentSpeedValue = v end)
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(extra, 36)
        UI.createOptionToggle(row, "CFrame", 6, speedUseCFrame, function(v)
            speedUseCFrame = v
            local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
            if hum and speedActive then
                hum.WalkSpeed = speedUseCFrame and originalWalkSpeed or currentSpeedValue
            end
        end)
    end
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, speedActive)
    UI.applyContainerState(extra, speedActive, UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 86))
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        speedActive = not speedActive
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, speedActive)
        if speedActive then
            extra.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(extra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 86)}):Play()
        else
            local tw = TweenService:Create(extra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
            tw:Play()
            tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                if not speedActive then extra.Visible = false end
            end)
            local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
            if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = originalWalkSpeed end
        end
    end)
end



function Builders.buildMovementJumpSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local box = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 44)
    UI.uiLabel(box, "Jump Power")
    local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
    local extra = UI.uiExtra(UILeft)
    UI.createRangeSlider(extra, "Jump Power", currentJumpPower, 200, function(v) currentJumpPower = v end)
    UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, jumpPowerActive)
    UI.applyContainerState(extra, jumpPowerActive, UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44))
    tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        jumpPowerActive = not jumpPowerActive
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, jumpPowerActive)
        if jumpPowerActive then
            extra.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(extra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)}):Play()
        else
            local tw = TweenService:Create(extra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
            tw:Play()
            tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                if not jumpPowerActive then extra.Visible = false end
            end)
            local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
            if hum then hum.JumpPower = originalJumpPower end
        end
    end)
end



function Builders.buildMovementFlySection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Infinite Jump")
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, infiniteJumpActive)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            infiniteJumpActive = not infiniteJumpActive
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, infiniteJumpActive)
        end)
    end

    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 44)
        box.Visible = false -- Fly hidden for now
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Fly", UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0))
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(box, UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, 0), flyKeybind)
        R.flyKeybindBtn = keyBtn
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            listeningForFlyKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        R.flyToggleBtn = tog
        R.flyToggleIndicator = ind
        local extra = UI.uiExtra(UIRight)
        extra.Visible = false
        R.flyExtra = extra
