        UI.createRangeSlider(extra, "Fly Speed", currentFlySpeed, 500, function(v) currentFlySpeed = v end)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, flyActive)
        UI.applyContainerState(extra, flyActive, UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44))
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.toggleFly(not flyActive)
        end)
    end

    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Spinbot", UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0))
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(box, UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, 0), spinKeybind)
        R.spinKeybindBtn = keyBtn
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            listeningForSpinKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, spinbotActive)
        local spinExtra = UI.uiExtra(UIRight)
        UI.createRangeSlider(spinExtra, "Spin Speed", spinbotSpeed, 50, function(v) spinbotSpeed = math.max(1, v) end)
        UI.applyContainerState(spinExtra, spinbotActive, UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44))
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.setSpinbot(not spinbotActive)
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, spinbotActive)
            if spinbotActive then
                spinExtra.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(spinExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)}):Play()
            else
                local tw = TweenService:Create(spinExtra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
                tw:Play()
                tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                    if not spinbotActive then spinExtra.Visible = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Noclip")
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, noclipActive)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            noclipActive = not noclipActive
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, noclipActive)
            if not noclipActive then
                local char = Player.Character
                if char then
                    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            part.CanCollide = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end



function Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local only = Builders._movBuildSection -- nil = build all (legacy)
    local _groupBox = UI.groupBox
    if only then
        UI.groupBox = function(parent, title, ...)
            local map = {
                Speed = "speed", ["Quick Stop"] = "quickstop", ["Instant Fall"] = "instantfall",
                ["Hip Height"] = "hip", Freecam = "freecam", Fly = "fly", Jump = "jump",
                Gravity = "gravity", Spinbot = "spin", Spin = "spin",
            }
            local key = map[title]
            if key and key ~= only then
                -- dummy invisible host so layout doesn't break
                local f = Instance.new("Frame")
                f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                f.Visible = false
                f.Parent = parent
                return f
            end
            return _groupBox(parent, title, ...)
        end
    end
    local function keyToggleRow(parent, label, getBind, setListening, getState, setState, btnStoreKey)
        local row = UI.uiSection(parent, 32)
        UI.uiLabel(row, label, UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), getBind())
        if btnStoreKey then R[btnStoreKey] = keyBtn end
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            setListening(true)
            listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, getState())
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            setState(not getState())
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, getState())
        end)
        return tog, ind, keyBtn
    end

    -- LEFT: Speed / Quick Stop / Instant Fall / Hip Height (separate groups)
    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Speed")
        local row = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Enabled", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), speedKeybind)
        R.speedKeybindBtn = keyBtn
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            listeningForSpeedKey = true
            listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
        R.speedToggleBtn = tog
        R.speedToggleIndicator = ind
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, speedActive)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            speedActive = not speedActive
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, speedActive)
            if not speedActive then
                local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
                if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = originalWalkSpeed end
            end
        end)
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Walkspeed", currentSpeedValue, 500, function(v) currentSpeedValue = v end)
        keyToggleRow(box, "CFrame",
            function() return Utils.mov.cframeKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.cframeListening = v end,
            function() return speedUseCFrame end,
            function(v)
                speedUseCFrame = v
                local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
                if hum and speedActive then
                    hum.WalkSpeed = speedUseCFrame and originalWalkSpeed or currentSpeedValue
                end
            end, "cframeKeyBtn")
    if selectedGameMode ~= "MM2" then
        keyToggleRow(box, "Fake Macro",
            function() return Utils.fakeMacro.keybind end,
            function(v) Utils.fakeMacro.listening = v end,
            function() return Utils.fakeMacro.active end,
            function(v) Utils.fakeMacro.active = v; Utils.fakeMacro.last = 0 end, "macroKeyBtn")
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Macro Speed", Utils.fakeMacro.speed, 120, function(v)
            Utils.fakeMacro.speed = math.clamp(v, 1, 120)
        end)
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Macro Intensity", Utils.fakeMacro.intensity, 100, function(v)
            Utils.fakeMacro.intensity = math.clamp(v, 1, 100)
        end)
    end
    end

    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Quick Stop")
        keyToggleRow(box, "Enabled",
            function() return Utils.mov.quickStopKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.quickStopListening = v end,
            function() return Utils.mov.quickStop end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.quickStop = v end, "quickStopKeyBtn")
    end

    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Instant Fall")
        keyToggleRow(box, "Enabled",
            function() return Utils.mov.instantFallKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.instantFallListening = v end,
            function() return Utils.mov.instantFall end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.instantFall = v end, "instantFallKeyBtn")
    end

    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Hip Height")
        keyToggleRow(box, "Enabled",
            function() return Utils.mov.hipKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.hipListening = v end,
            function() return Utils.mov.hipHeight end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.hipHeight = v; Utils.movApplyHip() end, "hipKeyBtn")
        UI.createDecimalSlider(box, "Hip Height", Utils.mov.hipValue or 2, 0, 20, function(v)
            Utils.mov.hipValue = v
            if Utils.mov.hipHeight then Utils.movApplyHip() end
        end)
    end

    -- RIGHT: Freecam / Fly / Jump / Gravity / Spin
    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Freecam")
        keyToggleRow(box, "Enabled",
            function() return Utils.mov.freecamKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.freecamListening = v end,
            function() return Utils.mov.freecam end,
            function(v) Utils.movSetFreecam(v) end, "freecamKeyBtn")
    end

    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Fly")
        keyToggleRow(box, "Enabled",
            function() return Utils.mov.flyKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.flyListening = v end,
            function() return Utils.mov.fly end,
            function(v)
                Utils.mov.fly = v
                if not v then
                    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid and Utils.getHumanoid() or nil
                    if hum then pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end) end
                end
            end, "flyKeyBtn")
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Fly Speed", Utils.mov.flySpeed or 50, 200, function(v)
            Utils.mov.flySpeed = v
        end)
    end

    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Jump / Gravity")
        keyToggleRow(box, "Jump Power",
            function() return Utils.mov.jumpKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.jumpListening = v end,
            function() return jumpPowerActive end,
            function(v)
                jumpPowerActive = v
                if not jumpPowerActive then
                    local hum = Utils.getHumanoid()
                    if hum then
                        hum.JumpPower = originalJumpPower
                        pcall(function() hum.JumpHeight = originalJumpPower * 0.15 end)
                    end
                end
            end, "jumpKeyBtn")
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Jump Power", currentJumpPower, 200, function(v) currentJumpPower = v end)
        keyToggleRow(box, "Gravity",
            function() return Utils.mov.gravKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.gravListening = v end,
            function() return Utils.grav.active end,
            function(v)
                Utils.grav.active = v
                if not Utils.grav.active then
                    pcall(function() workspace.Gravity = Utils.grav.original or 196.2 end)
                else
                    pcall(function() workspace.Gravity = Utils.grav.value end)
                end
            end, "gravKeyBtn")
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Gravity", math.floor(Utils.grav.value or 196), 500, function(v)
            Utils.grav.value = v
            if Utils.grav.active then pcall(function() workspace.Gravity = v end) end
        end)
        keyToggleRow(box, "Infinite Jump",
            function() return Utils.mov.infJumpKey end,
            function(v) Utils.mov.infJumpListening = v end,
            function() return infiniteJumpActive end,
            function(v) infiniteJumpActive = v end, "infJumpKeyBtn")
        local row = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Spinbot", UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(row, UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, 0), spinKeybind)
        R.spinKeybindBtn = keyBtn
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            listeningForSpinKey = true
            listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0))
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, spinbotActive)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.setSpinbot(not spinbotActive)
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, spinbotActive)
        end)
        UI.createRangeSlider(box, "Spin Speed", spinbotSpeed, 50, function(v) spinbotSpeed = math.max(1, v) end)
    end
    if only then UI.groupBox = _groupBox end
end

function Builders.buildEspPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local only = Builders._espSection
    local function want(key)
        return (not only) or only == key
    end
    local function one(parent, title, key, get, set, colGet, colSet)
        if not want(key) then return end
        local box = UI.groupBox(parent, title)
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Enabled", get(), function(tog, ind)
            set(not get())
            espActive = Utils.espAnyOn()
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, get())
            if not Utils.espAnyOn() then
                clearAllESP()
            end
        end, colGet(), colSet)
    end
    one(UILeft, "Names", "names", function() return espShowNames end, function(v) espShowNames = v end, function() return espObjects.cols.name end, function(c) espObjects.cols.name = c end)
    one(UILeft, "Distance", "distance", function() return espShowDistance end, function(v) espShowDistance = v end, function() return espObjects.cols.dist end, function(c) espObjects.cols.dist = c end)
    one(UIRight, "Box", "box", function() return espShowBox end, function(v) espShowBox = v end, function() return espObjects.cols.box end, function(c) espObjects.cols.box = c end)
    if want("box") then
    local boxOpts = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Box Style")
    UI.groupToggle(boxOpts, "3D Box", Utils.espCfg.mode3d, function(t, i)
        Utils.espCfg.mode3d = not Utils.espCfg.mode3d
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.espCfg.mode3d)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(boxOpts, "Gradient Fill", Utils.espCfg.gradient, function(t, i)
        Utils.espCfg.gradient = not Utils.espCfg.gradient
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.espCfg.gradient)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(boxOpts, "Glow", Utils.espCfg.glow, function(t, i)
        Utils.espCfg.glow = not Utils.espCfg.glow
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.espCfg.glow)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(boxOpts, "Corners", Utils.espCfg.edges, function(t, i)
        Utils.espCfg.edges = not Utils.espCfg.edges
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.espCfg.edges)
    end)
    end
    one(UIRight, "Health Bar", "health", function() return espShowHealth end, function(v) espShowHealth = v end, function() return espObjects.cols.health end, function(c) espObjects.cols.health = c end)
    if want("skeleton") then
    local skel = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Skeleton")
    UI.groupToggle(skel, "Enabled", Utils.espCfg.skeleton, function(t, i)
        Utils.espCfg.skeleton = not Utils.espCfg.skeleton
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.espCfg.skeleton)
        if not Utils.espCfg.skeleton then
            for _, obj in pairs(espObjects) do
                Utils.espHideLines(obj, "s")
            end
        end
    end, Utils.espCfg.skelCol, function(c) Utils.espCfg.skelCol = c end)
    end
    if want("extra") then
    local extra = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Extra")
    UI.groupToggle(extra, "Engine Health", Utils.espCfg.nativeHp, function(t, i)
        Utils.espCfg.nativeHp = not Utils.espCfg.nativeHp
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.espCfg.nativeHp)
        pcall(Utils.espNativeHealth, Utils.espCfg.nativeHp)
    end)
    end
    if selectedGameMode == "MM2" then
        local roles = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Roles (BETA)")
        UI.groupToggle(roles, "Murderer ESP", Utils.mm2.murdererEsp, function(t, i)
            Utils.mm2.murdererEsp = not Utils.mm2.murdererEsp
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.murdererEsp)
            Utils.mm2UpdateRoles()
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(roles, "Sheriff ESP", Utils.mm2.sheriffEsp, function(t, i)
            Utils.mm2.sheriffEsp = not Utils.mm2.sheriffEsp
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.sheriffEsp)
            Utils.mm2UpdateRoles()
        end)
        local drops = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Drops")
        UI.groupToggle(drops, "Gun Drop ESP", Utils.mm2.gunDropEsp, function(t, i)
            Utils.mm2SetGunDropEsp(not Utils.mm2.gunDropEsp)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.gunDropEsp)
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(drops, "See Traps", Utils.mm2.trapEsp, function(t, i)
            Utils.mm2SetTrapEsp(not Utils.mm2.trapEsp)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.trapEsp)
        end)
    end
end




function Builders.buildVisualFxSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local world = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "World")
    UI.groupToggle(world, "Fullbright", fullbrightActive, function(t, i)
        Utils.applyFullbright(not fullbrightActive)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, fullbrightActive)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(world, "Force Time", forceTimeActive, function(t, i)
        forceTimeActive = not forceTimeActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, forceTimeActive)
        if forceTimeActive then
            originalClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime
            Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue
        else
            pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = originalClockTime end)
        end
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(world, "Hour (0-24)", forceTimeValue, 0, 24, function(v)
        forceTimeValue = v
        if forceTimeActive then Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue end
    end)
end

function Builders.buildEffectsPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local only = Builders._fxSection
    local trailBox = nil
    if not only or only == "trail" then
    trailBox = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Trail")
    UI.groupToggle(trailBox, "Enabled", trail.active, function(t, i)
        applyTrail(not trail.active)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, trail.active)
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(trailBox, "Lifetime", trail.lifetime, 0.1, 5, function(v)
        trail.lifetime = v
        if trail.active then applyTrail(true) end
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(trailBox, "Width", trail.width, 0.02, 1, function(v)
        trail.width = v
        if trail.active then applyTrail(true) end
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(trailBox, "Light", trail.lightEmission, 0, 5, function(v)
        trail.lightEmission = v
        if trail.active then applyTrail(true) end
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(trailBox, "Brightness", trail.brightness, 0, 20, function(v)
        trail.brightness = v
        if trail.active then applyTrail(true) end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(trailBox, "Trail Color", trail.color1, function(c)
        trail.color1 = c
        trail.color2 = Color3.new(math.min(1, c.R * 1.2), math.min(1, c.G * 1.2), math.min(1, c.B * 1.2))
        if trail.active then applyTrail(true) end
    end)

    end -- trail block
    if only and only ~= "aura" then return end
    local auraBox = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Aura")
    UI.groupToggle(auraBox, "Enabled", aura.enabled, function(t, i)
        setAuraEnabled(not aura.enabled)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, aura.enabled)
    end)
    local names = {"Aura 1", "Aura 2", "Aura 3", "Aura 4", "Aura 5", "Aura 6"}
    local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
    dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    dropBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dropBtn.Text = "  " .. tostring(aura.selected) .. "   ▼"
    dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    dropBtn.TextSize = 11
    dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    dropBtn.Parent = auraBox
    UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
    local list = Instance.new("Frame")
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.ClipsDescendants = true
    list.Visible = false
    list.Parent = auraBox
    local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    listLay.Parent = list
    local open = false
    for _, n in ipairs(names) do
        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.Text = "  " .. n
        row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        row.TextSize = 11
        row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        row.AutoButtonColor = false
        row.Parent = list
        UI.regFont(row, 11)
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            aura.selected = n
            dropBtn.Text = "  " .. n .. "   ▼"
            if aura.enabled then applyAura(n) end
            open = false
            list.Visible = false
            list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        end)
    end
    dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        open = not open
        list.Visible = open
        list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, #names * 26) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        dropBtn.Text = "  " .. tostring(aura.selected) .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
    end)
end


function Builders.buildFogSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local body = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Fog")
    UI.groupToggle(body, "Enabled", fogActive, function(t, i)
        fogActive = not fogActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, fogActive)
        if fogActive then
            Lighting.FogStart = currentFogStart
            Lighting.FogEnd = currentFogEnd
            Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(currentR, currentG, currentB)
        else
            Lighting.FogStart = originalFogStart
            Lighting.FogEnd = originalFogEnd
            Lighting.FogColor = originalFogColor
        end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(body, "Fog Start", currentFogStart, 500, function(v)
        currentFogStart = v
        if fogActive then Lighting.FogStart = v end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(body, "Fog End", currentFogEnd, 2000, function(v)
        currentFogEnd = v
        if fogActive then Lighting.FogEnd = v end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(body, "Fog Color", Color3.fromRGB(currentR, currentG, currentB), function(c)
        currentR = math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5)
        currentG = math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5)
        currentB = math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)
        if fogActive then Lighting.FogColor = c end
    end)
end



function Builders.buildAmbienceSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local body = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Ambience")
    UI.groupToggle(body, "Enabled", ambienceActive, function(t, i)
        ambienceActive = not ambienceActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, ambienceActive)
        if ambienceActive then
            Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(ambR, ambG, ambB)
        else
            Lighting.Ambient = originalAmbient
        end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(body, "Ambient Color", Color3.fromRGB(ambR, ambG, ambB), function(c)
        ambR = math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5)
        ambG = math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5)
        ambB = math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)
        if ambienceActive then Lighting.Ambient = c end
    end)
end



function Builders.buildBrightnessSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local body = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Brightness")
    UI.groupToggle(body, "Enabled", brightnessActive, function(t, i)
        brightnessActive = not brightnessActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, brightnessActive)
        Lighting.Brightness = brightnessActive and currentBrightness or originalBrightness
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(body, "Brightness", currentBrightness, 0, 10, function(v)
        currentBrightness = v
        if brightnessActive then Lighting.Brightness = v end
    end)
end



function Builders.buildCameraFovSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local body = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Camera FOV")
    UI.groupToggle(body, "Enabled", fovActive, function(t, i)
        fovActive = not fovActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, fovActive)
        workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = fovActive and currentFovValue or originalFov
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(body, "FOV", currentFovValue, 120, function(v)
        currentFovValue = v
        if fovActive then workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = v end
    end)
end



function Builders.buildVisualLightingSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local light = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Lighting")
    if false then
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Text = Utils.realLight.used and "Realistic Lighting  (applied)" or "Realistic Lighting"
        btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        btn.TextSize = 12
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        btn.Parent = light
        UI.addCorner(btn, UDim.new(0, 10))
        UI.regCard(btn)
        UI.regFont(btn, 12)
        if Utils.realLight.used then
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        end
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            if Utils.realLight.used then return end
            Utils.applyRealisticLighting()
            btn.Text = "Realistic Lighting  (applied)"
        end)
    end
    UI.groupToggle(light, "Fog", fogActive, function(t, i)
        fogActive = not fogActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, fogActive)
        if fogActive then
            Lighting.FogStart = currentFogStart
            Lighting.FogEnd = currentFogEnd
            Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(currentR, currentG, currentB)
        else
            Lighting.FogStart = originalFogStart
            Lighting.FogEnd = originalFogEnd
            Lighting.FogColor = originalFogColor
        end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(light, "Fog Start", currentFogStart, 500, function(v)
        currentFogStart = v
        if fogActive then Lighting.FogStart = v end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(light, "Fog End", currentFogEnd, 2000, function(v)
        currentFogEnd = v
        if fogActive then Lighting.FogEnd = v end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(light, "Fog Color", Color3.fromRGB(currentR, currentG, currentB), function(c)
        currentR = math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5)
        currentG = math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5)
        currentB = math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)
        if fogActive then Lighting.FogColor = c end
    end)

    local bright = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Brightness")
    UI.groupToggle(bright, "Enabled", brightnessActive, function(t, i)
        brightnessActive = not brightnessActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, brightnessActive)
        Lighting.Brightness = brightnessActive and currentBrightness or originalBrightness
    end)
    UI.createDecimalSlider(bright, "Brightness", currentBrightness, 0, 10, function(v)
        currentBrightness = v
        if brightnessActive then Lighting.Brightness = v end
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(bright, "Camera FOV", fovActive, function(t, i)
        fovActive = not fovActive
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, fovActive)
        workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = fovActive and currentFovValue or originalFov
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(bright, "FOV", currentFovValue, 120, function(v)
        currentFovValue = v
        if fovActive then workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = v end
    end)

    local post = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Post FX")
    UI.groupToggle(post, "Bloom", Utils.pl.bloomOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.pl.bloomOn = not Utils.pl.bloomOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.pl.bloomOn)
        Utils.plFx("Bloom")
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Bloom Intensity", Utils.pl.bloomIntensity, 500, function(v)
        Utils.pl.bloomIntensity = v
        if Utils.pl.bloomOn then Utils.plFx("Bloom") end
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(post, "Sun Rays", Utils.pl.sunraysOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.pl.sunraysOn = not Utils.pl.sunraysOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.pl.sunraysOn)
        Utils.plFx("SunRays")
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Sun Rays Intensity", Utils.pl.sunraysIntensity, 100, function(v)
        Utils.pl.sunraysIntensity = v
        if Utils.pl.sunraysOn then Utils.plFx("SunRays") end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Sun Rays Spread", Utils.pl.sunraysSpread, 100, function(v)
        Utils.pl.sunraysSpread = v
        if Utils.pl.sunraysOn then Utils.plFx("SunRays") end
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(post, "Depth of Field", Utils.pl.dofOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.pl.dofOn = not Utils.pl.dofOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.pl.dofOn)
        Utils.plFx("DoF")
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Focus Distance", Utils.pl.dofFocus, 200, function(v)
        Utils.pl.dofFocus = v
        if Utils.pl.dofOn then Utils.plFx("DoF") end
    end)

    Utils.env = Utils.env or {
        outdoorOn = false,
        outdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        tintOn = false,
        tint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        origOutdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
    }
    Builders.buildAmbienceSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)

    local outdoor = UI.groupBox(UIRight, "Outdoor")
    UI.groupToggle(outdoor, "Enabled", Utils.env.outdoorOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.env.outdoorOn = not Utils.env.outdoorOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.env.outdoorOn)
        if Utils.env.outdoorOn then
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Utils.env.outdoor
        else
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Utils.env.origOutdoor
        end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(outdoor, "Outdoor Ambient", Utils.env.outdoor, function(c)
        Utils.env.outdoor = c
        if Utils.env.outdoorOn then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = c end
    end)

    local tint = UI.groupBox(UILeft, "Tint")
    UI.groupToggle(tint, "Enabled", Utils.env.tintOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.env.tintOn = not Utils.env.tintOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.env.tintOn)
        local cc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysTintCC")
        if Utils.env.tintOn then
            if not cc then
                cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                cc.Name = "RaysTintCC"
                cc.Parent = Lighting
            end
            cc.Enabled = true
            cc.TintColor = Utils.env.tint
        elseif cc then
            cc.Enabled = false
        end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(tint, "Tint Color", Utils.env.tint, function(c)
        Utils.env.tint = c
        local cc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysTintCC")
        if Utils.env.tintOn then
            if not cc then
                cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                cc.Name = "RaysTintCC"
                cc.Parent = Lighting
            end
            cc.Enabled = true
            cc.TintColor = c
        end
    end)
end




function Builders.buildBloomOnly(parent)
    local post = UI.groupBox(parent, "Bloom")
    UI.groupToggle(post, "Enabled", Utils.pl.bloomOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.pl.bloomOn = not Utils.pl.bloomOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.pl.bloomOn)
        Utils.plFx("Bloom")
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Bloom Intensity", Utils.pl.bloomIntensity, 500, function(v)
        Utils.pl.bloomIntensity = v
        if Utils.pl.bloomOn then Utils.plFx("Bloom") end
    end)
end
function Builders.buildSunraysOnly(parent)
    local post = UI.groupBox(parent, "Sunrays")
    UI.groupToggle(post, "Enabled", Utils.pl.sunraysOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.pl.sunraysOn = not Utils.pl.sunraysOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.pl.sunraysOn)
        Utils.plFx("SunRays")
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Intensity", Utils.pl.sunraysIntensity, 100, function(v)
        Utils.pl.sunraysIntensity = v
        if Utils.pl.sunraysOn then Utils.plFx("SunRays") end
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Spread", Utils.pl.sunraysSpread, 100, function(v)
        Utils.pl.sunraysSpread = v
        if Utils.pl.sunraysOn then Utils.plFx("SunRays") end
    end)
end
function Builders.buildDofOnly(parent)
    local post = UI.groupBox(parent, "Depth of Field")
    UI.groupToggle(post, "Enabled", Utils.pl.dofOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.pl.dofOn = not Utils.pl.dofOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.pl.dofOn)
        Utils.plFx("DoF")
    end)
    UI.createRangeSlider(post, "Focus Distance", Utils.pl.dofFocus, 200, function(v)
        Utils.pl.dofFocus = v
        if Utils.pl.dofOn then Utils.plFx("DoF") end
    end)
end
function Builders.buildOutdoorOnly(parent)
    Utils.env = Utils.env or {
        outdoorOn = false, outdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient, tintOn = false,
        tint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), origOutdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
    }
    local outdoor = UI.groupBox(parent, "Outdoor")
    UI.groupToggle(outdoor, "Enabled", Utils.env.outdoorOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.env.outdoorOn = not Utils.env.outdoorOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.env.outdoorOn)
        if Utils.env.outdoorOn then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Utils.env.outdoor
        else Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Utils.env.origOutdoor end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(outdoor, "Outdoor Ambient", Utils.env.outdoor, function(c)
        Utils.env.outdoor = c
        if Utils.env.outdoorOn then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = c end
    end)
end
function Builders.buildTintOnly(parent)
    Utils.env = Utils.env or {
        outdoorOn = false, outdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient, tintOn = false,
        tint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), origOutdoor = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
    }
    local tint = UI.groupBox(parent, "Tint")
    UI.groupToggle(tint, "Enabled", Utils.env.tintOn, function(t, i)
        Utils.env.tintOn = not Utils.env.tintOn
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.env.tintOn)
        local cc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysTintCC")
        if Utils.env.tintOn then
            if not cc then cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect"); cc.Name = "RaysTintCC"; cc.Parent = Lighting end
            cc.Enabled = true
            cc.TintColor = Utils.env.tint
        elseif cc then cc.Enabled = false end
    end)
    UI.createColorPicker(tint, "Tint Color", Utils.env.tint, function(c)
        Utils.env.tint = c
        local cc = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RaysTintCC")
        if Utils.env.tintOn then
            if not cc then cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect"); cc.Name = "RaysTintCC"; cc.Parent = Lighting end
            cc.Enabled = true
            cc.TintColor = c
        end
    end)
end

function Builders.buildVisualPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local only = Builders._visSection
    if only == "fullbright" then
        local world = UI.groupBox(UILeft or UIRight, "Fullbright")
        UI.groupToggle(world, "Enabled", fullbrightActive, function(t, i)
            Utils.applyFullbright(not fullbrightActive)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, fullbrightActive)
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "forcetime" then
        local world = UI.groupBox(UILeft or UIRight, "Force Time")
        UI.groupToggle(world, "Enabled", forceTimeActive, function(t, i)
            forceTimeActive = not forceTimeActive
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, forceTimeActive)
            if forceTimeActive then
                originalClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime
                Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue
            else
                pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = originalClockTime end)
            end
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(world, "Hour (0-24)", forceTimeValue, 0, 24, function(v)
            forceTimeValue = v
            if forceTimeActive then Lighting.ClockTime = forceTimeValue end
        end)
        return
    end
    if only == "fog" then
        Builders.buildFogSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "ambience" then
        Builders.buildAmbienceSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "brightness" then
        Builders.buildBrightnessSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "fov" then
        Builders.buildCameraFovSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "bloom" then
        Builders.buildBloomOnly(UILeft or UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "sunrays" then
        Builders.buildSunraysOnly(UILeft or UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "depthof" then
        Builders.buildDofOnly(UILeft or UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "outdoor" then
        Builders.buildOutdoorOnly(UILeft or UIRight)
        return
    end
    if only == "tint" then
        Builders.buildTintOnly(UILeft or UIRight)
        return
    end
    -- full page (no filter)
    Builders.buildVisualFxSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Builders.buildFogSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Builders.buildAmbienceSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Builders.buildBrightnessSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Builders.buildCameraFovSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    Builders.buildVisualLightingSection(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)

    -- Crosshair Cursor moved to Utilities
    if false then do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UIRight, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Crosshair Cursor")
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        R.cursorToggleBtn = tog
        R.cursorToggleInd = ind
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, customCursor.enabled)

        local extra = Instance.new("Frame")
        extra.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        extra.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        extra.BorderSizePixel = 0
        extra.ClipsDescendants = true
        extra.Visible = false
        extra.Parent = UIRight
        UI.addCorner(extra, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        lay.Parent = extra
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pad.Parent = extra

        local textBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
        textBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        textBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        textBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        textBox.Text = customCursor.text
        textBox.PlaceholderText = "Crosshair text..."
        textBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        textBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        textBox.TextSize = 11
        textBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        textBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        textBox.Parent = extra
        UI.addCorner(textBox, UDim.new(0, 4))
        UI.regFont(textBox, 11)
        textBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            customCursor.text = textBox.Text ~= "" and textBox.Text or "Ray's Panel"
            textBox.Text = customCursor.text
            Utils.applyCursorVisuals()
        end)

        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Width", customCursor.width, 0.5, 5, function(v)
            customCursor.width = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Length", customCursor.length, 1, 40, function(v)
            customCursor.length = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Radius", customCursor.radius, 0, 40, function(v)
            customCursor.radius = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Spin Speed", customCursor.spin_speed, 0, 400, function(v)
            customCursor.spin_speed = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Resize Speed", customCursor.resize_speed, 0, 400, function(v)
            customCursor.resize_speed = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Resize Min", customCursor.resize_min, 1, 30, function(v)
            customCursor.resize_min = v
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(extra, "Resize Max", customCursor.resize_max, 5, 50, function(v)
            customCursor.resize_max = v
        end)

        local function miniToggle(parent, name, get, set)
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            row.Parent = parent
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Text = name
            lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.Parent = row
            UI.regFont(lbl, 11)
            local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
            t.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -10)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, get())
            t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                set(not get())
                UI.applyToggleState(t, i, get())
            end)
        end

        miniToggle(extra, "Spin", function() return customCursor.spin end, function(v) customCursor.spin = v end)
        miniToggle(extra, "Resize Pulse", function() return customCursor.resize end, function(v) customCursor.resize = v end)
        miniToggle(extra, "Hide Default Cursor", function() return customCursor.hideDefault end, function(v)
            customCursor.hideDefault = v
            pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = not (customCursor.enabled and v) end)
        end)
        miniToggle(extra, "Center Mode", function() return customCursor.mode == "center" end, function(v)
            customCursor.mode = v and "center" or "mouse"
        end)

        UI.createColorPicker(extra, "Cursor Color", customCursor.color, function(c)
            customCursor.color = c
            Utils.applyCursorVisuals()
        end)

        local EXTRA_H = 780
        local function openExtra(open)
            if open then
                extra.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(extra, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, EXTRA_H)}):Play()
            else
                local tw = TweenService:Create(extra, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
                tw:Play()
                tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                    if not customCursor.enabled then extra.Visible = false end
                end)
            end
        end

        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.setCustomCursor(not customCursor.enabled)
            openExtra(customCursor.enabled)
        end)
        if customCursor.enabled then
            extra.Visible = true
            extra.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, EXTRA_H)
        end
    end end
end

function Builders.buildStatsPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        local function createStatRow(labelText, valueText)
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 36)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Parent = PageScroll
            UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 6))

            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Text = labelText
            lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.Parent = row
            UI.regFont(lbl, 11)

            local val = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            val.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0)
            val.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
            val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            val.Text = valueText
            val.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            val.TextSize = 11
            val.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            val.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            val.Parent = row
            UI.regFont(val, 11)
            return val
        end

        local gameNameLbl = createStatRow("Game", "Loading...")
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, info = pcall(function()
                return Utils.MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
            end)
            if ok and info and info.Name and gameNameLbl.Parent then
                gameNameLbl.Text = info.Name
            elseif gameNameLbl.Parent then
                gameNameLbl.Text = "Unknown"
            end
        end)

        createStatRow("Place ID", tostring(game.PlaceId))
        createStatRow("Server Job ID", tostring(game.JobId))
        R.statsPlayerCountLbl = createStatRow("Players Online", tostring(#Players:GetPlayers()))
        R.statsMaxPlayersLbl = createStatRow("Max Players", tostring(Players.MaxPlayers))
        createStatRow("Username", Player.Name)
        createStatRow("User ID", tostring(Player.UserId))
        createStatRow("Account Age (days)", tostring(Player.AccountAge))

        local regionLbl = createStatRow("Region", "Loading...")
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, region = pcall(function()
                return Utils.LocalizationService:GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync(Player)
            end)
            if ok and region and regionLbl.Parent then
                regionLbl.Text = region
            elseif regionLbl.Parent then
                regionLbl.Text = "N/A"
            end
        end)

        R.statsFpsLbl = createStatRow("FPS", tostring(currentFps))

end

function Builders.buildSettingsPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    -- Direct settings layout (no left "Features" master toggles)
    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(PageScroll, "Interface")
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Show Keybinds List", (Utils.keybindsHud and Utils.keybindsHud.enabled) or false, function(t, i)
            Utils.keybindsHud = Utils.keybindsHud or { enabled = false }
            Utils.keybindsHud.enabled = not Utils.keybindsHud.enabled
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.keybindsHud.enabled)
            pcall(Utils.refreshKeybindsHud)
        end)
    end
    do
        local box = UI.groupBox(PageScroll, "Theme Colors")
        UI.createColorPicker(box, "Accent", Utils.themeCustom.accent or Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(168, 168, 178), function(c)
            Utils.themeCustom.accent = c
            Theme.Accent = c
            pcall(Utils.applyAccentEverywhere)
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(box, "Text", Utils.themeCustom.text or Theme.Text or Color3.fromRGB(228, 228, 234), function(c)
            Utils.themeCustom.text = c
            pcall(Utils.applyThemeOverrides)
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(box, "Outline", Utils.themeCustom.outlineOn ~= false, function(t, i)
            Utils.themeCustom.outlineOn = not (Utils.themeCustom.outlineOn ~= false)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.themeCustom.outlineOn)
            if Utils.ui and Utils.ui.MainStroke then
                Utils.ui.MainStroke.Enabled = Utils.themeCustom.outlineOn
            end
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(box, "Outline", Utils.themeCustom.outline or Theme.Border or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), function(c)
            Utils.themeCustom.outline = c
            pcall(Utils.applyThemeOverrides)
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(box, "Background", Utils.themeCustom.bg or Theme.CardColor or Theme.GradMid or Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 26), function(c)
            Utils.themeCustom.bg = c
            pcall(Utils.applyThemeOverrides)
        end)
        local refreshBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        refreshBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        refreshBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        refreshBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        refreshBtn.Text = "Refresh Colors"
        refreshBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        refreshBtn.TextSize = 11
        refreshBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        refreshBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        refreshBtn.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(refreshBtn, 11)
        refreshBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            pcall(Utils.applyThemeOverrides)
            pcall(Utils.applyAccentEverywhere)
        end)
        local resetRow = UI.uiSection(box, 32)
        local resetBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        resetBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        resetBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        resetBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        resetBtn.Text = "Reset Theme to Default"
        resetBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        resetBtn.TextSize = 11
        resetBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        resetBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        resetBtn.Parent = resetRow
        UI.addCorner(resetBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(resetBtn, 11)
        resetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.themeCustom.accent = nil
            Utils.themeCustom.text = nil
            Utils.themeCustom.outline = nil
            Utils.themeCustom.bg = nil
            local preset = ThemePresets[currentThemeName]
            if preset then
                for k, v in pairs(preset) do Theme[k] = v end
            end
            pcall(function() Utils.applyTheme(currentThemeName) end)
            pcall(Utils.applyThemeOverrides)
            pcall(Utils.applyAccentEverywhere)
            if Builders.rebuildPageUI then
                Builders.rebuildPageUI(currentActiveTab or "SETTINGS")
            end
        end)
    end
    if false then
        local box = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Theme")
        local names = THEME_NAMES
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  " .. currentThemeName .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        lay.Parent = list
        local open = false
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do
            local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            row.Text = "  " .. n
            row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            row.TextSize = 11
            row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            row.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(row, 11)
            row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                Utils.applyTheme(n)
                if Builders.rebuildPageUI then
                    Builders.rebuildPageUI(currentActiveTab or "SETTINGS")
                end
            end)
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            open = not open
            list.Visible = open
            list.Size = open and UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, #names * 28) or UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            dropBtn.Text = "  " .. currentThemeName .. (open and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
        end)
    end

    if false and Utils.themeHasBgImage(currentThemeName) then
        local row = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Background Image", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, panelBgVisible)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            panelBgVisible = not panelBgVisible
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, panelBgVisible)
            Utils.updatePanelBgImage()
        end)
    end

    if false then
        local row = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 40)
        UI.uiLabel(row, "Glass Background", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, glassUIActive)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.applyGlassUI(not glassUIActive)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, glassUIActive)
        end)
    end

    UI.createRangeSlider(PageScroll, "FPS Cap", fpsCap, 2000, function(v)
        fpsCap = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 1, 2000)
        pcall(function()
            if setfpscap then setfpscap(fpsCap) end
        end)
    end)

        -- Font Size Section (no accent picker)
        local FontSizeMain = Instance.new("Frame")
        FontSizeMain.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)
        FontSizeMain.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        FontSizeMain.BorderSizePixel = 0
        FontSizeMain.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(FontSizeMain, UDim.new(0, 6))

        UI.createDecimalSlider(FontSizeMain, "Font Size", currentFontSize, 0.5, 2.0, function(v)
            currentFontSize = v
            applyFontSize()
        end)
        -- Hide Keybind Section
        local HideKeyContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
        HideKeyContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)
        HideKeyContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        HideKeyContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
        HideKeyContainer.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(HideKeyContainer, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(HideKeyContainer)

        local HideKeyLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        HideKeyLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0)
        HideKeyLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
        HideKeyLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        HideKeyLabel.Text = "Hide Script Keybind"
        HideKeyLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        HideKeyLabel.TextSize = 12
        HideKeyLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        HideKeyLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        HideKeyLabel.Parent = HideKeyContainer
        UI.regFont(HideKeyLabel, 12)

        local HideKeyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        HideKeyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 24)
        HideKeyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -82, 0.5, -12)
        HideKeyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        HideKeyBtn.Text = bindDisplayName(hideKeybind)
        HideKeyBtn.TextColor3 = hideKeybind and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
        HideKeyBtn.TextSize = 11
        HideKeyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        HideKeyBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        HideKeyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        HideKeyBtn.Parent = HideKeyContainer
        UI.addCorner(HideKeyBtn, UDim.new(0, 4))
        UI.styleCard(HideKeyBtn)
        UI.regFont(HideKeyBtn, 11)
        R.hideKeybindBtn = HideKeyBtn

        HideKeyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            listeningForHideKey = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            HideKeyBtn.Text = "..."
            HideKeyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)


end

function Builders.buildConfigsPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        local ConfigBox = Instance.new("Frame")
        ConfigBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 286)
        ConfigBox.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
        ConfigBox.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        ConfigBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ConfigBox.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(ConfigBox, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(ConfigBox)

        local ConfigTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        ConfigTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0.38, -12, 0, 26)
        ConfigTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4)
        ConfigTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ConfigTitle.Text = "Config Profiles"
        ConfigTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        ConfigTitle.TextSize = 12
        ConfigTitle.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        ConfigTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        ConfigTitle.Parent = ConfigBox
        UI.regFont(ConfigTitle, 12)

        local ConfigStatus = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        ConfigStatus.Size = UDim2.new(0.62, -12, 0, 26)
        ConfigStatus.Position = UDim2.new(0.38, 0, 0, 4)
        ConfigStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ConfigStatus.Text = Config.lastStatus or (Config.supported() and ("Active: " .. Config.activeName) or "File API unsupported")
        ConfigStatus.TextColor3 = (Config.lastStatus and Config.lastStatusGood) and Theme.Accent or Theme.TextDark
        ConfigStatus.TextSize = 10
        ConfigStatus.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        ConfigStatus.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        ConfigStatus.Parent = ConfigBox
        UI.regFont(ConfigStatus, 10)
        R.configStatusLbl = ConfigStatus

        local ConfigName = Instance.new("TextBox")
        ConfigName.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 30)
        ConfigName.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 32)
        ConfigName.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
        ConfigName.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        ConfigName.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ConfigName.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        ConfigName.PlaceholderText = "config name"
        ConfigName.Text = Config.activeName or "default"
        ConfigName.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        ConfigName.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        ConfigName.TextSize = 11
        ConfigName.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        ConfigName.Parent = ConfigBox
        UI.addCorner(ConfigName, UDim.new(0, 5))
        UI.regFont(ConfigName, 11)

        local function selectedConfigName()
            local name = Config.sanitizeName(ConfigName.Text)
            ConfigName.Text = name
            return name
        end

        local ConfigListTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        ConfigListTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -104, 0, 20)
        ConfigListTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 112)
        ConfigListTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ConfigListTitle.Text = "Saved configs"
        ConfigListTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        ConfigListTitle.TextSize = 10
        ConfigListTitle.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        ConfigListTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        ConfigListTitle.Parent = ConfigBox
        UI.regFont(ConfigListTitle, 10)

        local RefreshBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        RefreshBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 20)
        RefreshBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 112)
        RefreshBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        RefreshBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        RefreshBtn.Text = "REFRESH"
        RefreshBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        RefreshBtn.TextSize = 9
        RefreshBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        RefreshBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        RefreshBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        RefreshBtn.Parent = ConfigBox
        UI.addCorner(RefreshBtn, UDim.new(0, 4))
        UI.regFont(RefreshBtn, 9)

        local ConfigList = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        ConfigList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 138)
        ConfigList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 138)
        ConfigList.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        ConfigList.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        ConfigList.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ConfigList.ScrollBarThickness = 3
        ConfigList.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
        ConfigList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        ConfigList.Parent = ConfigBox
        UI.addCorner(ConfigList, UDim.new(0, 5))

        local ConfigListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        ConfigListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        ConfigListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
        ConfigListLayout.Parent = ConfigList
        local ConfigListPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        ConfigListPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
        ConfigListPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5)
        ConfigListPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5)
        ConfigListPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
        ConfigListPadding.Parent = ConfigList

        local function refreshConfigList()
            for _, child in ipairs(ConfigList:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end
            local names, listErr = Config.listProfiles()
            local selected = Config.sanitizeName(ConfigName.Text)
            if #names == 0 then
                local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 28)
                empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                empty.Text = listErr and "Config listing unsupported" or "No saved configs yet"
                empty.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
                empty.TextSize = 10
                empty.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                empty.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                empty.Parent = ConfigList
            else
                for _, name in ipairs(names) do
                    local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 28)
                    row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
                    row.BackgroundTransparency = (name == selected) and 0.05 or 0.35
                    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    row.AutoButtonColor = false
                    row.Text = "  " .. name .. (name == Config.activeName and "   [active]" or "")
                    row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                    row.TextSize = 10
                    row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                    row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    row.Parent = ConfigList
                    UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
                    row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        Utils.playClick()
                        ConfigName.Text = name
                        refreshConfigList()
                    end)
                end
            end
            task.defer(function()
                if ConfigList and ConfigList.Parent then
                    ConfigList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ConfigListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)
                end
            end)
        end

        ConfigListLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
            ConfigList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ConfigListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)
        end)
        RefreshBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            refreshConfigList()
        end)
        ConfigName.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            selectedConfigName()
            refreshConfigList()
        end)

        local function makeConfigButton(text, index, callback)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, -9, 0, 36)
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(index * 0.25, 6, 0, 72)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            btn.Text = text
            btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            btn.TextSize = 11
            btn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Parent = ConfigBox
            UI.addCorner(btn, UDim.new(0, 5))
            UI.regFont(btn, 11)
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                callback()
            end)
        end

        makeConfigButton("SAVE", 0, function()
            local ok, err = Config.save(selectedConfigName())
            if not ok then warn("[Zenith] Config save failed:", err) end
            refreshConfigList()
        end)
        makeConfigButton("LOAD", 1, function()
            local ok, err = Config.load(selectedConfigName(), true)
            if not ok then
                warn("[Zenith] Config load failed:", err)
                refreshConfigList()
            end
        end)
        makeConfigButton("DELETE", 2, function()
            local name = selectedConfigName()
            local ok, err = Config.delete(name)
            if not ok then warn("[Zenith] Config delete failed:", err) end
            if ok and name == Config.activeName then
                Config.rememberProfile("default")
                ConfigName.Text = "default"
            end
            refreshConfigList()
        end)
        makeConfigButton("RESET", 3, function()
            Config.rememberProfile(selectedConfigName())
            local ok, err = Config.reset(true)
            if not ok then warn("[Zenith] Config reset failed:", err) end
        end)
        refreshConfigList()

end

function Builders.buildSkinsPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local function sectionTitle(parent, text)
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = text
        t.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        t.TextSize = 12
        t.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        t.Parent = parent
        UI.regFont(t, 12)
        return t
    end

    local rebuildHooks = {}

    local function makeSkinDropdown(parent, weaponName)
        local current = skinChanger.selected[weaponName] or ""
        local labelName = weaponName:gsub("%[", ""):gsub("%]", "")

        local box = UI.uiSection(parent, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, labelName, UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), nil, 11)

        local dropOpen = false
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)

        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Visible = false
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        list.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(list)

        local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        listLay.Parent = list
        local listPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        listPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
        listPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
        listPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
        listPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
        listPad.Parent = list

        local ROW_H = 28
        local MAX_VISIBLE = 8

        local localFilter = ""
        local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
        search.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 26)
        search.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
        search.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        search.BorderSizePixel = 0
        search.Text = ""
        search.PlaceholderText = "Search skins..."
        search.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        search.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        search.TextSize = 11
        search.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        search.LayoutOrder = 0
        search.Parent = list
        UI.addCorner(search, UDim.new(0, 4))

        local function filteredSkins()
            local all = skinChanger.scanned[weaponName] or Utils.skinGetGunSkinList(weaponName) or {}
            local filter = Utils.skinLower(localFilter)
            local out = {"None"}
            for _, n in ipairs(all) do
                if filter == "" or Utils.skinLower(n):find(filter, 1, true) then
                    table.insert(out, n)
                end
            end
            return out
        end

        local function rebuildList()
            for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
            end
            current = skinChanger.selected[weaponName] or ""
            dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. (dropOpen and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
            local options = filteredSkins()
            for _, n in ipairs(options) do
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, ROW_H)
                local sel = (n == "None" and (current == "" or not current)) or (n == current)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
                row.BackgroundTransparency = sel and math.max((UI.cardTransparency() or 0.55) - 0.12, 0.2) or (UI.cardTransparency() or 0.55)
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.Text = "  " .. n
                row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                row.TextSize = 11
                row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                row.AutoButtonColor = false
                row.Parent = list
                UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
                UI.regFont(row, 11)
                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    skinChanger.uiWeapon = weaponName
                    if n == "None" then
                        Utils.skinSelect(weaponName, "")
                        current = ""
                    else
                        if not skinChanger.enabled then Utils.skinSetEnabled(true) end
                        Utils.skinSelect(weaponName, n)
                        current = n
                    end
                    dropOpen = false
                    list.Visible = false
                    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
                    dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. "   ▼"
                    rebuildList()
                end)
            end
            local contentH = #options * (ROW_H + 2) + 40
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, contentH)
            return contentH
        end

        search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            localFilter = search.Text or ""
            if dropOpen then
                local contentH = rebuildList()
                local visibleH = math.min(contentH, MAX_VISIBLE * (ROW_H + 2) + 40)
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, visibleH)
            end
        end)

        table.insert(rebuildHooks, function()
            if dropOpen then
                local contentH = rebuildList()
                local visibleH = math.min(contentH, MAX_VISIBLE * (ROW_H + 2) + 40)
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, visibleH)
            else
                current = skinChanger.selected[weaponName] or ""
                dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. "   ▼"
            end
        end)

        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            dropOpen = not dropOpen
            if dropOpen then
                if skinChanger.enabled and (not skinChanger.scanned[weaponName] or not skinChanger.scanned[weaponName][1]) then
                    pcall(Utils.skinScanInventory)
                end
                local contentH = rebuildList()
                local visibleH = math.min(contentH, MAX_VISIBLE * (ROW_H + 2) + 40)
                list.Visible = true
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, visibleH)
            else
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
                dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((skinChanger.selected[weaponName] ~= "" and skinChanger.selected[weaponName]) or "None") .. "   ▼"
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Skin Changer")
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, skinChanger.enabled)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.skinSetEnabled(not skinChanger.enabled)
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, skinChanger.enabled)
            if R.skinRefreshBrowser then pcall(R.skinRefreshBrowser) end
        end)
    end

    local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28)
    hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hint.Text = "If a skin breaks your weapon, reset your character."
    hint.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    hint.TextSize = 10
    hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    hint.TextWrapped = true
    hint.Parent = UILeft
    UI.regFont(hint, 10)

    sectionTitle(UILeft, "Main Guns")
    for _, wname in ipairs({
        "[Revolver]",
        "[Double-Barrel SG]",
        "[TacticalShotgun]",
        "[Shotgun]",
    }) do
        makeSkinDropdown(UILeft, wname)
    end

    sectionTitle(UILeft, "Melee")
    makeSkinDropdown(UILeft, "[Knife]")
    makeSkinDropdown(UILeft, "[Bat]")

    sectionTitle(UIRight, "Guns")
    for _, wname in ipairs({
        "[DrumShotgun]",
        "[Silencer]",
        "[Glock]",
        "[AK-47]",
        "[AR]",
        "[Silencer AR]",
        "[Drum Gun]",
        "[AUG]",
        "[P90]",
        "[Rifle]",
    }) do
        makeSkinDropdown(UIRight, wname)
    end

    R.skinRefreshBrowser = function()
        for _, fn in ipairs(rebuildHooks) do
            pcall(fn)
        end
    end

    if skinChanger.enabled and (not skinChanger.scanned or not next(skinChanger.scanned)) then
        task.spawn(function()
            Utils.skinScanInventory()
            if R.skinRefreshBrowser then pcall(R.skinRefreshBrowser) end
        end)
    end
end

function Builders.buildHcSkinsPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local function sectionTitle(parent, text)
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = text
        t.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        t.TextSize = 12
        t.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        t.Parent = parent
        UI.regFont(t, 12)
    end

    local rebuildHooks = {}

    local function makeHcDropdown(parent, weaponName)
        local current = Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName] or ""
        local box = UI.uiSection(parent, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, weaponName, UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), nil, 11)

        local dropOpen = false
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)

        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Visible = false
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        list.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(list)

        local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        listLay.Parent = list

        local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
        search.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 26)
        search.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
        search.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        search.BorderSizePixel = 0
        search.Text = ""
        search.PlaceholderText = "Search skins..."
        search.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        search.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        search.TextSize = 11
        search.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        search.Parent = list
        UI.addCorner(search, UDim.new(0, 4))
        UI.regFont(search, 11)

        local localFilter = ""
        local ROW_H = 28
        local MAX_VISIBLE = 8

        local function filtered()
            local all = Utils.hcSkins.scanned[weaponName] or {}
            local f = string.lower(localFilter)
            local out = { "None" }
            for _, n in ipairs(all) do
                if f == "" or string.lower(n):find(f, 1, true) then
                    table.insert(out, n)
                end
            end
            return out
        end

        local function rebuildList()
            for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
            end
            current = Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName] or ""
            dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. (dropOpen and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
            local options = filtered()
            for _, n in ipairs(options) do
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, ROW_H)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.Text = "  " .. n
                row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                row.TextSize = 11
                row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                row.Parent = list
                UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
                UI.regFont(row, 11)
                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName] = (n == "None") and "" or n
                    dropOpen = false
                    list.Visible = false
                    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
                    dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName] ~= "" and Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName]) or "None") .. "   ▼"
                    if Utils.hcSkins.enabled then Utils.hcRefreshAll() end
                end)
            end
            local contentH = 30 + #options * (ROW_H + 2)
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, contentH)
            return contentH
        end

        search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            localFilter = search.Text or ""
            if dropOpen then
                local contentH = rebuildList()
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, math.min(contentH, MAX_VISIBLE * (ROW_H + 2) + 40))
            end
        end)

        table.insert(rebuildHooks, function()
            current = Utils.hcSkins.selected[weaponName] or ""
