            dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. "   ▼"
            if dropOpen then rebuildList() end
        end)

        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            dropOpen = not dropOpen
            if dropOpen then
                if Utils.hcSkins.enabled then pcall(Utils.hcScan) end
                local contentH = rebuildList()
                list.Visible = true
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, math.min(contentH, MAX_VISIBLE * (ROW_H + 2) + 40))
            else
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(UILeft, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "HC Skin Changer")
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.hcSkins.enabled)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.hcSetEnabled(not Utils.hcSkins.enabled)
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, Utils.hcSkins.enabled)
            for _, fn in ipairs(rebuildHooks) do pcall(fn) end
        end)
    end

    local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 36)
    hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hint.Text = "Uses Hood Customs Wraps / Knives. If something breaks, reset."
    hint.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    hint.TextSize = 10
    hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    hint.TextWrapped = true
    hint.Parent = UILeft
    UI.regFont(hint, 10)

    sectionTitle(UILeft, "Skin")
    sectionTitle(UILeft, "Main Guns")
    for _, w in ipairs({ "DoubleBarrel", "Revolver", "TacticalShotgun", "Shotgun" }) do
        makeHcDropdown(UILeft, w)
    end
    sectionTitle(UILeft, "Other")
    makeHcDropdown(UILeft, "SMG")
    makeHcDropdown(UILeft, "Silencer")
    makeHcDropdown(UILeft, "Knife")

    local function makeBulletDropdown(parent, weaponName)
        local current = Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName] or ""
        local box = UI.uiSection(parent, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, weaponName, UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), nil, 11)
        local dropOpen = false
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. "   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(dropBtn)
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Visible = false
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        list.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.styleCard(list)
        local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        listLay.Parent = list
        local function rebuildList()
            for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
            end
            current = Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName] or ""
            dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((current ~= "" and current) or "None") .. (dropOpen and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
            local options = { "None" }
            for _, n in ipairs(Utils.hcSkins.bulletTypes or {}) do
                table.insert(options, n)
            end
            for _, n in ipairs(options) do
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 28)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.Text = "  " .. n
                row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                row.TextSize = 11
                row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                row.Parent = list
                UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
                UI.regFont(row, 11)
                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName] = (n == "None") and "" or n
                    dropOpen = false
                    list.Visible = false
                    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
                    dropBtn.Text = "  " .. ((Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName] ~= "" and Utils.hcSkins.bullets[weaponName]) or "None") .. "   ▼"
                    local tool = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                    if Utils.hcSkins.enabled and tool then Utils.hcApplyBullets(tool) end
                end)
            end
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 8 + #options * 30)
            return 8 + #options * 30
        end
        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            dropOpen = not dropOpen
            if dropOpen then
                pcall(Utils.hcScan)
                local h = rebuildList()
                list.Visible = true
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, math.min(h, 260))
            else
                list.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
            end
        end)
    end

    if UIRight then
        sectionTitle(UIRight, "Bullet Type")
        for _, w in ipairs(Utils.hcSkins.bulletGuns) do
            makeBulletDropdown(UIRight, w)
        end
    end

    if Utils.hcSkins.enabled then
        task.spawn(function()
            Utils.hcScan()
            for _, fn in ipairs(rebuildHooks) do pcall(fn) end
        end)
    end
end

function Builders.buildPlayerListPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local box = UI.groupBox(PageScroll, "Player List")
    local top = Instance.new("Frame")
    top.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    top.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    top.Parent = box
    local info = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    info.Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0)
    info.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    info.Text = "Friends first • refreshes every 20s"
    info.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    info.TextSize = 11
    info.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    info.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    info.Parent = top
    UI.regFont(info, 11)
    local refreshBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    refreshBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 24)
    refreshBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0.5, -12)
    refreshBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    refreshBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    refreshBtn.Text = "Refresh"
    refreshBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    refreshBtn.TextSize = 11
    refreshBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    refreshBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    refreshBtn.Parent = top
    UI.addCorner(refreshBtn, UDim.new(0, 4))
    UI.regFont(refreshBtn, 11)
    local list = Instance.new("Frame")
    list.Name = "PlayerCards"
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    list.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.Parent = box
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    lay.Parent = list
    local function paintBtn(btn, on)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Accent or Theme.Secondary
        btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    end
    local function makeAct(parent, text, xScale, onClick)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0.24, -4, 0, 22)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 2, 0, 0)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = text
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        b.TextSize = 10
        b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Parent = parent
        UI.addCorner(b, UDim.new(0, 4))
        UI.regFont(b, 10)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            onClick(b)
        end)
        return b
    end
    local function rebuild()
        for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end
        end
        local rows = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                local friend = false
                pcall(function() friend = Player:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) end)
                table.insert(rows, { plr = plr, friend = friend, name = plr.DisplayName or plr.Name })
            end
        end
        table.sort(rows, function(a, b)
            if a.friend ~= b.friend then return a.friend end
            return string.lower(a.name) < string.lower(b.name)
        end)
        local anyBl = false
        for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
            if Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(row.plr) then anyBl = true break end
        end
        if anyBl then
            whitelist.enabled = true
            for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
                if not Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(row.plr) then
                    whitelist.players[row.plr.UserId] = true
                end
            end
        end
        info.Text = #rows .. " players • friends first • 20s refresh"
        for i, row in ipairs(rows) do
            local plr = row.plr
            local card = Instance.new("Frame")
            card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 58)
            card.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
            card.BorderSizePixel = 0
            card.LayoutOrder = i
            card.Parent = list
            UI.addCorner(card, UDim.new(0, 6))
            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 22)
            title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 4)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Text = row.friend and (plr.Name .. "  (Friend)") or plr.Name
            if plr.DisplayName and plr.DisplayName ~= plr.Name then
                title.Text = (row.friend and (plr.DisplayName .. "  (@" .. plr.Name .. ")  (Friend)") or (plr.DisplayName .. "  (@" .. plr.Name .. ")"))
            elseif row.friend then
                title.Text = plr.Name .. "  (Friend)"
            end
            title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.Parent = card
            UI.regFont(title, 12)
            local acts = Instance.new("Frame")
            acts.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 22)
            acts.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 30)
            acts.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            acts.Parent = card
            local specBtn = makeAct(acts, "Spectate", 0, function(b)
                paintBtn(b, Utils.playerListSpectate(plr))
            end)
            paintBtn(specBtn, Utils.playerList.spec == plr)
            local wlBtn = makeAct(acts, "Whitelist", 0.25, function(b)
                local on = not Utils.isWhitelistedPlayer(plr)
                if Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(plr) then on = false end
                Utils.whitelistSetPlayer(plr, on)
                paintBtn(b, Utils.isWhitelistedPlayer(plr))
            end)
            paintBtn(wlBtn, Utils.isWhitelistedPlayer(plr))
            local blBtn = makeAct(acts, "Blacklist", 0.50, function(b)
                Utils.blacklistSetPlayer(plr, not Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(plr))
                rebuild()
            end)
            paintBtn(blBtn, Utils.isBlacklistedPlayer(plr))
            makeAct(acts, "Teleport", 0.75, function()
                Utils.playerListTeleport(plr)
            end)
        end
    end
    refreshBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        rebuild()
    end)
    rebuild()
    task.spawn(function()
        while list.Parent do
            task.wait(20)
            if list.Parent then rebuild() end
        end
    end)
end

function Builders.buildWhitelistPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Use Whitelist (Aim / Silent / Hitbox)")
        local tog, ind = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, whitelist.enabled)
        tog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            whitelist.enabled = not whitelist.enabled
            UI.applyToggleState(tog, ind, whitelist.enabled)
        end)
    end

    local note = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    note.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28)
    note.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    note.Text = "Click a player to whitelist them. Whitelisted players are ignored by aimbot, silent, and hitbox."
    note.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    note.TextSize = 10
    note.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    note.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    note.TextWrapped = true
    note.Parent = PageScroll
    UI.regFont(note, 10)

    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 420)
    list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
    list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    list.Parent = PageScroll
    UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
    UI.styleCard(list)

    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    lay.Parent = list
    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pad.Parent = list

    local function rebuild()
        for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
        end
        local count = 0
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                count = count + 1
                local selected = Utils.isWhitelistedPlayer(plr)
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Name = "WL_" .. tostring(plr.UserId)
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 34)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Secondary
                row.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 0.3
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.Text = ""
                row.AutoButtonColor = false
                row.Parent = list
                UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
                if selected then
                    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                    st.Thickness = 1.5
                    st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    st.Transparency = 0.35
                    st.Parent = row
                end

                local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0)
                lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
                lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                lbl.Text = plr.DisplayName ~= plr.Name and (plr.DisplayName .. " (@" .. plr.Name .. ")") or plr.Name
                lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                lbl.TextSize = 12
                lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                lbl.Parent = row
                UI.regFont(lbl, 12)

                local tag = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                tag.Size = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0, 22)
                tag.Position = UDim2.new(1, -94, 0.5, -11)
                tag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                tag.Text = selected and "Whitelisted" or "Click"
                tag.TextColor3 = selected and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDark
                tag.TextSize = 10
                tag.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                tag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
                tag.Parent = row
                UI.regFont(tag, 10)

                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    Utils.whitelistTogglePlayer(plr)
                    rebuild()
                end)
            end
        end
        if count == 0 then
            local empty = Instance.new("TextButton")
            empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 34)
            empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            empty.Text = "  No other players in this server"
            empty.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
            empty.TextSize = 11
            empty.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            empty.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            empty.AutoButtonColor = false
            empty.Parent = list
            UI.regFont(empty, 11)
            count = 1
        end
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, count * 38 + 12)
    end

    rebuild()
    Utils.track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
        task.defer(rebuild)
    end))
    Utils.track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
        if plr then whitelist.players[plr.UserId] = nil end
        task.defer(rebuild)
    end))
end

function Builders.buildMm2Page(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    local tools = UI.groupBox(UILeft or PageScroll, "Guns & Teleports (BETA)")
    local grab = Instance.new("TextButton")
    grab.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
    grab.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    grab.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    grab.BorderSizePixel = 0
    grab.Text = "Grab Gun"
    grab.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    grab.TextSize = 12
    grab.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    grab.AutoButtonColor = false
    grab.Parent = tools
    UI.addCorner(grab, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.regCard(grab)
    UI.regFont(grab, 12)
    grab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.mm2GrabGun()
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(tools, "Auto Grab Gun", Utils.mm2.autoGrab, function(t, i)
        Utils.mm2SetAutoGrab(not Utils.mm2.autoGrab)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.autoGrab)
    end)
    UI.groupToggle(tools, "Collect Coins", Utils.mm2.coins, function(t, i)
        Utils.mm2SetCoins(not Utils.mm2.coins)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.mm2.coins)
    end)
    local tpL = Instance.new("TextButton")
    tpL.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
    tpL.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    tpL.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    tpL.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tpL.Text = "TP to Lobby"
    tpL.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    tpL.TextSize = 12
    tpL.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    tpL.AutoButtonColor = false
    tpL.Parent = tools
    UI.addCorner(tpL, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.regCard(tpL)
    UI.regFont(tpL, 12)
    tpL.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.mm2TpLobby()
    end)
    local tpM = Instance.new("TextButton")
    tpM.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
    tpM.BackgroundColor3 = UI.cardColor()
    tpM.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    tpM.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tpM.Text = "TP to Map"
    tpM.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    tpM.TextSize = 12
    tpM.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    tpM.AutoButtonColor = false
    tpM.Parent = tools
    UI.addCorner(tpM, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.regCard(tpM)
    UI.regFont(tpM, 12)
    tpM.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.mm2TpMap()
    end)

    local box = UI.groupBox(UIRight or PageScroll, "Fling Player")
    local selectedPlr
    local drop = Instance.new("TextButton")
    drop.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
    drop.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    drop.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    drop.Text = "Select player"
    drop.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    drop.TextSize = 11
    drop.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    drop.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    drop.AutoButtonColor = false
    drop.Parent = box
    UI.addCorner(drop, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.addGlassStroke(drop, 1)
    UI.styleCard(drop)
    UI.regGlass(drop, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
    UI.regFont(drop, 11)
    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    list.Visible = false
    list.Parent = box
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Parent = list
    local function refreshList()
        for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end
        end
        local n = 0
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= Player then
                n = n + 1
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                row.Text = "  " .. plr.Name
                row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                row.TextSize = 11
                row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                row.AutoButtonColor = false
                row.Parent = list
                UI.regFont(row, 11)
                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    selectedPlr = plr
                    Utils.fling.target = plr
                    drop.Text = plr.Name
                    list.Visible = false
                    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                end)
            end
        end
        list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, n * 24)
    end
    drop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        if list.Visible then
            list.Visible = false
            list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        else
            refreshList()
            list.Visible = true
            list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140)
        end
    end)
    if Utils.fling.target then
        drop.Text = Utils.fling.target.Name
        selectedPlr = Utils.fling.target
    end
    local ft, fi = UI.groupToggle(box, "Fling", Utils.fling.active, function(t, i)
        if selectedPlr then Utils.fling.target = selectedPlr end
        Utils.flingSet(not Utils.fling.active)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.fling.active)
    end)
    R.flingToggleBtn = ft
    R.flingToggleIndicator = fi
    local keyRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    keyRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
    keyRow.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
    keyRow.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
    keyRow.Parent = box
    UI.addCorner(keyRow, UDim.new(0, 10))
    UI.addGlassStroke(keyRow, 1)
    UI.styleCard(keyRow)
    UI.regGlass(keyRow, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
    UI.uiLabel(keyRow, "Fling Key", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
    local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(keyRow, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0), Utils.fling.keybind)
    R.flingKeybindBtn = keyBtn
    keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        Utils.fling.listening = true
        listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
        keyBtn.Text = "..."
        keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
    end)
end

function Builders.buildWaypointPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    -- ===== Waypoints (Teleport tab) =====
    do
        local left = UI.groupBox(UILeft or PageScroll, "Waypoint Sets")
        local right = UI.groupBox(UIRight or PageScroll, "Manager")

        local function themedBtn(parent, label, callback)
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
            b.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
            b.Text = label
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            b.TextSize = 12
            b.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.Parent = parent
            UI.addCorner(b, UDim.new(0, 8))
            UI.styleCard(b)
            UI.regFont(b, 12)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                Utils.playClick()
                callback(b)
            end)
            return b
        end

        local listFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140)
        listFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        listFrame.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        listFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        listFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        listFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        listFrame.Parent = left
        UI.addCorner(listFrame, UDim.new(0, 8))
        UI.styleCard(listFrame)
        local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        listLay.Parent = listFrame

        local selectedSetLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        selectedSetLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
        selectedSetLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        selectedSetLabel.Text = "Selected: " .. tostring(Utils.waypoints.currentSet or "None")
        selectedSetLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        selectedSetLabel.TextSize = 11
        selectedSetLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        selectedSetLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        selectedSetLabel.Parent = left
        UI.regFont(selectedSetLabel, 11)

        local function refreshList()
            for _, ch in ipairs(listFrame:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
            end
            local names = Utils.wpListFiles()
            for _, n in ipairs(names) do
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 26)
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                row.Text = "  " .. n
                row.TextColor3 = (Utils.waypoints.currentSet == n) and Theme.Accent or Theme.Text
                row.TextSize = 12
                row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                row.AutoButtonColor = false
                row.Parent = listFrame
                UI.regFont(row, 12)
                row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    Utils.playClick()
                    Utils.waypoints.currentSet = n
                    Utils.wpLoadSet(n)
                    selectedSetLabel.Text = "Selected: " .. n
                    refreshList()
                end)
            end
            listFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(#names, 1) * 26)
            selectedSetLabel.Text = "Selected: " .. tostring(Utils.waypoints.currentSet or "None")
        end

        themedBtn(left, "Refresh", function()
            refreshList()
        end)

        local nameBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
        nameBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
        nameBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        nameBox.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        nameBox.PlaceholderText = "Set Name"
        nameBox.Text = Utils.waypoints.setNameInput or ""
        nameBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        nameBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        nameBox.TextSize = 12
        nameBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        nameBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        nameBox.Parent = left
        UI.addCorner(nameBox, UDim.new(0, 8))
        UI.styleCard(nameBox)
        UI.regFont(nameBox, 12)
        nameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            Utils.waypoints.setNameInput = nameBox.Text
        end)

        themedBtn(left, "Load", function()
            local n = Utils.waypoints.currentSet or nameBox.Text
            if n and n ~= "" then
                Utils.wpLoadSet(n)
                selectedSetLabel.Text = "Selected: " .. tostring(Utils.waypoints.currentSet or "None")
                refreshList()
            end
        end)
        themedBtn(left, "Save", function()
            local n = nameBox.Text
            if n == "" then n = Utils.waypoints.currentSet end
            if not n or n == "" then n = "Set1" end
            local pts = Utils.waypoints.sets[n] or Utils.waypoints.sets[Utils.waypoints.currentSet or ""] or {}
            Utils.wpSaveSet(n, pts)
            Utils.waypoints.currentSet = n
            nameBox.Text = n
            refreshList()
        end)
        themedBtn(left, "Delete", function()
            local n = Utils.waypoints.currentSet or nameBox.Text
            if n and n ~= "" then
                Utils.wpDeleteSet(n)
                refreshList()
            end
        end)
        themedBtn(left, "Add Waypoint Here", function()
            local n = nameBox.Text
            if n ~= "" then
                Utils.waypoints.currentSet = n
                Utils.waypoints.setNameInput = n
            end
            Utils.wpAddHere(nil)
            refreshList()
        end)
        themedBtn(left, "Delete Nearest Waypoint", function()
            if Utils.wpDeleteNearest() then
                refreshList()
            end
        end)

        task.defer(refreshList)

        -- Manager column
        UI.groupToggle(right, "Show Waypoint ESP", Utils.waypoints.showEsp, function(t, i)
            Utils.waypoints.showEsp = not Utils.waypoints.showEsp
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.waypoints.showEsp)
            if not Utils.waypoints.showEsp then Utils.wpClearDrawings() end
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(right, "Open Death Markers", Utils.waypoints.openDeathMarkers, function(t, i)
            Utils.waypoints.openDeathMarkers = not Utils.waypoints.openDeathMarkers
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.waypoints.openDeathMarkers)
            Utils.waypoints.logDeath = Utils.waypoints.openDeathMarkers
            Utils.wpHookDeaths()
        end)
        UI.createRangeSlider(right, "Max Distance", Utils.waypoints.maxDistance, 5000, function(v)
            Utils.waypoints.maxDistance = v
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(right, "Enable Icon", Utils.waypoints.enableIcon, function(t, i)
            Utils.waypoints.enableIcon = not Utils.waypoints.enableIcon
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.waypoints.enableIcon)
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(right, "Icon Size", Utils.waypoints.iconSize, 4, 48, function(v)
            Utils.waypoints.iconSize = v
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(right, "Icon Color", Utils.waypoints.iconColor or Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255), function(c)
            Utils.waypoints.iconColor = c
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(right, "Show Text", Utils.waypoints.showText ~= false, function(t, i)
            Utils.waypoints.showText = not (Utils.waypoints.showText ~= false)
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.waypoints.showText)
        end)
        UI.createColorPicker(right, "Text Color", Utils.waypoints.textColor or Color3.fromRGB(200, 230, 255), function(c)
            Utils.waypoints.textColor = c
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(right, "Text Size", Utils.waypoints.textSize or 14, 8, 28, function(v)
            Utils.waypoints.textSize = v
        end)
    end

end

function Builders.buildMiscPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)

        local tpBox = UI.groupBox(PageScroll, "Teleport to Player")
        local selectedPlr
        local drop = Instance.new("TextButton")
        drop.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        drop.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        drop.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        drop.Text = "Select player"
        drop.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        drop.TextSize = 11
        drop.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        drop.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        drop.AutoButtonColor = false
        drop.Parent = tpBox
        UI.addCorner(drop, UDim.new(0, 10))
        UI.addGlassStroke(drop, 1)
        UI.styleCard(drop)
        UI.regGlass(drop, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
        UI.regFont(drop, 11)
        local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
        search.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        search.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        search.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        search.PlaceholderText = "Search player..."
        search.Text = ""
        search.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        search.TextSize = 11
        search.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        search.Visible = false
        search.Parent = tpBox
        UI.addCorner(search, UDim.new(0, 10))
        UI.addGlassStroke(search, 1)
        UI.styleCard(search)
        UI.regGlass(search, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
        UI.regFont(search, 11)
        local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = tpBox
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        lay.Parent = list
        local function refreshList()
            for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end
            end
            local q = string.lower(tostring(search.Text or ""))
            local n = 0
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= Player then
                    local label = plr.DisplayName ~= plr.Name and (plr.DisplayName .. " (@" .. plr.Name .. ")") or plr.Name
                    local hay = string.lower(plr.Name .. " " .. plr.DisplayName)
                    if q == "" or string.find(hay, q, 1, true) then
                        n = n + 1
                        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
                        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        row.Text = "  " .. label
                        row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                        row.TextSize = 11
                        row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                        row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        row.AutoButtonColor = false
                        row.Parent = list
                        UI.regFont(row, 11)
                        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                            Utils.playClick()
                            selectedPlr = plr
                            Utils.tp.selected = plr
                            drop.Text = plr.Name
                            list.Visible = false
                            search.Visible = false
                            list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            if n == 0 then
                local empty = Instance.new("TextButton")
                empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
                empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                empty.Text = "  No matches"
                empty.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
                empty.TextSize = 11
                empty.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                empty.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                empty.AutoButtonColor = false
                empty.Parent = list
                UI.regFont(empty, 11)
                n = 1
            end
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, n * 24)
        end
        search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if list.Visible then refreshList() end
        end)
        drop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            if list.Visible then
                list.Visible = false
                search.Visible = false
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            else
                search.Visible = true
                refreshList()
                list.Visible = true
                list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140)
            end
        end)
        local tpBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        tpBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        tpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        tpBtn.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        tpBtn.Text = "Teleport to player"
        tpBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        tpBtn.TextSize = 11
        tpBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        tpBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        tpBtn.Parent = tpBox
        UI.addCorner(tpBtn, UDim.new(0, 10))
        UI.addGlassStroke(tpBtn, 1)
        UI.styleCard(tpBtn)
        UI.regGlass(tpBtn, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
        UI.regFont(tpBtn, 11)
        tpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            if selectedPlr then Utils.tp.selected = selectedPlr end
            Utils.tpTrigger(true)
            if Utils.tp.mode == "Press" then
                Utils.tpTrigger(false)
            end
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(tpBox, "Distance", Utils.tp.distance, 0, 30, function(v)
            Utils.tp.distance = v
        end)
        local modeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        modeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        modeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        modeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        modeBtn.Text = "Mode: " .. Utils.tp.mode
        modeBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        modeBtn.TextSize = 11
        modeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        modeBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        modeBtn.Parent = tpBox
        UI.addCorner(modeBtn, UDim.new(0, 10))
        UI.addGlassStroke(modeBtn, 1)
        UI.styleCard(modeBtn)
        UI.regGlass(modeBtn, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
        UI.regFont(modeBtn, 11)
        modeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            if Utils.tp.mode == "Press" then
                Utils.tp.mode = "Toggle"
            elseif Utils.tp.mode == "Toggle" then
                Utils.tp.mode = "Hold"
            else
                Utils.tp.mode = "Press"
                Utils.tp.active = false
                Utils.tp.holding = false
            end
            modeBtn.Text = "Mode: " .. Utils.tp.mode
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(tpBox, "Interval", Utils.tp.interval, 0.05, 5, function(v)
            Utils.tp.interval = v
        end)
        UI.groupToggle(tpBox, "Orbit", Utils.tp.orbit, function(t, i)
            Utils.tp.orbit = not Utils.tp.orbit
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, Utils.tp.orbit)
        end)
        UI.createDecimalSlider(tpBox, "Orbit Speed", Utils.tp.orbitSpeed, 0.1, 100, function(v)
            Utils.tp.orbitSpeed = v
        end)
        local keyRow = Instance.new("Frame")
        keyRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        keyRow.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        keyRow.BackgroundTransparency = UI.cardTransparency()
        keyRow.Parent = tpBox
        UI.addCorner(keyRow, UDim.new(0, 10))
        UI.addGlassStroke(keyRow, 1)
        UI.styleCard(keyRow)
        UI.regGlass(keyRow, UI.cardTransparency(), 0.62)
        UI.uiLabel(keyRow, "Teleport Key", UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), 11)
        local keyBtn = UI.uiKeybindBtn(keyRow, UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0), Utils.tp.keybind)
        R.tpKeybindBtn = keyBtn
        keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.tp.listening = true
            listenIgnoreUntil = tick() + 0.25
            keyBtn.Text = "..."
            keyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
        end)
        Builders.addTpClosestControls(tpBox)

        -- God Mode (Hood Customs)
        local GodToggleContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
        GodToggleContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)
        GodToggleContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        GodToggleContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
        GodToggleContainer.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(GodToggleContainer, UDim.new(0, 6))

        local GodLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        GodLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
        GodLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
        GodLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        GodLabel.Text = "God Mode (Hood Customs)"
        GodToggleContainer.Visible = false -- hidden for now
        GodLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        GodLabel.TextSize = 12
        GodLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        GodLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        GodLabel.Parent = GodToggleContainer
        UI.regFont(GodLabel, 12)

        local GodToggleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        GodToggleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 20)
        GodToggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, -10)
        GodToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        GodToggleBtn.Text = ""
        GodToggleBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        GodToggleBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        GodToggleBtn.Parent = GodToggleContainer
        UI.addCorner(GodToggleBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local GodToggleIndicator = Instance.new("Frame")
        GodToggleIndicator.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        GodToggleIndicator.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8)
        GodToggleIndicator.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        GodToggleIndicator.BorderSizePixel = 0
        GodToggleIndicator.Parent = GodToggleBtn
        UI.addCorner(GodToggleIndicator, UDim.new(1, 0))

        R.godToggleBtn = GodToggleBtn
        R.godToggleIndicator = GodToggleIndicator

        UI.applyToggleState(GodToggleBtn, GodToggleIndicator, godModeActive)

        GodToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.toggleGodMode()
        end)

        -- AFK Spoofer (Hood Customs)
        local AfkBox = Instance.new("Frame")
        AfkBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 44)
        AfkBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        AfkBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        AfkBox.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(AfkBox, UDim.new(0, 6))

        local AfkLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        AfkLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
        AfkLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
        AfkLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        AfkLabel.Text = "AFK Spoofer"
        AfkLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        AfkLabel.TextSize = 12
        AfkLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        AfkLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        AfkLabel.Parent = AfkBox
        UI.regFont(AfkLabel, 12)

        local AfkTog = Instance.new("TextButton")
        AfkTog.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 20)
        AfkTog.Position = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, -10)
        AfkTog.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        AfkTog.Text = ""
        AfkTog.AutoButtonColor = false
        AfkTog.BorderSizePixel = 0
        AfkTog.Parent = AfkBox
        UI.addCorner(AfkTog, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local AfkInd = Instance.new("Frame")
        AfkInd.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        AfkInd.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8)
        AfkInd.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        AfkInd.BorderSizePixel = 0
        AfkInd.Parent = AfkTog
        UI.addCorner(AfkInd, UDim.new(1, 0))

        R.afkToggleBtn = AfkTog
        R.afkToggleIndicator = AfkInd
        UI.applyToggleState(AfkTog, AfkInd, Utils.afkSpoofActive)

        AfkTog.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.toggleAfkSpoof()
        end)

        AfkBox.Visible = (selectedGameMode == "Hood Customs")
end

function Builders.buildAvatarPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    -- Headless + Korblox side by side
    do
        local row = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 44)
        local leftLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        leftLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -50, 1, 0)
        leftLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
        leftLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        leftLbl.Text = "Headless"
        leftLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        leftLbl.TextSize = 12
        leftLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        leftLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        leftLbl.Parent = row
        UI.regFont(leftLbl, 12)
        local ht, hi = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0.5, 0))
        UI.applyToggleState(ht, hi, headless.active)
        ht.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.applyHeadless(not headless.active)
            UI.applyToggleState(ht, hi, headless.active)
        end)

        local rightLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        rightLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -50, 1, 0)
        rightLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 12, 0, 0)
        rightLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rightLbl.Text = "Korblox Right Leg"
        rightLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        rightLbl.TextSize = 12
        rightLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        rightLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        rightLbl.Parent = row
        UI.regFont(rightLbl, 12)
        local kt, ki = UI.uiToggleSwitch(row, UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0))
        UI.applyToggleState(kt, ki, korblox.active)
        kt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Utils.applyKorblox(not korblox.active)
            UI.applyToggleState(kt, ki, korblox.active)
        end)
    end

    do
        local AccTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        AccTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28)
        AccTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        AccTitle.Text = "Accessories"
        AccTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        AccTitle.TextSize = 12
        AccTitle.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        AccTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        AccTitle.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.regFont(AccTitle, 12)

        local idBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
        idBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 32)
        idBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        idBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        idBox.PlaceholderText = "  Accessory asset ID..."
        idBox.Text = ""
        idBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        idBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        idBox.TextSize = 11
        idBox.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        idBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        idBox.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(idBox, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(idBox, 11)

        local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 32)
        addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
        addBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        addBtn.Text = "Add Accessory"
        addBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        addBtn.TextSize = 12
        addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        addBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        addBtn.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(addBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(addBtn, 12)

        local statusLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        statusLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18)
        statusLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        statusLbl.Text = ""
        statusLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        statusLbl.TextSize = 10
        statusLbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        statusLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        statusLbl.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.regFont(statusLbl, 10)

        addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            local ok, msg = Utils.addAccessoryById(idBox.Text)
            if ok then
                statusLbl.Text = "Added: " .. tostring(msg)
                statusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 100)
            else
                statusLbl.Text = "Error: " .. tostring(msg)
                statusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
            end
        end)

        local dropOpen = false
        local dropBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        dropBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
        dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        dropBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dropBtn.Text = "  Remove Accessory   ▼"
        dropBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        dropBtn.TextSize = 11
        dropBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        dropBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        dropBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        dropBtn.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(dropBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(dropBtn, 11)

        local list = Instance.new("Frame")
        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        list.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        list.BorderSizePixel = 0
        list.ClipsDescendants = true
        list.Visible = false
        list.Parent = PageScroll
        UI.addCorner(list, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        listLay.Parent = list

        local function rebuildAccessoryList()
            for _, ch in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
            end
            local accs = Utils.listCharacterAccessories()
            if #accs == 0 then
                local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 28)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.Text = "  (none equipped)"
                row.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
                row.TextSize = 11
                row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                row.AutoButtonColor = false
                row.Parent = list
                UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
            else
                for _, acc in ipairs(accs) do
                    local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 28)
                    row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
                    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    row.Text = "  Remove: " .. acc.Name
                    row.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                    row.TextSize = 11
                    row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
                    row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    row.AutoButtonColor = false
                    row.Parent = list
                    UI.addCorner(row, UDim.new(0, 4))
                    UI.regFont(row, 11)
                    row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        Utils.playClick()
                        Utils.removeAccessoryInstance(acc)
                        statusLbl.Text = "Removed: " .. acc.Name
                        statusLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
                        rebuildAccessoryList()
                        local n = math.max(#Utils.listCharacterAccessories(), 1)
                        list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, n * 30 + 6)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        dropBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            dropOpen = not dropOpen
            if dropOpen then
                rebuildAccessoryList()
                local n = math.max(#Utils.listCharacterAccessories(), 1)
                local h = n * 30 + 6
                list.Visible = true
                TweenService:Create(list, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, h)}):Play()
                dropBtn.Text = "  Remove Accessory   ▲"
            else
                local tw = TweenService:Create(list, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)})
                tw:Play()
                tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                    if not dropOpen then list.Visible = false end
                end)
                dropBtn.Text = "  Remove Accessory   ▼"
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Preview settings at the bottom
    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Live Sync (follows you)")
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, AP.liveSync)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            AP.liveSync = not AP.liveSync
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, AP.liveSync)
            if AP.liveSync and AP.open then
                task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
            end
        end)
    end
    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "No Delay Sync")
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, AP.liveNoDelay)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            AP.liveNoDelay = not AP.liveNoDelay
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, AP.liveNoDelay)
        end)
    end
    do
        local box = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 44)
        UI.uiLabel(box, "Live Spin")
        local t, i = UI.uiToggleSwitch(box)
        UI.applyToggleState(t, i, AP.liveSpin)
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            AP.liveSpin = not AP.liveSpin
            UI.applyToggleState(t, i, AP.liveSpin)
            if AP.liveSpin and AP.open then
                task.defer(Utils.refreshAvatarPreview)
            end
        end)
    end
end


Builders.rebuildPageUI = function(pageName)
    currentActiveTab = pageName
    Utils.updateSidebarSelection()

    R.statsPlayerCountLbl = nil
    R.statsMaxPlayersLbl = nil
    R.statsFpsLbl = nil
    R.hideKeybindBtn = nil
    R.camlockKeybindBtn = nil
    R.zoomAimKeybindBtn = nil
    R.zoomAimToggleBtn = nil
    R.zoomAimToggleIndicator = nil
    R.flyKeybindBtn = nil
    R.flyToggleBtn = nil
    R.flyToggleIndicator = nil
    R.flyExtra = nil

    for _, child in pairs(ContentContainer:GetChildren()) do
        child:Destroy()
    end

    do
        local prunedAccent = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(accentRegistry) do
            if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then table.insert(prunedAccent, e) end
        end
        accentRegistry = prunedAccent

        local prunedFont = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(fontSizeRegistry) do
            if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then table.insert(prunedFont, e) end
        end
        fontSizeRegistry = prunedFont
    end

    local PageScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    PageScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    PageScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    PageScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    PageScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    PageScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    PageScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Border
    PageScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    PageScroll.ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Always
    PageScroll.Parent = ContentContainer
    Utils.ui = Utils.ui or {}
    Utils.ui.PageScroll = PageScroll
    pcall(function()
        PageScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    end)
    do
        local pagePad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pagePad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
        pagePad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 16)
        pagePad.Parent = PageScroll
    end

    local PageListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    PageListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    PageListLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    PageListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    PageListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    PageListLayout.Parent = PageScroll

    local function refreshPageCanvas()
        PageScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, PageListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 24)
    end
    PageListLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(refreshPageCanvas)
    task.defer(refreshPageCanvas)
    task.delay(0.1, refreshPageCanvas)
    task.delay(0.35, refreshPageCanvas)

    -- Two-column layout for denser tabs
    local UILeft, UIRight = PageScroll, PageScroll
    local useTwoCols = (pageName == "VISUAL" or pageName == "EFFECTS" or pageName == "WAYPOINTS" or pageName == "AIMBOT" or pageName == "MOVEMENT" or pageName == "DH SKINS" or pageName == "HC SKINS" or pageName == "COMBAT" or pageName == "ESP" or pageName == "UTILITY" or pageName == "RAGE" or pageName == "AUTOKILL" or pageName == "UTILITIES")
    if useTwoCols then
        local ColHost = Instance.new("Frame")
        ColHost.Name = "ColHost"
        ColHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0)
        ColHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ColHost.Parent = PageScroll

        UILeft = Instance.new("Frame")
        UILeft.Name = "UILeft"
        UILeft.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, 0)
        UILeft.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        UILeft.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        UILeft.Parent = ColHost

        local LeftLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        LeftLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        LeftLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        LeftLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        LeftLayout.Parent = UILeft

        UIRight = Instance.new("Frame")
        UIRight.Name = "UIRight"
        UIRight.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, 0)
        UIRight.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0)
        UIRight.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        UIRight.Parent = ColHost

        local RightLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        RightLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        RightLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        RightLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        RightLayout.Parent = UIRight

        local function updateCols()
            local lh = LeftLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            local rh = RightLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            local h = math.max(lh, rh) + 10
            UILeft.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, lh + 4)
            UIRight.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, rh + 4)
            ColHost.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, h)
            PageScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, h + 10)
        end
        LeftLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
            updateCols()
            task.defer(refreshPageCanvas)
        end)
        RightLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
            updateCols()
            task.defer(refreshPageCanvas)
        end)
        task.defer(updateCols)
        task.delay(0.05, updateCols)
        task.delay(0.2, updateCols)
        task.delay(0.5, updateCols)
        task.delay(0.1, refreshPageCanvas)
        task.delay(0.4, refreshPageCanvas)
    end

    if pageName == "RAGE" or pageName == "FORCEHIT" then
        if selectedGameMode ~= "MM2" then
            UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatFH", {
                enabled = false, fov = false, bulletviz = false,
                targetviz = false, tpmode = false, tpreturn = false,
            }, {
                { key = "enabled", label = "Enabled", build = function(host)
                    Builders._fhSection = "enabled"

                { key = "bulletviz", label = "Bullet Visualizer", build = function(host)
                    Builders._fhSection = "bulletviz"
                    pcall(Builders.buildForceHitSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                    Builders._fhSection = nil
                end },
                { key = "targetviz", label = "Target Visualizer", build = function(host)
                    Builders._fhSection = "targetviz"
                    pcall(Builders.buildForceHitSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                    Builders._fhSection = nil
                end },
                { key = "tpmode", label = "Teleport Mode", build = function(host)
                    Builders._fhSection = "tpmode"
                    pcall(Builders.buildForceHitSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                    Builders._fhSection = nil
                end },
                { key = "tpreturn", label = "Return to Origin", build = function(host)
                    Builders._fhSection = "tpreturn"
                    pcall(Builders.buildForceHitSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                    Builders._fhSection = nil
                end },
            })
        end
    elseif pageName == "AUTOKILL" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatAK", {
            enabled = false, mode = false, target = false, fullbright = false, voidspam = false, spectate = false,
            predict = false, teleport = false, randomstomp = false, lowhp = false, status = false, abovehead = false, orbit = false,
        }, {
            { key = "enabled", label = "Enabled", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "enabled"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "mode", label = "Kill Mode", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "mode"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "target", label = "Target", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "target"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "fullbright", label = "Fullbright", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "fullbright"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "voidspam", label = "Void Spam", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "voidspam"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "spectate", label = "Spectate Target", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "spectate"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "predict", label = "Predict", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "predict"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "teleport", label = "Teleport Settings", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "teleport"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "randomstomp", label = "Random Stomp", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "randomstomp"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "lowhp", label = "Low HP Auto Stomp", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "lowhp"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "status", label = "Status", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "status"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "abovehead", label = "Teleport Mode", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "abovehead"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "orbit", label = "Orbit Settings", build = function(host)
                Builders._akSection = "orbit"
                pcall(Builders.buildAutoKillSection, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._akSection = nil
            end },
        })
    elseif pageName == "PLAYER LIST" then
        Builders.buildPlayerListPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    elseif pageName == "HOME" then
        local box = UI.uiSection(PageScroll, 52)
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = tostring(selectedGameMode or "Game") .. " — coming soon"
        lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        lbl.TextSize = 13
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(lbl, 13)
    elseif pageName == "COMBAT" then
    elseif pageName == "UTILITIES" then
        Builders.buildUtilitiesPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    elseif pageName == "UTILITY" then
        if selectedGameMode == "MM2" then
            Builders.buildMm2Page(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        else
            Builders.buildMiscPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
        end
    elseif pageName == "AIMBOT" then
        Builders.buildAimbotPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    elseif pageName == "MOVEMENT" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatMov", {
            speed = false, quickstop = false, instantfall = false, hip = false,
            freecam = false, fly = false, jump = false, gravity = false, spin = false,
        }, {
            { key = "speed", label = "Speed", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "speed"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "quickstop", label = "Quick Stop", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "quickstop"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "instantfall", label = "Instant Fall", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "instantfall"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "hip", label = "Hip Height", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "hip"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "freecam", label = "Freecam", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "freecam"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "fly", label = "Fly", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "fly"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "jump", label = "Jump", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "jump"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "gravity", label = "Gravity", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "gravity"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "spin", label = "Spinbot", build = function(host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = "spin"
                Builders.buildMovementPage(PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._movBuildSection = nil
            end },
        })
    elseif pageName == "ESP" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatEsp", {
            names = false, distance = false, box = false, health = false, skeleton = false, extra = false,
        }, {
            { key = "names", label = "Names", build = function(host)
                Builders._espSection = "names"
                pcall(Builders.buildEspPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._espSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "distance", label = "Distance", build = function(host)
                Builders._espSection = "distance"
                pcall(Builders.buildEspPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._espSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "box", label = "Box", build = function(host)
                Builders._espSection = "box"
                pcall(Builders.buildEspPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._espSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "health", label = "Health Bar", build = function(host)
                Builders._espSection = "health"
                pcall(Builders.buildEspPage, PageScroll, host, host)

            end },

                Builders._visSection = "depthof"
                pcall(Builders.buildVisualPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._visSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "ambience", label = "Ambience", build = function(host)
                Builders._visSection = "ambience"
                pcall(Builders.buildVisualPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._visSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "outdoor", label = "Outdoor", build = function(host)
                Builders._visSection = "outdoor"
                pcall(Builders.buildVisualPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._visSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "tint", label = "Tint", build = function(host)
                Builders._visSection = "tint"
                pcall(Builders.buildVisualPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._visSection = nil
            end },
        })
    elseif pageName == "EFFECTS" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatFx", { trail = false, aura = false }, {
            { key = "trail", label = "Trail", build = function(host)
                Builders._fxSection = "trail"
                pcall(Builders.buildEffectsPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._fxSection = nil
            end },
            { key = "aura", label = "Aura", build = function(host)
                Builders._fxSection = "aura"
                pcall(Builders.buildEffectsPage, PageScroll, host, host)
                Builders._fxSection = nil
            end },
        })
    elseif pageName == "WAYPOINTS" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatWp", { panel = false }, {
            { key = "panel", label = "Waypoints", build = function(host)
                pcall(function() Builders.buildWaypointPage(PageScroll, host, host) end)
            end },
        })
    elseif pageName == "DH SKINS" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatDhSkins", { panel = false }, {
            { key = "panel", label = "DH Skins", build = function(host)
                pcall(function() Builders.buildSkinsPage(PageScroll, host, host) end)
            end },
        })
    elseif pageName == "HC SKINS" then
        UI.buildFeatureGatePage(UILeft or PageScroll, UIRight or PageScroll, "uiFeatHcSkins", { panel = false }, {
            { key = "panel", label = "HC Skins", build = function(host)
                pcall(function() Builders.buildHcSkinsPage(PageScroll, host, host) end)
            end },
        })
    
    elseif pageName == "AVATAR" then
        Builders.buildAvatarPage(PageScroll, UILeft, UIRight)
    elseif pageName == "STATS" then
        pcall(function()
            Builders.buildStatsPage(PageScroll, PageScroll, PageScroll)
        end)
    elseif pageName == "CONFIGS" then
        pcall(function()
            Builders.buildConfigsPage(PageScroll, PageScroll, PageScroll)
        end)
    elseif pageName == "SETTINGS" then
        -- Full page layout (not feature-gate toggles)
        pcall(function()
            Builders.buildSettingsPage(PageScroll, PageScroll, PageScroll)
        end)
    elseif pageName == "MISC" then
        local box = UI.groupBox(PageScroll, "Misc")
        local note = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        note.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 28)
        note.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        note.Text = "Coming soon"
        note.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        note.TextSize = 12
        note.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        note.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        note.Parent = box
        UI.regFont(note, 12)
    end
    pcall(function()
        if Utils.setAvatarPreviewVisible then
            Utils.setAvatarPreviewVisible(pageName == "AVATAR")
        end
    end)
    pcall(function() Utils.applyGlassUI(glassUIActive) end)
    pcall(function() Utils.unifyFeatureSurfaces(ContentContainer) end)
    pcall(function() Utils.updateSidebarSelection() end)
end
