    ["PLAYER LIST"] = { icon = "", label = "Player List", group = "UTILITY" },
    MISC = { icon = "", label = "Misc", group = "UTILITY" },
    SETTINGS = { icon = "", label = "Settings", group = "OTHER" },
    STATS = { icon = "", label = "Stats", group = "OTHER" },
    HOME = { icon = "", label = "Configs", group = "OTHER" },
    CONFIGS = { icon = "", label = "Configs", group = "OTHER" },
}

Utils.buildSidebarTabs = function()
    for _, ch in ipairs(Sidebar:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("TextButton") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") or ch:IsA("TextBox") or ch:IsA("Frame") then
            if ch.Name ~= "UIListLayout" and ch.Name ~= "UIPadding" and ch.Name ~= "RaysTitle" and ch.Name ~= "SideSearch" then
                ch:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    for k in pairs(tabButtons) do tabButtons[k] = nil end
    local order = { "COMBAT", "VISUAL", "UTILITY", "PLAYER", "OTHER" }
    local grouped = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(tabNames) do
        local meta = Utils.tabMeta[name] or { icon = "•", label = name, group = "PANEL" }
        grouped[meta.group] = grouped[meta.group] or {}
        table.insert(grouped[meta.group], name)
    end
    local layoutOrder = 0
    local function addHeader(text)
        layoutOrder = layoutOrder + 1
        local hdr = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hdr.Name = "SideHead"
        hdr.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 16)
        hdr.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hdr.Text = text
        hdr.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        hdr.TextTransparency = 0.45
        hdr.TextSize = 10
        hdr.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        hdr.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hdr.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
        hdr.Parent = Sidebar
        pcall(function()
            hdr.FontFace = Font.fromName("BuilderSans", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
        end)
        UI.regFont(hdr, 10)
    end
    local function addTab(name)
        local meta = Utils.tabMeta[name] or { icon = "•", label = name }
        layoutOrder = layoutOrder + 1
        local TabBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        TabBtn.Name = name
        TabBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 26)
        TabBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        TabBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        TabBtn.Text = "  " .. (meta.label or name)
        TabBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        TabBtn.TextSize = 12
        TabBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        pcall(function()
            TabBtn.FontFace = Font.fromName("BuilderSans", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
        end)
        TabBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        TabBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        TabBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        TabBtn.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
        TabBtn.Parent = Sidebar
        UI.addCorner(TabBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(TabBtn, 12)
        TabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            Builders.rebuildPageUI(name)
        end)
        tabButtons[name] = TabBtn
    end
    for _, group in ipairs(order) do
        local list = grouped[group]
        if list and #list > 0 then
            addHeader(group)
            for _, name in ipairs(list) do
                addTab(name)
            end
        end
    end
    if not Utils.sideSearch then
        local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
        search.Name = "SideSearch"
        search.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 26)
        search.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -34)
        search.Parent = Sidebar
        search.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary
        search.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp((Theme.CardT or 0.5) + 0.15, 0.2, 0.85)
        search.BorderSizePixel = 0
        search.PlaceholderText = "  Search features..."
        search.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        search.Text = ""
        search.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        search.TextSize = 11
        search.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        search.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        search.ZIndex = 4
        UI.addCorner(search, UDim.new(0, 6))
        UI.regFont(search, 11)
        Utils.sideSearch = search
        search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            local q = string.lower(search.Text or "")
            for name, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
                local meta = Utils.tabMeta[name]
                local hay = string.lower((meta and meta.label or name) .. " " .. name)
                btn.Visible = (q == "") or string.find(hay, q, 1, true) ~= nil
            end
        end)
    end
    Utils.updateSidebarSelection()
end

Utils.finishPanelBoot = function()
    rebuildTabNames()
    Utils.buildSidebarTabs()
    pcall(function()
        ScreenGui.Enabled = true
        ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 100000
        MainFrame.Visible = true
        MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, PANEL_H)
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not getgenv().RaysPanelIntroPlayed then
            getgenv().RaysPanelIntroPlayed = true
            local s = Instance.new("Sound")
            s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://99453815582587"
            s.Volume = 0.55
            s.Parent = Utils.SoundService
            s:Play()
            game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s, 60)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Config and Config.supported and Config.supported() then
            Config.activeName = Config.getRememberedProfile()
            Config.load(Config.activeName, false)
        end
    end)
    -- Always start with game lighting (no saved pink fog)
    pcall(function()
        fogActive = false
        fullbrightActive = false
        forceTimeActive = false
        brightnessActive = false
        ambienceActive = false
        if Utils.restoreGameLighting then Utils.restoreGameLighting() end
        if Utils.resetSessionDefaults then Utils.resetSessionDefaults() end
    end)
    pcall(function() Utils.applyTheme("Midnight") end)
    local okBuild, errBuild = pcall(function()
        Builders.rebuildPageUI(tabNames[1] or "AIMBOT")
    end)
    if not okBuild then
        warn("[Zenith] UI build error:", errBuild)
    end
    pcall(function() Utils.applyGlassUI(glassUIActive) end)
    silentAim.enabled = false
    pcall(Utils.updatePanelBgImage)
end

Builders.startRaysPanel = function()
    -- All locals live HERE (fresh register set), not in the main chunk
        local GS = {}
    pcall(function()
        ScreenGui.Enabled = true
        ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 100000
        MainFrame.Visible = false
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if gethui then
            ScreenGui.Parent = gethui()
        end
    end)

    local function refresh()
        if not GS.frame then return end
        if currentThemeName == "Midnight" or currentThemeName == "Cotton Candy" or currentThemeName == "Lavender" or currentThemeName == "Ash" or currentThemeName == "Bubblegum" then
            GS.frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        else
            GS.frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
        end
        if GS.grad then
            GS.grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.GradTop or Theme.Primary),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.GradMid or Theme.Primary),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.GradBottom or Theme.Primary),
            })
        end
        if GS.stroke then
            GS.stroke.Color = (currentThemeName == "Midnight") and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or (Theme.Border or Color3.fromRGB(99, 18, 22))
        end
        local id, t = nil, 1
        if currentThemeName == "Midnight" then id, t = "rbxassetid://17793479623", 0.88
        elseif currentThemeName == "Crimson" then id, t = "rbxassetid://79903315865408", 0.94
        elseif currentThemeName == "Cotton Candy" then id, t = "rbxassetid://14424561053", 0.78
        elseif currentThemeName == "Bubblegum" then id, t = "rbxassetid://97023542098667", 0.82
        elseif currentThemeName == "Ash" then id, t = "rbxassetid://138505460833612", 0.9
        elseif currentThemeName == "Galaxy" then id, t = "rbxassetid://1025773173", 0.9
        end
        if GS.bg then
            if id and panelBgVisible then
                GS.bg.Image = id
                GS.bg.ImageTransparency = t
                GS.bg.Visible = true
            else
                GS.bg.Visible = false
            end
        end
        if GS.title then GS.title.TextColor3 = Theme.Text end
        if GS.sub then GS.sub.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark end
        if GS.themeDrop then
            GS.themeDrop.Text = "  Theme: " .. tostring(currentThemeName) .. (GS.themeOpen and "   ▲" or "   ▼")
            GS.themeDrop.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            GS.themeDrop.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
        end
        if GS.bgBtn then
            GS.bgBtn.Visible = false
            local has = Utils.themeHasBgImage(currentThemeName)
            GS.bgBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
            GS.bgBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            if has then
                GS.bgBtn.Text = panelBgVisible and "Background Image: On" or "Background Image: Off"
            end
        end
        if GS.list then
            for _, ch in ipairs(GS.list:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
                    ch.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
                    ch.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                    local tag = ch:FindFirstChild("Status")
                    if tag then tag.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark end
                end
            end
        end
        if GS.search then
            GS.search.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Secondary or Theme.Primary
            GS.search.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
            GS.search.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
        end
    end

    GS.frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    GS.frame.Name = "GameSelect"
    GS.frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 460)
    GS.frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0.5, -230)
    GS.frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Primary
    GS.frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    GS.frame.ZIndex = 50
    GS.frame.Parent = ScreenGui
    UI.addCorner(GS.frame, UDim.new(0, 12))

    GS.bg = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    GS.bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    GS.bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    GS.bg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    GS.bg.ZIndex = 50
    GS.bg.Visible = false
    GS.bg.Parent = GS.frame
    UI.addCorner(GS.bg, UDim.new(0, 12))

    GS.grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    GS.grad.Rotation = 45
    GS.grad.Parent = GS.frame

    GS.stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    GS.stroke.Thickness = 2
    GS.stroke.Parent = GS.frame

    GS.title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    GS.title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 36)
    GS.title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 12)
    GS.title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    GS.title.Text = "R a y ' s  P a n e l"
    GS.title.TextSize = 18
    GS.title.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    GS.title.ZIndex = 51
    GS.title.Parent = GS.frame

    GS.sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    GS.sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
    GS.sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42)
    GS.sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    GS.sub.Text = "Select a game"
    GS.sub.TextSize = 11
    GS.sub.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    GS.sub.ZIndex = 51
    GS.sub.Parent = GS.frame

    GS.search = Instance.new("TextBox")
    GS.search.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32)
    GS.search.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 68)
    GS.search.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    GS.search.BorderSizePixel = 0
    GS.search.PlaceholderText = "Search games..."
    GS.search.Text = ""
    GS.search.TextSize = 13
    GS.search.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    GS.search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    GS.search.ZIndex = 51
    GS.search.Parent = GS.frame
    UI.addCorner(GS.search, UDim.new(0, 6))

    GS.list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    GS.list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 210)
    GS.list.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 108)
    GS.list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    GS.list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    GS.list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    GS.list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    GS.list.ZIndex = 51
    GS.list.Parent = GS.frame
    local listLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    listLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    listLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    listLay.Parent = GS.list

    local games = {
        { name = "Da Hood", status = "Ready" },
        { name = "Hood Customs", status = "Ready" },
        { name = "MM2", status = "BETA" },
    }

    local function pickGame(mode)
        Utils.playClick()
        applyGameMode(mode)
        if GS.frame then GS.frame:Destroy() end
        GS.frame = nil
        Utils.finishPanelBoot()
    end

    local rows = {}
    for i, g in ipairs(games) do
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = g.name
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 38)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = "  " .. g.name
        btn.TextSize = 14
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 52
        btn.LayoutOrder = i
        btn.Parent = GS.list
        UI.addCorner(btn, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local tag = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        tag.Name = "Status"
        tag.Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 1, 0)
        tag.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0, 0)
        tag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tag.Text = g.status
        tag.TextSize = 11
        tag.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        tag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        tag.ZIndex = 53
        tag.Parent = btn
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            pickGame(g.name)
        end)
        rows[#rows + 1] = btn
    end
    listLay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        GS.list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, listLay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 8)
    end)

    GS.search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local q = string.lower(GS.search.Text or "")
        for _, btn in ipairs(rows) do
            btn.Visible = (q == "") or string.find(string.lower(btn.Name), q, 1, true) ~= nil
        end
    end)

    GS.themeDrop = Instance.new("TextButton")
    GS.themeDrop.Visible = false
    GS.themeDrop.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32)
    GS.themeDrop.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 326)
    GS.themeDrop.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    GS.themeDrop.BorderSizePixel = 0
    GS.themeDrop.TextSize = 13
    GS.themeDrop.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    GS.themeDrop.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    GS.themeDrop.AutoButtonColor = false
    GS.themeDrop.ZIndex = 52
    GS.themeDrop.Parent = GS.frame
    UI.addCorner(GS.themeDrop, UDim.new(0, 8))

    GS.themeList = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    GS.themeList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 0)
    GS.themeList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 360)
    GS.themeList.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    GS.themeList.BorderSizePixel = 0
    GS.themeList.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    GS.themeList.Visible = false
    GS.themeList.ZIndex = 55
    GS.themeList.ClipsDescendants = true
    GS.themeList.Parent = GS.frame
    UI.addCorner(GS.themeList, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    lay.Parent = GS.themeList
    GS.themeOpen = false

    for i, name in ipairs(THEME_NAMES) do
        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Text = "  " .. name
        row.TextSize = 12
        row.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
        row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        row.AutoButtonColor = false
        row.ZIndex = 56
        row.LayoutOrder = i
        row.Parent = GS.themeList
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Utils.playClick()
            pcall(function() Utils.applyTheme(name) end)
            GS.themeOpen = false
            GS.themeList.Visible = false
            GS.themeList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0)
            refresh()
        end)
    end

    GS.themeDrop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        GS.themeOpen = not GS.themeOpen
        if GS.themeOpen then
            GS.themeList.Visible = true
            GS.themeList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, math.min(#THEME_NAMES * 28, 88))
            GS.themeList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #THEME_NAMES * 28)
        else
            GS.themeList.Visible = false
            GS.themeList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 0)
        end
        refresh()
    end)

    GS.bgBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    GS.bgBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32)
    GS.bgBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 362)
    GS.bgBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    GS.bgBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    GS.bgBtn.TextSize = 13
    GS.bgBtn.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    GS.bgBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    GS.bgBtn.ZIndex = 51
    GS.bgBtn.Parent = GS.frame
    UI.addCorner(GS.bgBtn, UDim.new(0, 8))
    GS.bgBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Utils.playClick()
        panelBgVisible = not panelBgVisible
        pcall(Utils.updatePanelBgImage)
        refresh()
    end)

    local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 40)
    hint.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -40)
    hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hint.Text = "Click a game to load the panel."
    hint.TextSize = 10
    hint.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSans
    hint.TextWrapped = true
    hint.ZIndex = 51
    hint.Parent = GS.frame

    refresh()
end

pcall(function()
        local ok, err = xpcall(Builders.startRaysPanel, function(e)
        return debug.traceback(tostring(e), 2)
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[Zenith] Boot error:", err)
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "RaysBootError"
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Size = UDim2.new(0, 400, 0, 80)
        t.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -200, 0.1, 0)
        t.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 0, 0)
        t.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        t.TextWrapped = true
        t.Text = "Rays Panel failed to open:\n" .. tostring(err):sub(1, 180)
        t.Parent = sg
    end
end)
